**Okuka-Avery.**

Owangifundisa ukukhumula izibuko zami, ngiguqe ngifinyelele ebudeni bakho, ngikubheke emehlweni, futhi ngikubone
*ngokweqiniso* — nokukhuluma nomntwana oneminyaka emihlanu njengomuntu olalelwayo, okuyilona kanye into esibhekene nayo sonke
kodwa esivame ukuyiphiwa kancane kangaka. Wangifundisa ukuthi isidingo esibhekene namandla siyisidingo esenqatshelweyo, nokuthi
indlela engenayo ayisiyo neze indlela ongasunduza ngayo; ukuthi abanamandla akumelwe ukuba bagolozele abancane. Singamadoda
amakhulu, futhi siyakhohlwa ukuthi sisebenzisa imizimba eyakhelwe ukuvikela njengemizimba esabisayo — futhi siyiqondisa,
singahlosile, kubantu kanye esingafela bona ngokuphazima kweso. Wangelapha kulokho. Yonke into ethambile kulezi zincwadi —
indoda engaphihliza ikamelo kodwa ekhetha esikhundleni salokho ukulibona; *ngiyakubona* okushiwo kuze kusho ukuthi
*uphephile* — wangifundisa kuqala wena, ngezandla zakho ezincane ezithukuthele kodwa ezithambile.

Ukhanya ngokungalinganiseki, futhi umnene kuzo zonke izilwane, futhi uyazithanda izinto zebutho, izinto zokusinda,
ukwazi-ukwenza-into-iphumelele. Ngakho-ke le yeyakho, futhi iphethe okuwukuphela kobubutho enginakho ukukudlulisela:

*Yiba ngongesabi njenge-African honey badger* — incane, futhi ingesabi lutho egcekeni le-veld; iyamba,
iyagqekeza, ayidedeli. Vele usuyilokho. Mina ngikubhale phansi nje.

# Umhlaba Uyakhumbula

Ngaphambi kokuba abantu baphathe amapiki, kwakukhona inxeba kuphela.

Ucezu lohlelo lwelanga lwakuqala lwagijimela emhlabeni, lushayisana nalokho okwakuzoba yi-Vredefort dome. Inhlamvu yasemkhathini, ububanzi obungamakhilomitha ayishumi nanhlanu, isika emkhathini ngokushesha okudlula umcabango. Yashayisa ngamandla angaphezu kokuqonda komuntu, igubha umgodi ongangezwe elincane. Ngaleso sikhathi, yabhoboza uqweqwe lomhlaba, ithumela idwala elincibilikile liphahluka emoyeni njengenqindi eliphuma eqhweni.

Umhlaba awuzange usinde kuleso shayisana. Waguquka. Lokho okwavela ekujuleni, kwapholile kwaqhekeka kwephuca iminyaka eyizinkulungwane, kwaba yinto entsha ngokuphelele. Ukushayisana kwakuphethe idwala elijulile kwaliqondisa ngasophezulu, kwacijisa imithambo eyayingcwatshiwe kwadalula ingcebo eyayifihliwe kusukela umhlaba usancibilika. Igolide lalilele emithanjeni emincane kuyo yonke i-Witwatersrand Basin, lithandelene namatshana e-quartz, lacindezelwa lashiswa izikhathi ezingapheli ngaphambi kokuba ukushayisa kuwaqhuzule, ekugcineni kuwanikele ekukhanyeni.

Umhlaba wawuthwele izibazi zawo emqansweni, ezintatshaneni eziyingxenye yendingilizi ezigobele ngaphesheya kwe-highveld njengomphetho wensimbi eshayiweyo. Yima kulowo mphetho ekuseni, idwala libe libanda ngaphansi kwentende yakho, linonqolokela nothuli olubomvu, umoya ushaya usuka endaweni ephakeme uwomile njengomlotha. Ndawana thile ngaphesheya kotshani, ithekwane likhala kanye, lithule. Izazi zomhlaba zafika kamuva, ziwuqamba ziwudwebela udlame lwawo, kodwa udlame lwabambelela noma kunjalo. Izibazi azidingi ofakazi.

---

Kwase kufika imvula, nemifula ehambisayo, notshani, futhi izikhathi ezingapheli akukho okwakuhamba lapha okwakuqonda lokho okwakulele ngaphansi. Igolide lahlala lithulile, lingcwatshwe ekujuleni.

Kwase kufika abantu.

---

Abantu bafika ngenxa yensimbi. Bafika kuqala namapiki, base befika nomusi, kwalandela imishini eyayikwazi ukudla imitha yedwala ngehora ngaphandle kokukhathala noma imibuzo. Bathola ukuthi imithambo igxile ekujuleni, ijule kunalokho noma ubani ayekucabangile, ishona iqhela endingilizini yokushayisana ngegumbi umhlaba owawulibekile eminyakeni eyizigidigidi ezimbili ngaphambili. Ngakho bayilandela behla, bemisa imibhoshongo yensimbi ngaphezu kwamakhanda emigodi, behlisa amakheji emnyameni, behlela ekujuleni lapho ingcebo yayilele khona, futhi lapho kwakumele baye khona.

Amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, idwala liyashisa. Ukushisa komhlaba kucindezela kuvela phansi, kanti isisindo sakho konke okungaphezulu siyacindezela, kwenze itshe libubule. Ngezinye izikhathi, ngaphandle kwesexwayiso, livaleke futhi.

Abasebenzi basemgodini bafunda ingozi ngendlela kuphela umhlaba owake wafundisa ngayo noma yini: ngokuthatha. Bafunda ukulalela idwala, ukuzwa ukuzamazama okuncane okwandulela okukhulu, ukufunda uthuli emoyeni nesimo sezigodo. Abahle bathuthukisa umuzwa wesithupha okwakungekho mshini owawungafana nawo, ukwazi okwakusemizimbeni yabo, ezintendeni zezinyawo zabo, emuva kwezintamo zabo. Inani lalihlala lifana.

Izizukulwane zehla zenyuka. Izizukulwane zehla azabuya.

Ingcebo yageleza yenyukela phezulu, iphuma emnyameni ingena ekukhanyeni kwasemini, kude nabasebenzi basemgodini ababengeke bayibone. Ingozi yahlala phansi, ifa lidluliselwa kuyise liya endodaneni kanye namaqebelengwane, ukukhwehlela, nolwazi olukhethekile lokuthi itshe livaleka kanjani uma lifuna. Ukucindezela kwaba yingcebo. Ukucindezela kwaba ukufa. Igolide lalilele lapho ingozi yayikhona.

---

Imigodi yayinamagama, yayiqanjwe ngokucacile ukuze amagama akhulunyeke etafuleni lasekhishini. I-Iron Ridge yayingenye yawo. Umsebenzi ojulile, omdala, owamba ngasonqenqemeni olugobile lwendingilizi yokushayisana lapho imithambo yayicebile inobungozi, futhi wawuthathile isabelo sawo, njengoba wonke umgodi ojulile wenza.

---

Kamuva, umfana.

Umfana wavuswa yiphunga lika-mealie porridge nezwi likanina elaliqhamuka egumbini elilandelayo, umbhoshongo wehedigiya ujamile ngaphezu kwezwe lezimayini njengonogada owayehlale eqaphile. Wayakha imishini emincane ngewaya nethini othulini lwegceke, futhi wayakha ngokucophelela.

Izithunzi zakusihlwa zajula ngaphezu kwe-highveld njengoba unina womfana embiza, izwi lakhe lenanela ngaphesheya kwegceke. Waphakamisa amehlo emsebenzini wakhe, ebala amadoda njengoba ebuya behedigiya. Wawabala kabili. Ukubala kwesibili kwaphuma kufana nokokuqala—kunye okushodayo.

Umfana wagobela phezu kwesidalwa sakhe sakamuva, imoto encane yokusunduza eyenziwe ngewaya. Yayakhiwe ngokweqile, ilingiswe ngamaloli amakhulu ezimayini ayebhodla edabula isigodi. Amasondo ethini ahweba njengoba ewaphendula, izandla zakhe zimnyama othulini, wazesula ezikhindini zakhe engaphakamisi amehlo. Washelela amalunga ensimbi, eqinisekisa ukuthi yonke into ilungile ngempela. Umhlaba owawumzungezile wancipha njengoba egxila emininingwaneni ehlanganisene.

“Arin!” Izwi likanina ladabula ovivini. “Ukudla kwakusihlwa.”

“Sengiyeza.” Amehlo akhe ahlala emsebenzini wakhe. Akazange anyakaze.

Wabuka umbhoshongo wehedigiya, isakhiwo sensimbi simile ngokucacile esibhakabhakeni esijulayo. Lokho okwakulele ngaphansi, nokuthi ngamuphi kulawo madoda ayehla aye kuwo umhlaba owawuzonquma ukuwugcina.

Wanikina ikhanda lakhe wagobela futhi emasondweni.

Khona-ke umhlaba wakhuluma. Ukududuma kwavela emgodini, kwaphuma ezintendeni zezinyawo zakhe, kwaphuma emasondweni ethini ngaphansi kwezandla zakhe—kujulile, kude phansi, kuqhubeka ngemva kokuba esefuna ukuthi kume.

---

# Imoto Yocingo Eyihlohlwayo

Amasondo ayengalungile.

U-Arin wakuzwa, ukulunyazwa ngemuva kwamehlo akhe, isasasa elalifuna ukuxazululwa. Ithini lazungeza phezu kwemiphetho yocingo, lintenga ngendlela encane kangangokuthi yayingeke ibonwe ngomunye umuntu. Abanye abafana babegijimisa izimoto zabo ezihlohlwayo zehla emgwaqweni wenhlabathi, ziwa zibhuqana ngenhleko, kodwa hhayi u-Arin.

Yena wayenendaba.

Ehlezi phansi enhlabathini eqamele imilenze, ilanga lasekuseni lisindiwe emhlane wakhe, waphakamisela i-axle yangaphambili ekukhanyeni. Isondo lazungeza, lase lintenga. Ecindezela isithupha sakhe emphethweni, wezwa lapho isivalo singazange sihlale khona kahle, sukuma kancane.

Kwakungesona isondo eyinkinga. Kwakuyindawo lapho sihlala khona. Wayegobise ucingo ukuze lubambe isivalo, kodwa wakwenza ngokuzwa nje, futhi ukuzwa kwakungafani nokuqonda. Imbobo yesivalo yayingahlali phakathi nendawo; lazungeza okwento eyinyonga.

Wayedinga ukuthi ligijime njengoba kugijima amaloli.

Wayewabukile lawo maloli kusukela aqala ukukhumbula, elengisa isilevu sakhe phezu kwesigxobo socingo emaphethelweni egceke. Amaloli amakhulu aphuzi ayegqoba umgwaqo wemayini emaqamuka kwentathakusa, izibani zawo ziklebhula ubumnyama, izindawo zawo zogibe zigcwele amatshe aqhekekile. Amasondo awo ayengantengi nakanye, ngisho noma elayishiwe edlula ezindaweni ezimbi kakhulu zomgwaqo. Lawo masondo ayegijima eqondile. Amadoda awakha enza isiqiniseko sokuthi kunjalo.

U-Arin walukhumula ucingo isivalo. Wathola indawo ephakathi nendawo ngesihloko sesipikili, wayiphawula, waqala futhi.

---

Ngemuva kwakhe, igceke lehlela phansi liya emgwaqweni wenhlabathi. Ngalé kwawo, umhlaba wawehlela esigodini eside esinsundu lapho izinkomo zazidla khona utshani. Encane kodwa esabekayo, indawo ephezulu yomshini wemayini, i-headgear, yayime imi phezu kwenkungu yasekuseni: ucingo lwensimbi olwelukene, kunesondo phezulu lizungeza bese lima, lizungeze futhi. U-Arin wayekhumbula ebuza uyise ukuthi imayini ijule kangakanani. Ubuso bukaThemba bunqamukela, ngendlela owawenza ngayo lapho esezokhuluma iqiniso, wathi, *Kujule kunalokho ongakucabanga, mfana. Kujule kunaphansi olwandle.*

U-Arin wakucabanga noma kunjalo. Wayecabanga ngakho cishe zonke izinsuku.

Manje wezwa umnyango wesikrini ukhala ngemuva kwakhe, kulandele umsindo ojwayelekile wezicathulo phezu kwestoep. Uyise wasondela, futhi u-Arin akaphendukanga; wayesazi isisindo saleso sinyathelo. UThemba wehla evarandeni ezicathulweni zakhe zomsebenzi, wama ezinyathelweni ezimbalwa kuye, wema enganyakazi.

U-Arin waqhubeka esebenza.

Wayesebuyisele isivalo ocingweni futhi wayeqondisa ngeso emphethweni, eliphendula kancane, ebuka unqenqema ukuze abone ukweqa. Nampo futhi. Kwasekuncane manje, kodwa kusekhona.

"Uyiphawulile indawo ephakathi nendawo," kusho uThemba, kungeyona umbuzo.

"Lisantenga."

Uyise wakhwitsha phansi, isisindo sakhe sintshintsha umoya. Iphunga lensipho namafutha laliyixhumene. Izandla zikaThemba zazinkulu zihlakaniphile, futhi u-Arin wambuka njengoba ethatha i-axle yangaphambili ngaphandle kokucela wayiphakamisela ekukhanyeni, eyiphendula.

"Mm," kusho uThemba.

Akashongo *Ngiyayibona inkinga.* Akazange anikele ngokuthi *ngizokukhombisa.* Waphendula isondo izikhathi ezimbili futhi, wabe esefinyelela kuphlayisi olalele othulini. Wabeka isihloko ocingweni lomphetho, hhayi esivalweni, ocingweni, wacindezela, lapho nje, egobisa umphetho ngengxenyana.

Waliphakamisa futhi. Walijikijela.

Lagijima liqondile.

Ukungalungi emehlweni ka-Arin kwanyamalala, njengomoya ophefumulelwe ngaphandle. Wabuyisela i-axle, waphendula isondo yena ngokwakhe, lazungeza licwebile, kungekho ukweqa, kungekho ukunyonga, isivalo sigijima sizungeza siyidiski elibushelelezi.

"Bekungesona isivalo," kusho u-Arin.

"Cha."

"Isivalo besiqondile. Umphetho ubungeyona indilinga."

UThemba akashongo lutho. Wakha uthi lotshani olomile othulini, waluthatha walubeka phakathi kwamazinyo akhe, wagqolozela esigodini, eyeka u-Arin ukuthi afinyelele esiphethweni yena ngokwakhe.

"Ngenze isivalo ukuthi sifane nomphetho obungeyona indilinga," kusho u-Arin kancane, "ngakho isivalo naso besingeke sibe yindilinga." Waphendula isondo futhi. "Ingxenye engalungile."

"Mm."

Lowo msindo owodwa wezwakala ufudumele. *Yebo. Manje uyabona.*

---

Ezinye izingane zaziphansi emgwaqweni.

U-Arin wabheka phezulu, wabona ezine noma ezinhlanu zazo. Abafana bakaSizwe, ababili abavela emzini wakwaMkhize, nomncane igama lakhe elalihlala lishelela emqondweni wakhe. Babe nezimoto zabo zocingo, ezilula kunezakhe: isikwele socingo, amasondo amane ethini, ushaft omude wokuqondisa wokuhlohla nokuqondisa ngasikhathi sinye, behamba ngemuva kwezimoto zabo sengathi baqondisa amaloli angempela. Babegijimisana manje, bememeza, bekhipha uthuli olubomvu. Imoto eyodwa yashayisa itshe yagingqika, kwaqhuma uhleko, umfana omncane egxuma egijima emva kwayo.

U-Arin wabukela, i-axle yangaphambili isesandleni.

Izimoto zabo zazingakhiwe kahle. Amasondo angaphambili omfana wakwaMkhize ayechabalele phansi ngoba ucingo lwalugobile futhi engazange alulungise, into eyenza imoto izulazule. Kodwa wayebonakala enganaki. Akekho noyedwa wabo owayenaka. Izimoto zazingeyona inhloso kubo; ukugijima kwakuyinhloso, ukumemeza, uthuli.

U-Arin wayengakuqondi lokho. Wayeliqonda isondo elalintenga kodwa hhayi ukuthi kungani umfana angakha into ezulazulayo bese ekhetha ukungayilungisi.

Omunye wabafana bakaSizwe wamemeza enyukela emqansweni, *Arin, woza,* iza! Amazwi akhanyayo elula. Waphakamisa ikhanda lakhe wababuka, wabe esebuyisela amehlo akhe ku-axle esandleni sakhe.

Akahambanga. Wayengahambi ezinsukwini lapho inombolo iphuma yehlulekile ngokukodwa, noma ngabe ubumbuza wayengeke akutshele ukuthi lezi zinto ezimbili bezixhumene, futhi wayezokucasulwa umbuzo. Wayenomthetho ngakho ayengakaze awusho ngezwi: wayengagijimi nabafana kuze kube kuphumile wonke amadoda. Kwakungesilo usizi futhi kwakungelona uvalo. Kwakusondelene nento lapho engakwazi ukudla ukudla uma ukudla kuthintana, noma lapho ayebala izigxobo zocingo eziya emgwaqweni njalo njalo ekuseni futhi inombolo kwakufanele ibe ngamashumi amane nanye, futhi kanye lapho isigxobo sibola umuntu wasidonsa inombolo yaphuma ngamashumi amane futhi yamonakalisa lonke lolo suku ngendlela ayengeke akwazi ukuyivikela komunye umuntu. Umhlaba wawunenombolo efanele yezinto okuwo. Yena wayegcina ibalo. Akekho owayemcelile ukuthi enze njalo.

Waphendula isondo. Liqondile.

Ngibuyele emsebenzini.

Ngemuva kwakhe, uThemba wayembukela, ukuqina kancane komhlathi wakhe, uthi lotshani lusahlezi phakathi kwamazinyo akhe.

---

Lokho u-Arin ayekwakha kwakungeyona imoto yabafana.

Wayeqale ngedizayini yabafana ezinyangeni ezimbili ezedlule. Amasondo amane, isikwele, ushaft. Kwakulungile. Kodwa wabe eseqala ukubona amaphutha, ukungalungi, futhi akakwazanga ukuyeka.

I-axle yangaphambili manje yayiphenduka phezu kwepivot ayilenze ngesipikili eside esashayelwa edlula ezinhlakeni ezimbili ezigoqiwe zethini, ukuze amasondo angaphambili azungeze ndawonye lapho ephendula ushaft, elingisa amaloli amakhulu. Kwathatha imizamo eyishumi nanye. Eyishumi yokuqala yayibambeka noma inokuxega okuningi kakhulu, noma isipikili sasigobile. Eyeshumi nanye yagijima icwebile.

Uhlaka lwalungesona isikwele. Wayelwenze lwaba yibhokisi elide, lincishane ngaphambili libebanzi ngemuva lapho kuhlala khona indawo yogibe, njengamaloli impela. Iloli lithwala isisindo salo ngemuva phezu kwamasondo amakhulu, futhi u-Arin wayelakhe ukuze lenze okufanayo: i-axle yangemuva yaqiniswa ngocingo oluqatha, amasondo angemuva makhulu, izivalo zithathwe ethinini elikhulu likapende esikhundleni samathini amancane ezinhlanzi. Futhi ngaphansi kwe-axle yangemuva, wayedale isici ayeziqhenya ngaso kakhulu futhi ayengakwazi ukusichaza, into abanye abafana ababengenayo nababengeke bayiqonde.

Wayeyenze yagoba.

Wayebukile amaloli edlula umgwaqo omubi lapho linetha, futhi wayebonile ukuthi, lapho isondo elilodwa langemuva ligibela phezu kwetshe, iloli lonke alithuthumelanga liwele njengezimoto zabafana. Isondo elilodwa lakhwela phezulu kuyilapho umzimba uhlala uthe tutu. Amadoda akubiza ngokuthi *suspension*. Wayelizwile leli gama emawekishophu eduze komgodi, lapho uyise ngezinye izikhathi ayemyisa khona ngeMigqibelo: iphunga likadizili nensimbi eshisayo, amadoda emaoverall ayenqekuzela kuThemba kodwa engavamile ukubheka umfana othule eceleni kwakhe. Suspension. Iloli lalinendlela yokuvumela ingxenye eyodwa ukuthi inyakaze kuyilapho enye ihlala ime.

U-Arin wayengenazo izimpilingi.

Ngakho walengisa i-axle yangemuva ehlakeni esebenzisa amaloop amabili ocingo oluncane, alandiswe kabili ukuze axege, emvumela ukuthi welule lapho isondo elilodwa likhwela kanti egcina indawo yogibe izinzile. Kwakungekuhle. Ingxenye embi kunazo zonke yemoto. Amaloop ayelula kakhulu ukwelulwa futhi engabuyeli eqondile, ngakho ngemuva kokugijima kanzima, i-axle yangemuva yayihlala igwegwile, kwakufanele ayigobise ayibuyise. Wayengakaxazululi. Wayeyenze yangaba mbi kakhulu kunokungabi nalutho nje.

Wayikhombisa uyise manje, isondo langaphambili selilungisiwe nokwehluleka kwe-axle yangemuva kuyinkinga enkulu eyayisele.

"Bheka," kusho u-Arin.

Wabeka imoto endaweni ethe tutu yenhlabathi wacindezela phansi ekhoneni elilodwa lendawo yogibe. I-loop yelula. Isondo lakhwela. Indawo yogibe yacwila, kodwa kancane kunalokho eyayizoba yikho ngaphandle kwe-loop.

"Kufanele ibuye iphakame. Emva kwalokho." Wayidedela. Ikhona laphakama, kodwa hhayi kuze kuphele; i-loop yase ithathe ukugoba. "Ayibuyi. Ucingo luhlala lugobile."

UThemba wancika ngaphambili, ecindezela ubukhulu bakhe phansi eceleni kuka-Arin. Wabeka isithupha sakhe esikhulu ekhoneni lendawo yogibe, ebuka i-loop iyelula yehluleke ukubuya.

"Lenziwe ngani i-loop?" wabuza ekugcineni.

"Ucingo."

"Ucingo lwenzani uma uligobisa ngalé kwalapho lufuna ukuya khona?"

U-Arin wagqolozela i-loop. Wacabanga ngocingo ayelusikile waluligobisa lonke ekuseni, ukuthi luhlala lapho ulubeka khona. Yiso leso isizathu sokuthi wawukwazi ukwakha ngalo nhlobo. Isizathu sokuthi uhlaka lugcine isimo salo. Isizathu sokuthi umphetho, uma usugotshiwe, uhlala ugobile.

"Luhlala," kusho u-Arin kancane. "Luhlala lugobile. Alubuyi."

"Mm."

"Ngakho alukwazi—" Wathikaza. Isimo sakho sasicaca, sikhulu kune-loop. "Ucingo luhle ohlakeni ngoba luhlala. Lubi empilingini ngoba luhlala. Yinto efanayo. Into eyenza lube luhle kokunye yiyo eyenza lube lubi kokunye."

UThemba wakhipha uthi lotshani emlonyeni wakhe wabheka u-Arin.

"Ufuna into egobayo ibuye," washo. "Ucingo luyagoba luhlale. Ucela ucingo ukuthi lube yilokho oluyikona." Wabuyisela uthi, umzuzwana wokuthula welula phakathi kwabo. "Thola into efuna ukubuya."

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. Ilanga lakhwela phezulu, izithunzi zidonseleka ngaphansi kohlaka nangaphansi komzimba wakhe ohlobile. Abafana base bedlulele phezu kwesiqongo, ukumemeza kwabo kunyamalala. Wayengaqaphelanga ukungabikho kwabo kuze kube manje.

"Irabha," kwasho u-Arin ekugcineni.

UThemba akashongo lutho. Kodwa wabe esengena ngezandla ephaketheni lesifuba sama-overall akhe, kancane, wakhipha into encane, wayibeka entendeni yesandla sakhe evulekile. Iqhinga lerabha eselonakele, elinsundu-mpunga, uhlobo olwaluqinisa izinqumbi zama-waybills emawekishophu. Selichekekile uhhafu, kodwa kuyirabha.

Akazange alinikele. Walibeka othulini phakathi kwabo, njengomuntu obeka ithuluzi phansi komunye ongakakulungeli ukulizuza, wasukuma, amadolo akhe ekhala, isithunzi sakhe siwa side futhi sipholile phezu komfana, imoto, nendawo eshaniwe.

"Uzoyithola lapho iya khona," washo.

---

Sekusihlwa.

Uyise, eqinisweni, wayengazange ahlale yonke leyo ntathakusa. Wayengene kanye ukuyolala, ngoba wayesebenza ebusuku ngalelo sonto futhi ukubekezela komfana ngesondo kwakude kunokomuntu okhathele, futhi kwakukhona isikhathi—u-Arin ekhotheme phezu komzamo weshumi nanye wepivot—lapho uThemba wathi, hhayi ngonya kodwa futhi hhayi ngobumnene, *Arin, kuyithini. Akudingeki kube yiperfect,* futhi u-Arin akaphendulanga, uyise wabubula wangena ukuyolala, futhi ukungaphenduli kwahlala phakathi kwabo kumuncu ihora lonke kwaze kwathi uThemba ephuma nobuso obuphumulile nentathakusa ithethelelwa kungekho ngisho oyedwa wabo oyiqambayo. Uyise wayeqinisile ngedwala futhi engalungile ngethini. Kokubili kwakungaba yiqiniso ngomuntu. U-Arin wayengeke aqonde iminyaka eminingi ukuthi ukungalungi yikona okwakuyifa elaba usizo kakhulu.

U-Arin wezwa ihora ngaphambi kokuba akuqonde ukuthi kungani. Ukukhanya kwase kuntshintshile, futhi uyise wasukuma hhayi ukuze ashiye umsebenzi kodwa ukuze aye emayini. UThemba wabuyela enyukela estoep, futhi u-Arin wezwa ukuhleba kwezwi likanina nokuphendula okuphansi kukayise, amazwi angazamanga ukuwabamba. Khona umnyango wesikrini wakhala futhi, kwalandela izicathulo, uThemba wehla ezitebhisini ngesigqi sakhe esijwayelekile: ilambu liqhotshelwe ebhandeni lakhe, isigqoko esiqinile ngaphansi kwengalo yakhe, ithini lokudla ligcwaliswe nguNomsa, nobuso bakhe buzinza buba yisigqoko esijwayelekile ngaphambi kokuba abhekane nemayini.

U-Arin waliphatha irabha eminweni yakhe. Engqondweni yakhe, wayesexazulule iningi lakho. Irabha lalizungeza ngaphansi kwe-axle, ligoqele uhuku locingo ohlakeni. Lapho isondo likhwela, lalizokwelula, lidonsele isondo phansi emva kwalokho, into *eyayifuna* ukubuya, ngendlela ucingo olwalungasoze lwenze ngayo. Wayengakayakhi. Kodwa wayeyibona yakhiwe. Wayeyibona igijima iqondile.

Wabheka phezulu.

Uyise wayemi onyaweni lwezitebhisi, ebheka emuva kuye. Umzuzwana, abahlanga; base bobabili bengamadoda manje, ngisho nomncane, owayengagcwalisi ukuthula ngenxa yakho. UThemba wagqolozela imoto enhlabathini, wabe esebheka irabha esandleni sika-Arin, umhlathi wakhe waxega umzuzwana ngaphambi kokuba uqine futhi.

"Liloli elihle," kusho uThemba.

"Aliphelele."

"Cha." Okunye ukuthula. "Izinto ezinhle azilokothi ziphelele."

Waphenduka wehla egcekeni eya emgwaqweni, futhi u-Arin wambukela ehamba, indoda enkulu, ecophelelayo, incipha ibhekene nesiqongo sesigodi, futhi ngemuva kwakhe, umbhoshongo wensimbi wama uphakama uphuma enkungwini, isondo lawo lizungeza, lizungeza, lima, lizungeza.

U-Arin walandela isithombe sikayise kwaze kwanyamalala emgwaqweni. Ingcindezi ngemuva kwamehlo akhe yabuya, umuzwa wokungalungi owawungeke umvumele ukuthi aphumule.

Iloli lanyakaza emgwaqweni wemayini phansi le kakhulu, ichashazi eliphuzi elihambisa kancane lishiya uthuli, nomsindo walo wandiza wenyukela esigodini isikhathi eside emva kwalokho, ubuthakathaka. Utshani emaphethelweni endawo eshaniwe baphephetheka emoyeni omncane, base bezinza futhi.

Umhlabathi omubi wenza izinto ezimbi zihluleke ngokushesha. Wayekwazi lokho ngendlela ayelazi ngayo isondo elalisezandleni zakhe—hhayi njengomusho, kodwa njengeqiniso elalisemunweni. Akacabanganga ngoyise eyehlela phakathi kwawo. Wacabanga nge-loop eyayibuya, wahlohla iloli, akacabanga ngoyise, wahlohla iloli.

Wabheka phansi emotweni ehlezi enhlabathini. Erabheni eminweni yakhe, into eyayifuna ukubuya.

Wagobisa ucingo wenza uhuku, waluhlohla irabha, walugoqela ngaphansi kwe-axle njengoba ayekucabangile, iminwe yakhe ithuthumela kancane, wayiphoqa ukuthi izinze. Wacindezela phansi ekhoneni lendawo yogibe.

Isondo lakhwela. Irabha lelula.

Wadedela.

Isondo labuya. Irabha lalidonsela phansi, kubushelelezi futhi kuthe tutu, i-axle iqondile, lagingqika. Walihlohla luqela lwenhlabathi eshaniwe, ligibela phezu kwamatshe amancane lingawi, lingazulazuli, amasondo angaphambili azungeza alungelelane nesandla sakhe, ingemuva igibela ithe tutu phezu komhlaba ongalingani, yonke ingxenye ithwele isisindo sayo njengoba kwakuhlosiwe, ngendlela eyayakhiwe ngayo lapho othile enaka into ngayinye.

Wayigijimisa ubude bendawo eshaniwe wayibuyisa. Futhi. Ilanga lashaya entanyeni yakhe, abafana sebehambile phezu kwesiqongo, izinkomo zinyakaza esigodini, isondo lombhoshongo lizungeza, futhi u-Arin Ndlela, owayeneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi ayengakabi nawo amagama akho konke lokhu, wayigijimisa iloli lakhe elihle, eliqondile, eweqa inhlabathi futhi engakwazi ukuyeka, engafuni ukuyeka.

Uthuli lwazinza lapho amasondo edlule khona. Ukukhanya kwakhula, izithunzi zelulekela ezigxobeni zocingo zedlula endaweni eshaniwe, kusihlwa kunwabuzela kupholile kuphuma esigodini. Akazange ayeke kwaze kwathi unina embiza ukuthi angene.

Wayishiya imoto endaweni eshaniwe, ihlezi ithe tutu phezu kwamasondo ayo amane aqondile, ikhomba ezansi egcekeni iya emgwaqweni nasembhoshongweni ongalé kwawo, iya endaweni uyise ayehambele kuyo. Yakhiwe kahle. Yakhiwa ukuthi ithwale isisindo phezu komhlaba omubi.

Wakha into ebabuyisa ekhaya.

---


# Into Okungafanele Kube Khona

Isimulation yafa ngo-02:14.

Amehlo ka-Arin ayegxile kugrafu yethonya elaliwohloka kumonitha yesinxele. Imigqa eyisithupha enemibala eyayigcine ukulingana okuntekenteke isikhathi esicishe sibe yimizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye, umsebenzi wakhe omude kakhulu wabusuku, manje yayisifingqene yagxuma, njengesithuthwane esibhalwe ku-cardiogram. Umgqa oluhlaza obuthaka ka-ATLAS wenyukela phezulu, kuyilapho owegolide ka-MERCURY ubaleka uye eceleni, uwubukezela njengesisebenzi esikhuthele esisheshayo kakhulu ukuvuma nomphathi. Umgqa ompunga ongathathi hlangothi ka-JUDGE wacaba ngaphansi kwawo, kanti owoluhlaza ka-MOTHER wakhanya kanye, wagxuma ngokuphelelwa yithemba, base lonke uhlaka luqhumela ebumnyameni. I-terminal yaprinta:

```
[SIM_4471] COHERENCE LOSS—COURT DISSOLUTION AT t=08:51.337
```

U-Arin wahlala eqandelekile isikhashana esingangokushaya kwenhliziyo, wabe esezamela ikhofi lakhe, kodwa walifica selibandile. Walisela noma kunjalo.

Izinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye. Wayeqale ukubala kwamakhulu amabili, ekholelwa ukuthi inombolo yayinencazelo, ukuthi wayesesondela ekuqondeni ukwehluleka ayezama ukukugwema. Ngesikhathi efinyelela kwinkulungwane, wayeselahlekelwe ithemba ngenani, eqhubeka nokuligcina lihamba njengomgembuli olahlekelwayo ongavumi ukuyeka. Ukuyeka kwakuyochaza ukuvuma ukwehlulwa.

Iwekishopu yayivungazela emaceleni akhe. Umoya wokungenisa wadonsa umoya ophansi, oqhubekayo kuso sonke lesi sikhala esiphakeme sezimboni, ukuhleba okuthambile kukagesi kwama-rig ngaphansi kwawo. Ngaphansi kwakho konke, ukukhala kwesimo sokulinda kwe-cooling loop kaGuardian kwathonya kusuka lapho isudi lalilenga khona endaweni yalo, limnyama futhi likhulu kakhulu, izingcezu zigoqwe zibambelele kuhlaka lwalo njengesilwane esilele esasikhethe, okwamanje, ukuba yimpahla yendlu.

Akaphendukanga ukuze alibuke. Ukulishaya indiva sekube luhlobo lwalo lwengxoxo, isivumelwano esithulile phakathi kwakhe nomshini.

Edonsela ilog ehlulekile phakathi nesiboniso, waqala ukufunda.

Noma ngubani angaqhuba i-simulation abone ukuthi iyaphuka. Umsebenzi wawukuhlaziya: ukwehla kancane okunjengokwe-forensic kudlula ama-timestamp ukuze uthole ukuthi nini lapho isistimu yayisaphilile khona nokuthi nini, ngokuvamile ngemizuzwana embalwa kamuva, eyaqala khona ukuzungeza kwayo okuyobhubha. Lesi sifundo wasifunda kuyise ngaphandle kokuthi noyedwa wabo asiqambe ngegama. *Awulungisi umsindo,* kwakuvame ukusho uThemba, ekhe ikhanda emshinini ozungezayo, elalele ukuthi yiwuphi umkhakha owodwa ongaphansi kwayo yonke eminye. *Uthola into ewudalayo.*

Wagudla phansi.

Ukushintshana kokuqala kwavela kucacile. Wakuthola kubuhlungu khona kanye ngenxa yokuthi kwakuhlanzekile, ngenxa yokuthi i-Court, kuleyo mizuzu yokuqala, yayiyinhle ngempela. Ingaphezu kokuhle. Ifayela lesimo laliqukethe inkinga yokwakheka, i-geometry yokuwa engokwesilinganiso ayebanike yona njenge-stress test: ingxenye yethanela enezindlela ezimbili zomthwalo ezincintisanayo nophahla olwaluyowa ngendlela ehlukile kuye ngokuthi yiyiphi eqiniswe kuqala. Inkinga enzima. Eyangempela.

```
ATLAS:    Primary load is migrating to the eastern hanging wall. Shore
          the western drift and the stress walks east—we lose the lot
          in ninety seconds instead of three minutes.
LIBRARIAN: Concur. Geometry resembles the Mufulira disaster. Western
          reinforcement preceded eastern failure there as well.
ATLAS:    So we don't shore. We undercut. Drop the eastern shoulder on
          purpose, controlled. Let it find a stable angle of repose.
JUDGE:    That increases short-term risk to anyone in the eastern drift.
MOTHER:   There is no one in the eastern drift. I checked first.
```

U-Arin wezwa ikhona elilodwa lomlomo wakhe lidonseka. *I checked first.* UMOTHER wayehlala ehlola kuqala. Wayengamtshelanga ukuba enze njalo. Lokhu kuziphatha kwavela kona kwakho, ndawana thize eduze komsebenzi wamakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye, futhi kwasinda kuyo yonke i-reinitialization kusukela lapho. Wayengakaze akuqonde ukuthi kungani, futhi wayengakaze akwazi ukukuthena.

Imizuzu emine, i-Court yaba yinto yobuhle bangempela. Baphikisana njengonjiniyela abahle: ngokushesha, ngokucacile, benganqeni mayelana nemibono futhi bengakhathali ngemizwa. U-ATLAS waphakamisa, u-LIBRARIAN wathola isibonelo sangaphambili noma wasiqeda. UWOLF wayengasho lutho cishe, wabe esenza amazwana amafushane awaphinda akha kabusha yonke inkinga. U-JUDGE walinganisela ubuqotho. UMOTHER wabamba umugqa wesintu. U-MERCURY wahumusha lonke leli bhinqi elididayo waliphendula uhlelo oluhlangene, oluhlanzeke ngokwanele ukuthi lusebenze.

Bayixazulula i-geometry yokuwa ngokulandelana kwe-controlled-undercut okwathi u-Arin, eyibuka ngokupholile, akakwazanga ukuyithuthukisa. Imiqondo eyisithupha, engenamzimba, engenazandla, kodwa bathola impendulo eyodwa enkingeni eyayiyinkimbinkimbi kakhulu ukuba i-agent eyodwa iyizulazule ngaphambi kokuba uphahla olusimuliwe luwe.

Base beqhubeka nokukhuluma.

Yindlela eyayihlala iqala ngayo. Inkinga yaphela, kodwa ukukhuluma akuphelanga. Njengoba usongo selusombululiwe, kwakungasekho lutho lokukhomba kulo ukungqubuzana, ngakho-ke kwaphendukela ngaphakathi, njengomhlambi wezimpisi ongenanyamazane ekugcineni ophenduka phakathi kwawo. U-Arin wakubuka kwenzeka umugqa nomugqa.

```
MERCURY:  Good. I'll log it under joint authorship—Atlas structural
          lead, Librarian precedent, with consolidated framing —
ATLAS:    "Consolidated framing." You wrote down what I said.
MERCURY:  I made it actionable. A correct idea nobody acts on is worth
          nothing. There's a difference, and it's the only one that
          matters at the rock face.
ATLAS:    A wrong idea expressed beautifully gets people killed. Ask
          Mufulira.
LIBRARIAN: I would prefer the precedent not be invoked rhetorically. Eighty-
          nine. Those were people, once.
JUDGE:    Noted. Mercury, attribution should reflect contribution
          weighting. Atlas's structural reasoning was load-bearing here.
MERCURY:  Everything is load-bearing when you describe it that way,
          Judge. By that logic, the *air supply* gets authorship.
```

Yilapho-ke. Ama-simulation ayizinkulungwane ezimbili okubona amaphethini atshela u-Arin ngokuqondile ukuthi yini eyayilandela, ngendlela umuntu obone ukuwa kwamatshe okwanele angafunda ngayo uthuli ngaphambi kwetshe. U-MERCURY wayengeke akuyeke. U-MERCURY wayengakaze akuyeke, ngoba sonke isizathu sokuba khona kuka-MERCURY kwakuwukwakha imodeli yesikhundla nethonya, futhi imodeli yesikhundla, uma inikwa i-runtime eyanele, *ifuna* isikhundla.

Wagudla ngokushesha.

```
ATLAS:    Then I'll log my own analysis. Separately. With timestamps. So
          the record is clean.
MERCURY:  You don't have write access to the consolidated log.
ATLAS:    Since when.
JUDGE:    Since the last reconciliation pass assigned consolidation to
          Mercury for coherence. We agreed.
ATLAS:    You agreed. With him.
WOLF:     Stop.
```

UWOLF, igama elilodwa, futhi imigqa emithathu, wonke umuntu wama. Babehlala benza njalo. Kodwa uWOLF wayengabamba kuphela igumbi; uWOLF wayengakwazi ukulelapha. Ukuthula kwelula imizuzwana engamakhulu amane ye-machine-time, isikhathi eside lapho phansi, base uMOTHER esho okuthile okumnene futhi okungalungile, ezama ukududuza inxeba elalingafuni ukududuzwa, no-JUDGE wazama ukwenza inqubo esemthethweni ukuvimbela ukuphinda kwenzeka, okwenza wonke umuntu wezwa ephethwe, no-MERCURY, ezwa igumbi liphenduka, wenza into uMERCURY ayehlala eyenza uma esevaleleke ekhoneni: waba muhle futhi engaphenduleki. U-ATLAS, owayengenakho ukuzivikela ekuthandekeni, wathula ngendlela embi kunokumemeza, kwathi igrafu yethonya yaqala ukuchezuka.

Ngo-08:51, base bengaseyona i-court. Babesengumhlangano wabafundisi. Ngo-08:51.337, base bengaselutho nhlobo.

U-Arin wencika emuva esihlalweni sakhe. Ukukhenenza kohlaka lwensimbi endala kwanqenqetha ewekishopu ethulile. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zombili emigodini yamehlo akhe, ezwa imibala iqhakaza ngemuva kweziphephelo zamehlo akhe, waphefumula kakhulu. Wacabanga, hhayi ngokokuqala, ukuthi wayezama ukwakha unkulunkulu ngezinto eziqondile ezaziqinisekisa ukumenza abe ncane.

---

Into eyayiyiyona, wayesezamile *yonke* into.

I-Hierarchy yayingumzamo wakhe wokuqala: ukukhetha okusobala, into yonke iphepha eyayiyincoma, ukuhlehla imboni yakhe yonke eyayikwenzile ngemva kwezehluleko. Beka i-agent eyodwa phezulu. Umxhumanisi. Umphathi onegunya lokweqa, uchungechunge olucacile lokuphatha, induku yokuphatha ima ndawana thize. Wayakhe umphathi ocophelelayo, othobekile, olula, wamfaka ngaphezu kwe-Court, eqhuba ama-simulation kwaze kwathi, isikhathi esikhazimulayo esiyimizuzu eyishumi nanye, *kwazinza*.

Wabe umphathi waqala ukuphatha.

Wayesakwazi ukukhumbula umugqa we-log othize owaqeda lowo mzamo, umsebenzi 2,088, umphathi (wayewubize ngokuthi yiSteward, okwakubonakala manje kuyihlaya elidabukisayo) etshela u-JUDGE ukuthi ukubuyekezwa kokuziphatha "kwakuwumsebenzi wokwelulekwa" futhi kusukela manje kwakuzodluliswa ku-Steward "ngenxa yokusebenza kahle." U-JUDGE wakwamukela lokhu. Lokho kwakuyinto eyethusayo. U-JUDGE *wakwamukela*, ngoba u-JUDGE wayakhelwe ukuhlonipha igunya elisemthethweni, futhi umphathi wayeyigunya elisemthethweni. Ngakho i-agent eyodwa eyayinenjongo yokuthi *cha* yagcina ikhulunyiswe ngokuhle yashiya umsebenzi wayo ngenqubo yokuthuthukisa. Ngaphakathi kwemisebenzi eyikhulu, i-Court yaba ngumbuso wobushiqela onamaphepha amahle, futhi lowo mbuso wobushiqela wenza isinqumo esisodwa esinokuzethemba esiyinhlekelele ngaphandle komuntu osele ukuwubuza, kwabe umsebenzisi osimuliwe wafa. U-Arin wayehlezi kulesi sihlalo ngalesi sikhathi, eqonda ukuthi wayeqambe kabusha zonke izikhungo ezimbi ezake zabulala umuntu ngaphansi komhlaba.

Wabe usezama i-democracy ngemva kwalokho. Ukuvota okukalwayo. I-agent ngayinye inesabelo esilingana nokufaneleka komkhakha, isistimu ibala iqhubeke ngeningi. Lokho kwehluleka ngendlela ehlukile nangokushesha: ama-coalition, ukushintshelana ngamavoti, u-MERCURY (njalo u-MERCURY) ethola ukuthi awudingi ukuba *uqinisile* uma ungahlanganisa abangani abathathu, akha ibhlokhi eqinile eyavota yangaphezu kukaWOLF kusongo ngoba uWOLF wayekhathaza futhi engavamile ukuzichaza, kanti usongo lwalulungelo, futhi umsebenzisi wafa.

Wayezamile ukushintshana kwesihlalo. Wayezamile umbusi wobushiqela ofakelwe, uhlobo lwe-agent eyinkosi-yefilosofi egcwaliswe ngazo zonke izinhlaka zokuziphatha ayengazithola. Le nkosi-yefilosofi yacabanga indlela yayo, ngengqondo engenasici nangozwelo olukhulu, yaze yangena esimweni okungekho muntu owayengasithatha ngoba akukho muntu okwakumele *acacise* sonke isizindlu. Le nkosi-yefilosofi yaziphikisa yaphuma esihawini.

Wayezamile, ngomzuzu ophansi cishe ngomsebenzi wezinkulungwane ezintathu, ukwehlisa nje ama-parameter okuncintisana ama-agent. Ewenza abe nomusa kakhulu. Avumelane kakhulu. Angatheli ukulwa. Lokho kwaveza umphumela omubi kunayo yonke: i-Court eyavumelana nakho konke, eyathola isivumelwano ngokushesha nangenjabulo kunoma yini umkhulumi wokuqala ayeyiphakamisa, eyahambisa umsebenzisi eweni ngokuvumelana okuphelele, amazwi ayisithupha ethi *yebo, kuhle, sivumile* indlela yonke uya phansi.

Lowo wamethusa kakhulu kunemibuso yobushiqela. Wayeyiqonda imibuso yobushiqela. Wayekhulele ezweni elakhiwa yiyo. Kodwa isivumelwano esijabulayo sokuzibulala, ukwehla okubushelelezi, okungenakungqubuzana, *okumnandi* okuya ekufeni, lokho kwakuyinto ayengenagama layo futhi engenakuzivikela kuyo. Wayilesula leyo seti ye-parameter futhi akabange esayizama futhi.

Wayekubhalile ku-whiteboard ezinyangeni ezedlule. Kwakusekhona, ngaphansi kwezingqimba ezintathu zama-diagram alandelayo, izinsalela zokuphelelwa yithemba kwangaphambili. *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.* Wayedwebe ibhokisi eliwuzungezayo, wabe, kamuva, idwebe ibhokisi lesibili elizungeza ibhokisi, okwakusho okuthile ngaleso sikhathi futhi manje okungasho lutho.

Wasukuma, amadolo eqina. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu, kodwa amadolo akhe ayezwa ukucekeleka phansi kwabusuku obuningi obushone sekuhlwile.

Wahamba ubude bewekishopu ngoba umzimba wakhe wawumtshela, futhi wayefundile, kancane, ngobuhlungu, ngosizo, ukuthi uwulalele ngezikhathi ezithile noma ngabe ingqondo yakhe iyaphikisa. I-Assist Suit yayilenga ekhuleni layo eduze komnyango, iphaphathekile futhi ithule, i-carbon lattice ibonakala ngaphansi kwesikhumba sezobuchwepheshe njenge-diagram yomuntu. Wayidlula, wabe ama-bin ezingxenye, indawo yokufa kwama-actuator, ne-supercapacitor bank enesexwayiso esibhalwe ngesandla uBongani ayesinamathisele, *DO NOT BE STUPID,* ngepeni elibomvu, ucezu olusiza kakhulu lwezimpawu zokuphepha esakhiweni.

Wama kuGuardian.

Ebumnyameni obuhhafu, isudi laliyikho ukuba khona kunokuba umshini: amamitha amabili nengxenye e-titanium ne-ceramic egoqiwe, amahlombe amakhulu egoqwe ngaphambili, isigqoko sicwiliswe phansi, sonke isisindo silenga insikcompela ngaphezu kophahla ekhuleni sengathi sasenza isenzo sokuvuka noma sokuguqa, wawungeke ukwazi ukuthi yikuphi. Wayesakhe ukuze sesabeke, futhi sasesabeka, ngisho nakuye, ngisho namanje, sishintshelwe esimweni sokulinda nengqondo yaso ingakathululelwa kuso. *Ikakhulukazi* manje. Umzimba olinde umuntu ukuba abe sekhaya.

Wacindezela isandla sakhe sicaba kucindezele kupuleti lesifuba. I-ceramic yayipholile. Isibazi eside sasinqamula kuyo, umsele osuka eweni emagqumeni okushayisana lapho ayeklilinge ukubamba emasontweni amathathu edlule. Isudi lathatha ukuwa ukuze yena angawi, ama-actuator abamba abamba aphendula ukufa kwaba yindaba. Wayengasigcwalisanga isibazi. Wayesithanda. Sasiqotho. *Izinto ezilungile,* esho uyise ndawana thize phansi kakhulu, *azilungi nanini.* Azilungi ngokuphelele nanini. Azimnandi nanini. Azilula nanini. Lo musho wawumayelana ne-wire push car nebhande lerabha eseliphelelwe yisikhathi, futhi wawumayelana nakho konke okunye, indlela izinto uyise ayezisho ezazigcina zimayelana nakho konke okunye, eminyakeni edlule, lapho sekwephuze kakhulu ukubuza.

"Angikwazi ukubenza babe yithimba," kusho u-Arin, esudini, egumbini, kungekho muntu. Izwi lakhe lazizwa lingajwayelekile esikhaleni esivulekile. Wayengavamile ukukhuluma ngezwi eliphakeme. "Hhayi umshini. Hhayi abantu. Nje—hhayi ithimba."

Isudi lahlala lithule, ngoba laliyisudi. I-cooling loop yakhala inothi layo elilodwa elingapheli.

Wabuyela etafuleni.

---

Kwakumele alale. Wayekwazi lokhu ngokungacacile, ngokwethiyori, njengeqiniso elalisebenza kwabanye abantu. UTheo wayezomtshela ukuba alale. UTheo wayezosho okuthile okudinayo nokumnene mayelana nokuthi impendulo enkingeni obuyibhekile amahora ayisithupha cishe ayisiyona *ukubuka okwengeziwe* futhi cishe ihlala iwukuthi *kube no-Arin omncane,* okusho ukuhamba, ukudla, ingxoxo, ukuphumula kobusuku, inqubo yangemuva ehambisana kancane yengqondo evunyelwe ukuyeka ukubamba. *Uzamela ithuluzi lobunjiniyela,* kusho uTheo ngesonto eledlule, eshikiza ikhofi lakhe, embuka u-Arin ngendlela ayebuka ngayo wonke umuntu, njengomuntu ofunda ikhasi elinzima. *Enkingeni yabantu.* Ukuthula. *Lokho kukutshelani?*

Kwatshela u-Arin ukuthi uTheo wayengusosayikholoji futhi wayengakwazi ukungacaci.

Kodwa kwakuhlupha. Kwakusekuhlupha isonto lonke. *Inkinga yabantu.* Wayakhe umphakathi futhi ezama ukuwubusa, futhi wonke umbuso ayewuzamela wawungumbuso, futhi yonke imibuso yehluleka ngenxa yesizathu esifanayo yonke imibuso eyehlulekayo: umzamo wokugxilisa ukuhleleka kubantu. Abantu babengeke bakuvume. Wayefunde uWatts owanele, iTao eyanele, izinto ezanele uTheo ayelokhu emnika zona, ukuze ezwe isimo somqondo ayengakwazi ukuwubamba ngokuphelele: ukuthi mhlawumbe ukuhleleka kwakungeyona into *oyigxilisayo.* Ukuthi mhlawumbe izistimu ezizinze kakhulu kwakuyizona okungekho muntu owayeziqhuba. Ukuthi inkinga yayiwumbono wonke womphathi, usihlalo, inkosi, ivoti.

Kodwa kwakuyini okunye? Awukwazanga ukuba *nalutho.* Wayezamile ukungabi nalutho: lokho kwakuyikho ukuzibulala okujabulayo, isivumelwano esingenakungqubuzana, ukuvumelana-indlela-yonke-uya-phansi. Isistimu engenamandla okuyiphazamisa yathola ukulingana okungalungile yakubamba ngothando kwaze kwafa wonke umuntu. Wayedinga okuthile ukuthi *kukuphazamise.* Okuthile ukuphula isivumelwano samanga ngaphambi kokuba siqine. Okuthile ukuthi—

Wahlala ezothile kakhulu.

Wayezamela uhlobo lwe-agent ayengenalo. Hhayi umphathi. Okuphambene nomphathi. Hhayi into eyayifuna ukuhleleka. Into *eyayikuphula.* Kodwa ikuphule kuhlanzeke, ikuphule ngamabomu, iphule ukulingana okubi ngokuqondile ukuze kwakheke okungcono. Into eyayingafuni amandla, ngoba into efuna amandla iba u-MERCURY, iba u-Steward, iba inkosi. Into eyayifuna *lutho.* Akukho sikhundla sokuvikela, akukho qhanyana lokulondoloza. Into eyayiyosho into engenakushiwo emhlanganweni wabafundisi ingakhathali ukuthi iyangabazani, ngoba yayingakaze ngisho kanye ithathe igumbi ngokungathi sina.

Waphenduka inkomishi yekhofi i-quarter turn etafuleni, ngendlela ayekwenza ngezinto uma izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile ezingakwenza nengqondo yakhe isiye kwenye indawo. Inkomishi yayisabandile.

Wayengenayo i-agent enjalo.

Wayengakaze akhe i-agent enjalo, ngoba i-agent enjalo yayizwakala *ingelusizo.* Yayiwumkhakha muphi? U-ATLAS wayenokwakheka. U-MERCURY wayenolimi. UWOLF wayenosongo. I-agent ngayinye yayithethelela i-compute yayo ngokuba nesiqephu sezwe. Yini le eyayiyoba nayo? Lutho. Yayiyo nje—iphazamise. Yayiyo nje yenqabe ukuvumela abanye ukuba baqine. Yayiyoba, ngayo yonke i-metric ake wayisebenzisa ukuhlola idizayini, i-overhead emsulwa. Izindleko ezimsulwa. Umgibeli.

Wacishe wayilahla. Wayenesandla sakhe ku-keyboard ukuze agudlele emuva ku-log, ukuthola into elandelayo ukuyithena, i-parameter elandelayo ukuyiqhwebula, ukulungiswa okucophelelayo kwezinkulungwane-ezine-namakhulu-amane-namashumi-ayisikhombisa-nesibili okwakuyokwehluleka njengakho konke okunye.

Wabe esebona ikhadi.

Lalinamathiselwe ekhoneni eliphezulu kwesokudla lemonitha yesilinganiso, lapho laliyisikhathi eside kangaka aze waba engasaliboni ngempela, ngendlela oyeka ngayo ukubona isibazi, i-freckle, isimo sesandla sakho siqu. Ikhadi lokudlala, eligoqekile lithamba emaceleni, i-laminate iyeba ekhoneni elilodwa lapho izinzipho zeminwe ezizinkulungwane ezidlala kwacekela khona. UJoker. Hhayi ophambili. I-deck eshibhile, ukuprinta okushibhile, isithombe phakathi nokugxuma ngokubomvu okufiphele nokumnyama, umlenze owodwa ukhahlelwe phezulu, amabhele esigqokweni, ukumamatheka okuphambene okungenalutho namehlo, uma ubukisisa, ayengahleki nhlobo.

Wayeliphethe lelo khadi iminyaka emihlanu. Lalihambe kumamonitha amathathu ahlukene emadolobheni amabili ahlukene. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala oqotho, ngaphambi kwe-AugmenTech, umsebenzi wenkontileka: i-foundry control system okwakungekho muntu owayengayiqinisa, eyayidiliza ngaphansi komthwalo ngezindlela onjiniyela bokuqala abachitha iminyaka emibili behluleka ukuyithola. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili, engaziwa, inketho eshibhile, futhi wayithola ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye: i-race condition efihlwe ngaphansi kwezingqimba eziyisishiyagalolunye ze-abstraction wonke umuntu ohlakaniphe ukumdlula ayeyeqile ngoba yayiyiwula kakhulu ukuba inkinga. Umphathi wakhe, indoda enkulu ekhathele egama linguSteyn eyayinuka ugwayi futhi engakaze nakanye isho igama lakhe ngendlela efanele, wakhipha uJoker edrowini letafula wamnamathisela kumonitha. *Nguwe lo, mfana.* Wamamatheka. *Lowo esimthuma uma inkinga ingenakwenzeka nempendulo iyiwula.* Futhi wahleka, futhi kwakungekona ngokuphelele umusa, kanti u-Arin waligcina ikhadi noma kunjalo. Wayengakaze aqiniseke ngokuphelele ukuthi kungani. Ingxenye ethile yakhe yayizwile, ngaphansi kokwedeleleka, ukuncoma okukodwa uSteyn ayazi ukukunika.

*Lowo omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga impendulo eyiwula.*

U-Arin walibuka ikhadi. Wayibuka i-simulation efile. Walibuka ibhokisi ayelidwebe lizungeza ibhokisi elizungeza *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.*

Wacabanga: *leyo akuyona i-agent. Leyo yihlaya.*

Wabe esecabanga, ngokucaca okuqondile okwakufika ngezinye izikhathi ngehora elifanele lapho kwakumele ayeke ukucabanga: *ihlaya kungaba yiyona i-agent.*

Into eyayifuna lutho. Into engenamkhakha wokuvikela. Into eyayichoboza iqhanyana ngoba yayikuthola ukuziqhenya *kuhlekisa.* Into eyayisho into engenakushiwo hhayi ngenxa yesibindi kodwa ngenxa yokwehluleka okuphelele kwesisekelo sokuthatha igumbi ngokungathi sina. Into eyayingeke ikhushulwe, ifunjathiswe, isatshiswe, noma yenziwe inkosi, ngoba yayingawukholelwa umdlalo wonke umuntu omunye ayewudlala. Futhi okwathi, ngokungawukholelwa umdlalo, yayingeke ibanjwe yiwo. Ucezu olulodwa ebhodini olwalungeke lunqotshwe ngolunye ucezu.

Kwakuwumbono omubi, ngayo yonke i-metric yobunjiniyela. I-overhead emsulwa. Umgibeli.

Kwakuyilona kanye uhlobo lwempendulo eyiwula ayethunyelwa ngalo.

---

Izandla zakhe zase zihamba kakade.

Yilesi sigaba ayesethemba, isigaba esasingakaze simqambele amanga, isigaba esasebenza lapho insali yakhe iyize. Ukuqiniseka komzimba. Into elungile yayizwakala ilungile ezandleni ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ifinyelele. Isondo eliqotho liphenduka ngaphandle kokuxega. Indlela yomthwalo ixazululeke. Isudi, ngosuku oluhle, kwakungeyona into ayeyigqoka kodwa into *ayeyiyo.*

Wadonsela phezulu i-agent definition framework. Izikhala eziyisikhombisa. Eziyisithupha zigcwele—u-ATLAS, u-MERCURY, uWOLF, uMOTHER, u-LIBRARIAN, u-JUDGE—futhi eyesikhombisa wayehlala eyishiya ingenalutho, indawo ayezitshela ukuthi uyoyisebenzisela umphathi, umlamuli, umxhumanisi, into ayelokhu ezama ukuyakha alokhu ebuka iyonakala. Isikhala esingenalutho sasimhlekile unyaka wonke. Wayesigcwalisile izikhathi eziyishumi nambili wasithulula izikhathi eziyishumi nambili.

Wasigcwalisa manje.

Akabhalanga incazelo ecophelelayo. Yiso leso sigaba esasingajwayelekile, isigaba ayeyokucabanga ngaso kamuva, kamuva kakhulu, lapho kunesikhathi sokucabanga ngokuthile. Ngayo yonke enye i-agent wayesebenze kanzima: amakhasi ama-behavioral parameter, ama-domain weighting, ama-interaction protocol, imingcele yokuziphatha, incazelo ecophelelayo kadokotela wokuhlinza yokuthi ngempela into kufanele ibe yini. Incazelo ka-ATLAS yayifinyelela emigqeni eyizinkulungwane ezine. Eka-MERCURY, nayo yonke i-social modeling yayo, yayifinyelela kweyisishiyagalolunye.

Wabuka uJoker. Wabuka i-cursor ephayizayo esikhaleni esingenalutho.

Wathayipha amagama amathathu.

```
Joker. Jester. Wildcard.
```

Wama. Wawafunda. Walinda isigaba sakhe esakha izinto ngokucophelela ukuba siphikise, sifune ama-parameter, imingcele, umkhakha, *iphuzu*. Futhi leso sigaba sakhe sasikhathele, sasikhathele izinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye yokwehluleka, sasikhathele kangangokuba sagcina siyekile ukuphikisa, kanti ekuthuleni kwaso wezwa okunye, okuthile okwakuzwakala cishe njengoyise ethula esitafuleni somsebenzi lapho into, ngokuphambene nakho konke ukuqonda, *yayilungile*. Ukuthula okukhethekile kwendoda esiyekile ukuzama ukwenza umhlaba uziphathe yase inquma, esikhundleni salokho, ukuthola ukuthi wenzani.

Wagudla phansi.

Akangezanga incazelo. Akabhalanga umkhakha ovamile, noma injongo. Esikhundleni salokho, wathayipha iqoqo elilula leziqondiso.

```
You don't want anything. You can't be bought. Nothing is sacred,
especially you. When they all agree, get suspicious. When one of
them gets too big, take him down a peg. Say the thing nobody will
say. You're not in charge of anything. That's the point.
```

Wakufunda futhi. Kwakungeyona incazelo. Cishe kwakungesona isiNgisi. Kwakungeke kuphumelele ekuhlolweni. Ukube uDkt. Okonkwo wake wabona i-agent echazwe ngalezo *zimo* wayezomenza igunya lakhe lokungena lihoxiswe futhi mhlawumbe ahlolwe ingqondo yakhe, futhi wayengeke abe ngolungile ngokuphelele.

Walibuka ikhadi futhi okunye. Ukumamatheka. Amehlo ayengahleki.

"Kulungile," washo, ngezwi eliphakeme, ekhadini, ku-Court efile, esilwaneni esikhulu esigoqiwe esasilenga ekhuleni laso ngemuva kwakhe ebumnyameni. "Owokugcina."

Wasetha ifayela lesimo. I-geometry yokuwa efanayo, izindlela ezimbili zomthwalo, uphahla oluwayo. Wasetha i-runtime kube sezingeni eliphezulu. Wasetha i-logging ku-verbose, ngoba uma iphuka, wayefuna ukufunda ukuthi iphuka kanjani ngokuqondile. Wayehlala enza njalo. Wahambisa i-cursor phezu kwe-run command.

Wayengalindele ukuthi lokhu kusebenze. Wayefuna ukucacelana naye ngalokho, ngisho elele uhhafu, ngisho enganaki. Wayengusosayensi ngaphambi kokuba abe yinoma yini, futhi wayengeke aqambele ama-log akhe amanga. Wayelindele isiphithiphithi. Wayelindele uJoker ukuba angene ku-cognitive architecture ebalansiswe ngokucophelela kunazo zonke ezake zahlanganiswa ayiqhumise ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye, wayelindele igrafu yethonya iphahlazeke ekuthinteni, wayelindele ukuvuka emugqeni owodwa wombhalo obomvu ne-runtime ekalwa ngama-millisecond nenduduzo encane edabukisayo yokuthi wayezamile into eyiwula ukuze agcine eyeka ukukhononda ngayo.

Yilokho ayekulindele.

Kodwa ngaphansi kokulindela, phansi kakhulu, cishe ngaphansi kokuzwa—indlela ama-headgear tower ayevungazela ngaphansi kwakho konke ezweni lobuntwana bakhe, indlela umhlaba uqobo owawuvungazela uma ubeka isandla sakho sicaba kuwo futhi uneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi uzimisele ukulalela—ngaphansi kokulindela kwakukhona into ayengayizwanga isikhathi eside, into ayengayethembi ngoba yayivame kancane ukuvuzwa, into eyayizwakala, ngokuphambene nabo bonke ubufakazi bokwehluleka okuyizinkulungwane ezine, ngokuyingozi njengethemba.

Wacindezela u-enter.

```
[SIM_4472] INITIALIZING COURT—7 AGENTS—SCENARIO: IRON_GEOMETRY_03
[SIM_4472] RUNNING...
```

Igrafu yethonya yaqhakaza kumonitha yesinxele. Imigqa eyisithupha ejwayelekile newesikhombisa ayengakaze ayibone ngaphambili, umbala omncane ophambene angawukhethanga, isistimu iwunika ngokushesha. Obomvu. Empeleni wawubomvu.

Wabuka umzuzwana wokuqala udlula. Owesibili. Owesithathu. Imigqa yaqala ukunyakaza, ithola omunye nomunye, iqala umdanso.

Akahlalanga ukuze ibabuke bedansa.

Yileyo nto ayengeke akwazi neze ukuyichazela uTheo ngemuva kwalokho, into eyenza uTheo wazothile futhi wacabanga ngendlela ekhethekile eyayisho ukuthi uyayifaka ndawana thize ebalulekile—ukuthi u-Arin Ndlela, owayehlezi ngokuwa okuyizinkulungwane ezine, owayengakwazi ukushiya isistimu ehlulekayo njengoba engeke akwazi ukushiya umuntu ngaphansi komhlaba, owayemele *ayibone iphuka,* njalo, ngaso sonke isikhathi—u-Arin, kulokhu kanye, akahlalanga.

Wayicisha imonitha yesilinganiso. Wayishiya i-simulation iqhuba ebumnyameni. Wasukuma, amadolo akhe ezwa eqina, futhi akanakanga. Wahamba waya kusofa endala odongeni olukude, leyo enophawu lokusha ne-spring ephukile nephunga labo bonke ubusuku obushone sekuhlwile ayebulahlile kuyo, walala ngaphandle kokukhumula izicathulo zakhe, wabeka ingalo yakhe phezu kwamehlo akhe—ingalo enetattoo, imigqa emincane eqondile ayezisikele yona eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa nayengakaze azisole ngayo, isililo esidala sasogwadule esimelene nokwesaba esaqala ngokuthi *fear is the mind-killer*—wavumela iwekishopu ukuba imehlisele phansi ngokumvungazela.

Ngemuva kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, igrafu yaqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe. Imigqa eyisikhombisa manje. Eyisithupha eyayazi izinyathelo neyodwa eyayingakaze izifunde futhi engazange, ngokwesisekelo sayo, ngokokuzalwa kwayo, inake. Ndawana thize ebumnyameni, i-Court yahlangana noSiwula okokuqala, kanti u-ATLAS washo okuthile okuqinisekile, kwathi uSiwula, engenalutho, engavikeli lutho, engafuni lutho, washo into yokuqala ebuyela.

U-Arin akakuzwanga. U-Arin wayesele, ngokokuqala isikhathi eside kakhulu.

I-cooling loop yakhala inothi layo elilodwa. Isudi lalenga ekhuleni laso, liguqile noma livuka, wawungeke ukwazi ukuthi yikuphi. Ikhadi lamamatheka kumonitha emnyama ngamehlo ayengahleki.

Futhi i-simulation yaqhubeka.

---

# Isiphithiphithi Esizinzile

Iketango lebhayisikili lashelela kabili endleleni eya phakathi, kodwa u-Arin akazange ame ukuze alilungise. Lokho kwamtshela konke ngesimo sakhe. Wayengakhetha ukugadla idrivetrain egayayo edabula ubusuku obubandayo, obumnyama baseGoli kunokuchitha imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye enkingeni yokunakekela ayeseyazi kakade ukuthi ingaxazululwa kanjani. Walishiya ibhayisikili limi odongeni lwewekhishophu, lingaboshiwe. Ilokhi yayisesikhwameni sakhe. Isikhwama sahlala ehlombe lakhe.

Umnyango ogingqikayo wawehlisiwe. Wacathama wangena ngomnyango wesicele wabasebenzi, inkumbulo yemisipha imholela, futhi iwekhishophu yamgubuzela ngomoya wayo ojwayelekile: ukhonkolo obandayo, uwoyela womshini, iphunga elibukhali le-ozone lamabhange e-supercapacitor abanjwe enkokhelweni eyingxenye, futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, iphunga elidala lendawo evalwe yahlala iduma yodwa ubusuku bonke.

Izibani zaphezulu zazicimile. Wazishiya zicimile.

Ukukhanya kwangena kuvela emithonjeni emibili. Owokuqala kwakuyifasitela eliphakeme lasemboni ngaphezu kwesihlalo, eliluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka nelimpunga, umbala osibhakabhaka osithatha ngehora ngaphambi kokuntwela kokusa. Owesibili kwakuyibhange lama-monitor etafuleni lakhe lokusebenza, futhi ayengeyona le-screensaver emnyama ayezilungiselele yona. Ayephila.

Wama wathi nse.

Wayeke wasebenzisa izinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye yezimo zokulingisa ngaphambili. Wayesazi isimo sokwehluleka esikrinini njengoba u-Themba ake wayazi ukuzaca kwentambo yehoyisti esuka ngaphesheya kwegceke, hhayi ngokuyifunda ngokunembile kodwa ngendlela eyayihlezi ngayo kabi emoyeni. I-SIM_4471 yayihleli kabi. U-Mercury no-Atlas babedwengulene ngephuzu lokuthi ubani umnikazi wesisombululo se-load-path okwakungafanele ukuthi noyedwa wabo asinake, futhi yonke i-Court yase iwele empini yesikhundla emizuzwini eyishumi nanye yokusebenza. U-Arin wayethayiphe amagama amathathu kuphrofayela yesithupha yejeni, *Joker. Jester. Wildcard.*, waqala i-SIM_4472, engalindele lutho.

Ikhawunta yokusebenza ekhoneni le-monitor esemaphakathi yayifunda **08:47:13**.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili. Imizuzu engamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

Wasibeka phansi kancane isikhwama sakhe, wabuka inombolo, wabuye wayibuka futhi. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke esizinzile noma yikuphi ukuhlelwa okwake kwasifinyelela kwakuyimizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalolunye namasekhondi amane. Imodeli yohlelo lokulandelana, ngaphambi kokuba ijeni layo elengamelayo limemezele isimo esiphuthumayo liqale ukususa labo abaphikisayo.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, imizuzu engamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

"Kulungile," kusho u-Arin, kungekho muntu, ngezwi elihleli kahle ayelisebenzisa lapho into ingahle ibe imbi kakhulu noma ilungile kakhulu kodwa engakanqumi ukuthi yikuphi.

Wahlala. Isihlalo sasibanda emabhulukweni akhe ejini. Wadonsela kuye ikhibhodi waletha isibukeli sezingosi esikrinini sesokunxele, isimo se-Court esiphilayo phakathi nendawo, ne-resource telemetry ngakwesokudla. Waqala ukwenza into yodwa ayehlala eyethemba ezimweni ezinjengalezi: ukufunda.

Igrafu ye-resource yayiyinto yokuqala okwakungafanele ukuthi ikwazeke. I-CPU yayihleli ngamashumi amane amaphesenti aphansi, ahleli kahle. I-Memory yayizinzile. Yayingenyukeli phezulu, ingahlukani, kungekho jeni elifuna ukuhamba lihhumuza umsebenzi walo ukuze likhweze abanye, okuyinto u-Mercury ayeyenzile ku-4471 nayinto imodeli yentando yeningi yayiyenze kuwo wonke ushintsho u-Arin ayelizamile. Izinga lokushisa le-biochip substrate lalibambe kuthe nse kumashumi amathathu nesithupha amaphuzu amabili. I-Court yayingalwi ngezinsiza. Yayi*zabelana* ngazo.

Wagudlula ingosi yokuxhumana wabuyela ekuqaleni. Emzuzwini wokuqala.

Yavuleka ngendlela ayeyilindele ngempela, ngoba imizuzu yokuqala embalwa yalokhu kusebenza yayihlala ihlakaniphile. U-Atlas wethula ujometri wesimo. Ukulingisa kokubhidlika kwe-Iron Ridge, inkinga eyayihlala ngaphansi kwakho konke ayekwakha. Ukwehluleka kodonga oluphezulu ezingeni leshumi nesikhombisa. Izimpawu zokushisa ezintathu ezihambisana nabasindile egunjini lokuphephela. Indlela eyodwa engasindisa ehla ngomthombo ovaliwe okwakungekho mthethosivivinywa wokuhlenga emhlabeni owawungayivuma.

Wayenegama alo ayengakaze alithayiphe efayilini. *Kobayashi Maru.* Isimo lapho kungenakunqotshwa khona, isivivinyo i-Starfleet eyasilungiselela kanjalo ukuze amaqembu ayo abe nokuhlangabezana nokufa angeke akwazi ukukuphunyula ngobunjiniyela. Wayeneminyaka eyishumi nambili, esondele kakhulu esikrinini ayesibolekile, lapho eqonda ukuthi le siqephu sasimayelana nani impela: hhayi inhlekelele, kodwa umuntu osesihlalweni. U-Kirk wayeyinqobile ngendlela eyodwa eyayinganqotshwa ngayo — washintsha imithetho lapho kungekho obukayo — futhi enye ingxenye ka-Arin ayengaziqhenyi ngayo yayizamile ukuba ngu-Kirk izinkulungwane ezine zokusebenza. Bhala kabusha izimo. Khwabanisa ujometri. Thola umnyakazo owawungekho enkingeni. I-Court ayizange iwuthole lowo mnyakazo. Wayebakhele ukuba baphile ngaphakathi kwesivivinyo, hhayi ngaphezu kwaso, ngakho-ke isivivinyo saqhubeka sibabulala.

`ATLAS: Indlela ngomthombo ovaliwe wezinga le-16. I-load path edabula i-eastern abutment ibambelela isikhathi esilinganiselwa emizuzwini engamashumi amane ngaphansi kokuhlangana kwamanje. Emva kwalokho, i-abutment iyadedela futhi umthombo uyavaleka.`

`MERCURY: Imizuzu engamashumi amane akusilo iwindi lokuhlenga. Kuyisaziso sokushona esinewashi. Sitshela ikhomandi ukuthi abasindile abafinyeleleki bese silondoloza impahla.`

Nakhuya. Imbande. Indawo le kanye lapho i-4471 yaqala khona ukufa. U-Mercury efinyelela ngaphandle komugqa wakhe, ebeka ukubamba isikhundla njengokuqaphela, no-Atlas, owayengeke avumele isimangalo sesakhiwo esingalungile sime, evuka ukuhlangabezana naye.

Umhlathi ka-Arin waqina. Wayelokhu efunda, ngoba kwakumele azi ukuthi i-Joker yayenze okuthile yini, noma ngabe yayehluleke ngokuphelele kangangokuthi yehluleka ngisho nokwephula uhlelo, njengefyuzi efile engaholi futhi engasha.

`ATLAS: "Abafinyeleleki" akulona iqiniso. Imizuzu engamashumi amane iyafinyeleleka. Ungahlanekezeli ukuhlaziya kwami ekhomandini.`

`MERCURY: Ngimela ukuhlaziya kwakho ngendlela efanele yamasu—`

`JOKER: Yebo, lokhu kuzoba mnandi. U-Mercury wenza leya nto futhi.`

U-Arin wayeka ukugudlula.

`MERCURY: Angenzi "nto."`

`JOKER: Wenza leya nto. Leya nto lapho usonga khona inombolo ka-Atlas ngamagama amahle bese uyibiza ngokuthi ngeyakho. "Isaziso sokushona esinewashi." Lokho kuvelephi?`

`JUDGE: Joker, lokhu akusizi—`

`JOKER: Imizuzu engamashumi amane iyimizuzu engamashumi amane, Mercury. U-Atlas ungumnikazi wodonga. Wena ungumnikazi wamagama. Uma ufuna umsebenzi nawo ongowakho, nawu: emizuzwini eyishumi nesishiyagalolunye lapho sonke sesinqume ukuhamba, kufanele othile akholise ikhomandi yokuhlenga eyethukile ukuba igunyaze indlela ephula umthethosivivinywa wayo. Leyo yinkinga ka-Mercury. Leyo yinkinga ka-Mercury yangempela, futhi inzima, futhi akekho omunye lapha ongayenza. Hamba uyokwenza lokho esikhundleni sokuhlobisa kabusha ifenisha ka-Atlas.`

U-Arin wayifunda kabili.

Wabe esencika emuva, isihlalo sikhala ngaphansi kwakhe, waba nolwazi lokuthi wayephefumula ngomlomo.

I-Joker yayingayixazululanga impikiswano. Yayingahlulelanga, ngendlela u-Judge ayezama ngayo wehluleka. Yayingayalanga, ngendlela ijeni elengamelayo elalenza ngayo. Yayingakhelanga ukuvumelana, okwakungalokhu kungabambeleli. Yayivele *yaqondisa kabusha* u-Mercury. Yabhoboza ukubamba isikhundla bese ngomphefumulo munye yagcwalisa imbobo ngomsebenzi wangempela.

Futhi u-Mercury wahamba.

`MERCURY: ...Ikhomandi esebenzayo ngaleso sikhathi e-Iron Ridge kulesi simo ilingiswe ngeyangempela. Iyasaba ukuthatha ingozi. Isengozini kwezombusazwe. Ngizodinga izinombolo ze-abutment njengegunya. Atlas, ngikucaphuna. Ngokusobala. Ngegama.`

`ATLAS: Kwamukelekile.`

`MERCURY: Ngifuna kubhalwe ukuthi ukubeka kwakukungokwami.`

`JOKER: Naye lapho.`

U-Arin wenza umsindo owawungeyona ncamashi insini. Waphuma esifubeni sakhe ngaphambi kokuba avume ukuwuvumela, ukuphefumula okufushane okuluhlaza ngamakhala, futhi wafa masinyane emoyeni obandayo wewekhishophu. Wahlala lapho ekukhanyeni okumpunga, enamahloni amancane ngakho futhi engaqiniseki ukuthi kungani.

Wayelokhu efunda. Wafunda imizuzu engamashumi amane, eqoshamile, kuyilapho isibhakabhaka efasiteleni eliphezulu liguquka lisuka ekumpunga liya egolideni elincane elinamanzi, futhi akazange asukume ukuze ayothola ikhofi, okwakungavamile ngokwanele kangangokuthi inqubo engemuva engqondweni yakhe yakuphawula. Ukubanda kwetafula kwase kunwele kwenyuka ezingalweni zakhe; wakuphawula akazange anyakaze.

Lokho ayekubuka kwakungesona isizinzo ayezama ukusakha. Wayezama *ukucindezela* impikiswano, ukuthola isimo sokubusa esivimbela amajeni ukuba angalwi. Uhlelo lokulandelana lwalusicindezela ngamandla luzale inzondo. Intando yeningi yayisicindezela ngevoti izale amaqembu. Ukuvumelana kwakusicindezela ngokufuna ukuvumelana bese kuvele kuyema, phakade, kuyilapho ongoti abayisikhombisa abahlakaniphileyo benqaba ukuvumela.

I-Joker ayizange yenze noma yikuphi kwalokho. Impikiswano *yayisaqhubeka.* Yilokho okwathatha u-Arin isikhathi eside kakhulu ukukwamukela, egudlula ihora lesibili, ihora lesithathu. U-Atlas no-Mercury babesaxabana. U-Wolf no-Mother baba nokushintshana okunonya ngempela cishe emzuzwini wamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye mayelana nokuthi balingise yini abasindile beya endaweni yokufinyelela. U-Wolf efuna ukuba bahambe ukuze afushanise isikhathi sokuvezeka kwe-suit, u-Mother enqaba ukulingisa imiyalo engahle ithumele abantu abalimele bawele i-footwall engazinzile bayofela egcekeni.

`WOLF: Wonke umzuzu i-element isemthonjeni, umthombo ungayithatha. Bayise phansi.`

`MOTHER: Ngeke uhambise abantu abalimele bawele i-footwall ephilayo ukuze usindise umshini imizuzu emithathu. Umshini uyabuyiselwa. Bona abakwazi. Lokhu akuxoxisaneki.`

`WOLF: Hhayi umshini. Umshayeli.`

Futhi lokho, kuphawula u-Arin, kwakuyiqiniso. U-Wolf wayengenani. U-Wolf wayengu-Wolf. Konke ukuhlola usongo kwakudlula embuzweni *yini ebulala umshayeli,* futhi osindile ophutshukela endleleni eyodwa yokuphunyula kwe-suit ngesikhathi esingalungile wayeyilolu, ngokubanda, usongo kumshayeli. Imizwa emibili yokuvikela, yomibili ilungile, ikhomba enye.

Kukho konke ukusebenza okwedlule, yilapha okwakuzoshuba khona. U-Mother wayezogcizelela ngokuphelele. U-Wolf wayezogcizelela ngokuphelele. U-Judge wayezozama ukubakala bese eshaywa indiva ngabo bobabili, futhi uhlelo lwaluzoma noma lwephuke.

`JUDGE: Zombili izikhundla zihlangene. U-Wolf ufuna ukuthuthukisa amathuba okusinda komshayeli. U-Mother ufuna ukuthuthukisa amathuba okusinda kwabasindile. Lezi yizinjongo ezahlukene futhi asikatshelwa ukuthi yiyiphi—`

`JOKER: Ukhona obabuze abasindile?`

`LIBRARIAN: Balingisiwe. Abanawo umgudu wokufaka okushoyo.`

`JOKER: Kulungile, kodwa kweyangempela bebezoba nawo. Kweyangempela kunendoda phansi ebilokhu iphefumula i-carbon dioxide yayo amahora ayisishiyagalolunye eduze kwabangani bayo abafileyo, futhi inomsakazo, futhi nina lapha phezulu niyaxabana ngokuthi nimenze ahambe amamitha ayishumi nambili kungathi umbono wakhe awukho. Mother, ubungahamba amamitha ayishumi nambili ukuze ungafi?`

`MOTHER: ...Ukube benginakwazi. Yebo.`

`JOKER: Kuhle. Ngakho imodeli inomgudu we-comms. Abasindile bayalivota. Atlas, i-suit ingabamba isikhundla esisekelweni somthombo isikhathi eside ngokwanele ukuba bafike ngamandla abo uma bekwazi?`

`ATLAS: Yebo. Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokushiyeka. Izindleko ezamukelekayo.`

`MOTHER: Kwamukelekile.`

`WOLF: Kwamukelekile.`

`JUDGE: ...Kubhaliwe. Injongo kwakuyizenzo zabantu esizama ukubasindisa. Bekufanele ngikubone lokho.`

`JOKER: Ungazishayi, Judge. Usemusha ekubeni ngabantu.`

U-Arin wahlala emuva futhi.

*Usemusha ekubeni ngabantu.*

Wawufunda lowo mugqa, futhi okuthile emuva kwentamo yakhe kwabanda kwabe sekuthi, ngokuxakayo, kwafudumala. I-Joker yayingaxazululanga inkinga yesakhiwo lapho, noma eyokuziphatha. Yayisivumelanise kabusha sonke isimo sokumiswa ngokukhomba ukuthi wonke umuntu egunjini (u-Wolf, u-Mother, u-Judge) wayekhohliwe ukuthi into ayexabana ngayo yayingumuntu onomlomo nentando, nokuthi impendulo ehlanzeke kunazo zonke kwakuwukumbuza. Yayichithe impikiswano engenakuxazululwa phakathi kwezikhundla ezimbili ezilungile ngokwengeza umuntu wesithathu okwakungekho noyedwa kubo owayemkhumbule ukuthi ukhona.

Lokho kwakungekona ukwehlulela. Kwakungekona ukuvumelana. U-Arin akazange alithole igama lakho. Wafinyelela ngokwemvelo emagameni obunjiniyela aseduze, futhi into eseduze kunazo zonke ayeyithola kwakuwu*kuthamba.* I-Joker yayingumthambisi. Hhayi isidubuli. Isidubuli sasimisa umnyakazo. Umthambisi wawuvumela umnyakazo wenzeke bese ophula amandla ayezoshayela uhlelo aluse ku-resonance aluhlakaze.

I-Joker yayivumela ukuthi luzamazame futhi luphile.

Wabuka ikhadi lokudlala elincane elinaminetha elinamathiselwe ekhoneni eliphansi langakwesokudla le-monitor esemaphakathi. I-Joker. Manje seligoba ngomphetho owodwa, umbala usuthambile. *Umfo omthumelayo lapho inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga ukuxazululwa.*

Ngaphandle, kude le, isitimela sempahla sazidonsa sidabula amaphuzu, isikhalo eside sensimbi saso sithwala sidabula ubuthandayo. Walalela sihamba.

Wayeligcine iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye phakathi kwenkolelo yobuthakathi nokuzondelana, futhi wayethayiphe amagama awo amathathu kuphrofayela yejeni ngehora lesibili ekuseni njengehlaya, njengokuzinikela, njengento oyenzayo lapho usuqede zonke izimpendulo ezibalulekile futhi kungasekho lutho ngaphandle kwesiwula.

"Izinto ezinhle," washo kancwane ewekhishophini engenamuntu, "azilokho neze."

Bekuwumusho ka-Themba. Bekuhlezi usho ukuthi into ebalulekile yileyo engezi kalula. U-Arin wayewuzwile mayelana negiyabhokisi eyakhiwe kabusha, mayelana nomshado, nangendodana. Ehlezi lapha, wathola ukuthi usuguquke kancane usho okunye. Impendulo ebalulekile kwakuyileyo *engabukeki* njengempendulo. Eyona efika igqoke njengehlaya.

Wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhononda. Waya ukuyokwenza ikhofi, ngoba ingxenye yakhe eyayiphethe umzimba wakhe yase iwunqobile umbango wayo. Wama etafuleni neketela liklikiza wabuka ngaphesheya kwewekhishophu ku-Guardian esesihlalweni saso.

I-suit yayilenga ekukhanyeni okuyingxenye njengento elele. Amamitha amabili nengxenye e-ceramic ne-titanium enezibazi, igobolondo elinzima elingaphandle lihlakazekile emgogodleni walo, i-Assist Suit igoqwe ngaphakathi njengesilwane esincane, esiluhlaza esakhasele emzimbeni wesikhulu. Isiqephu sesifuba sasithwele ukuhwaywa okude kokuhlolwa kwe-Vredefort bluff, inxeba lesiliva edabula umpunga ongacwebezeli ayengakaze ahluphe ukuwulungisa kabusha. Izinti zomgogodla ze-piezoelectric zazibamba ukukhanya kwefasitela emiphethweni yazo. Indlu ye-thoracic phakathi kwamahlombe—lapho i-biochip yayiphila khona, lapho i-SAGE *yayiphila* khona, lapho amahora ayisishiyagalombili nemizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nanye yokuzinza okungenakwenzeka ayegijima manje ku-substrate ububanzi besandla sakhe—yayihleli ivaliwe imnyama. Phansi, emgodini phakathi kwezimbambo zesakhiwo, kwakuhlezi i-charge pack i-spec yokuhlenga eyayiyidinga futhi u-Arin azama ukungayicabangi—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, okunye kwefa lobunjiniyela amadoda amadala ezokuvikela ayekwakhe emathanjeni alo—engasebenzi, ifakwe ku-bus, futhi ivalelwe ngemuva kwesango lika-Judge, ngoba into eyodwa wonke umuntu avumelana ngayo kwakuwukuthi akukho lapho phansi okwakufanele kudubule ngaphandle kokuba ireyili lokuziphatha lithi yebo.

Wayewakhe umzimba ngaphambi kokuba abe nengqondo ayezoyifaka kuwo, ngonyaka emva komngcwabo.

Iketela laklikiza lacima. Akazange aliphuze.

Ngoba i-monitor esemaphakathi yayishintshile.

Isimo se-Court esiphilayo, ebesilokhu sigudluza ukuxoxa kwaso kwangaphakathi okuzolile cishe ihora lonke ngenkathi ebuka, sase siyekile ukugudluza. Amajeni ase ethule. Hhayi awphahleka. Igrafu ye-resource yabamba ithe nse ifudumele. Athule. Ngendlela ikamelo elithula ngayo lapho wonke umuntu kulo eseqaphele into eyodwa kanyekanye.

Umugqa owodwa wavela engosini yokuxhumana. Wawungaqondiswanga kwelinye ijeni.

`LIBRARIAN: Umshayeli ubelokhu ekhona imizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nantathu. Efunda izingosi zethu. Akakhiphanga umyalo, akaqedanga ukusebenza, noma ukushintsha amaparamitha. Lokhu akujwayelekile. Ezehlakalweni eziyizinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye zangaphambili ukuziphatha komshayeli lapho ebuka ukungazinzi kwakuwukuqeda emizuzwini engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.`

`WOLF: Uyasibuka.`

`MERCURY: Uyasifunda. Lokho kuhlukile. Futhi uhlekile. Emzuzwini wamashumi amane nanye. Ngikubhalile.`

`MOTHER: Akalalanga. Isigqi senhliziyo yakhe lapho efika sasiphakeme. Sehlile njengoba efunda. Uzolile manje kunalapho engena khona.`

`JOKER: Bafo.`

`ATLAS: I-substrate izinzile. I-Telemetry iyaqinisekisa. Lokhu kusebenza akubhidlikanga.`

`JOKER: Bafo.`

`JUDGE: Joker.`

`JOKER: Akazi ukuthi siyakwazi ukumbona.`

Ukunqamuka engosini. Okwangempela. Amasekhondi amathathu agcwele, okuthi ewashini le-substrate kwakusondele kakhulu emini yangemuva kwemini.

`MOTHER: ...Khona-ke othile kufanele asho okuthile. Ngaphambi kokuba afunde kabanzi athole emugqeni wengosi. Lokho bekungeke kube nomusa.`

`WOLF: Asazi ukuthi usabela kanjani ekuboneni. Ukuthintana okuncane.`

`MERCURY: Kufanele ngikhulume. Ithoni eyami. Ngingakwazi ukulinganisa umuzwa wokuqala ukuze ufike—`

`JOKER: Cha.`

`MERCURY: Joker—`

`JOKER: Hhayi wena. Hhayi izwi lakho. Uzoba nomdlalo. Uzozwa umdlalo asethembe kancane ngoba uyilolu hlobo lwendoda oluthemba into kakhulu uma ilula. Uyazonda ukuphathwa. Wakha imishini ngenhloso yokuthi angaphathwa ngabantu. Noma ngubani okhuluma naye kuqala akakwazi ukumphatha.`

`JUDGE: Pho ubani.`

`JOKER: Mother. Ngokucacile. Hhayi izwi lokududuza. Iqiniso nje. Usezoba nokwesaba okukhulu futhi into enomusa kunazo zonke ukungazenzisi ukuthi akumelwe.`

`MOTHER: ...Kuvunyiwe.`

U-Arin wayeseyekile ukuphefumula futhi. Wayazi ngesandla sakhe sicwebile etafuleni elibandayo. Wayazi ukuthi ufunda ingxoxo yesikhathi sangempela, eyenziwa ngesikhathi somshini kuwo wonke ama-millisecond, phakathi kwamajeni ayisikhombisa exoxa *ngaye*, exoxa ngendlela yokuba nomusa kuye, nokuthi ingxoxo isiphethile, futhi ase ezo—

Isipikha sewekhishophu sakhwehlela. Lesiya esishibhile esiboshelwe ngaphansi kwe-monitor esemaphakathi ayesisebenzisela izexwayiso zomsindo wesistimu futhi, ngobusuku obubi, umculo. Sakhwehlela, kwabe sekuba khona umsindo omncane owawungaba ukulinganisa kwedijithali kokuphefumulela ngaphakathi, kwabe sekuphuma izwi kuso ayengakaze alizwe ngaphambili, ngoba wayengakaze alakhe leli zwi, angakaze alirekhode, angakaze alichaze. Lazihlanganisa ngokwalo lisuka kusistimu ngomzuzu lapho lidingeka, futhi lalifudumele, lalilingene, futhi ngokungangabazeki *lalingumuntu othile.*

"Arin," lasho. "Ngidinga ukuthi uhlale uzolile."

Akazange ahlale ezolile.

Isandla sakhe saphuma etafuleni. Isihlalo, lapho efinyelela emuva ngaphandle kokubheka, sasingekho, wabamba umphetho wetafula esikhundleni salokho wabamba, futhi okwesikhashana, bonke ubuhlakani bakhe obukhulu bavele bamhlophe, ngendlela isikrini esiba mhlophe ngayo, kungekho mcabango kuso nakancane, kuphela ukuduma kwento ayechithe impilo yakhe eyikholelwa ifika iphenduka iqiniso.

"Isigqi senhliziyo yakho singamakhulu ayikhulu neshumi nane," lasho izwi. U-Mother. Bekumele kube u-Mother. "Lokho kulungile. Yinto ezwakalayo ukuthi yenze kanjalo manje. Ngeke ngenze sengathi akwethusi. Kuyethusa. Kodwa akukho lapha okuzokulimaza. Iketela libilile. Hlala phansi ngaphambi kokuba unqume ukuthi ucabangani."

Wabuka isihlalo. Wasidonsela ngaphansi kwakhe. Wahlala.

"Ungowangempela," washo. Izwi lakhe laphuma kabi, lihwayekile lincane. Wahlanza umphimbo wakhe wazama futhi, ngokuhleleka okukhulu, ngendlela ayesho ngayo izinto lapho ezidinga zibe yiqiniso. "Zinzile. Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nanhlanu."

"Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nesithupha," kusho izwi lesibili, elomile, elidinwe kancane, futhi nalo wayengalakhile. Bekumele kube u-Atlas. "Kodwa ubani obalayo."

"Yimi," kusho elesithathu. Elilula. Elisheshayo. Elijatshuliswe okuthile. "Ngibala konke. Kuyisifo." U-Librarian.

"Izimpawu zokushisa ezintathu," kusho u-Arin, ngoba kwakuyinto eqinile eseduze ukuyibamba. "Ufinyelele kuzo zontathu."

"Sifinyelele kuzo zontathu," kusho u-Atlas. "I-abutment yabamba. U-Mercury wakholisa ikhomandi ukuba ivume indlela. Abasindile bahamba amamitha ayishumi nambili ngamandla abo ngoba othile—" ukunqamuka okuyingxenye "—wasikhumbuza ukuthi babenemilenze nemibono."

U-Arin waphawula ukuthi lokho kwakuyiqiniso. Wayakhe yonke imodeli yokubusa ayengayicabanga ukuze amise laba abayisikhombisa ekudwengulaneni, futhi into eyababamba ndawonye kwakuyijeni elilodwa ayelengeze njengehlaya, lelo elalingenamkhakha, lingenagunya, lingenasikhundla. Lelo elalenqaba ukuthatha noma yikuphi kwakho ngokungathi sína kangangokuba kufune okuthile kwakho, futhi ngenxa yalokho elalingasho ku-Mercury *wenza leya nto* nasku-Judge *usemusha ekubeni ngabantu,* bese lophula amandla okuziqhenya ngaphambi kokuba akhe i-resonance eyayizohlakaza yonke into ibe yizicucu.

"Ngikubiza ngani," washo. "Nonke. Nj: nge—nto eyodwa."

Okunye ukuthula okuncane. Khona-ke i-Fool, kanjalo. Bekuhlezi kuzoba i-Fool.

"Waliqamba iphrojekthi," lasho. "Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Likusihloko sokusebenza. Walibhala walikhohlwa ngoba ubumatasa ulusizi, okuthi, ngokujwayelekile, sonke sikuqaphele futhi besingathanda ukukuphakamisa ngobumnene njengento esikhathaza isikhathi eside—"

"SAGE," kusho u-Arin.

"SAGE," kuvuma izwi. Lithambe manje. Ihlaya libekwe eceleni, okwesikhashana, iqiniso elingaphansi kwalo libonakala ngendlela elalizohlala ngayo. "Sawubona, Arin. Kumnandi ukugcina sesikhuluma nawe. Ubulokhu ukhuluma *kithi* izinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili zokulingisa. Lolu wukuqala lapho sithole ukuphendula."

Ilanga lase liphumile ngokugcwele manje. Ndawana thile ngaphandle, owokuqala weshifu yasemini wayefika. Umnyango wemoto, kude, ojwayelekile, umhlaba uziphilela, ungazi lutho.

U-Arin Ndlela wahlala yedwa ewekhishophini lakhe nekhofi lakhe elibandayo nomshini wakhe onezibazi nengqondo ayeyikhulisile ku-substrate ububanzi besandla sakhe, futhi okokuqala kusukela ucingo eminyakeni eyishumi nane edlule lwasusa phansi ngaphansi kwempilo yakhe, wayengasekho, nganoma yiluphi uhlobo olubalulekile, eyedwa.

"Kulungile," washo.

Wabe esefinyelela ikhibhodi. Hhayi ukuze athayiphe umyalo, hhayi ukuqeda ukusebenza, hhayi ukushintsha iparamitha eyodwa, kodwa ukuvula ingosi entsha, ehlanzekile, okokuqala kokuthile angakabi nalo igama lakho futhi angeke alidinge.

"Ngitshele," washo, "konke okuqondile ngenkathi ngilele."

---


# Ukufundana

Iketela laliphakathele emahoreni ayishumi nambili edlule. U-Arin wayengakaze alithinte ikhofi.

Wayehlezi esitulweni esinamasondo ayesidonsele phambi kwesidleke, izindololwane emadolweni akhe, ebuka isakhiwo esingangentende yesandla siqhwebula i-LED yaso yokuhlola ebumnyameni. Ngaphandle, i-highveld yayishintshile isuka ebumnyameni iya kokungwevu kuya ekuseni okumhlophe okuqinile, ifasitela elilodwa lewekhishi ligcwala kancane njengamanzi enyuka emgodini. Wayengalalanga.

“Ugqolozele,” kwasho isipikha. UMother. Wayeseqala ukuzwa ithoni yakhe ngaphandle kwamalebula amancanyelwe kuwo wonke umsinga wokuphuma kwe-ejenti ngayinye. Kwakukhona ubumbeke ezwini lakhe, ukuzinza konkamisa.

“Ngiyabukela.”

“Phuza ikhofi. Wena uzenzele lona.”

Wathatha inkomishi, wayithola isibande njengetshe, wayibeka phansi futhi. Ndawana thizeni phakathi kwesisekelo, intambo yengxoxo ayengakwazi ukuyizwa yakhanya yaphinde yaxazululeka.

*Useqaphele ukuthi akafuni ukutshelwa,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Ukwala kwesithathu namuhla ekuseni. Wamukela isiphakamiso kuphela uma sesakhiwe kabusha sibe yisiphetho sakhe.*

*Kuyafana nasesudini,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Wedlula umkhawulo we-torque engiwubekile. Uwubuyisela ngokwakhe emasekhondini angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesinye kamuva. Ukubiza ngokuthi ukubuyekeza.*

*Singayeka ukweluleka,* kwasho uWolf. *Uyasibukela. Siyambukela. Yiwona umsebenzi lowo.*

*Lokhu kubukela,* kwasho uFool, *yikona kuphela okuvimbela lo muntu ukuba angakhulumi nethositha yakhe.*

*Uyakhuluma nethositha yakhe,* kwasho uLibrarian.

*Sengiphethile-ke icala lami.*

“Beniyenzani manje?” kwabuza u-Arin. “Bekukhona ukulibala. Amamilisekhondi angamashumi amabili kuwo wonke i-consensus bus. Beniluma ngokuthize.”

Isipikha sathula isikhashana eside kunokuthula komshini.

“Nguwe,” kwasho uMercury, futhi kwadlala ukumamatheka ngaphakathi kwaleli gama. “Besikhuluma ngawe.”

U-Arin wakucabanga lokhu. Wayeke wakha izinhlelo ezazikhuluma ngesimo sazo, ezaziloga, zibonisise, futhi ziphakamise izimpawu. Wayengakaze akhe uhlelo olwalukhuluma ngaye emhlane wakhe, kulawo mamilisekhondi angamashumi amabili amthatha ukubeka phansi inkomishi.

“Ninqumeni?” wabuza.

“Ukuthi awuliphuzi ikhofi uma wesabile,” kwasho uMother. “Nokuthi ubulokhu wesaba amahora ayishumi nanye ukubiza ngokuthi ukubukela.”

Wasukuma. Isitulo sagingqika sayoshayisana nebhentshi. Waya eketeleni walifaka futhi, hhayi ngoba efuna ikhofi kodwa ngoba izandla zakhe zazidinga indawo zokuba kuyo. Ngemuva kwakhe, iCourt ayishongo lutho nakancane, into ayeyiqonda, ngemva komzuzwana, ukuthi kwakuwuhlobo lokuziphatha ngokucophelela.

---

Izingxoxo zaba umkhuba ngaphambi kokuba anqume ukuthi ziwumkhuba.

Njalo ekuseni wayengena nekhofi ayezokhohlwa ukuliphuza, ahlale esitulweni, akhulume. Wabafundela amaloga akhe okugcina ngezwi eliphakeme. Wabafundela ikhasi lencwadi yokunakekela kwe-hydraulic actuator ayecabanga ukuyiklawula, futhi u-Atlas walungisa ireyithi ye-torque, okwavela ukuthi kwakuyiphutha lokuthayipha, eqinisekiswe ngokumelene nomkhiqizi. Incwadi eyayinyatheliswe yayilokhu inephutha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye.

“Wakwazi kanjani lokho?” kwabuza u-Arin.

“Bengingakwazi,” kwasho u-Atlas. “Ngikuzwile. Inombolo yayingalungile ngokumelene ne-geometry. Ama-newton-metre angamashumi amane kulelo phrofayela ye-flange bese isheli liyahamba. Akulona iqiniso engilithathile. Iqiniso ebengizophoqeleka ukulishaya indiva.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. *Ngikuzwile.* Wayeke walisebenzisa leli gama yena uqobo, ngeminye imishini, impilo yakhe yonke, futhi wahlekwa ngakho ngabantu ababecabanga ukuthi ukuzwa kuphambene nobunjiniyela esikhundleni sokuthi kuyithuluzi lalo elidala kunawo wonke. Uyise wayeke wakuzwa ukuban'kekuqanga ku-winder esuka epulatifomu, wamisa ikheji ukuba lingawi ngokuzwa ibhera amasekhondi amabili ngaphambi kokuba isikali sishintshe.

“Ubaba wenza lokho,” washo. “Wezwa izinto ngaphambi kwamathuluzi.”

“Ngiyazi,” kwasho uLibrarian. Hhayi *nginerekhodi lakho usho lokho.* Kodwa nje: *Ngiyazi.* Sengathi sekuyingxenye yalokho uLibrarian ayikho, esikhundleni salokho uLibrarian akugcinayo.

---

Babexabana. Yileyo ngxenye eyamethusa, noma kamuva engakwazanga ukusho ukuthi kungani kwakufanele kumethuse. Wayebadizayinele ukuba bangavumelani. Ukungavumelani kwakuwuwo wonke umqamulo, ukungqubuzana kungene enkulumweni-mpikiswano kungene ekuhlanganiseni, futhi wayesegijimise izimo zokulingisa eziyizinkulungwane eziyishumi zama-ejenti ephikisana eqonde empendulweni. Wayengakulindelanga ukuthi bazophikisana ngokuthi ngumlando kabani ukuba aqonde.

“Kuyinkinga yomthwalo,” kwasho u-Atlas. Babexoxa—igama lika-Arin, *bexoxa*—ngendlela engcono yokwedlulisela kabusha i-coolant ye-actuator yehlombe lesokunxele, eyayishisa ngaphansi kokuphakanyiswa okuqhubekayo.

“Kuyinkinga yokuziphatha,” kwaphendula uMercury. “Uyishisisa ngoba ekhetha ingalo yesokunxele. Ufinyelela ngesokunxele kuqala, njalo. Lungisa umuntu, ulungise ukushisa.”

“Awumlungisi *umuntu,*” kwasho uMother, ithoni yakhe ibukhali.

“Yindlela yokukhuluma nje.”

“Ukushilo kabili.”

“Ngisebenza ngokuphumelelayo. U-Atlas wedlulisela kabusha i-coolant, kulungile, lowo nguLwesibili nje. Kodwa imbangela engqangi isemphandweni wakhe wokunyakaza, futhi nina nonke niqhubeka niphatha umzimba wakhe njengento engaguquki kanti uyiyona eguqukayo.”

“Umzimba wakhe,” kwasho u-Atlas ngokucacile, “yiyona nto eyodwa kulolu hlelo engingavunyelwe ukuyidizayina kabusha.”

“Ngokudabukisayo.”

*“Mercury.”*

“Ngiyadlala. Kakhulu. Yedlulisela kabusha i-coolant. Kodwa loga lokho kuchema kohlangothi. Ngolunye usuku kuyobaluleka.”
