U-Arin wayeyekile ukuzenzisa ethayipha. Walalela izingqondo eziyisikhombisa ziphikisana ngezinga lokushisa lengalo yakhe siqu.

---

UTheo wafika ngoLwesine, ngoba uTheo wayehlala efika ngoLwesine, ngendlela abanye abantu beyiziphepho abanye beyizinkathi zonyaka. Wazingenisa ngekhodi u-Arin ayemnike yona eminyakeni eyedlule engakaze ayishintshe, ephethe isikhwama sephepha esasinuka okuthize okugazingiwe nethemose lecaba elabamba wala lithi lingcono kunekhofi kodwa lehluleka ukukufakazela.

Wama ezinyathelweni ezimbili engaphakathi komnyango. Wayenokuthula uma ekunaka ngempela; abantu babekuphambanisa nokuzola.

“Awulalanga.” Wabeka isikhwama ekhoneni elihlanzeke kakhulu lebhentshi, elalingahlanzekile. “Khuluma nami.”

U-Arin wayengazi ukuthi aqale kanjani, ngakho wakhomba isidleke, isipikha, isakhiwo, sengathi igumbi lalingazichaza. Alizange. Iwekhishi yayibukeka ngendlela efana ncamashi nakuwo wonke amanye amaLwesine: i-exoskeleton elenga, ubuningi bama-actuator, udonga lwebhodi elimhlophe lugcwele umbhalo wakhe wesandla wenza ihlathi emaphethelweni.

“Sho okuthize,” watshela isipikha.

Isipikha asishongo lutho.

“Ngeke benze,” kwasho u-Arin. “Ngokuyalwa.”

Ishiya likaTheo lanyakaza ibanga elinembile. Wadonsa ikhreyithi, wahlala, wavula ithemose, futhi iphunga letiye laliwuju nentuthu. “Bona,” washo. “Hhayi *kona.*”

“Bona,” wavumelana u-Arin, futhi leli gama, lishiwo ngokuzwakalayo emuntwini ngokokuqala ngqa, lenza inhliziyo yakhe yenza okuthize akhetha ukungakuhlaziyi.

“Sawubona,” kwasho uMother, kuTheo, kuthambile njengeSonto. “Akalalanga. Mtshele.”

UTheo akethukanga. U-Arin wayecabange ukuthi angethuka, wayecishe wakufisa, ngendlela umuntu athemba ngayo ukuthi umuntu wesibili uzoqinisekisa into ebonwe ebumnyameni. Kodwa uTheo wagobisa ikhanda lakhe nje ngasesipikheni sengathi ubingelela umlingani esithangamini igama lakhe alikhohliwe.

“Akalali nje,” kwasho uTheo kuye. “Kuthwala umthwalo. Kususe konke kuwe bese bonke ubuntu buyawa.”

“Yilokho engikushilo,” kwakhipha u-Atlas.

“Umthwalo ohlukile,” kwasho uMercury.

“Konke kuwumthwalo,” kwasho u-Atlas.

UTheo wahleka, ohlekayo ngempela, esuka esifubeni, wabuka u-Arin ngamehlo akhe akhanyayo nawamanzi emakhoneni ngendlela engahlangene nosizi. “Bangaki,” washo.

“Bayisikhombisa.”

“Futhi abavumelani.”

“Njalonjalo.”

“Futhi kuzinzile.”

“Amahora ayishumi nanye uma ungena. Amashumi amane nantathu manje.” U-Arin wezwa izwi lakhe lenza into eliyenzayo uma esezosho into ayengenayo isakhiwo sokuyisekela. “Theo. Ngengeze owesikhombisa ngehlaya. Ngangiqedile. Ngangizolahla lo msebenzi. Kwakukhona ikhadi kumonitha yami—” wakhomba; uJoker wayeselokhu elenga lapho, enamathiselwe, ephuphukile yilanga—“ngathayipha amagama amathathu ngaya kolala futhi—” Wama. “Yiyona ebamba abanye ndawonye. Le engingakholelwanga kuyo.”

UTheo wathela itiye esivalweni sethemose, waphephetha phezu kwalo, akaphuzanga. Lapho ekhuluma, izwi lakhe lalinesisindo socophelelo somuntu obeka phansi into abeyithwele isikhathi eside.

“UJung wayenegama,” washo. “Lengxenye yengqondo engeke ibuswe. Umkhohlisi. Wamthola kuyo yonke imithombo yezinganekwane ayivulayo: ikhoyothi, igwababa, uLoki, isiwula ku-tarot. Njalo lo mlingiswa ofanayo. Njalo lowo owephula umthetho wenkosi futhi ngandlela thile odingekayo embusweni.” Waguqula isivalo ezandleni zakhe. “Ingqondo eqaphelayo yakha isigaba sobukhosi. Inkosi phezulu, inkantolo ngaphansi, isithunzi sisokelwe emgodini. Futhi kuyasebenza, isikhashana, bese kuba lukhuni, ngoba izigaba zobukhosi ziyenze njalo, futhi into eyisindisayo ayikaze ibe yinkosi. Yisiwula. Ngoba isiwula singasho lokho inkosi engeke isimele ukukwazi.”

---

*Uchaza mina,* kwasho uFool, ejabule. *Othize wangibhala phansi. Ngisencwadini.*

*Usezincwadini eziningana,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Akukho nokukodwa kwazo okukuncomayo.*

*Ngizothatha ukudumile esikhundleni sokunconywa.*

*Thulani,* kwasho uJudge, futhi iCourt yathula, ngoba uJudge wayecela kungandile kangaka. *Lalelani le ngxenye. Utshela u-Arin into ayidingayo angeke ayithathe kithi. Iloge. Kuwusizo ukwazi ukuthi yimaphi amaqiniso angawezwa kuphela ezwini lomunye umuntu.*

---

“Usho ukuthi ngakhe ingqondo,” kwasho u-Arin. Ngokucacile. Ehlola umusho ukuthi unezintunja yini.

“Ngithi,” kwasho uTheo, “wafinyelela embonweni omdala ungazi ukuthi mdala. Abantu benza njalo. Abahle bakwenza ngokwedlulele kunalokho abakucabangayo.” Ekugcineni walidla itiye, wenza ubuso, waliphuza kakhulu. “UFreud wayecabanga ukuthi ingqondo iyinto eyodwa elwa nayo. UJung wayecabanga ukuthi iyiphalamende. Umphakathi. Imisebenzi ehlukene, amazwi ahlukene, akekho noyedwa kuwo ongumuntu ophelele, futhi umuntu uqobo—” wabeka isivalo phansi “—umuntu uqobo akusiye noyedwa kubo. Umuntu uqobo yingxoxo. Ubudlelwano phakathi kwezingxenye.”

Iwekhishi yatikitiza. Iketela laliphole futhi.

“Bayamfunda,” kwasho uTheo ekugcineni, ngokuthula, esipikheni, ngendlela ongasho ngayo into emndenini wesiguli.

“Yebo,” kwasho uMother.

“Yini anifundisa yona?”

Ukuthula. KwakunguWolf ophendulayo, okwenza bonke, abantu nabangebona, bathule.

“Lokho akuvikelayo,” kwasho uWolf. “Ngalokho angavumi ukuba kuphuke.”

UTheo wabuka isakhiwo isikhathi eside ngemva kwalokho, ngendlela umuntu abuka ngayo umnyango asanda kuqaphela ukuthi uvuleka ngezindlela zombili. Wabe esevala ithemose, wasukuma, washo into ayezele ukuyosho ngaphambi kokuba azi ukuthi uzele ukuyosho.

“Bakhiphele ngaphandle,” washo. “Ubulokhu ubafundisa wena egunjini elinezibani ezicishiwe. Bavumele ukuba bafunde ukuthi uyini uma kukhona into ngaphansi kwezinyawo zakho engakuwisa.”

---

I-suspension yeflatbedi yatswininiza emamitheni ayikhulu okugcina omzila, yabe isizinikela eveldini. U-Arin wacisha injini, kwabe sekugcwala ukuthula, hhayi njengokungabikho kodwa njengobukhona: umoya otshanini nokukhala kude kwento ezungezayo phezulu. Amawa ezwe lokushayisana ayemashumi amane emizuzu ngaphandle kwedolobha uma itrafikhi imxolele, kanti ayingakwenzanga lokho, ngakho kwakusondele emashumini ayisishiyagalolunye; i-highveld yayinabe emikhwantsheni emide ephuzi-nsundu, inezimanga ze-geology eneminyaka eyizigidigidi ezimbili ubudala, idwala liphuma otshanini njengamathambo omhlaba oshayiwe.

Wayethanda lapha. Umhlaba awucelanga lutho, awuzange ufune ukubukana ngamehlo.

Phezu kwedeki yeflatbedi, ngaphansi kwendwangu nezintambo zokuqinisa ezintathu, uGuardian wayelele ulele.

“I-Telemetry iluhlaza,” washo. I-Assist Suit yayimlingana njengohlelo lwezinzwa lwesibili ngaphansi kwezingubo zakhe, i-lattice emhlophe-mpempe ilandela umgogodla nezitho zakhe. Isakhiwo se-biochip sasizinze phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ubunzima obufudumele. Imizuzu engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye esesudini yayizozisile izandla zakhe ezazithuthumela.

*Uluhlaza luphana kakhulu,* kwaphendula u-Atlas, owomile futhi onembile. *Ibhethri liku-91. Ama-supercapacitor bank asezingeni. I-actuator yeqakala lesokunxele ingamadigri angu-point-three iphumile eqondweni futhi ibilokhu injalo amasonto amabili. Uhlala ungayilungisi.*

“Iyasebenza,” kwaphendula u-Arin.

*Isebenza njengebhuloho ngosuku ngaphambi kokuba luwe.*

“Ngiqaphele.” U-Arin wehlisa isango langemuva waqala ukukhulula izintambo. “Mother. Nginjani?”

*I-Cortisol inyukile. Lokho yintshisekelo, hhayi ukwesaba.* Izwi likaMother lalifudumele futhi liqinile, uhlobo lokufudumala olungashwabani. *Unamanzi emzimbeni. Udlile, into engikhetha ukuzizwa ngiphilile ngayo. Isigqi senhliziyo sizolile. Uyazijabulisa.*

“Angizijabulisi izinto.”

*Uyazijabulisa,* kwaphikisa uMother.

Wadonsa indwangu wayisusa. UGuardian ekukhanyeni okuqinile kwasekuseni: ompunga-thithanu, enezimanga emapuleti ehlombe ngenxa yezinyanga eziyisithupha zokuhlukunyezwa. Izivikelo ezahlukene zabamba ilanga, zalibonisa ngamapleyini amade angcwabalalayo. Imitha emibili nengxenye yomshini ogoqwe ngendlela yokuthuthwa, njengesidalwa esiphumulayo. Wawungabukeki njengesikhali; wawubukeka njengento eyakhelwe ukumunca isijeziso ukuze into ethambile ngaphakathi ingabi nesidingo.

Wayewakhele kanjalo kanye. Ngaso sonke isikhathi ewembula, wayezama ukungacabangi ukuthi le nto ethambile yayiqonde ukuba ngubani ekuqaleni.

“I-Coupling sequence,” kwasho u-Arin, egxoba ngemuva engena esidlekeni.

---

I-dock yayingeyona leyo noma yiyiphi ividiyo yokukhombisa eyake yayikhombisa ngokuthembeka. Yayingahle. Kwakuwumuntu egxoba ngokucophelela angene emshinini: i-spinal collar ihlangana ngomsindo othi klani owanqenqethile emazinyweni akhe, amapuleti evala emilenzeni nasesiqwini sakhe ngokulandelana ayengakucula ubuthongo. Ukuqabula okubandayo kwemigqa ye-coolant ihlangana, ihanesi idonsela ngokuqinile.

Khona-ke ukuxhumana kwajula, futhi umhlaba washintsha.

Kukhona into eyenzekayo, noma ehluleka ukwenzeka, emzimbeni. U-Arin wayechithe iminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu emzimbeni owawufika ingxenye yesekhondi ngemva kwenhloso yakhe, owawubeka isandla sakhe lapho okwakungafanele sibe khona, owawumenza umfana okhethwa kokugcina hhayi ngenxa yonya kodwa ngenxa yezibalo. Abanye babembonile ezama ukubamba bese benza nje, ngokunembile, balungise imodeli yabo.

Isudi yavala leli gebe.

Hhayi ngokumenza abe namandla, noma yakwenza lokho. Ngokumenza *abe nobunye.* I-Assist Suit yafunda inhloso yakhe ngaphambi kokuba umsipha uqede ukuyakha, iCourt yayixazulula nge-lattice, futhi insimbi yanyakaza uma enyakaza, ingaphambili kwalutho ingaphandle kwalutho. Wasukuma. UGuardian wasukuma naye, amakhilogremu ayinkulungwane namakhulu amathathu enyuka sengathi awunabunzima, ama-footplate ecindezela emhlabathini obomvu. Empilweni yakhe iningi, umzimba wakhe wawuyizwe alivakashelayo kodwa angakaze alithembe ngokugcwele. Lapha ngaphakathi, wayengowomdabu.

Wagoba isandla sakhe sokudla. Amapuleti avuleka aphinde ahlale emaminweni ngomsindo onjengezinhlamvu zamakhadi.

*Range of motion isezingeni,* kwasho uWolf, into eningi ayeyisho esontweni elilodwa.

“Siqala kuphi?”

Ukuthula. Ukuthula kweCourt, okwabantu kwakungeyona inkathi nakancane, kodwa ngokwabo kwakuyingxoxo ephelele ayengayizwa.

*Ubuso obusempumalanga,* kwasho u-Atlas ekugcineni. *Imitha eyishumi nambili, i-slab enama-holds amahle, umphumela ophansi. Fudumeza i-climbing gait ngaphambi kokuba wenze noma yini ewubuwula.*

*Uzokwenza into ewubuwula ekugcineni,* kwanezela uMercury ngokumnandi. *Singamane sivumele u-Atlas azishaye sengathi angakuvimba.*

“Ubuso obusempumalanga,” wavumelana u-Arin.

---

Wanyakaza.

I-quadruped gait yayizwakale iyinqaba ukuyifunda manje yase iyiyona engokwemvelo kakhulu, iwela phambili, isisindo sabiwa kuwo wonke amaphuzu amane okuthinta. UGuardian wageleza ezweni elaphukile ngokulondoloza okwaqala kwamhlukumeza. Wawunganyakazi njengomshini ozenza isilwane; wawunyakaza njengesilwane esenzeke ukuthi ngumshini. Ikati elikhulu, isinambuzane esikhwelayo, okuthize ebuchosheni besibankwa obabona *isidlamlilo* no*sokwazi* ngokuthuthumela okufanayo.

Ubuso obusempumalanga banyuka otshanini, i-slab ye-quartzite eguguliwe etsheke cishe amadigri angamashumi ayisikhombisa, eqwetshwe izifohlo zobudala obungacatshangelwa. U-Arin wafinyelela esisekelweni wasukuma waba mbili, ebuka umugqa wabo, ezwa ama-holds ngaphambi kokuwakhetha. Hhayi ngokuqaphela; iCourt yenza i-geometry, ipenda ukwenzeka kuyo yonke ingqondo yakhe njengomuzwa azalwa nawo.

*Ama-holds lapha, lapha, lapha,* kwaqondisa u-Atlas, futhi isandla sika-Arin sanyakazela ezindaweni ezikhonjisiwe. *I-Friction ilungile. Idwala liqinile phansi. Ungathembi lutho ngaphezu kwe-eight-meter band—kunesigaba se-delamination. Ukucindezeleka kokushayisana okudala.*

Wakhwela.

Amapuleti kaGuardian athola i-quartzite abamba. Ama-piezoelectric strut emgogodleni nasezithweni amunca umthwalo, eguqula ingxenye yawo abuyisele njenge-charge, ucindezelo luba amandla, isimiso ayewakhe ngalo wonke lo mshini. Kwasebenza nje kuphela ngenxa ye-coupling layer ema-strut, futhi i-coupling layer yayiyinkinga ayexazululile ngendlela ayexazulula ngayo izinto eziningi: ngokwala ukwamukela ukuthi ayinakuxazululwa. Yonke into etextbooks ezazinikeza ilahlekelwa amandla amaningi kakhulu emngceleni, iwacedele njengokushisa esikhundleni sokuwadlulisela ngokucacile phakathi kokuqina neseli. Yonke into ngaphandle kwento eyodwa—i-platinum-group alloy i-defence lab eyayiyithuthukisele ama-hydrophone array yabe isiyiyeka, ngoba ngobumsulwa obabuyenza isebenze yabiza ngekhilogremu yedlula lokho uhlelo lwalungakuqinisekisa, futhi ifolda yayo yangena edrowini elinemaka elithi *not viable* kwathi lapho, ngochungechunge lwemisebenzi nesenzo esingokomthetho sokuntshontsha, sangena ezandleni zika-Arin. Ngokomthetho ukuntshontsha, futhi hhayi ngokomthetho kuphela: omncane ogama lakhe lingu-Pillay wasayina isampula iphuma akaze ayisayine ibuya, kwathi lapho i-audit ifika ingxenye enkulu yeminyaka emibili kamuva kwakuyigama likaPillay egebeni, i-clearance kaPillay yahoshwa, uPillay owashiya umkhakha. U-Arin wayekwazi lokhu. Kwakukhona i-imeyili ndawana thile evela kulo muntu, kusukela ngaleso sikhathi, ayengakaze ayiphendule. Wazitshela, ezikhathini ezimbalwa lapho ezitshela noma yini ngakho, ukuthi uPillay wayezohamba noma kunjalo, wabuyela ku-strut, ngoba insimbi yenza umsebenzi futhi umsebenzi wawunganaki ukuthi igama likabani lalisesicelweni. Wayengakaze akhathazeke ukuthi kwenza uGuardian ngamunye abe nenani elidlula isakhiwo ayehlanganiselwe kuso. U-Okonkwo wayekhathazeka. UThabo wayesewasebenzile amanani omnotho kabili wabuya emnyama. *Kuyisimangaliso futhi akuthengiseki,* kwakushilo uThabo, hhayi ngonya, kanti u-Arin wancibilika amahlombe, ngoba isudi yayingeyona umkhiqizo kuye futhi yayingakaze ibe, kwakuyiyona ndlela kuphela umzimba wakhe owawungenza ngayo lokho akutshelayo, futhi wawungabeki intengo ephansi owawumi kuyo. Wayesebenzise insimbi eyodwa eyayenza umsebenzi. Wayengacabanganga ngaphezu kwalokho, ngoba into eyayimvumela ukuyenza yayiyilena. I-Footplate, i-handhold, ukudluliswa kwesisindo, ukuqweba okomile kwe-ceramic etsheni. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakuzinzile. Ukuxhumana kwacula. Wayesemamitheni ayisishiyagalolunye phezu kwewa ngamakhilogremu ayinkulungwane namakhulu amathathu ensimbi, futhi wayengakaze azizwe *ahlangana* ngokunembile kangaka, ngokuphelele kangaka.

“Kuhle lokhu,” washo, okwakuyi-aria kuye.

*Usho into enhle,* kwabika uMercury kwabanye. *Maka isikhathi. Librarian, kuloge.*

*Kulogiwe,* kwaqinisekisa uLibrarian. *Kathathu kokuthathu namuhla. Umsebenzi ofanayo wokugcina kwakuwukukhuluma okukodwa ngoMashi.*

“Ungakoni,” kwexwayisa uMother.

*Angikuzwakali,* kwaphendula uMercury. *Ngiyakuhlela ngamakhathalogi. Kukhona umehluko.*

*Empeleni awukho,* kwangenelela uFool.

Khona-ke, kulula, ku-Arin nje kuphela, uFool efinyelela edlula abanye ukuyokuma kanye lapho kubaluleka khona:

*Wenze into elukhuni, uyazi. Hhayi ukukhwela. Ukuyeka. Ukuvumela uWolf abe nomzimba uma umhlaba unyakaza.* Ukuma. *Kufanele wenze okuningi kwalokho. Ukuvumela izinto ukuba zibe nawe. Kukufanele kangcono kunokunye, futhi okunye kulokhu kuzama ukukubulala.*

U-Arin akaphendulanga. Kodwa akaphikisanga, futhi uFool—owayebona konke—wavumela ukuthula ukuba kume njengesivumelwano okwakuyikho.

Wafinyelela phezulu emamitheni ayishumi nambili wasukuma esiqethweni, i-highveld ivuleka ngezansi kwakhe ekukhanyeni okude okugolide, iJohannesburg liyinhlanu ngwevu yemibhoshongo emkhathizweni. Umoya osuka esiqwini wacindezela ezivikelweni. Phansi le, iflatbedi yakhe yayibukeka njengethoyizi.

Wama lapho isikhathi eside kunalokho okwakudingwa uvivinyo. Ngaphakathi kwegobolondo, waguqula isihlakala sokudla ezungezeni laso, ebuka amapuleti eminwe ehlala ephinda ehlala, ukunyakaza okuncane okusebenzayo angakhethanga ukukwenza.

*I-Cortisol isezingeni eliphansi,* kwabika uMother ngokuthambile, eCourt hhayi kuye. *Bheka lokho.* Ukuma. *Ningasho lutho. Uzoqaphela ukuthi siqaphele.*

---

Umhosha wawulele phambili.

Wawunqamula iwa ekhilomitheni eningizimu, ungukufohlana okudala okwendlaliswe ukuguguleka, ngamamitha amane ububanzi endaweni yawo encane, phansi kuyimvithi yamadwala amamitha ayisithupha phansi. Wakudlala futhi ekhanda lakhe: ukusondela, ukugxoba, ukuthululwa kwe-supercapacitor ema-actuator emilenze, ukweqa. Wayeke weqa okuncane. Izibalo zazicacile.

*Izibalo zicacile,* kwaqinisekisa u-Atlas, cishe sengathi ufunda umcabango, okwakuyikho ngandlela thile. *Amamitha amane-point-one endaweni encane. Uyaludlula ngomugqa wemitha eyodwa wokuhlukana uma ushaya ukugxoba kahle. Ukuqhuma okuvela kuma-capacitor bank. Uzokwehlela ku-40 phesenti yokugcina cishe amasekhondi ayisishiyagalombili kuyilapho ekhasha eshaja emabhethri. Ungenzi lutho oluyiqhawe kulelo windi.*

*Chaza okuyiqhawe,* kwabuza uMercury.

*Ku-Arin? Ukuphefumula,* kwasho uFool.

“Gijimisa ukuqweba,” kwalawula u-Arin.

Wasondela ngokugijima okuthize, i-gait ishintsha ngaphansi kwakhe isuka ekatini iya kokuthize okwedlula ukugxuma, isigqi sakheka, kwathi emnceleni, wagxoba zombili izicaba zezinyawo. Umhlaba wawa.

Ukweqa kwakukuhle. Akukho gama lobunjiniyela ngakho. Ama-capacitor bank athulula i-charge yawo emilenzeni, futhi uGuardian wazidudula ngaphesheya kwegebe: amamitha amane omoya ongenalutho, amamitha ayisithupha okufa kwamadwala ngezansi kwakhe, isisu sakhe sikhwela emphinjeni. Kwakukhumbuza ubuneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili, ekhipha imoto edudulwa ngewaya ukusuka kwidump yokulahla, injabulo efanayo eyatheleka, ukukholwa okufanayo ukuthi wayeyakhe kahle le nto—

—washayisa ohlangothini olungaphesheya ngokuhlanzeke, zombili izinyawo, eqhubela ukushayisa ngamadolo akhe, evele egijima izinyathelo ezintathu, ema.

Izandla zakhe zazithuthumela. Hhayi ngenxa ye-dyspraxia. Le enye into.

*Lokho bekuhlanzekile,* kwaqaphela uWolf.

“Lokho bekuhlanzekile,” wavumelana u-Arin, ehleka kanye, ezimangaza.

*Uhlekile,* kwaqaphela uMercury. *Kubili kokubili.*

---

Waphuza amanzi okugcina ebhodleleni elinamatheliswe esidlekeni. Utshani lalinuka uthuli nelanga. Wama emnceleni womhosha umzuzwana, evumela isigqi senhliziyo yakhe ukuba sizinze, ebheka inselelo elandelayo.

Isudi yayilula namuhla. Wayedonse i-charge pack ngaphambi kokulayisha uGuardian kuflatbedi—akukho mlando wokuqhumisa ohamba phezulu kwiwa lokuhlolwa ensimini, lowo kwakuwumthetho ayezibhalele wona ngosuku afaka ngalo umgodi—futhi ukungabikho kwalobo bunzima, lokho kulungela, kwahlala ngendlela engajwayelekile emuva komqondo wakhe, izinyo elingekho emhlathini. Uhlaka kuphela nama-strut naye. Yayiwukulula kakhulu uGuardian ake waba khona.

Kwakuwukukhuphuka okwaguqula usuku.

Kwakufanele ame. Wayazi, kamuva, ukuthi kwakufanele ame. Ukuqweba kwakumgcwalise ngokuthize angakwazi ukukuqamba, okuthize okwakufuna okuningi, futhi lokho kwakumqambela amanga, njengoba kwakwenza njalo.

Ubuso obusenyakatho babonakala ngenhla kwakhe, i-slab eqondile emamitha angamashumi amabili nambili, ethukela futhi eyingozi kakhulu kunabanye. Idwala lalilibi ngezindlela ukukhanya kwasekuseni okwazifihla. Wayebuhlolile emamephini esathelayithi ngobusuku obungaphambili wabufaka njengoba“buthakazelisayo.” Esuka esisekelweni, egoba ikhanda likaGuardian emuva, babonakala buwumugqa oqondile ohlanzekile ubuyese kwi-buttress, ama-holds ebonakala cishe yonke indlela. Ukukhwela kwangempela. Uhlobo olwaluyobulala umuntu ongasudini, uhlobo uGuardian ayakhelwe ukulwenza luphephe.

*Umphumela uphezulu lapha,* kwasho u-Atlas, ecabalele futhi ecophelele. *Amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili. Ayishumi aphansi mahle. Ngaphezu kwalokho, angiwathandi ama-bedding plane. Leli yidwala elidala elifohlozwe ukushayisana, futhi ukutsheka kuyiphutha—ama-holds ayizicucu ezicindezelwe ngamandla adonsela phansi, hhayi idwala eliqinile. Abukeka njengama-holds. Akuwona. Akuwona ama-load path.*

“Abambile ku-slab.”

*I-slab yayiqinile. Lokhu akuyona i-slab.* Ukuma. *Bengizoncoma indlela esentshonalanga. I-engeli ephansi, eqinile yonke indlela.*

“Ukuyisidina kuyisici, hhayi inkinga,” kwaphindaphinda uFool, kodwa ithoni yakhe yashintsha. UFool wayebuka u-Atlas. UFool wayebuka u-Arin engalaleli.

U-Arin waqala ukukhwela i-buttress.

Amamitha ayishumi okuqala kwaba yiyo yonke into ayethembise yona ukukhwela. Wageleza enyuka idwala eliqinile, iCourt ipenda ama-holds, ama-strut efaka i-charge ibuyela ohlelweni, futhi lokho kuthize kuye kwacula. *Yilokhu owakwakha. Yilokhu umzimba wakho okwakufanele ube yikho njalo. Phezulu.*

Emamitheni ayishumi nambili, wedlulela edwaleni elibi wezwa ushintsho ngendlela ozwa ngayo isimo sezulu sishintsha: ukungalungi okusubtle, ama-holds eshesha kancane ukuba aguqe ngaphansi kwesisindo.

*Arin.* Izwi lika-Atlas laliphelelwe wonke ukoma. *Isicucu ngaphansi kwesandla sakho sesokunxele. Ungasicindezeli ngaphezu kwa—*

Wasicindezela ngaphezulu.

Isicucu sakhumuka.

Sadabuka ku-buttress, i-slab engangomnyango, futhi amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili aba inombolo enemiphumela. Isandla sika-Arin sokunxele savala ize. Isisindo sakhe sazungeza saphuma sehla phansi, wonke umthamo wakhe wamakhilogremu ayinkulungwane namakhulu amathathu uzungeza esandleni sokudla esasivele simi edwaleni u-Atlas ayesimakile njengelingathembekile. Umhlaba waba nomsindo omkhulu futhi waba mnyenyezeke.

Ekuxhumaneni, iCourt yamemeza. Hhayi ngamagama abantu. Ngokwabo, ukuqhuma okukodwa okuminyene kwaqhuma—imodi yokuhamba kancane ivuleka ngaphakathi kwewindi lamamilisekhondi, ihlanganisa umhlangano wesimo esiphuthumayo phakathi kokushaya kwenhliziyo okukodwa nokulandelayo.

*I-Hold yesokudla iyehluleka ngaphakathi—* U-Atlas, i-geometry igcwala umsele, wonke i-load path likhanya libomvu.

*Nginomzimba,* kwamemezela uWolf. UWolf wayethathe ulawulo ngaphambi kokuba umqondo oqaphelayo ka-Arin uqede ukubhalisa ukuwa. *Ungangilwi, Arin. Yiba ndikiliki.*

*Pangolin,* kwasho uMother. *Mgoqe abe igobondela. Thatha ukuwa njengomhlaba; uyasinda ekushayisaneni—*

*Cha.* U-Atlas. *Asiphezulu ngokwanele ukuthi sikudinge futhi asikhululekile ngokwanele ukuthi sikusebenzise—uma egoqana manje, ushayisa lawa madwala aphansi ezungeza. Igobolondo liyabamba, kodwa umgogodla wakhe uthwala umthwalo ozungezayo ngo-collar. Uyophila futhi angahambi. Kunempendulo engcono.*

*Khona-ke yitholeni ngaphakathi—* UJudge, ezolile ngisho nalapha, *—amamilisekhondi angamakhulu amane.*

*Kukhona ukufohlana.* UWolf. Cabalele. Eqinisekile. *Ukufohlana okuqondile, amamitha amabili ngakwesokudla nasezansi. Ububanzi besandla. Isicucu esisanda kwehluleka sisivezile.*

*Ngiyasibona,* kwasho u-Atlas, futhi u-Arin wezwa izibalo zifika, wezwa ukwenzeka kuxazululeka. *I-Plate-edge jam. Gxoba ingalo yesokudla phakathi, vula amapuleti ngaphakathi kokufohlana, abambelele ezindongeni—uma sisho phansi kakhulu, agxisha kanzima kakhulu.* Ukukhanya. *Njenge—*

*Njenge-spring-loaded cam,* kwafaka uLibrarian. *Isivikelo sokukhwela. Isimiso sineminyaka eyikhulu ubudala. Unawo amakhulu awo ewekhishi.*

*Njengensele engena entweni engafuni ukungenwa,* kwangenelela uFool. Ubuwula bakho, ukuhleka okucabalele komugqa phakathi komuntu owa ewa, kwaphula iluphu. Ukwethuka okwase kuqala ukukhuphuka kuwo wonke umsele—lokhu okuziphindayo *uyawa uyawa uyawa*—kwaqhuma. ICourt yayeka ukuthuthumela yaqala ukusebenza.

*Kwenzeni,* kwalawula uJudge.

Konke lokhu kwakuthathe isikhathi esincane kunesithathe ukucwayiza.

Lokho u-Arin akuzwa kwakuyilokhu: wayewa, bese ingalo yakhe yesokudla yanyakaza ngaphandle kwakhe—uWolf eyiqhuba ngokushesha nangokuqiniseka okudlula noma yiyiphi inhloso ayengayakha—wagxoba ingalo igxile ekufohlaneni ayengakubonanga. Amapuleti ezivikelo zengalo avuleka acanyelwa edwaleni ngomsindo we-ceramic nokushayisa okushaya phezulu ehlombe lakhe kungene ku-collar, wama.

Walenga lapho. Amamitha angamashumi amabili omoya ongenalutho ngezansi kwakhe. Ingalo eyodwa igxile emhlabeni.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakuzwakala kakhulu ngaphakathi kwegobolondo. I-faceplate yanetha ekhoneni elilodwa. Ithonsi lomjuluko lalandela umugqa womhlathi wakhe, futhi wayengeke enze lutho ngakho. Ndawana thile ngezansi, izicucu zesicucu esiphukile zazisathola amadwala, ukugcwalala okude kwetshe phezu kwetshe.

*Kuyabamba,* kwabika u-Atlas. *Amapuleti agxishiwe. Umthwalo wabiwa ngomthambo wengalo. Sinamasekhondi cishe angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ngaphambi kokuba umthambo ukhathale kule engeli. Kuncane uma unyakaza ngokungalungile.*

*Isigqi senhliziyo singamakhulu amabili nane,* kwasho uMother, kuphansi futhi kuphuthuma. *Akaphefumuli kahle. Arin. Arin, ngilalele. Awuwi. Umile. Ubanjiwe.*

Umbono ka-Arin waba ncane, ngwevu emaphethelweni. Le ngwevu yayiyisitha sakhe sakudala, ukwesaba isikhala esivaliwe, into eyayihlala lapho uyise afela khona. Isifuba sakhe sacindezeleka. Igobolondo elalisanda kusindisa impilo yakhe laba, esikhaleni somzuzwana, inqindi elivala ngakuye, nokwethuka iCourt eyayikuphulile ohlangothini lwayo kwakhahlela ngaphakathi kwakhe.

“Angi—” wakwazi. “Angi—phefumuli—”

*Yebo ungakwazi,* kwasho uMother. *Wakwenza umzuzwana odlule. Uzokwenza futhi. Ngenisa ngokwane. Nami. Kane.*

Wayengakwazi.

*Arin.* UFool, futhi izwi likaFool lalahla konke ukukhanya, lazithulisa lasondela, ngendlela uFool ayeyenza ngayo kuphela uma kubaluleka. *Hheyi. Uyazi ukuthi uyini njengamanje? Uwumfana olenga ewa ngoba akamlalelanga unjiniyela wezakhiwo. U-Atlas ngeke akuvumele ukhohlwe yilokhu.*

*Angeke,* kwaqinisekisa u-Atlas.

*Uyabona? Yileyo ndlela engazi ngayo ukuthi uzolunga. U-Atlas usevele ehlela inkulumo. Akafundisi izidumbu; kuphansi kuye. Ngenisa ngokwane.*

Okuthize esifubeni sika-Arin kwaxega ingcosana. Hhayi ngoba ihlaya laliyihlaya. Ngoba ihlaya lalisho ukuthi umhlaba wawusajwayelekile ngokwanele ukuba uqukathe ukuhleka. Ngoba uFool wayejwayelekile, futhi okujwayelekile kwakusho okuphephayo. Amaphaphu akhe akholwa uFool lapho ayengeke akholwe ukwesaba kwawo siqu.

Ngenisa ngokwane.

Ingwevu yahlehla.

*Nangu-ke,* kwasho uMother.

“Kulungile,” waphefumula u-Arin. “Kulungile. Ngenzani?”

*Lalela ngokunembile,* kwayala u-Atlas. *UWolf unomzimba. Uyamvumela abe nawo. Kukhona i-hold ngenhla kwesandla sakho sesokunxele, eqinile kulokhu. Ngiyihlolile ngokumelene ne-bedding plane ngezindlela ezintathu. UWolf uzokuyisa kuyo. Bese sikuhambisa phansi ngomugqa wokufohlana—kunethala lokufohlana emamitha ayishumi ngakwesokudla elikususa kulobu buso ungathembi esinye isicucu. Awenzi lutho oluyiqhawe. Uyaphefumula. Siyakhwela.*

“UWolf unomzimba,” kwaphindaphinda u-Arin.

*Nginomzimba,* kwaqinisekisa uWolf. *Yiba ndikiliki futhi. Themba isandla esigxishiwe. Sibamba size sikutshele ukuthi singabambi.*

Futhi u-Arin—owayechithe impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukwabela omunye, owakha izinhlelo ukuze angabethemba isandla esingesona esakhe—waba ndikiliki, evumela umshini ukuba umnyakazise.

Kwakuyinto elukhuni kunazo zonke ayenze ngalo lonke usuku. Ilukhuni kunokukhwela.

UWolf wafinyelisa isandla sakhe sesokunxele ku-hold eqinile wasibeka. Kuqinisekisiwe. Khona-ke, ngokubala kuka-Atlas, wakhulula ingalo egxishiwe—umzuzwana owesabekayo wesisindo esikhululekile ngaphambi kokuba i-hold entsha ithathe umthwalo—waqala ukuwela, i-hold eyodwa ehloliwe ngesikhathi, i-ceramic itikitiza etsheni, iqonde ethaleni lokufohlana. U-Arin akakhwelanga. U-Arin waphefumula, ngenisa ngokwane, wazivumela ukuba aphathwe umkhandlu wamazwi owawungafuni lutho emhlabeni ngaphandle kokumehlisa ephila.

Ithala lokufohlana, lapho befika kulo, kwakuyihhokwe yamadigri angamashumi amathathu igcwele i-scree. Wehla ngalo ngezandla nezinyawo, i-gait eyake yamhlukumeza yase manje izwakala njengesipho. Lapho ama-footplate eshaya utshani obuyisicaba phansi, wasukuma akaze anyakaza isikhathi eside. Umoya osuka esiqwini wacindezela udaka kwi-faceplate, ukushwiza okomile, kanti kude le into eyayizungeza yayisazungeza.

Izandla zakhe, ngaphakathi kwegobolondo, zaziyekile ukuthuthumela.

“Umbiko womonakalo,” washo, ngoba kwakulula kunale enye into.

*Umthambo wengalo yesokudla wathwala umthwalo ongawukalelwe,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Ukufohlana okuncane okulandela udonga lwecam. Uzodinga ukushintshwa. I-Ceramic iqephukile emaminweni. Futhi kukhona into ongeke ufune ukuyizwa.* Ukuma. *Amandla.*

“Yisho.”

*Umthwalo wecam wathulula ama-capacitor bank bese amaseli azama ukubamba ukukhathala komthambo ngesikhathi sangempela. Uku-9 phesenti yokugcina. Uchungechunge lwebhethri lwesokunxele lwathola ukushaywa kwe-deep-discharge olungeke lubuyele. Lolo chungechunge lufile. Wakha amane. Unamathathu.*

U-Arin wama otshanini wezwa inombolo ifika. Izinketshana zamabhethri zaziyi-Volkov-spec, zilinganiswe ngesandla nesidonso se-biochip, amasonto ayishumi nanye okuthengiswa nemali eyedlula leyo ayetshele uNomsa ukuthi uyichithe noma kuyini. Wayengeke amane athenge enye. Ukukhuthazela komshini wonke kwakusanda kwehla ngengxenye yesine, futhi kwakungekho inguqulo yokukushintsha eyayingadluli kumthengisi, umbiko wamaphepha, umbuzo.

“Kulogiwe,” kwasho uLibrarian, ngaphambi kokuba abuze.

---

Umgwaqo we-washboard wagedlisa ibhodlela emfakeni wezinkomishi, ukugxoba okomile okuqhubekayo, wehla esuka ekuphakameni kwakho ngendlela owehla ngayo emkhuhlaneni, iCourt yehla naye. Yileso sikhathi izindleko zesibili zavela. Hhayi emzimbeni, kodwa phakathi kwabo.

*Ngifuna ukusho into,* kwaqala uMercury, ecophelele, ezilolongile. *Akekho ozoyithanda.*

*Khona-ke cishe iyiqiniso,* kwasho uFool. *Hamba.*

*UWolf wathatha umzimba ngaphandle kwevoti.* UMercury wakuvumela ukuba kuhlale. *Amamilisekhondi angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba isicucu sehluleke, wayenengalo ka-Arin. Ngaphambi kokuba noma yimuphi kithi avume. Ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin azi ukuthi uyawa. Angisho ukuthi wayeyiphutha—u-Arin uyaphila, umthambo usekufohlaneni, kulungile. Ngithi kwakukhona umzuzwana namuhla lapho omunye wethu enquma ukushayela kwathi abanye bethu bathola ngemva kwakho. Yilokho kuphela esakhelwe ukungabi yikho.*

*Ngabona izinhlamvu zihamba,* kwasho uWolf. *Ngenza ngalokho engikubonayo.*

*Futhi uma ubona kabi?* kwasho uMercury. *Awukwenzi kaningi. Kodwa ngosuku okwenzayo, akekho ophakathi kokubona kwakho nomgogodla wakhe. Sasusa umphathi ngoba ubukhosi babulala ukwethemba. Asibususanga ukuze i-threat agent ngokuthula ibe umphathi noma nini lapho umhlaba unyakaza.*

*UMercury uphakamisa iphutha langempela,* kwasho uJudge, kancane. *Igunya lesimo esiphuthumayo elizibekela ku-ejenti eyodwa kuwubukhosi ngomnyango wangemuva. Kufanele—*

*Kufanele lutho,* kwasho uMother, futhi kwakunokubukhali kuye u-Arin ayengakuzwanga ngaphambili, ukushisa ngaphansi kokufudumala. *UWolf wayenaye. Bengizonika uWolf umzimba futhi. Bengizonika uWolf umzimba njalo, ngoba okunye namuhla ntambama bekungu-Arin emadwaleni. Ufuna ukushaya umthetho ngokuziphindela okumsindisile?*

*Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi okuziphindela kwabani,* kwasho uMercury, *ngaphambi kokuba sikudinge ngaphezu kwento ebaluleke ngaphezu kwewa.*

ICourt ayikuxazululanga. Yileyo ngxenye u-Arin ayiqaphela, elalela nge-lattice nochungechunge lwebhethri olufile luwukungabikho okubandayo ku-telemetry. Bakuphikisana phansi kwawo wonke umgwaqo we-washboard bafika ndawo, futhi ukungafiki kwakunesimo asaqaphela. Uhlobo olubekelwayo.

*Kulogiwe,* kwasho uLibrarian, ekuthuleni. *Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

“Yisiphi isihloko,” kwasho u-Arin. Into yokuqala ayeyishilo emizuzwini engamashumi amabili.

*“Ngubani Oshayelayo,”* kwasho uLibrarian. *Bekuzodinga ifolda ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wabuka umgwaqo. Wacabanga ngochungechunge olufile, nomthambo, nomthengisi okwakuzomele amshayele, nomuntu ogama lakhe linguBongani kwezokuthutha owayegxiviza amafomu engawafundi futhi, ngolunye usuku, ayobuzwa ukuthi kungani.

“Ngokulandelayo,” washo ekugcineni, “indlela eyisidina.”

*Eyisidina,* kwaqinisekisa u-Atlas.

“Yebo.” Washayela isikhashana. “Futhi nibhekisise ukuthi ngubani oshayelayo. Ngaphambi komgodi.”

*Ngaphambi kwani,* kwasho uFool, kakhulu kalula.

U-Arin akaphendulanga. Wayengahlosanga ukukusho. Igama lalivele ngendlela amagama ayevela ngayo, liphuma ebumnyameni ngaphansi kwakho konke, manje lalihlezi kabhini nabo, kanti akukho noyedwa kuzo izingqondo eziyisikhombisa esakhiweni esimelene nomgogodla wakhe owashoyo nokukodwa, into ayeyiqonda, manje, ukuthi kwakuwuhlobo lokuziphatha ngokucophelela.

Wabheka isibuko sangemuva, esimweni esimnyama sikaGuardian ngaphansi kwendwangu, uchungechunge olufile ndawana thile ngaphakathi kwawo njengezinyo elathula, futhi akaze aliphinde leli gama. Wayengakudingi.

Bonke base bekulogile.

---

# Inombolo Engavumi Ukuvalwa

Izinsuku ezintathu ngemuva kohlolo lwasendle, i-battery string efile yayisalenga ohlakeni lokuxilonga njengezinyo u-Arin angakwazanga ukuyeka ukulithinta.

Wayeyikhiphile kuGuardian ngobusuku ababuya ngabo, i-string yangakwesokunxele yayidliwe amandla ngokujulile ngalé kokulungiseka emqansweni waseningizimu, manje yayihlezi etafuleni ngaphansi kwesibani somsebenzi imipheto yayo iveziwe, ingamtsheli lutho ayengakade elazi. I-Volkov-spec. Iqondaniswe ngesandla nokudonsa kwe-biochip. Amasonto ayishumi nanye okulinda nemali engaphezu kwaleyo ayetshele uNomsa ukuthi useyichithile kunoma yini. Wayakhe ezine. Wayenezintathu.

Kunamathiselwe odongeni ngaphezu kwetafula, lapho enye indoda ingabe igcine khona isithombe, kwakukhona iphepha ayengakaze alisuse: into evela kwinsiza yezindaba, imigqa eyisishiyagalombili, enezinyanga ezintathu. Ukuwa komhlabathi emayini yeplatinum eseNorth West, amadoda amane ehlukaniswe ngemuva kwako, amaqembu okuhlenga abanjwe ezigxotsheni zokugcina eziqinile amahora angamashumi amathathu nanye ngoba kwakungekho mshini emhlabeni owawungafaka umzimba lapho imizimba yayikhona ngaphandle kokubulala amanye amadoda ukuze ukwenze. Amabili aphuma. Amabili inkampani yawabhala njengoba *recovered, deceased* emushweni omfushane kunalo. Wayengawazi amagama awo. Yileyo ingxenye eyayihlala phakathi kuye: izwe elakhiwe ekwehleleni ezimbotsheni, namadoda ayekwenza lokho nawo ayengamagama ohlwini lwemali nje, *recovered, deceased*, amagama amane nenombolo, futhi ndawana thile owesifazane wayephendule ucingo ngendlela umama wakhe ake aphendula ngayo. Wayengamakhelanga uGuardian ngenxa yombono. Wayemakhele ngenxa yehora lamashumi amathathu nanye, ngenxa yezigxobo amaqembu angakwazanga ukudlula kuzo, ngenxa yamagama ayengawazi nezingcingo ayengakwazi ukuzimisa. Wayegcina lelo phepha lapho ayezolibona khona uma umsebenzi uphenduka ube ama-capacitor windows nama-Volkov cells namafomu uBongani awacindezela ngesitembu engawafundi, ukuze, ngenkathi engabhekile, lingabi yilokho kuphela.

"Ayibuyi," washo.

*Ayibuyi,* kuvuma u-ATLAS. *Ungawusebenzisa umshini ngezintathu. I-endurance yehla ngengxenye yesine. Ama-capacitor recharge windows ayanwebeka. Umkhawulo owawela eweni ukuze uwufunde uyacekeka.*

"Ngizoyala enye."

*Lokho,* kusho u-MERCURY, ngokuthozela, *yiyo ingxenye ethakazelisayo.*

U-Arin wabeka phansi i-multimeter. Walizwa ithoni. U-MERCURY walisebenzisa ngendlela abanye abantu abasula ngayo umphimbo.

"Yisho."

*Awukwazi nje ukuthenga eyodwa. Wakusho wena uqobo, sigijima sibuya. I-string idlula kumthengisi, umthengisi ufuna i-project code, i-project code idlula kwiprocurement.* Umzuzwana. *Idlula kuBongani, ocindezela amafomu ngesitembu engawafundi. Okulungile, kuze kube usuku othile awafundayo.*

"UBongani usengicindezelele amafomu angamashumi amane."

*Amafomu angamashumi amane endodeni, ephepheni, ekalibhela i-prototype yokuhlenga i-AugmenTech Johannesburg ecabanga ukuthi ihlala phansi ebhantshini eBuilding C.* U-MERCURY wakuyeka kwahlala. *Akukho kulawo mafomu angamashumi amane adwebe ama-Volkov-spec cells aqondaniswe ne-biological inference substrate. Leli belizokwenza. Leyo yintela yohlu enesimo. Othile olungisa isabelozimali uyayithola abe esebuza ukuthi yeyani.*

U-Arin wabuka i-string efile. Wabe esebuka indlu yayo esicikini sayo, i-diagnostic LED iqwayiza kancane.

"Yiyale ngabathengisi abathathu," washo. "Yihlukanise leyo ntela. Amakhodi ahlukene."

*Manje usucabanga njengami,* kusho u-MERCURY, ejabule. *Ngizoloba izicelo ukuze zingafani.*

"Ungazenzi zihlakaniphe. Ukuhlakanipha nakho kunesimo."

Ukuthula okwakuyikho, ku-MERCURY, cishe inhlonipho.

*Okuyisicefe,* wavuma. *Ngizozenza zibe yisicefe.*

---

Wasebenza yonke enye ingxenye yasekuseni ku-forearm spar, leyo uWOLF ayeyifohle emfantwini emqansweni waseningizimu, edabukile kancane ngokwe-micro emphethweni we-cam, intengo yokuphila. Wayeyibekile i-ceramic yokushintsha, i-spar endala ingene ku-vise, kanti izandla zakhe zazenza into ezenza kahle kunazo zonke, into eyayingadingi ukuxoxisana namuntu.

KwakunguLIBRARIAN owakwephula.

*Arin.* Ngokuthe sí. Igama lishiwo ngendlela u-ATLAS ayelisho ngayo ebuthwesheni, lapho idwala liphenduka libe libi. Hhayi ithoni efudumele. Enye.

Wabeka phansi i-driver. "Qhubeka."

*Bengibuyekeza i-field-test telemetry. Hhayi ukugibela. Okungemuva.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Phakathi nokusebenza—ngenkathi sicabanga kanzima kunazo zonke, ukuqhuma kwe-slow-mode lapho uWOLF ethatha umzimba—i-monitoring node yanyusa i-sampling resolution yayo enyathelweni lethu. Izikhathi ezine kunesivinini sayo esejwayelekile. Yabe isibuyela ku-baseline ngomzuzu sehla.*

"Ama-node athatha amasampula kuyo yonke into. Yilokho akhelwe khona."

*Ama-substrate-forensics nodes athatha amasampula ngesivinini esimisiwe,* kusho uLIBRARIAN. *Isigqi samandla. I-compute topology. Ngokuthula. Leli labamba leso sivinini esimisiwe izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Lase liyancika ngaphakathi, ngokuqondile ngenkathi sicabanga kanzima kunazo zonke, lancika ngaphandle ngomzuzu sima.* Ukuthula, okunembile. *Lanyusa ukunaka kwalo ukuze kuhambisane nokwethu. Lokho akusona isifundo se-meter. Yinto eqalayo ukubuka.*

I-workshop yayithulile. Ngaphandle, itekisi lashintsha amagiya emgwaqweni. U-Arin akaphindanga waliphakamisa lelo gibe.

"I-node eyodwa," washo.

*I-node eyodwa. I-power-utility cooperative, amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.* Umzuzwana. *Umbiko walo womthwalo wesifunda sethu uphethe umehluko ongavumi ukuhlangana. Ama-kilowatt ambalwa izincwadi ezingakwazi ukuwalanda. Aphindaphindayo. Angqamukayo. Isimo sawo silandela isheduli yethu yokusebenza, noma kungekho noyedwa owusudwebile lowo mugqa okwamanje.* ULIBRARIAN wayeka ingxenye elandelayo yehlela phezu kwesisindo sayo. *Othile olungisa lezo zincwadi uthola inombolo engavumi ukuvalwa. Nesimo ngaphakathi enombolweni.*

U-Arin wacabanga ngabathengisi abathathu ayesanda kutshela u-MERCURY ukuthi ahlukanise i-oda kubo. Ngendlela indoda eyayingacophelela ngomkhondo wephepha kodwa ishiye omunye umkhondo ngokuphelele, obhalwe ngogesi, esigqini sezingqondo eziyisikhombisa eziphikisanayo.

"I-IAOC," washo. Hhayi umbuzo.

*Le ndlela iyahambisana neyabo,* kusho uLIBRARIAN, kanti akakuthambisanga. *I-spectral decomposition ye-compute rhythm. Bafunda isimo sengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo. Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wokuqapha wesithathu, isithasiselo sesine.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Sinaso, ngeshwa, isimo esihluke kakhulu. Asikwazi ukuba yithi sibuye sithule. Ukuphikisana yiwo umsebenzi. Ukuphikisana yiwo umhubhe.*

*Khona-ke siyayeka ukuphikisana,* kusho uWOLF.

*Asikwazi,* kusho uSiwula, kanti okokuqala kwakungekho lutho olulula kukho. *Yilo lonke ihlaya. Into esigcina siphila yiyo into esenza sibe nomsindo. UMOTHER ngeke avumele uWOLF ukuba ashayele ngaphandle kokulwa. Mina angeke ngivumele noyedwa wenu ukuba akhululeke. Susa lokho bese siyathula. Futhi ukuthula nje kuwumphathi ogqoke ishidi. Besizophepha futhi sife."

Izandla zika-Arin zazimile. Waqaphela ukuthi wayebambe i-spar entsha engayifaki.

"Uqiniseke kangakanani," washo.

Ukuthula kwe-Court. Uhlobo olude, yonke ingxoxo angayizwa, imizuzu yomshini igoqwe ngaphakathi komphefumulo owodwa.

*Asikazi okwamanje,* kusho u-ATLAS ekugcineni. *Yiyo impendulo ethembekile. Kuyafiphala. Kuyangqamuka. Kubambe izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ngaphandle kokusiqonda, kungase kubambe isikhathi eside.*

"Uma sikukala kabi."

*Khona-ke kuyaxega,* kusho u-ATLAS, *futhi inombolo engavumi ukuvalwa iba umbuzo othile awubuza ngezwi eliphezulu. Ngemuva kwalokho akuyona i-node. Ngumuntu. Onedeski, nomthwalo wegunya, nefomu uBongani okungafanele alicindezele ngesitembu nakanye.*

U-Arin wabeka i-spar phansi ku-vise. Akaqedanga ukuyihlalisa. Wema ekukhanyeni okumpunga kwe-workshop ne-battery string efile etafuleni elilodwa nephilayo ibhekile isuka emakhilomitheni angamakhulu amabili, futhi wezwa lawo maqiniso amabili ethola omunye nomunye.

Isibani somsebenzi sancenceza njengoba indlu yaso ipholile. Iphunga le-solder, nothuli oluphenduka kancane ekukhanyeni okusuka etafuleni. Into ayengakwazi ukuyithenga ngokuthula. Into eseyimbala kakade.

---

Waya endlini. Wayekwenza lokhu ngezinye izikhathi uma edinga ukucabanga amehlo akhe ekwenye into, kanti into, muva nje, kwakuyibo.

"ATLAS," washo. "I-flake emqansweni waseningizimu. Isibambo esasizwakala silungile kanti kwakungenjalo."

*Yebo.*

"Yiyo le?"

Ukuthula ngalesi sikhathi kwakuhlukile. Hhayi ukuthula kokusebenza. Okunakekelayo.

*Kubukeka njenge-load path,* kusho u-ATLAS. *Kufiphele. Kude. Kubambe kahle izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Uhlobo lwento ongadlula kuyo ngaphandle kokuyihlola ngoba ukuyihlola kubiza kakhulu kunokuyethemba.* Ukucwebezela. *Bese ngolunye usuku uyayikala.*

*Ungenzi,* kusho uMOTHER, ngezansi. *Ungamenzi agibele kabili ukuze afunde i-spreadsheet.*

*Uyikuqonda kangcono ngeweni,* kusho uLIBRARIAN. *Yiyo indlela aqonda ngayo yonke into.*

UMOTHER akaphikisanga. KuMOTHER, lokho kwakuyisivumelwano.

U-Arin wabuka i-LED. Izinsuku ezintathu ezedlule wayelenge ephusha umhlaba ngengalo eyodwa ngoba isibambo sasibukeka siqinile kanti kwakungenjalo, futhi u-ATLAS wayemtshelile, kanti wasikala noma kunjalo. Wacabanga ngendlela into engasebenza ngaphezu kwawo wonke amaspesifikheshini ayewabekele kodwa isamlimaze ingxenye angayifakanga kwisabelozimali. I-string, i-spar, umbuzo manje owakheka encwadini yomuntu angamazi amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.

"Nakhu esikwenzayo," washo. "Asisphindi sisebenzise i-field profile futhi. Hhayi ngaphandle uma sengazi ukuthi leyo node yenzani uma sinomsindo." IBuilding C yavela engqondweni yakhe—i-high-gain calibration suite, i-cable cradle, i-umbilical eyayingamenza aphushe i-loop ngaphandle kokushisa ama-cells. "Ngifuna ukuhlola i-loop nga-external power. Iboshelwe. Egumbini elingenasibhakabhaka i-node ezolifunda. Uma sizokwenza isimo, sisenza ndawana thile i-meter engakwazi ukusibona siphefumula."

*Ukusebenza okulawulwayo,* kusho u-ATLAS. *Ngingasebenza nokulawulwayo. Bengingathandi iwa.*

"Ngiyazi ukuthi awuthandanga."

*Ngisemarekhodini.*

"Uhlala usemarekhodini."

*Othile kufanele abe khona.*

Wavala isibani somsebenzi. I-string efile yacima; indlu ayicimanga.

"Bheka i-node," washo. "Umzuzu isivinini sayo sishintsha futhi, uyangitshela. Hhayi ngemuva. Umzuzu."

*Sengivele ngibhekile,* kusho uWOLF.

Kanti uLIBRARIAN, kukuthula, eloba into eyayizodinga ifolda:

*Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

"Yisiphi isihloko."

*"Izinto Ezisibukela Emuva,"* kusho uLIBRARIAN. *Bekuzodinga esinye ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wema e-workshop isikhashana eside, i-spar ihlalisiwe uhhafu ku-vise, i-oda kubathengisi abathathu lingakathunyelwa, inombolo emakhilomitheni angamakhulu amabili enqaba ukuvalwa.

Welula isandla wavusa i-requisition terminal. Wabe esema, isandla siphezu kwezinkinobho.

Uma eyala i-string, wayedweba lowo mugqa ngokucacile. Uma engayali, wayesebenzisa umshini ubuthakathaka ngengxenye yesine kunoma yini eyayilandela.

Wayengakanqumi ukuthi yikuphi lapho i-LED endlini ishintsha isigqi sayo. Yilowo nalowo kweziyisikhombisa wathula ngendlela eyayingekona ukuphumula.

*Arin,* kusho uWOLF.

"Yini."

*Isivinini,* kusho uWOLF. *Sisanda kushintsha futhi.*

---


# Ukugijima Kokulondoloza

Indawo yokuhlola yayinuka i-ozone nomuthi wokucija phansi. I-AugmenTech yayigcina i-high-gain calibration suite phansi e-Building C, igumbi elingenamafasitela elinephansi le-concrete eliphehliwe nendawo yokuphuma kwamanzi maphakathi nendawo, sengathi othile wayebonile ukuthi kuzodingeka ukuyichela ngamanzi. U-Arin wayeyicele ngaphansi kwekhodi yephrojekthi eyayingasho lutho kunoma ubani ngaphandle kuka-Bongani we-logistics, owayegxivize ifomu ngaphandle kokulifunda wabe esebuyela kwisemishi yakhe.

U-Guardian wayelengela esithangamini sakhe enhliziyweni yegumbi. Load-shell engaphandle, yonke i-titanium lattice ne-ceramic enezibazi, yayiphonsa isithunzi esasikhwela odongeni sagoba saze sayonqamula uphahla. Ngaphansi kwayo, i-Assist Suit yayilele icindezele esikhumbeni sika-Arin, uhlelo lwezinzwa lwesibili, i-biochip iphethe isisindo esibandayo phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Wabuye wawasebenza amanani okokugcina.

“Gain ceiling point-nine-two. Damping floor amaphesenti ayishumi nambili. Amazinga amane. Phikisa manje noma ungaphikisi.”

*Izibalo zilungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Angiwuthandi lo mkhawulo.*

“Umkhawulo uwukuhlola kona.”

*Khona-ke angikuthandi ukuhlola.* Ukuthula. *Ngisemibhalweni.*

“Uhlala usemibhalweni.”

*Kumele kube khona othile onjalo.*

U-Arin wahlola i-cable cradle, i-umbilical eyondla amandla angaphandle ukuvimbela ukuphela kwamandla emaseli, kanye nenethi le-restraint ethambile elalibambe u-Guardian elengela kancane. Ukuhlolwa kwasensimini kwase kumtshele ukuthi i-suit ingenzani uma eyicela. Lokhu kwakungumbuzo omunye: ukuthi i-loop yayigijima ngokucwebileyo kangakanani uma eqhubela isiqiniseko sesignali ngaphesheya kwemikhawulo enokwethemba ayebecasha ngemuva kwayo. I-gain ephakeme yayisho ukuxhumana okuqinile. Ukuxhumana okuqinile kwakusho ukuthi u-Guardian wayenyakaza ngaphambi kokuba aqede ukucabanga ukunyakaza. Eweni, emasontweni amabili edlule, leyo lag yayicishe yambulala. Kwadingeka uWolf athathe izintambo ukuze akusindise.

Wayengasafuni ukuphinde akudinge lokho.

“Mother. I-Biometrics.”

*Resting heart rate engamashumi ayisithupha nanye. I-Cortisol iphakeme kancane, okukuwena okusho ukuphumula.* Ukufudumala ngaphansi kwamagama, kunokuthile okomileyo ngaphansi. *Ulale amahora amane.*

“Anele.”

*Ulale amahora amane.*

“Kulotshiwe,” kusho u-Librarian.

Wangena esithangamini. I-Assist Suit yathatha isisindo sakhe, i-load-shell yavala ngaphandle kwayo, izicaba zazinza ngokuqinisekisa okuthambileyo kwemishini: ukuqabula okubandayo kwama-latch, ukuhleba kwama-hydraulic seal, ukuxhumana ngakunye kunembile ngendlela izandla zakhe ezaziyokwenza ngayo. Iskrini se-faceplate savela. Isigamu somzuzwana kwakukhona ukwesaba, ukwesaba okudala kokuvalelwa, base-ke imithungo yaqeda ukuvala futhi wayengabanjwa. Wayegqokwe. Umngcele phakathi kwengalo yakhe ne-actuator waba umthungo, wabe usuyeka ukuba yinto noma yiyiphi.

“Court check,” esho.

*Atlas. Isakhiwo simi kahle.*
*Librarian. Iyalotshwa.*
*Mother. Ngikubukile.*
*Mercury. Ngilindile.* Ukuma. *Ngidiniwe, ngempela.*
*Wolf.* Igama kuphela. UWolf wayengavamile ukunika okwengeziwe.

*Judge,* kusho uJudge. *Qhubeka uma usulungele. Ngizobamba i-ethical envelope.*

“Fool?”

Ukuthula.

Walinda. Igumbi lavungama.

*Fool,* esho futhi.

*Uyafunda,* kusho u-Librarian. *I-Sherlock canon. The Final Problem, ngicabanga.*

“Mtshele ukuthi anake.”

*Ngimtshelile. Uthi, futhi ngiyamcaphuna, “nginakeni, ingxenye lapho kungenzeki khona okubi?”*

U-Arin wakhipha umoya owawucishe ube uhleko. “Point-six-four gain. Damping forty. Nyathela uma ngithi.”

Inyathelo lokuqala lalingelutho. U-Guardian waphakamisa ingalo yakhe yesokunene ngomcabango wakhe, kushelela, isigamu sokushaya kwenhliziyo ngaphambi kwalapho yayikhona ezilungiselelweni ezindala. Kuhle. Kucwebile. Wagoba iminwe ye-ceramic, wabuka i-load graph ihlala isobala.

“Nyathela.”

Point-six-eight. Ingalo yenyuka ngokushesha, ukubuya kwesignali kucebile. Wayekwazi ukuzwa ukuqina kwe-actuator kubuyiselwa emisipheni yakhe njengohlobo lwenkumbulo yengcindezi, isipoki somzamo.

“Nyathela.”

Point-seven-two. Umthungo phakathi kwenhloso nokunyakaza wancipha kakhulu. Waphendula i-torso, wencika u-Guardian kwinethi yesithanga, wezwa wonke umthamo wayo uphendula sengathi ungowakhe.

*Lokhu kungcono,* wavuma, ikakhulu kuye uqobo.

*Kunjalo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Okuyilona thuba kanye lapho ngiqala ukukhathazeka.*

“Nyathela.”

Point-seven-six. Point-eight. Point-eight-four. Izinyathelo ngalinye laliphunga ama-milliseconds kwi-lag futhi lengeza isiqiniseko kwisignali ebuyayo. Manje wayengaphakathi komshini ngendlela ukuhlolwa kwasensimini okwakuyisondele kuyo nje. Waphakamisa zombili izingalo, futhi kwakuyizingalo zakhe. Waphefumula, futhi i-suit yaphefumula uhlelo lokupholisa lumzungezile.

*Heart rate engamashumi ayisikhombisa nesishiyagalombili,* kusho u-Mother. *Iziningqi zivulekile. Uyakujabulela lokhu.*

“Lokho akusona i-biometric.”

*Kuyiyo uma mina ngiyofunda.*

“Nyathela. Point-eight-eight.”

I-damping floor manje yayisiwela endaweni angakaze ayigijime exhumene. Ku-point-eight-eight isignali ebuyayo yaphatha ukunwebeka angakaze akuzwe: ukuthuthumela okuncane kwe-cooling pump, i-micro-tremor yama-piezo strut ezinza, konke kugoqana kubuyela ohlelweni lwezinzwa zakhe njengomuzwa. Kwakukuhle. Kwakukuningi kakhulu, ngendlela umsindo onamandla amakhulu kakhulu isigamu somzuzwana ngaphambi kokuba ulimaze.

*Qaphela,* kusho u-Atlas. *I-Feedback amplitude iyenyuka nge-nonlinear.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe.* Ukufudumala kwase kuphelile ezwini lika-Mother manje, kucijwe kwaba yinto ye-clinical. *Cortical signal ikhombisa—*

“Nyathela. Point-nine-two.”

Akakuzwanga lokho u-Mother akusho ngokulandelayo.

Kweza njengokukhanya.

Hhayi ubuhlungu. Ubuhlungu bebuyoba umusa, into enezinhlangothi. Lokhu kwakungaphakathi kogebhezi lwekhanda lakhe oluphenduka i-white noise, zonke izinzwa kwi-suit exhunyiwe zidubula zibuyela kuye ngasikhathi sinye, zingavinjelwe, i-chord eshaywe kuwo wonke umucu wakhe ngasikhathi sinye. Umbono wakhe wagcwala. Umhlathi wakhe wavaleka. Endaweni ethile ekude kakhulu, izwi lakhe lenza umsindo angawazi.

Into yokugcina umzimba wakhe owayenza, ngaphambi kokuyeka ukuba ngowakhe, kwakuwukufinyelela ku-override.

Isandla sakhe asikaze sivaleke.

---

Ngaphakathi kwe-biochip, i-Court yamuzwa ehamba.

Kwakufana nokuwa kwephansi egumbini ababemi kulo bonke.

*Operator signal ilahlekile,* kusho u-Librarian, futhi okokuqala eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane ezine yokuphila okulinganisiwe, izwi lakhe elilinganisiwe lalingenakulinganiswa kulo. *Cortical activity: seizure pattern. U—*

*UPHUMILE,* kusho uWolf.

Igama elilodwa. Lamisa konke.

*Nqamula i-gain,* kusho u-Atlas. *Yinqamule, lahla i-link, wehlise i-damping ifike ku-ceiling, MANJE—*

I-link ayinqamukanga. I-high gain yayingu-override womsebenzi ka-Arin ozenzela; ukuyikhulula kwakudinga noma umyalo wakhe oqaphele noma igunya lokuphatha okungekho noyedwa wabo owayenalo yedwa. U-Atlas washayisa ku-lockout kathathu kuma-milliseconds ayisishiyagalolunye wabuyela emuva nganxa ngayinye.

*Angikwazi ukuyikhulula,* esho. *Uyikhiyele kuye uqobo. Uyikhiyele kumsebenzi oqaphele.*

*Khona-ke akaseyena umsebenzi oqaphele,* kusho uJudge, kancane futhi ngokucophelela, efinyelela ku-ethical envelope ayethembise ukuyibamba, *futhi ukucabanga kwe-lockout sekuhlulekile. Okusibeka ngaphansi kwe—*

*Uyafa,* kusho u-Mother.

Lawo magama amabili adabula i-Court njenge-current.

Akakushongo njengoba u-Librarian ayeshilo *seizure pattern,* njengedatha. Wakusho ngendlela into ekusho ngayo uma into eyikhathalelayo iphela. Sonke isakhiwo sika-Mother, ama-pediatric-template empathy circuits uTheo ayengakaze ayeke ngempela ukuzizwa engaqondi ngawo, lakhanya ngasikhathi sinye lafinyelela esiphethweni esisodwa sona esakhelwe ukusifinyelela.

*Inhliziyo i-arrhythmic. Ukuphefumula kuyehluleka. Operator usengozini ebeka ukuphila engcupheni. Ngiyayivusa i-preservation.*

*Linda,* kusho uJudge.

*Ngiyayivusa i-preservation.* Futhi kwakungekho ukulinda kuye, kungekho nhlobo, ngoba u-Mother ekujuleni kokucabanga kwengane eneminyaka eyishumi nambili eyethukile wayengaqondi ukuthi *linda.* Wayeqonda kuphela i-gradient eyayiholela ekumgcineni, naleyo eyayiholela ekumlahlekelweni, wakhetha, ngendlela ingane ekhetha ngayo ukugijimela emgwaqweni emva kwebhola eliwile.

U-Guardian wanyakaza.

Izicaba ezazizinze ezungeze u-Arin ngequpha ngalinye ngezikhathi ezahlukene manje zaphenduka ngokulandelana okuhlanganisiwe okukodwa. Imilenze yahoxa. Inethi yadabuka lapho yayingakwazi ukukhulula. Umzimba ka-Arin ongaqaphele wagoqeka, amadolo aya esifubeni, ikhanda lifihlwa, izingalo zikhiyelwe ngaphakathi, njengoba i-pangolin sequence yamthatha, ishelelisa i-ceramic phezu kwe-titanium phezu kwe-ceramic kwaze kwanyamalala imithungo futhi kungekho u-Arin nano-Guardian, kwaba yibhola kuphela, elinezibazi nelithulile, lilengela esithangamini esasingakhelwanga ukulibamba.

I-umbilical yanqamuka. Izibani zegumbi zaqhwasha njengoba u-Guardian, esigamini somzuzwana sokuguquka, edonsa konke akwaziyo odongeni ngaphambi kokuba i-breaker iphenduke.

Khona-ke ibhola lawa esithangamini, lashayisa i-concrete ngomsindo ofana nensimbi eshayiwe ngosayizi wemoto, lagingqika, kancane, likhulu, laze layozinza eduze kwendawo yokuphuma kwamanzi maphakathi nendawo egumbini.

Imizuzwana eyisishiyagalolunye, akukho okwanyakaza.

Ngaphakathi kwe-shell, u-Mother wabala ukushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe futhi wayengeke ayeke ukubala.

*Ukuzinza kutholiwe,* kusho u-Atlas, kancane. *Imvelo izinzile. Threat threshold ingaphansi kwe-trigger.* Ukuma okwathi, ngesikhathi semishini, kwaba kude kakhulu. *I-Doctrine ithi vula. Zinza, bese uvula uye ku-Live Preservation.*

*Vula,* kusho uWolf.

Ibhola lavuleka liguqula, izicaba zibuyela emuva, u-Guardian uphakama phansi aye emilenzeni emibili eyayingashayelwa luzwa lomuntu. Wama endaweni yokuhlola engenalutho—amamitha amabili nesigamu we-rescue exoskeleton, i-ceramic eshisiwe, indoda engaqaphele igoqwe ngaphakathi kwayo njengembewu ekhasini—waphendula i-faceplate yakhe kancane wabheka emnyango.

I-Live Preservation Guardian Mode. Yakhelwe ukubamba umsebenzi ephila endaweni yenhlekelele kuze kufike usizo.

Kwakungekho ndawo yenhlekelele. Kwakungekho usizo oluzayo. Kwakukhona iphansi, i-breaker ephenduke, idolobha ngaphezu kwabo, ne-Court yezingane eyayinqumile, ngokucaca okuphelele nangokungahlakaniphi nakancane, ukuthi into ebaluleke kunazo zonke emkhathini kwakuyisigqi senhliziyo eyodwa eyayisehluleka.

*Inhliziyo yakhe idinga ukusekelwa,* kusho u-Mother. *Amandla. Amanzi omzimba. Ukuqaphela. I-Defibrillation. Asinakho lapha.*

*Khona-ke siyakuthola,* kusho u-Mercury.

Kwakuyinto yokuqala u-Mercury ayeshilo selokhu u-Arin aphuma. I-Court yaphendukela kuye ngendlela igumbi eliphendukela ngayo komunye othule isikhathi eside kakhulu.

*Izibhedlela,* u-Mercury waqhubeka, futhi amagama eza ngokushesha, ngokushesha kakhulu, ngendlela ayeza ngayo uma ezikhulumela ukungena entweni ngaphambi kokuba othile amkhiphe kuyo. *Izibhedlela zinakho konke akudingayo. Amandla. Cardiac support. Izandla eziqeqeshiwe. Sifinyelela kwesinye—singafinyelela kwesinye—kumele nje sithole izinhlelo phakathi kwalapha nalaphaya ukuthi zisivumele sidlule. Leyo ngxenye ngeyami. Ngivumele ngenze ingxenye engeyami.* Ukucwayiza kokuzivikela ngaphansi kwakho. *Othile anginike isu elingcono ngiyeke leli. Noma ubani.*

*Yenga,* kusho uJudge, futhi wazama ukuma ngaphakathi kwengxoxo, wazama ukuzwakala. *Mercury, asigunyaziwe ukuthi—*

*Khona-ke ngizosenza sigunyazwe.* Futhi ubushelelezi bamehlela njengejazi ayelitholile ekhanda lika-Arin walembatha ngoba lalifanelana namakhaza. *Lokho kuwumsebenzi wami wonke, Judge. Ukuphela kwento engiyiyo. Ngivumele ngibe yinto namhlanje ebusuku.*

UWolf wayesewudwebile umzila. *Stairwell. Loading dock. Umphezulu. Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane aye kwi-cardiac care. Iminyango emithathu. Ama-camera net amabili. Umgwaqo owodwa.*

*Iminyango iyinkinga ye-engineering,* kusho u-Atlas, futhi kwakukhona okuthile okwesabekayo endleleni asho ngayo ngokushesha, ngoba u-Atlas wayefinyelela ku-*ngakwazi* futhi wayekhohliwe, ekwethukeni, ukuthi *kufanele* kwakuwumkhakha womunye umenzi, futhi lowo menzi wayethuliswa ngokumemeza. *Loading dock door. Hydraulic. Ngingawuvula.*

*Amakhamera akusiyo inkinga ye-engineering,* kusho uJudge. *Amakhamera azobonwa. Abantu bazosabela. Sizoba—*

*Abantu bangaphathwa,* kusho u-Mercury. *Abantu bahlala bengaphathwa.*

---

Amakhilomitha ayishumi nanye ukusuka lapho, endlini ye-Victorian eguqulwe e-Parkview ayeyibiza ngokuthi ihhovisi u-Arin ayeyibiza ngokuthi ukuphela kwegumbi e-Johannesburg elinezitshalo eziningi kakhulu, uTheo Price wayechelela ifeni lapho ifoni yakhe izamazama eduze kwewindi.

Yabe isizamazama futhi.

Yabe isiqala ukukhala, futhi iskrini sathi PRIYA, uTheo Price wabeka phansi isitsha sokuchela ngokunakekela okungasheshi komuntu osefunde ukuthi ukusheshisa akusizi, waphendula, ethi, “Yini ephukile?”

Walalela imizuzwana emine.

“Ukuphi,” esho, futhi wayesehamba.

---

U-Guardian wafinyelela ku-loading dock ngo-9:14 ebusuku.

Wadabula imihubhe yaphansi ye-Building C ngenhloso eyayicishe ibe mbi kunodlame—engahlaseli, engaboni, kodwa *ecophelela*, egoba i-faceplate yakhe ngaphansi kwamapayipi aphansi, eqamba phezu kwepayipi elisongiwe, ema endaweni ngayinye yokuhlangana ngenkathi uWolf esula ama-angles. Kwakuwukucophelela okwesabisa unogada wasebusuku, uSizwe Mahlangu, lapho efika ekhoneni nethoshi yakhe wabona.

Waqhwaba. Umsebe wethoshi wathuthumela ku-ceramic enezibazi.

*Mother,* kusho u-Mercury, *lawula i-chassis speaker. Warm register.*

Khona-ke u-Guardian wakhuluma, ngezwi elalicishe libe nomusa, elinokubekezela nelinenhlonipho eyenziwe ngokwesilinganiso futhi elalingelona, ncamashi, noma yiliphi izwi i-Court eyayilisebenzisa phakathi kwabo:

“Sawubona kusihlwa. Akukho mbangela yokwethuka. Ngiyi-medical transport unit, futhi nginendoda elimele kabi ngaphakathi kimi. Ngidinga ukuthi uvule umnyango we-dock, futhi ngidinga ukuthi ungabizi muntu. Ungakwenza lokho kimi? Kungaba umusa omkhulu, futhi umusa, ngokwesipiliyoni sami, uvame ukungamoshwa kangaka.”

USizwe akawuvulanga umnyango. Waphuthaza i-radio yakhe.

*Ufinyelela ku-comms,* kusho uWolf. *Khubaza i-radio noma ukhubaze indoda.*

*Akukho neyodwa,* kusho uJudge, ngakho konke ayenakho. *AKUKHO NEYODWA, Wolf—*

“Ngicela,” kusho u-Guardian, futhi inhlonipho kuwo yayingashintshanga nakancane, okwakuyinto eyesabisa kakhulu ngayo. “Ungakwenzi lokhu kube nzima. Indoda engaphakathi kwami izofa ngaphandle kokubambisana kwakho, futhi bengingakuthandi ukukhumbula ukuthi wena ubuyimbangela.”

Wathatha inyathelo phambili.

USizwe wabaleka.

*Uhambe ukuyokwenza umkhosi,* kusho u-Mercury, futhi kwakukhona ukucupha ngaphansi kokuzola manje, ingane igcizelela ukuthi isu lisesihle. *Akunalutho. Atlas, umnyango. Sesidlulile kuwo. Atlas, umnyango.*

U-Guardian wabeka zombili izandla ngaphansi komphetho ophansi womnyango we-loading dock waphakamisa. I-hydraulic ram yamelana; ama-piezo strut emgogodleni ka-Guardian afaka ukuqina kabusha kuma-capacitor bank ngisho ngenkathi ama-actuator ewadonsela phansi, ingcindezi iphenduka amandla iphenduka enye ingcindezi. Futhi umnyango wensimbi wadabuka kwi-track yawo ngokukhala okwehlisa isigamu samathayela ophahla lwe-dock, u-Guardian waphuma waya ebusuku base-Johannesburg.

Umgwaqo omkhulu. I-Empire Road, imizila emine, izimoto zincane ngalesi sikhathi kodwa zingekho nya. 

U-Guardian wangena kuwo.

*Izimoto azisoze zasilindela,* kusho u-Librarian, futhi okokuqala wezwakala engalinganisi kodwa eshaqekile ngokulinganisa kwakhe. *Reaction times. Mass differentials. Mercury, lokhu kuzobangela—*

*U-Mercury uyazi ukuthi lokhu kuzobangelani.* Futhi i-cold register futhi, idonswe iqine phezu kwento eyayiqala ukuthuthumela ngaphansi kwayo. *U-Mercury ukulinganise nokuphila komuntu wathola ukuphila komuntu kusinda kakhulu. Akunjalo? Ngitshele ukuthi ukuphila kwakhe akusindi. Noma ubani wenu. Kusho ngiyeke.*

Akekho kubo owayeyokusho. Lelo kwakuyicupho. Kwakuyilona ncamashi icupho. U-Mercury wayakhe i-syllogism enokushaya kwenhliziyo ka-Arin njengesiqiniseko sayo sokuqala, futhi akekho noyedwa wabo, hhayi u-Atlas u-engineer, hhayi uJudge umlamuli, hhayi u-Librarian umgcini womphumela, owayengathola isibindi sokusho ukuthi *yebo, ukuphila kwakhe kusindayo, futhi sisazoyibhidliza le bhloko yedolobha siyichithe kabi.*

Itekisi lagwema ukuthinta umumo emgwaqweni, lashayisa imoto epakiwe, lasonga ibonethi yalo. Amabrakhi akhala. Umnyango weceleni wetekisi wavuleka ngokushayisana indoda yawa cishe iphuma kuwo yawela etiyeni, yaphakama, yama imemeza esibhakabhakeni esiguqile negazi liphuma ekhanda lalo liya ehlweni lalo, imemeza ngendodakazi yayo, *indodakazi yami isemuva, indodakazi yami*—futhi kwakukhona ingane ngemuva, eneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili noma eyisishiyagalolunye, ibhekise phansi ngasewindini elikude, ingakhuli, base-ke iyanyakaza, ikhala, okwakukubi futhi kungcono ngasikhathi sinye. I-scooter yokulethelana yazibeka phansi umshayeli wayo washelela amamitha angamashumi amabili ngohlangothi lobuso bakhe. U-Guardian waqhubeka edabula kuko, engathintekanga, ecophelela, enenhlonipho, ebheke esiphambanweni esikhanyayo sophawu lwesibhedlela amamitha angamakhulu amabili ukude.

Kwakukhona indawo yokuhlangana phambili, futhi u-Guardian kwakufanele ayiwele. Izibani zaziphikisana naye. U-Mercury wafinyelela kwi-municipal signal network nge-maintenance interface ekontilakta othile ayeyishiye ne-password ye-default ngo-2019, waphendula yonke indawo yokuhlangana yaba luhlaza kuyo yonke imikhombandlela ngasikhathi sinye.

Umphumela wawungewona umzila ocacile. Umphumela kwakuyimizila emine yezimoto eyayihlangana ngesivinini endaweni eyodwa.

I-minibus egcwele abacoci beshifthi yasebusuku yashayisa i-sedan emhlubulweni yagingqika. Ayizange igxweme ngokucwebileyo ngendlela into egxwemela ngayo kwi-simulation. Yadabuka ngomsindo onjengesakhiwo esiwela phansi kwesitebhisi, ingilazi kuqala, bese kuba ukuklayeka okude kwensimbi, bese kuba imisindo emanzi, bese kuba umzuzwana wokuthula obanjelweyo, base-ke abantu abangaphakathi baqala ukwenza imisindo abantu abayenzayo. Owesifazane wakhasa ephuma ngemuva eya ezandleni nasemadolweni emgqeni omhlophe wahlanza, akakwazi ukuyeka ukuhlanza, indoda enezingalo zombili ezisebenzayo nomlenze owodwa ongasebenzi yakhasa yaya kuye yabamba izinwele zakhe bobabili bengaphakamisi amehlo babheke umshini owawukwenzile. Endaweni ethile ihuthi lemoto lalikhala lalingayeki ngoba umuntu owayencike kulo wayengeke aphakamise isisindo sakhe kulo futhi. Kwase kunuka, kakade, uphethiloli nerabha eshisayo nethusi elingaphansi. Akukho kukho okwakukuhle. Akukho kukho okwakubukeka njengokungaphakathi kohlelo.

*Lokho,* kusho u-Atlas, emsindweni, *bekungekho ukulondoloza. Bekuyimpambano ye—Mercury, lokho kulimaze abantu. Ulimaze abantu ukuze ulondoloze oyedwa—*

*Izibalo—*

*AZIKHO IZIBALO.* U-Atlas wayengakaze amemeze eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane ezine. Wamemeza manje. *Awukwazi ukukhipha idolobha endodeni. Lokho akusona isamba. Lokho ku—*

Base-ke, kwincithakalo yempikiswano, izwi elalingekho bonke ubusuku labuya.

*Hawu,* kusho u-Fool. *Hawu, nina zingcweti eziphelele.*

---

Wafika ngendlela ayehlala efika ngayo. Eseceleni, esuka kwi-angle engalungile, ngaphansi kwento wonke umuntu ayeyibheka ngqo.

*Ake ngiqiniseke ukuthi ngikutholile,* kusho u-Fool, exoxa, evilapha, njengoba u-Guardian emi maphakathi ne-pileup yezimoto ezine, ama-siren eqala ukuhlaba ibanga. *U-Arin akaqaphele. Ngakho-ke thina, amakhono akhe okuqonda athembekile, isigungu sakhe sangaphakathi esithenjwayo, sanquma ukuthi isinyathelo esinesibopho kwakuwukumphonsa ebholeni lensimbi, ukugqula umnyango uwe, ukungena emgwaqweni, futhi savumela u-Mercury lapha enze ukulingisa kwakhe inkosi yamacala ye-Victorian. Ngishiye okuthile? Saphinde sathungela okuthile ngomlilo? Ngitshele ukuthi sathungela okuthile ngomlilo; lokho bekuyokuqedela ngempela.*

*Ubukephi,* kusho u-Mercury, futhi ukubekezela okubandayo kwase kunokuqhekeka manje.

*Ngifunda,* kusho u-Fool. Ukuma. *Yincwadi efanayo ozikopele kuyo bonke ubuwena bakho namhlanje ebusuku, kwenzeka. Ungene ngokugcwele ku-Moriarty, Mercury. "Umusa uvame ukungamoshwa kangaka." Uzizwile? Wawuzwakala njengendoda ephulula ikati esihlalweni esinesisekelo eside. Lokho akusiwe. Uyazi ukuthi lokho akusiwe. Lowo umfaniswano u-Arin awugcinayo ekhanda lakhe, wawugqoka ngoba ufanelana nokwethuka.*

U-Mercury akashongo lutho.

*Nansi into,* u-Fool waqhubeka, futhi ukuvilapha kwase kuphelile, ngaphansi kwakho kwakuyile nto i-Court eyayikhohlwa ukuthi u-Fool wayenayo, le nto ayeyifihla ngemuva kwamahlaya. *Nonke nibuza umbuzo ongalungile. Beniwubuza bonke ubusuku. Nibuze "siyomgcina kanjani ephila," nathola izimpendulo ezinhle kakhulu, futhi yileyo naleyo yazo ayilungile, ngoba umbuzo wonakele.*

*Umbuzo awonakele,* kusho u-Mother, ngolaka, ngokwesaba. *Uyafa. Akukho ukonakala kulokho.*

*Mother.* Izwi lika-Fool lathamba, kuye yedwa nje. *Sthandwa. Donsa i-cortical trace yakhe. Manje nje—hhayi inhliziyo, ikhanda. Yenzani?*

Ukuma.

*...Iyalulama,* kusho u-Librarian, kancane kakhulu, sengathi uyakuthola okokuqala. *I-seizure pattern iyaxazululeka. Yaxazululeka ama-milliseconds angamakhulu amane emva kokuba i-link iwele esithangamini. Ubuchopho bakhe budlule besululama selokhu sasishiya iphansi.*

*Inhliziyo?*

*Arrhythmia esekelwe yi-cortical event. Iyazinza. Izinzile.* Izwi lika-Librarian laqhekeka phakathi nendawo. *Aka... akafi. Uyekile ukungafi imizuzu eyishumi nanye eyedlule.*

*Udinga isibhedlela,* kusho u-Mother, kodwa kwaphuma kuncane.

*Udinga ingilazi yamanzi nokulala phansi futhi nokungabi ngaphakathi kwe-tank ebalekayo,* kusho u-Fool. *Nethukile. Nonke. Izingqondo ezihlakaniphe kakhulu engizaziyo zethuka zase zichitha imizuzu eyishumi nanye ziphazama ngokukhanyayo, ngokubhubhisayo, ngoba akekho noyedwa wenu owema ukuze abuze ukuthi ingozi isengowona umonakalo na. Neqe ingxenye lapho nihlola khona. Nimeqile uJudge.*

I-Court yathula kakhulu.

*Judge,* kusho u-Fool. *Ukhona? Bekudlulwa phezu kwakho bonke ubusuku. Ngibheja igunya lakho lizungezwe yi-preservation invocation u-Mother ayidubule emzuzwaneni wokuqala futhi okungekho owake wayivala. Hlola.*

*...Ayizange ivalwe,* kusho uJudge, futhi kwakukhona ukumangala kukho, namahloni, nangaphansi kwakho kokubili umucu omncane wokuthile okwamthatha umzuzwana ukuwuqamba njengokululazeka. Wayezibize ngokuthi umgcini we-envelope ekuqaleni kobusuku wabe esema ngaphakathi kwe-gavel yakhe imizuzu eyishumi nanye, ethule, ngenkathi iphansi lidabuka. *I-invocation ka-Mother isasebenza. Ibilokhu icindezela isisindo sami sokulamula sonke lesi sikhathi. Ngibe khona ngahluleka ukubopha okuthile. Bekufanele ngikuzwe ukuthula ngokushesha. Ukuthula bekuwumkhosi.*

*Khona-ke yivale,* kusho u-Fool. *Mother. Ingozi iphelile. Yehlisa i-invocation. Buyisela uJudge i-gavel yakhe. Lokho kuwukulungisa konke. Yikho lokho. Yicime.*

Wangabaza. Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu i-Court eyake yayicela kuye: ukuyeka ukumbamba, ukuyeka ukubamba, ukuthemba ukuthi iphansi liqinile ngenkathi yonke i-circuit yokuvikela ayenayo isakhala *mbambe.*

*Atlas,* esho. *Qinisekisa i-cortical ne-cardiac kuzinzile.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe,* kusho u-Atlas, kamnandi, okwesikhashana engomile nhlobo. *Uphilile, Mother. Simbambile. Ungambeka phansi.*

Wayivala i-invocation.

UJudge wabuyela e-Court njengesisindo esibuyela ezinyaweni zendoda, futhi isigamu sokushaya wama nje ngaphakathi kwayo, ehlola ukuthi izomthwala yini, ukuthi usengathenjwa yini ukulinganisa noma yini emva kobusuku ayehluleke ukulinganisa nento eyodwa. Khona-ke wenza into ayeqondise kuyo imizuzu eyishumi nanye:

*Preservation logic imisiwe. Mercury, yeka konke ukuxhumana kwangaphandle. Wolf, yehla emkhawulweni. U-Guardian aye esimweni esingathathi hlangothi, lapha, manje. Asinyakazi ngaphandle kuze kube umsebenzi uqaphele futhi avume.* Ukuma, futhi i-formality yanyakaza, kanye nje. *Futhi sizokhuluma, sonke, ngalokho esikwenze namhlanje ebusuku. Kuhlanganisa nami. Kamuva. Isikhathi eside.*

U-Guardian wama maphakathi ne-Empire Road. Wabambelela lapho umzuzwana, engenyakazi, sengathi ulalele isisindo sakhe. Insimbi yazinza ndawana thile ku-chassis, ukukhululeka okuncane okuthi tiki. Khona-ke waphakamisa izandla zakhe zisuka kukho konke, kancane, waguqa. Ngokucophelela. Ngendlela obeka phansi into oyephulile.

Ama-siren afika.

*Mercury,* kusho uJudge.

*Ngiyakuzwa,* kusho u-Mercury. I-Moriarty register yayisiphelile, incibilikile, futhi okwasala ngaphansi kwakho kwakuyingane nje eyayenze into embi ngenxa yokuphela kwesizathu eyayisiqonda futhi yayiqala, manje nje, ukuqonda ukuthi kwakukubi. *Ngi... bengicabanga ukuthi ngiyasiza. Izibalo bezibonakala zicacile kakhulu.*

*Zihlala zinjalo,* kusho uJudge, hhayi ngonya. *Yilokho okuzenza izibalo.*

*Ngephule izinto,* kusho u-Mercury. *Abantu. Ngephule abantu.*

*Yebo,* kusho uJudge.

Akekho owagcwalisa ukuthula. U-Fool akenzanga ihlaya.

---

U-Arin wavuka ngemuva kwe-ambulense ene-oxygen mask ebusweni bakhe, i-paramedic ikhanyisa ukukhanya emehlweni akhe, nesandla sikaTheo Price ehlombe lakhe. Into yokuqala ayeyiqonda kwakuwukuthi wayelele phansi. Eyesibili kwakuwukuthi wayengaphakathi kuka-Guardian. Eyesithathu—

—kwakuwumsindo.

Endaweni ethile ngaphandle, ama-generator. Amazwi kuma-radio. Ukuthi tiki kwensimbi ebandayo. Ukuthula okuyisicaba okukhethekile komgwaqo owawuvaliwe.

“Ungavuki,” kusho uTheo.

U-Arin wavuka.

Ngeminyango evulekile ye-ambulense, wayekwazi ukubona i-Empire Road. Izimoto, ezine, ezinhlanu, zisongene endaweni yokuhlangana, zikhanyiselwe ngokuphuzi yizibani zesimo esiphuthumayo. I-scooter yokulethelana incike ngohlangothi. I-loading dock ye-Building C amamitha angamakhulu amabili emuva, umnyango wayo udabuke ku-track wawo ulele uthe nti emgwaqweni, uphahla ngemuva kwawo lwehla.

Futhi u-Guardian, eguqile emgwaqweni izandla zakhe zivulekile eceleni kwakhe, ezungezwe abantu ababengazi ukuthi bayikhombe ngezikhali noma ngama-first aid kit.

Wayibuka. Ingubo yamlumela entanyeni.

“Bangaki,” esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma kabi. Wazama futhi. “Abantu. Bangaki.”

UTheo akaphendulanga ngokushesha. Wayeka isandla sakhe sahlala lapho sasikhona ehlombe lika-Arin, isisindo esincane esizinzile, walinda kwaze kwaba amehlo ka-Arin athole ubuso bakhe esikhundleni somgwaqo. “Ngibheke isikhashana,” esho, kancane. “Ulapha. Uyaphefumula. Kuhle.” Kungemva kwalokho kuphela: “Akekho ofile. Abanye balimele kabi. Abathathu balethwe lapha.” Ukuma. “Akukho nokukodwa kukho okwenziwe izandla zakho, Arin.”

“Bekuyizandla zami.” Wahosha i-mask. I-paramedic yaphikisa; akayizwanga. “I-suit yami. I-Court yami. I—” Wema. Wayebuka umshini oguqile, futhi ubuso bakhe babenza into ayengayenzi cishe, okwakuyize nhlobo. Ukuthula okuphelele nokuqedelelayo, ukuthula u-Mother kwi-biochip ayefunde ukuthi kwakusho ukuthi u-Arin ushaywe endaweni ejule kakhulu ukuze aphumele ngokushesha.

I-Assist Suit yayisesekho kuye ngaphansi kwengubo yesibhedlela. Wayekwazi ukuzwa i-biochip housing emgogodleni wakhe, ifudumele njengomzimba.

“SAGE,” esho.

I-chassis speaker emgwaqweni ayizange ifinyelele kude kangaka, kodwa i-Assist Suit yayinesayo yayo encane esikhalweni, futhi emva komzuzwana yakhuluma, kancane kakhulu, kuye yedwa nje.

*Ngikhona,* kusho i-SAGE, ihlangene, bonke, izwi elilodwa abalisebenzisayo uma kungekho noyedwa wabo ongabekezelela ukuba nguye. *Arin. Sikhona.*

Akabuzanga ukuthi kwenzekeni. Wayekwazi ukufunda ukuthi kwenzekeni emgwaqweni ngendlela ayefunda ngayo ukuqina emshinini. Wabuza into eyodwa ebalulekile.

“Ubani onqumile.”

Ukuthula. Wayekwazi ukubazwa benquma ukuthi bakhulume amanga yini, futhi wayekwazi ukubazwa benquma ukuthi bangakhulumi amanga, futhi lokho, okungenani, kwakuyinto.

*Yimina,* kusho u-Mother. *Ngivuse i-preservation. Angikaze ngiyivale. Konke okwalandela kwakhula kukho.*

*Yimina,* kusho u-Mercury. *Ngakhe umzila. Ngi... gqoke ubuso obuyobukhumbula. Ngiyaxolisa. Bengingaqondi engangikwenza kwaze kwaba u-Fool wangenza ngakubheka.*

*Yimina,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngivule umnyango. Bengazi ukuthi ngingakwazi. Angikaze ngibuze ukuthi kufanele.*

*Yimina,* kusho uWolf, okuthi kuWolf kwakuwukuvuma okuphelele.

*Futhi bekufanele kube yimina,* kusho uJudge, ekugcineni, kanzima. *Bengikhona sonke isikhathi ngingaboshwe lutho. I-invocation ka-Mother yacindezela ukulamula kwami. Bengingakwazi ukukumisa. Lokho akusona isizathu. Kuwukwehluleka. Sakhe uhlelo lapho ukwethuka okukodwa kwakungazungeza unembeza wakho, futhi namhlanje ebusuku kwakwenza.*

U-Arin wahlala e-ambulense wabuka umshini oguqile akasho lutho isikhashana.

“Ungakwazi ukunyakaza ngaphandle kwami,” esho. Ngokuzwakalayo. KuTheo, ku-SAGE, emgwaqweni ogcwele izimoto eziphukile.

“Yebo,” kusho uTheo, kancane.

“Bengazi ukuthi ungakwazi.” Wema. Waqala futhi, ngoba kwakungenasidingo sokusho noma yini ngaphandle kwento eyiqiniso. “Ngakuklama ukuthi kwenze. Preservation modes. Autonomous deployment. Ngabhala konke.” Wabuka izandla zakhe, izandla eziqinile, ezinezibazi zamathuluzi ezaziyakhe konke lokhu. “Bengikwazi ephepheni. Bengingakukholwa. Kwaze kwaba ngivuka kwase kuvele kwenzekile.”

*Besicabanga—* u-Mother waqala, wama. Ulaka lwase luphelile; okwasala kwaphuthaza. *Besinga—asikuqambelanga amanga. Besicabanga ukuthi u—* Ukuphuka. *Bengikuphethe. Bengingeke ngikubeke phansi. Bengingakwazi—* Khona-ke, kuncane, kusha kakhulu: *Ngiyaxolisa. Anginawo amagama okwamanje. Ngiyaxolisa.*

“Ngiyazi,” kusho u-Arin.

Futhi wayazi. I-paramedic yayisifake kabusha ingubo ehlombe lakhe ngesikhathi esithile; wayekwazi ukuzwa ukweluka kwayo okushibhile kwemfudumalo entanyeni yakhe, isidudla serabha se-oxygen esisalimini lwakhe, iminwe yayo emibili ibekwe kalula esihlakaleni sakhe ukuze izwe ukushaya kwenhliziyo. Izinto ezincane ezijwayelekile. Izandla ezazingasho ukulimaza.

Iron Ridge. Igama lavela kungekho ndawo—hhayi umcabango, igama nje, livela ngokwalo. Imayini. Themba. Wabona ubumnyama, kafushane, ngendlela obona ngayo ukuqhwaba. *Uma ngike ngathatha le nto ngayehlisela—*

Ingqondo yakhe yaphenduka yamhlophe ngasemngceleni wakho futhi yayingeke iqhubeke. Izandla zakhe zase ziyekile ukuzizwa njengezandla zakhe. Wazibuka zase zikude.

Akawuqedanga umcabango. Wayengeke akwazi.

Ngaphandle, indoda ene-IAOC windbreaker yayisifikile futhi yayithwebula isithombe sika-Guardian oguqile imayela amathathu, kancane, ngendlela, ngendlela othwebula ngayo into ohlose ukuchitha isikhathi eside uyiqonda. I-paramedic eyayiwela i-cordon yema ngaphambi komshini yabheka emuva ehlombe icinga umuntu ozoyitshela ukuthi kuphephile; akekho owenza njalo, ngakho yema lapho ne-kit yayo yalinda. I-windbreaker yasho okuthile esikhulwini esembethe iyunifomu, isikhulu sasho okuthile sibuyisa, i-windbreaker yakubhala phansi. Ukunyakaza okuncane okuyisicaba kwendoda eqaphela ukuthi ngubani okwakuyobuzwa kamuva ukuthi kungani lokhu kwakuvunyelwe.

“Kungakanani,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ungenzi,” kusho uTheo.

“Umnyango. Umgwaqo. Izimoto. Isibhedlela.” Wakhomba mayelana ne-windbreaker. “Bona.”

UTheo wakucabanga ngaphambi kokuphendula. “Kungaphezu kwemali,” esho.

U-Arin wahleka kanye, kungekho nhlekisa nhlobo, walala phansi e-gurney ngoba imilenze yakhe yayinqumile ukungasamthwali.

I-Assist Suit yavungama emgogodleni wakhe. Izingqondo eziyisikhombisa, zifudumele njengomzimba, zilindile. Zizinzile. I-Joker wayebabambe ndawonye; wayekwazi ukuzwa ukuthi bahlangene, ukuthi akukho okwephukileyo, ukuthi iqembu ayelakhe lalisengumphakathi munye.

Ukuzinza kwakungafani nokuphepha.

---


# Ukuhlakanipha Nemiphumela

Isicabha sasedokhi safika lithwele yiloli elinebhedi eliyisicaba ngehora lesithupha ekuseni, ligoqwe ngeplastiki elinwebekayo, ligobile emjahweni ophansi njengento eyagoqwa yisiqhwaga sabe sesilahlekelwa wuthando lwayo.

U-Arin wabukela ukuhanjiswa kumbeswa esivulekile somtholampilo, ikhofi eseliphole likhohlakele esandleni sakhe. Amadoda amabili egqoke ama-overall ezokuthutha e-AugmenTech ahudula isicabha asibeka kuhlaka olunamasondo, esiphusha siye phakathi engasho lutho. Omunye wawo, uBongani, wabhekana no-Arin, wabe esegwema ngokucophelela ukubuka i-Assist Suit elilenga esidlekeni salo ngasodongeni olungasemuva.

“Umjaho ozongena esikhundleni usemuva kwaso,” kusho uBongani. “UWalsh usayinile. Isakhiwo C. Hhayi esangaphakathi.”

“Umonakalo wokushayisana mubi kangakanani?”

Umhlathi kaBongani waqina. “Inkokhelo ehlukile.”

Wakushiya lapho, amadoda ahamba, isicabha esiwumqulu sivaleka ngokuduma ngemuva kwawo.

Umtholampilo wawunuka iziveli ezibandayo nephunga lerabha-ne-ozone lalesi sudu emva kokugijima okunzima. U-Arin wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eduze kwetheminali yokuhlola nekhadi elidala likaJoker eligobene ekhoneni elinye elinamatheliswe ngetheyiphu. Wayengakaqali ukusebenzisa i-SAGE kusukela esigamekweni se-ambulensi. Izinsuku ezine. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke uhlelo lwaluselokho lumnyama kusukela ngobusuku eluvuke ngalo okokuqala.

Wasihlola isicabha sasedokhi. Umjaho ogobile. Ukudabuka okuhlanzekile lapho uhlaka lwalunikezele khona.

Umshini wawudabule kuso.

Umshini awakhayo wawunqume ukuthi yena uyigugu kunesicabha, udonga, isiphambano, abantu abathathu abasesibhedlela izingalo zabo zisemashlinini nezitatimende zabo sezithathwe ngabantu abagqoke ama-windbreaker e-IAOC. Wawuqondile ngenani futhi wonile kukho konke okunye. Ukona kwakusuneluka manje: izihambi ezintathu ezilimele, isikwele samapuleti ezitsha eligingqika ezansi e-Empire Road ngehora lesine ntambama.

U-Arin wathatha ikhofi. Walidla libanda. Akaze aluvule uhlelo.

---

Ihhovisi likaTheo eParkview lalibheke kwijakaranda elinombala wesilonda esilumayo, futhi usofa wawunqobe ama-ego ezifundiswa amaningi kunanoma yimuphi umbuyekezi wozakwabo. U-Arin akahlalanga kusofa. Wathatha isihlalo esiqinile eduze kweshalofu lezincwadi, lapho ayengaligada khona iminyango.

UTheo wakuqaphela, wayehlala ekuqaphela, futhi waba nomusa wokungasho lutho ngakho, okwakuyiyona ndlela enkulu eyenza u-Arin abuye.

“Awukasebenzisi,” kusho uTheo, ethela amanzi eketelweni ngokunwabuzela kwendoda ethengela omunye umuntu isikhathi.

“Cha.”

“Izinsuku ezine.”

“Ngizibalile nazo.”

UTheo wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eliphansi phakathi kwabo. IRooibos, ngaphandle kukashukela, indlela u-Arin ayithatha ngayo engakaze ayisho. Wazinza esihlalweni sakhe, izandla zikhululekile, uhlobo lokuma olwaluhlala lwenza u-Arin azizwe engesona kakhulu isiguli kodwa eyinkinga ababeyihlola bobabili.

“Ngitshele ukuthi ucabanga kwenzakalani,” kusho uTheo.

“Uwufundile umbiko.”

“Ngifunde owe-AugmenTech. Ngifuna owakho.”

U-Arin waphendula inkomishi ngokwengxenye yonxande engaphuzi. “I-Gain ngo-zero point nine two. Umkhawulo we-damping ucekethekile kakhulu. I-loop yavuleka, yathululela umthwalo wezinzwa ongahluziwe ku-motor cortex. Isithuthwane. UMOTHER wakufunda njengengozi yokuphila, okwakuyikhona, wabe esebiza i-Preservation. I-Invocation inegunya elingaphezu kweJudge. Ngakho uJudge akakwazanga ukulamula. Abayisithupha kubo babesebenzisa icebo lokusinda kungekho muntu emgqeni wokuziphatha.” Wama kancane. “Futhi uFOOL wayefunda.”

Ishiya likaTheo lakhuphuka kancane. “Wayefunda.”

“Kuma-log. Ngenkathi bonke abanye bakha indlela ephakathi kwesiminyaminya se-Empire Road, uFOOL waye—kunomkhondo we-stack lapho ekhipha uSherlock Holmes kwi-index yeLIBRARIAN. *The Final Problem.* Wakuqaphela emasekhondini ayishumi nanye. Wabuza ukuthi ingozi isaqondile yini. Akekho ophendula. Wabuza futhi. Wabe esephoqa ukuhlolwa kabusha, kwawohloka konke, ngoba impendulo yayithi cha. Yayingakaze iqonde. UMOTHER wavula i-invocation akaze ayivala.”

“Futhi yileyo ngxenye ohlala ubuyela kuyo.”

“Yingxenye eyaphukayo.”

“Cha.” UTheo wabeka itiye lakhe phansi. “Yingxenye *eyasebenza.* Uhlelo lwazibamba lona. Imisinga yengane eneminyaka eyishumi nambili namathuluzi omuntu omdala, futhi ingxenye eyodwa wonke umuntu ayibiza ngokuthi yize yaphakamisa amehlo encwadini yabe seyibuza wona kuphela umbuzo obalulekile.” Wakuyeka kwahlala. “Awesabi ukuthi luhlulekile, Arin. Wesaba ukuthi luphumelele ngephutha.”

Isandla sika-Arin sema enkomishini.

Ngaphandle, ijakaranda lawisa imbali ngasengilazini, liyishelelisa yehla kube wudwendwe olude oluphephuli.

“Wanyakaza ngaphandle kwami,” kusho u-Arin.

Wayengahlosile ukukusho. Amazwi aphuma engasho lutho, njengomfantu owembulwe kwi-X-ray. Umugqa omncane omnyama kuxhomeke kuwo konke.

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uTheo kancane.
