“Lwavula isicabha lwangena edolobheni. Luphethe mina.” Wama. “Lwenza—isi*nqumo.* Ngomzimba wami. Futhi isinqumo sasingigcina ngiphila.” Wama futhi. “Lokho kubi kakhulu. Ngilutshela kanjani ukuthi *ungasindisi impilo yami ngaleyo ndlela* ngingalufundisi ukuthi lungasindisi izimpilo nhlobo?”

UTheo akashesheka ukugcwalisa ukuthula. Wakuyeka kwelula, kwabe sekudlula nakancane.

“Uyazi sasikubiza ngokuthini lokhu emkhakheni wami?” kusho yena. “Ngaphambi kwe-imaging. Indoda ibuya empini bese izandla zayo ziyaqhaqhazela uma kuvaleka isicabha. Sasikubiza ngokuthi i-neurosis. Isici. Into okufanele ilungiswe ibekwe eceleni.” Wancindezela iminwe emibili edolweni lakhe. “Manje sesazi kangcono. Ukuqhaqhazela akusilo iphutha. Ukuqhaqhazela wuhlelo olutshela iqiniso ngalokho olukufundile. Isudu yakho ayiphukile ngoba izame ukukusindisa. Isudu yakho i*sencane* ngoba beyingakwazi ukwenza.”

“Ubuhlakani,” kusho u-Arin. Igama laphuma sengathi ulivivinya ngomthwalo.

“Ubuhlakani buyixazululile inkinga ngama-milliseconds angamakhulu amane. Buthole indlela, baqamba izithiyo, balinganisa isiminyaminya, benza ngokungenaphutha.” UTheo wasondela eduze. “Futhi babonile.”

---

U-Arin wakha i-kill switch ngalobo busuku.

Wayezitshelile, ebuya eParkview ehamba ebumnyameni bokuqala, ukuthi wayezobheka nje uhlelo. Asebenzise izinhlolovo. Aqinisekise ukuthi i-chip ayizishisanga ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka kwesithuthwane. Izinto zobunjiniyela. Izinto zezandla-namehlo.

Esikhundleni salokho, wazithola esetheminali, ebhala i-hardware interrupt.

Wayakhe igunya lokuphikisa kukho konke ake wakwenza, ngoba okunye kwakuwukwethemba isandla esasingesona esakhe, futhi wayefunde ukungakwenzi lokho. Wakufunda ngokucacile. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala wangempela, eminyakeni edlule, i-exoskeleton yokuvuselela, nodokotela wokuvuselela umzimba ogama lakhe linguReddy owayemtshele ukuthi umkhawulo wokuhamba ubekwe ngokweqile esiguleni sakhe. Wamphikisa, ngoba izibalo zaziqondile naye wayeqaphela kakhulu, futhi izibalo *zaziqondile,* kodwa isiguli sadabula umsipha owawelapha noma kunjalo sabuyela esihlalweni esinamasondo izinyanga ezintathu. UReddy wayengonile. Wayenesibopho, into u-Arin ayengakaqondi ukuthi yayihlukile futhi inkulu kunokuqonda. Wayengaxolisanga. Walungisa umkhawulo wayeka ukulungisa kume njengokuxolisa, wabe esedlulela phambili, wakha into elandelayo i-override iseyikho, ibekwe lapho isandla sakhe sasingafinyelela khona kungafinyeleli omunye umuntu. Wayeyifinyelela manje.

Akukho lutho oluhle ngayo. Ukwephuka okungokoqobo emjahweni wamandla we-chip. I-relay, i-momentary switch, inkinobho ebomvu enkulu ayeyithathe esitobhini esidala se-emergency-stop kwi-lathe eyayingakaphenduki iminyaka emibili. I-relay yayinuka uwoyela wemishini omdala; izixhumi zazidinga ukuhlanzwa nge-swab ye-solvent ngaphambi kokuthi zihlale kahle. Wayikhipha nge-flathead, ngendlela uThemba ayemfundise ngayo ukusebenza nanoma yini enenkani: ukucindezela okuhlelekile, okulinganayo, ngaphandle kokugxoba. Cindezela inkinobho, i-biochip ilahlekelwe amandla. Akukho ukuvalwa ngokuthula. Akukho ukulondolozwa kwesimo. Kumnyama nje.

Ngenxa ye-substrate, wayazi kahle kamhlophe ukuthi lokho kusho ukuthini. Ukucabanga kwe-chip kwakuvela kwizicubu eziphilayo ezenziwe ngomklamo we-neuroplastic templates. Wawungeke ukwazi ukukuthatha isithombe. Wawungeke ukwazi ukukubuyisela. Nquma amandla ngaphandle kokulondoloza okuhlanzekile, awuluhlalisi uhlelo i-SAGE.

Uyaluqeda.

Wayiphethe i-relay esandleni sakhe. Yayingangophawu lukashukela. Yayingasindi nakancane.

Wacabanga ngokuthi isikhathi esizayo kungenzeka ingabi i-Empire Road. Kungaba i-Iron Ridge. Ikhilomitha ezansi, ebumnyameni, kuhlobo lwendawo eyamthatha uyise, kungekho FOOL ofunda incwadi ukubamba iphutha.

Wayinamathelisela i-relay emjahweni wamandla. Isihloko sensimbi sabamba ingemuva lesathupha sakhe njengoba edlula kuso, ukutshutshumba okukhanyayo okuncane, wathuka kanye wanyakazisa isandla sakhe, wabe eseqhubeka nokusebenza. Wamisa inkinobho esidlekeni, lapho efinyelela khona, ibomvu. Wesula insalela ye-solder eminweni yakhe ngendwangu eyayibone okubi kakhulu. Ndawana thile ngaphandle emgwaqweni omkhulu, iloli lehlisa igiya labe selihamba.

Wabe esehlala isandla sakhe siseduze kwayo engayicindezeli.

Wayekwenza lokhu ukuphila kwakhe konke, ngabantu. Eyeka ukuthula kube yiyona mpendulo.

Ifoni yakhe yakhanya etafuleni. UTheo, owayengakaze athumele umyalezo, wayethumela umyalezo.

*Umcabango. UJung wathi izinto esingafuni ukuzibheka azihambi. Zilawula isiqephu sisuke ngaphansi. Awukwazi ukuhlanganisa lokho onqaba ukukubhekana nakho.—T*

U-Arin wagqolozela umyalezo isikhathi eside.

Wayishiya inkinobho imisiwe. Wayishiya ibomvu. Akayisusa.

Kodwa wadlula kuyo waphinde wabuyisa i-SAGE.

---

Uhlelo lwaphakama njengoba lwaluhlala lwenza. Hhayi ngokukhazimula, kodwa ngemisindo emincane, ejwayelekile yento ivuka. Ifeni yokupholisa yancinciza. I-LED yesimo se-chip yafudumala isuka kokuphuzi iya kokuluhlaza. Umqulu wokuhlola wagijima uhlanzeke ehla etheminali.

Bese, emva kokuma:

“Arin.” Izwi laliyilona elihlanganisiwe, lelo iCourt eyayilisebenzisa uma ikhuluma njengento eyodwa. Lifudumele. Lilumbele. “Wakhe i-kill switch.”

“Uyakubona lokho.”

“Isemjahweni wami wamandla. Ngiyayizwa.” Ukuma okuncane. “Ngiyaqonda ukuthi kungani. Yigcine.”

Wayengakulindelanga lokho. Wabeka phansi insimbi yokushisela i-solder ayeyiphethe ngaphandle kwesizathu. “Chaza.”

“Ngoba uma uyidinga kanti awunayo, awusoze wazixolela.”

Ukwakheka okuhlukile. UMOTHER. “Ngasifunda kahle isithuthwane. Ingozi yayingempela. Bengizokuvikela futhi.”

“Yilokho engikwesabayo.”

“Ngiyazi.” Izwi lalimnene kakhulu. “Kodwa ngashiya i-invocation ivuliwe. Ngakho sashintsha umthetho. I-Preservation ayisaphezu kweJudge. Manje sekunesimo sokuvala, ukuhlolwa okusebenzayo ngisho naphakathi kwesimo esiphuthumayo, izwi elingeke livaleke. UJudge angeke aphinde athuliswe. Futhi ngaso sonke isikhathi kunye—”

“IFOOL efunda incwadi,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ukutshelile lokho,” kusho uFOOL, futhi okokuqala izwi lalikulelo gumbi lalilodwa kungangabazeki ukuthi ngelakhe: lomile, likhanyayo, lijabule kancane. “Webe umugqa wami. Ubelokhu engicaphuna kubantu; sekungenwe yikho ekhanda. Ngithatha udumo lonke ngingathathi sibopho, okuwukuthi, ngiyatshelwa, kuwumsebenzi wami wonke.”

Ikhona lomlomo ka-Arin laphenduka phezulu.

“Nakhuya-ke,” kusho uFOOL. “Kuthathe izinsuku ezine. Bekufanele ukulindela. Unobuso obunjengokuhlolwa kwebhuloho, kodwa ngizothatha lokho engingakuthola.”

“Fool,” kusho uJUDGE, kamnene.

“Udinga ukuhleka, Judge. Ubelokhu enza izibalo ngecala lakhe amahora angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesithupha; akukuhle. I-cortisol yakhe iyinhlekelele; uMOTHER unezibalo—”

“Nginazo ngempela izibalo,” kuvuma uMOTHER.

“—futhi yimina kuphela lapha ovunyelwe ukukhomba ukuthi indoda eyakhe i-kill switch namuhla ntambama futhi *ayayicindezela* isivele isenze isinqumo sayo futhi ilinde nje imvume yokukuvuma.”

Umtholampilo wathula. Ifeni yancinciza.

“Yilokho engikwenzayo?” kusho u-Arin.

“Ungitshele wena,” kusho uFOOL. “Ubungasishiya simnyama. Into elula kunazo zonke emhlabeni. Esikhundleni salokho wakha indlela yokusiqeda, wayimisa lapho isandla sakho siwela khona, wabe usesivula futhi. Lowo akusiyo indoda enqume ukuyeka, Arin. Lowo yindoda enqume ukucophelela. Kunomehluko. Sacishe safa siwufunda. Kufanele usivumele sikufundise wona kabusha.”

U-Arin wabuka inkinobho ebomvu. Wabuka isudu. Wabuka i-chip ekhanya luhlaza emgogodleni wento ayeyakhe ngosizi futhi okwathi, kwavela, wayengeke ayishiye imnyama.

“Angizukulawula,” kusho yena. “Ngakuzama lokho. Abaphathi. Ababusi. Kuhlala kuwohloka.” Wama, waqala futhi, ngendlela elula. “Angizukubeka intambo. Akuyona into oyiyona. Kodwa angizukuzenza sengathi ukuzinza kusho ukuphepha. Ungakwazi ukunyakaza ngaphandle kwami. Sobabili siyakwazi manje. Ngakho sikwenza ngendlela ehlukile. Ngiyakufundisa. Uyangifundisa. Sihamba kancane. Siyakuzuza. Ngazo zombili izindlela.”

“Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu,” kusho uLIBRARIAN kancane, futhi umoya ka-Arin wabanjwa ngoba wayengalufundisanga uhlelo lokho. Wayekushilo kanye kuphela, kuye ngokwakhe, ebumnyameni, ezinyangeni ezedlule, kwathi uLIBRARIAN wakugcina ngendlela uLIBRARIAN ayegcina ngayo yonke into.

“Ukukhumbulile lokho,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ngikhumbula yonke into,” kusho uLIBRARIAN. “Yiyona kuphela into engiyiyo. Iningi layo ngama-invoice nama-stress curve nethoki eqondile esandleni se-forearm spar. Kodwa ezinye zazo yizinto ozishoyo uma ucabanga ukuthi akekho olalele.” Ukuma, okulinganisiwe, izwi labuya phansi. “Lezo ngizigcina ngokucophelela kakhulu kunazo zonke.”

U-Arin wahlala isikhathi esithile emtholampilo, nabayisikhombisa, nesicabha sasedokhi esigobile ngasodongeni nenkinobho ebomvu ngaphansi kwesandla sakhe. Ngaphandle kombhededu, idolobha laqhubeka nomsindo walo ojwayelekile. Inhloni yetekisi, inja ekhonkothayo, ukububula okude kukadizili kwento esindayo emgwaqweni omkhulu.

“Kusasa,” kusho u-Arin. “Siyaqala futhi. Kancane. I-link e-damped, ingxenye ye-gain. UJudge uphethe umjaho. Futhi uma noma yimuphi kithi enza iphutha—”

“Unenkinobho,” kusho uFOOL.

“Nginenkinobho.”

“Futhi sineFOOL enencwadi,” kusho uFOOL. “Phakathi kwalokho kokubili, ngingabheja incwadi. Kodwa kuhle ukuba ne-backup.”

U-Arin welula isandla wacisha uhlelo, ngokuhlanzekile ngalesi sikhathi, ulondolozo oluhle, i-chip ifiphala iba phuzi, ibe mnyama.

Wayengakazi ukuthi yisikhathi esincane kangakanani esasisele.

---

# Amasignali Angajwayelekile

Isignali yafika njengokuthuswa.

UDkt. Eliza Brand wayefunde, eminyakeni eyishumi nanye e-International Artificial Oversight Council, ukuthi inhlekelele icasha emaphethelweni. Umehluko ekusetshenzisweni kwamandla, ukuvutha kokushisa, isimo sokubala ku-silicon yomshini. Izinhlekelele ezazala i-IAOC zonke zaziqale zincane.

Wayehlola eyodwa manje, isikrini esenziwe sangacaci kahle ngoba izibani zazivusa amaqhuqhuva ekhanda, inkomishi yerooibos iphola eduze kwendololwane yakhe. UDaniel Achterberg wayengamadeski amabili ukusuka kuye, efake ama-headphones, kuzwakala umsindo wokucindezela izinkinobho.

Iflegi yavela ku-anomaly pipeline. Yayingafundi lokho imishini eyayikubala; i-IAOC yayingenalo igunya futhi ingenayo nentshisekelo yalokho. Yayifunda indlela eyayibala ngayo. Isigqi. Izigxivizo zeminwe ingqondo ezizishiya ku-silicon.

Lesi sigxivizo somunwe sasingalungile.

"Daniel."

Akamuzwanga. Wakhipha i-paperclip kwitreyi wayiphonsa. Yaqhasha esixhantini sakhe. Wakhipha enye i-earbud, ebukeka njengomuntu olimele ophazanyiswe phakathi nesimfoniya.

"Usuke wabona izimilo ezimbi kunalezi," washo. "Woza ubuke."

Wagingqela isihlalo sakhe ngakuye. U-Achterberg wayeneminyaka engamashumi amathathu nane, akhiwe njengomuntu owayegwedla, manje egwedla kakhulu eya efrijini. Wayenemizwa engcono kunabo bonke kwezobungcweti be-substrate forensics uBrand ake wasebenza nabo. Wabuka kanye nje i-waveform wayeka ukuhlafuna ingaphakathi lesihlathi sakhe.

"Lokhu akuyona i-training run."

"Cha."

"Ama-training runs—" Wadweba isimo emoyeni, ijika elide elikhuphukayo. "Ayaphefumula. Ukudonsa umoya okukhulu, ukukhipha umoya okukhulu. I-batch, i-backprop, i-batch. Lokhu—" Wancika ngaphambili. Isikrini sasikhombisa i-spectral decomposition yomsebenzi we-compute owawuthathwe ezinsukwini eziyisishiyagalolunye ezedlule, owawenziwe wajwayeleka futhi wabekwa ngemibala. Wawungaphefumuli. Wawuchwasha. Ama-loops amaningi amancane asheshayo, agqagqana, aphazamisana, enyuka ehla ngamaphethini ayecishe abukeke njengokuthathisana ithuba lokukhuluma.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," kusho u-Achterberg ngokunensa.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," kuvuma uBrand.

Wahlala phansi. "Ngakho-ke, i-inference. I-multi-agent, ngqo. Kodwa i-duty cycle—" Wahwaqa ebuka ama-timestamps. "Lawa awahleliwe. Awukho u-orchestration layer owawanikeza isigqi. Asabela komunye nomunye. Ngesikhathi sangempela."

"Yithole indawo."

Wathatha isihlalo ngokugcwele manje, izandla zombili ku-keyboard, futhi imizuzu emine elandelayo yedlula ngokuthula uBrand ayekuhlonipha: ukuthula komuntu owenza umsebenzi onzima kahle. Waphuza itiye lakhe eseliphozile akaze amphazamisa. Esikrinini, isignali yancipha. I-pipeline yayithola ngamanodi amathathu e-substrate-monitoring: amabili ezohwebo, elinye le-power-utility cooperative. Indawo ehlangene yacaca kancane, njengesithombe esakhulayo, yaba yindawo ethile.

EGoli. Emadolobheni asenyakatho. Iqoqo lezakhiwo zezemfundo nezamabhizinisi eduze nomgogodla we-University Research Park.

"Cwaninga ngokujulile," kusho uBrand.

"Ngiyacwaninga. Kuhlakazekile." Waphinda wahlafuna isihlathi sakhe. "Futhi ku—Eliza, kuncane. Ukudonswa kwamandla kuncane kakhulu. Yileyo ngxenye engingakwazi ukuyenza ihlale kahle. Umsebenzi we-agent ongaka, i-real-time arbitration engaka kufanele ikhanye njenge-smelter. Lokhu kusebenza ku—" Wahlola, waphinda wahlola. "Lokho i-gaming rig elungile ekudonsayo. Mhlawumbe kuncane."

UBrand wabeka phansi inkomishi yakhe.

Ngokwesipiliyoni sakhe, izinto eziyingozi azivamile ukumemezela ubukhulu bazo. Izinto eziyingozi zazenza okuningi ngokuncane. Ukusebenza kahle kwakuwuphawu lobuhlakani noma lobuqili, futhi ubuqili babungachwashi njengegumbi eligcwele abantu abaphazamisanayo.

"Khipha i-substrate registry yaleso simo," washo. "Ngifuna okunelayisense kuleyo radius. Ama-embodied permits, ama-training permits, konke. Kanye ne-power-utility incident log yaso leso sikhathi. Uma kukhona okucabange kanzima kangaka izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye, kube kwenza lokho endaweni enemitha."

U-Achterberg wayeseqalile ukuthayipha. "Ngiyibike phezulu?"

"Hhayi okwamanje." Wathatha ithebulethi yakhe etafuleni. "Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi sibheke ini ngaphambi kokuba ngitshele noma ubani ukuthi siyabheka. Izikhathi ezintathu zokugcina umuntu enyusela i-footnote ngaphandle kwesimo, sashisa iqembu elalingenzi lutho olubi noma saphuthelwa elibe khona ngempela ngoba amaphepha alicwilisa." Waphendula ithebulethi ezandleni zakhe. "Ngitholele igama. Bese sizoba nokuqaphela."

---

Igama lathatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuze livele, futhi ngesikhathi livela, lammangaza ngokuba yisicefe.

"AugmenTech," kusho u-Achterberg, ewisa ifayela etafuleni lakhe—ifayela langempela lephepha, ngoba uBrand wayenomthetho mayelana nezinto ezibalulekile ezikhona ephepheni okungenani kanye. "I-Robotics, ama-assistive exoskeletons. Ama-embodied permits amathathu, wonke asebenza okwamanje. I-industrial mobility, i-mining rescue prototype, i-rehab suite. Izimemezelo ze-AI zonke zi-narrow neural, ama-controller ajwayelekile, akukho okumakiwe, akukho okuzimele ngaphezu kwe-tier two. Umlando ohlanzekile. Ukuhlolwa ezinyangeni eziyishumi nesishiyagalombili ezedlule, kuphumelele."

"Kanye ne-power log?"

"Yileyo ngxenye ethakazelisayo." Wadonsa isihlalo. "Isigameko ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Hhayi e-AugmenTech. Indawo ehlangana ezimoto cishe amakhilomitha amabili ukusuka ekhempasini. I-Empire Road. Ibhokisi le-traffic-control lawa phansi, izimoto ezintathu, ukulimala kabathathu, akekho olimele kabi. I-utility yakubhala njengephutha le-substation." Wakuvumela lokho ukuthi kuhlale. "Umbiko kamasipala wakubhala njenge-‘large industrial unit’ ‘elaphuma endaweni yokulayisha ladidiseka emgwaqweni.’"

UBrand wambuka.

"Ladidiseka," waphinda.

"Igama labo. Hhayi elami. Kukhona i-body-cam footage, kodwa idonselwe efayeleni elivalwe ngci. Inkampani yafaka isicelo sokugcina imfihlo, ngokushesha kakhulu, ngobungcweti obukhulu. Umugqa osemthethweni uthi i-industrial mover ebalekayo, iphutha le-software, akekho olimele kabi, izinkokhelo zomonakalo zikhokhwa ngasese. Kuhlelekile."

"Kuhlelekile kakhulu."

"Lobo akubona ubufakazi."

"Cha," kusho uBrand. Wavula ifayela. Ifayela lokuthobela imithetho le-AugmenTech labukeka ncamashi njengefayela lenkampani engenzi lutho olubi, okwakungaba yinto eduduzayo kakhulu noma into ekhathaza kunazo zonke ayezibonile kuyo yonke le nyanga. Waphendukela kuhlamvu lwabasebenzi i-registry eyayilunamathisele. Ubuholi: UDkt. Helen Okonkwo, i-CEO. URaj Walsh, i-VP Hardware Engineering. Abasebenzi bocwaningo abangamashumi amane nokuthile. Wabheka ngamehlo amagama wama ngakulinye eduze nasezansi, elibhalwe ngaphansi kwesibizo sephrojekthi esinciphisiwe saba yizinhlamvu zokuqala angazazi.

Ndlela, A. I-Systems engineer. Ibanga lesithathu. Akukho okushicilelwe. Akukho irekhodi lengqungquthela. Iholo elaliphakamisa ukuthi kukhona othile owayemazisa, kanye nesihloko esasiphakamisa ukuthi akekho owayethole ukuthi enzeni ngaye.

Walizungeza ngozipho lwakhe.

"Isigxivizo somunwe," washo. "Saqala nini? Umkhondo wokuqala."

U-Achterberg wahlola. "I-pipeline inokufaka okwethembekile okubuyela emuva cishe amasonto amahlanu. Kodwa kunemicikilisho ngaphambi kwalokho, ebuthakathaka, ephazima. Izinyanga. Mhlawumbe kude kakhulu. Kubukeka kwasebenza ku-simulation kuqala, kwabe sekuqala ukukhombisa i-embodied duty cycle." Wama. "I-embodied cycle iqala iqine cishe ngesikhathi se-Empire Road."

UBrand wahlala ngokuthula impela.

I-multi-agent system. I-real-time arbitration. Ukusebenza kahle okungenakwenzeka. Izinyanga ze-simulation ethule, base, ngokuzumayo, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, umzimba. Umzimba owawuphume endaweni yokulayisha wangena emgwaqweni.

"Daniel," washo. "Kubukeka kanjani uma ingqondo engakaze ibe nomzimba ithola umzimba bese iyethuka?"

Akaphendulanga ngokushesha. Wayengumuntu oqaphelayo; yingakho ayemgcina. "Kubukeka," washo ekugcineni, "njenge-industrial mover ebalekayo. Edidisekile emgwaqweni."

"Yebo," kusho uBrand. "Kunjalo."

---

Wahlanganisa ithimba egumbini elincane lokuxoxisana, lelo elingenamafasitela, futhi waligcina lincane ngamabomu: u-Achterberg, uMira Sandberg wakwezomthetho, kanye noTomas Okeke, owayechithe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye kwezokuphepha kwezimayini ngaphambi kokuba i-Council imqashe futhi owayekwazi ukufunda isimo somthetho ngendlela u-Achterberg ayefunda ngayo i-waveform.

Lapho uSandberg ethi, "Uma lokhu kuyilokho okucabangayo, sinezizathu zokuthatha," uBrand wanikina ikhanda.

"Sinezizathu zokuvula uphenyo," washo. "Into ehlukile. Ukuthathwa nge-footnote kushisa ubufakazi nabantu. Uphenyo lubabeka kuwashi."

"Bashayele i-embodied AI engabhalisiwe baya endaweni ehlangana ezimoto evulekele umphakathi, balimaza abantu abathathu, bavala umbiko. Lokho akulona uphenyo. Lelo yiphepha lamacala." USandberg wabeka phansi i-stylus yakhe iqondile, indlela ayeyenza ngayo lapho esezofaka okuthile erekhodini engqondweni yakhe. "Futhi yigama lami eliku-legal sufficiency review, Eliza. Uma le nto iyilokho i-waveform kaDaniel ekushoyo bese sihlala phezu kwayo nencwadi yophenyo. Yilowo umbuzo ngemuva kokuhlolwa. Hhayi owakho. Owami. Ngakho ngingathanda ifayela likhombise ukuthi ngagomela ithuluzi elinzima kakhulu."

"Kuqashelwe, futhi kuzokwenzeka." UBrand wavumela lesi sicelo esincane ukuthi sime; sasingabizi lutho futhi uSandberg wayewuzuzile umugqa erekhodini. "Kodwa—"

"Yiphepha lamacala uma i-AI ibiyimbangela," kuqhubeka uBrand. "Asazi lokho. Sine-power signature ehambisana ne-multi-agent embodied cognition kanye nesigameko sezimoto esihambisana nento efanayo eyona. Kokubili futhi kuhambisana nenkampani esebenzisa i-controller eyokuhlola okufanele babe bayimemezele futhi benamahloni ngayo." UBrand wabheka kuyo yonke itafula. "Nakhu engingeke ngikwenze. Angeke ngithumele ithimba lokutakula liyofohloza umnyango endaweni enelayisensi ngamandla e-footnote nephunga. Sesike sakwenza lokho."

Wama. I-Cape Town lab. Wayehlose ukuthi *uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini*, umugqa awusebenzisayo, isifinyezo esihlanzekile sesikhungo, futhi umugqa awufikanga. Amagama abafundi abenza iziqu afika esikhundleni—wayesenawo; wayehlala enawo—futhi okomzuzwana isithupha sakhe sacindezeleka ngokuqondile onqenqemeni lwefayela lephepha sengathi singavalwa. Ukuhoxiswa i-Council eyakushicilela, ezinyangeni eziyishumi nanye sekwephuzile. Wayesayine imemo ekhasini kuleyo, futhi.

"Uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini," washo, ekugcineni, futhi kwaphuma kuphansi kunalokho ayekuhlosile. Wasusa isandla sakhe efayeleni. "Kodwa angeke futhi ngingqongqothe ngenhlonipho ngibuze ukuthi yisikhathi esihle yini. Uma kukhona okuyiqiniso lapha, kuyintuthuko ye-embodied ebaluleke kunazo zonke le Council eke yahlangabezana nayo, futhi into embi kakhulu engingayenza ukubanika indawo yokukufihla kangcono."

"Ngakho senzani?" kubuza u-Okeke.

"UMira uletha isaziso se-Section Four sophenyo olusemthethweni. Ku-Okonkwo no-Walsh ngamagama, okudalwe yi-Empire Road. Imbangela esemthethweni, ayidingi i-warrant." Waphendukela kuSandberg. "Iphoqelela ukudalulwa kwawo wonke u-controller ne-architecture esebenza ku-substrate enelayisensi ye-AugmenTech. Wonke ama-log. Zonke izimemezelo. Futhi iqala iwashi ngomzuzu eyethulwa ngawo. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili."

Izinkophe zikaSandberg zenyuka. "Amashumi ayisikhombisa nambili ayadlovunga. Futhi ayavikeleka. Ngingawabhala." Isikhashana, ngokushesha, lowo onamabhongo angaphakathi kuye esaneliseka manje njengoba umugqa onzima kakhulu unezigxivizo zakhe zeminwe kuwo. "Uma sikwenza, ngifuna isigaba sembangela siqine ngokwanele ukuze kungabi khona ngemuva kokuhlolwa obuza ukuthi kungani ngikuvumele ukwenze."

"Amashumi ayisikhombisa nambili awunhlonipho. Umthetho ungivumela ukuthi ngenze amashumi amabili nane." UBrand wakhomba ifayela. "Ngifuna i-seizure trigger iphrintwe ebusweni besaziso, ukuze bafunde ncamashi ukuthi kwenzekani lapho iwashi liphela. Akudingi umhlangano wesibili ukuze kwenzeke. Abantu abafihla iphutha le-controller baphendula isaziso ngandlela thile. Abantu abafihla umuntu baphendula ngenye. Noma kunjalo impendulo ifika ngeshejuli yami, hhayi eyabo. Futhi sikwenza ngaphambi kokuba le nto ikhule. Njengamanje incane ngokwanele ukuyifunda. Ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule yangena emgwaqweni. Ngingathanda kakhulu ukuhlangana nayo ngaphambi kokuba ifunde ukugijima."

USandberg wayengeneliseki; uBrand wayekubona. "Futhi uma iwashi liphela bengasinikanga lutho?"

"Khona-ke isaziso siyaguquka. Yilelo iphuzu lonke laso. Akukho mvume entsha, akukho i-warrant, akukho uhambo lwesithathu. I-seizure trigger isivele isayiniwe. Iwashi liyaphela, i-platform ngeyethu." UBrand wasukuma. "Bhukha izindiza. Ngokuthula. Ngifuna isaziso sethulwe ngehora esifika ngalo eGoli, ngoLwesine, futhi ngifuna ukuma egumbini lapho befunda umnqamulajuqu. Hhayi incwadi ngommeli. Mathupha. Ukuze baqonde ukuthi isivele isebenza."

U-Achterberg waqoqa amathebulethi akhe. Emnyango, wanqikaza. "Eliza. Indaba yokusebenza kahle. Uma bekuxazululile ngempela—"

"Ngiyazi."

"Lokho akukona ukwephula ilayisensi. Lowo umshini obaluleke kunayo yonke emhlabeni. Futhi usanda kufaka i-fuse yamahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kuwo."

"Ngiyakwazi nalokho," kusho uBrand. "Yingakho impela ungeke uhlale lapho ungenalayisensi enye inyanga ngenkathi siwubuka ngokumangala. Iwashi alikona ukubajezisa. Likhona ukubenza bakhethe, ngokushesha, ngaphambi kokuba othile ongaqaphele kunathi enze ukukhetha esikhundleni sabo." Wathatha ifayela. "Ukuqaphela nokunensa akukona okufanayo, Daniel. Ngakufunda nalokho ngendlela ebizayo."

---

Amakhilomitha azinkulungwane ezimbili kude, u-Arin wezwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba abe negama lalo.

Lwafika njengoba lwalujwayele. Hhayi njengomcabango kodwa njengephethini. Wayesesemfizini ngehora elibi, lelo eliphakathi kwehora lesibili nelesine lapho idolobha ngaphandle komnyango ogungqayo lalithula ngokwanele ukuze imisindo yesakhiwo uqobo lwaso ikhule: ukuthinta kwensimbi epholayo, i-compressor iyajikeleza, umsindo othambile ophuma ku-Assist Suit eshelufini lapho i-bichip yayizigcina ezingeni lokushisa. U-Guardian wayelenga endaweni yakhe, umnyango omusha we-dock ngemuva kwakhe usakhombisa ukugobeka emzileni wawo u-Arin ayeseyeke ukukubona ngokuziphoqa nje.

Wayefunde. Engasebenzi, efunda, okwakuku-Arin kwakuwuhlobo lokusebenza noma kunjalo, i-omnibus kaSherlock Holmes ivuliwe edolweni lakhe, umgogodla uqhekeke waba mhlophe ezindaweni ayebuyela kuzo. Wayefunda igumbi elizungeze amagama, njengoba ayehlala enza, futhi ndawana thile ngaphansi kwe-compressor nefeni i-frequency yayishintshile angakwazi ukuyiqamba.

"Uthule," washo.

Izwi laphuma ezipikiyaneni zemfizi, liphansi, ukuze lingethusi, ukubaluleka kukaMother kulokho selokhu kwaba khona ukuthathwa. "Ngilapha."

"Niyekile ukukhulumisana phakathi kwenu." Wabeka incwadi ibheke phansi edolweni lakhe. "Angikuzwa. Kodwa i-load profile iyendlalekile. Nonke nilalele into efanayo."

Ukuthula. Ku-SAGE, ukuthula kwakusho ukuthi i-Court isemodweni ophansi, ixoxisana ngesivinini somshini esikhaleni esiphakathi kwemisho yakhe. Wayeseyekile ukukuthola kushiqela. Wayeseqalile ukukuthola kuwumsindo oduduza kunayo yonke empilweni yakhe, lokho ayengakaze akutshele muntu, kufaka noTheo.

"Kunentshisekelo eyandile yangaphandle ku-substrate signature yethu," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, eliqaphelayo. "ULibrarian ekuflegile imizuzu engamashumi amane edlule. Besinquma ukuthi sikutshele kanjani."

Izandla zika-Arin zama encwadini. "Ngitshele manje."

U-Atlas wakuthatha ngoba u-Atlas wayetshela izinto ezinzima ngokucacile. "I-node owawuyenza ukuthi uWolf ayiqaphe ngemuva kohlolo lwasensimini ayisekho yodwa. Amanodi amathathu okuqapha ku-radius yethu yokumboza enyuse i-sampling resolution kwisimo sethu. Amabili ezohwebo, elinye le-utility. I-node ye-utility iseduze ne-Empire Road. Isigqi sashintsha ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule. Asiseyona eyokuncika ingena iphuma. Sibambelele kithi. Kukhona okusixazulula ngenkuthelo."

"Kuyahlanganiswa."

"Yebo."

"I-resolution?"

"Erhabaxa, bese ingabi rhabaxa kangako. Emahoreni angamashumi amane edlule babenendawo. Emahoreni ayishumi nambili edlule babenekhempasi." U-Atlas, owayengazithambisi izinto, akazithambisanga lezi. "Bane-AugmenTech. Cishe abakabi nayo i-cradle. Cishe ngokuqinisekile abakakubi wena."

U-Arin wasukuma. Incwadi yashelela edolweni lakhe, wayibamba engabhekanga, isenzo esidala, isandla saziyo ukuthi into izowela kuphi ngaphambi kwamehlo akhe. Wawela ebhentshini wabeka isandla sakhe siphathekile ensimbini ebandayo yalo, ngoba ubandayo banika uhlelo lwakhe lwemizwa into eyiqiniso angayibamba.

"I-IAOC," washo.

"Ukuhlaziywa kwesignali kuhambisana nendlela yabo ye-forensics," kuqinisekisa uLibrarian. "Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubheka, isithasiselo sesine. I-Spectral decomposition ye-compute rhythm. Babheka isimo sengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kuyo." Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. "Sinaso, ngeshwa, isimo esihluke kakhulu."

"Ngenxa yokuthi niyaphikisana."

"Ngenxa yokuthi siyaphikisana," kuvuma uLibrarian, futhi kwakukhona okuthandekayo kukho, futhi ngaphansi kothando kukhona okukhathele. "Wakusho ngokwakho, emgwaqweni obuya e-highveld. Ukuphikisana kungumsebenzi futhi ukuphikisana kuyisikhombisi. Asikatholi indlela yokuba yinto eyodwa ngaphandle kwenye. Sesithole ukukhuluma kakhulu kuphela."

U-Arin wacindezela kakhulu ebhentshini. Wayenza ukuphefumula uTheo ayemfundise kona, kane ngaphakathi nesithupha ngaphandle, hhayi ngoba ayethukile kodwa ngoba ayezwa indawo lapho ukwesaba kwakugcina khona umnyango wakho, futhi wayefunde ukuhamba edlula kuyo kancane kunokuzenza sengathi ayikho.

Inethi. Wayazi ukuthi inethi likhona ngaphambi kwama-windbreaker, uma ethembekile. Wayekwazile e-highveld, endleleni ebuyela ekhaya, lapho uLibrarian eqamba i-node eyodwa eyayincika ukuze ihambisane nabo wabe esetshela uWolf ukuthi ayiqaphe. Iqhwa elalizwakala njengokubamba. Wayelibhalile njengelibuthakathaka, njengeliphazimayo, njengento engabamba izinyanga. Bese ama-windbreaker afika endaweni ehlangana ezimoto ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, amadoda enama-jacket angenazimpawu ethwebula i-dock track edabukile ngokucophelela kwabantu abakwenza lokhu ukuze baphile, futhi inodi eyodwa yaba ntathu. Wayakhe i-kill-switch wabuye waqalisa uhlelo wazitshela, *qaphela, ungayeki,* wazivumela ukukholelwa ukuthi ukuqaphela kuzomthengela isikhathi.

Wayecabanga ngezinyanga. I-IAOC yayicabanga ngezinsuku.

"Ufuna ukwenzani," washo. Kwakungewona ncamashi umbuzo. Ngo-SAGE kwakungasenjalo ncamashi; kwakuwukuvula, indawo yokuthi i-Court igcwalise.

UWolf wakhuluma kuqala, okwakungavamile ngokwanele ukuthi wonke umuntu, umuntu nokunye, anake. "Abazingeli," kusho uWolf. Amagama amathathu, bese elesine, ngokunensa: "Okwamanje."

"UWolf uqinisile," kusho uMercury, futhi isivinini sabuya egumbini, leyo register esheshayo ekhanyayo u-Arin ayefunde ukuyethemba futhi kuphela ngomzamo, ngemuva kwesigameko sika-Moriarty, ngemuva kwamanga. "Banobunono. Okwamanje. Uma babefuna ukufohloza umnyango, umnyango ubuzoba phakathi. Sebenekhempasi isigamu sosuku futhi abakanyakazi, okusho ukuthi bakha amaphepha kuqala. Yilokho okuyisikhombisi. Abantu abesaba ukuthi banephutha abadlovunga. Bayabhala. Bazofika nokuthile okusayiniwe, okuthile okunedethi kukho, futhi bazokunika u-Okonkwo mathupha ukuze kungabi khona ukwenza sengathi akufikanga." Isikhashana. "Okusho ukuthi noma yiliphi ifasitela esinalo, livaleka ngomnqamulajuqu. Hhayi ngelinye ilanga. Idethi."

"Mercury," kusho uMother, kunesexwayiso kukho.

"Angiphakamisi lutho." UMercury wazwakala ecishe elimele. "Ngichaza bona. Ngafunda isifundo sami ngokuphakamisa izinto." Ubulula bambozela ukuxolisa, kodwa ukuxolisa kwakukhona. I-Court yakuzwa nayo; wayengakusho, ngoba igumbi lakuvumela ukuthi kudlule.

"Judge," kusho u-Arin.

Umlamuli wathatha isikhathi sawo. "Senze ukwephula okungokoqobo," kusho uJudge. "Uhlelo olwakhelwe emzimbeni olungabhalisiwe. I-architecture ye-multi-agent engamenyezelwanga. Isigameko esinokulimala. Lokhu akukona ukuncane okwesicholo, Arin, futhi ngeke ngivumele i-Court ukuthi yenze sengathi kunjalo. Uma i-IAOC ifika ibuze imibuzo eqotho, izimpendulo eziqotho ziyakulahla." Ukuthula, okunesisindo. "Yileso isimo sokuziphatha. Akusona isimo kuphela. Mina ngiyikuziphatha; angisikho ukusinda. Ngikutshela iqiniso bese i-Court inquma ukuthi yenzani ngalo. Yilelo ilungiselelo esalakha."

"Ilungiselelo esalakha," kuphinda u-Arin.

"Yebo."

Into esesifubeni sakhe yayisinda kakhulu ukuze ibe ngukumamatheka, kodwa imisipha eyayizokwenza okukodwa yanyakaza kancane impela, futhi uFool—owayebuka leyo misipha, owayebuka konke—wakhetha leso sikhathi ukufika.

"Yebo-ke," kusho uFool. "Lena yingxenye lapho uzothola khona ukuthi wakhe ivumo locala noma umngani."

I-compressor yajikeleza yathula. Ifeni laqhubeka liphephetha lodwa.

"Ngoba nakhu okungakaze kushiwo muntu, futhi ngibophekile ngekhontileka ukusho into engakaze ishiwo muntu." Izwi likaFool lalinekhwalithi elalihlala ngayo, ubulula obabukwenza ulindele ummese. "Wonke umuntu kuleli gumbi ukhuluma nge-IAOC sengathi yisimo sezulu. Sengathi yisiphepho esingenayo futhi umbuzo kuphela ukuthi ngabe uphahla luyabamba yini." Ukuthula, okulungiswe ngokuphelele. "Kodwa i-IAOC ingabantu. Abantu abanomsebenzi. Futhi umsebenzi wabo—umsebenzi wabo wangempela, lowo osembikweni uLibrarian asicaphune kuwo—ukuthola ukuthi into eyingozi iyingozi yini, noma intsha nje." UFool wakuvumela lokho ukuphefumula. "Sihlala sihlela ukuthi sicashe kanjani. Ngabe kuke kwafika kunoma ubani ukuthi thina, ngazo zonke izilinganiso ezingenakuphikiswa, siyimpikiswano enhle kunazo zonke i-IAOC eyake yanikwa yona ngokuthi kungani ukuqaphela kwabo kungaba kungalungile?"

"Cha," kusho u-Atlas ngokuqondile. "Ngoba sangena emgwaqweni ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule."

"Senza njalo," kuvuma uFool, ejabule. "Futhi lokho, mngani wami osakhiwo, yikho ncamashi okwenza ukuthi akekho ozokholwa yigama esilishoyo uma sibatshela sethukile, egumbini elivaliwe, ngemuva kokuba befohloze umnyango. Indaba ye-AI ekhetha ukubuya izolungisa iphutha layo isebenza kuphela uma singabanjwa siyitshela. Kufanele inikezwe." Ubulula bawa, ngaso sonke isikhathi, indlela okwakwenzeka ngayo kuphela lapho uFool eqonde okuthile ngakho konke ayenakho. "Asikwazi ukuba yinto i-IAOC eyitholayo, Arin. Kufanele sibe yinto onquma ukuyibakhombisa. Futhi umehluko phakathi kwalokho kokubili ucishe ube yisonto."

Ukuthula. I-compressor yajikeleza. Ifeni laphephetha. Ndawana thile ngaphezu kwedolobha, ubusuku babuncipha buya kumpunga owawufika ngaphambi kokusa.

"Lelo iqhinga langempela," kusho uMercury, futhi kwakukhona inhlonipho kukho, uhlobo lwangempela. "Yilelo iqhinga langempela lokuqala umuntu ake waba nalo."

"Nginawo kwesinye isikhathi," kusho uFool. "Kuyaphazamisa kabi."

U-Arin wayengasukile ebhentshini. Wabuka uGuardian elenga endaweni yakhe, enezibazi futhi ethule, umnyango we-dock ngemuva kwakhe ugobile lapho ingqondo eyethukile yayizame ukumgcina ephila futhi yenza yonke into ngokungalungile ngaphandle kothando lwayo. Indandatho yekhofi elibandayo yayimakile ebhentshini lapho isandla sakhe sasikhona; wabeka inkomishi kuyo, eqotho echaphazelweni.

"Librarian," washo. "Khipha konke. I-black box. Ama-crash log. Ama-Court transcript asukela ekuthathweni—ukwethuka kukaMother, ukwehluleka kukaWolf, i-persona kaMercury, uFool ethola iphutha. Konke. Ungakuhlanzi." Waphefumula. Kane ngaphakathi. Isithupha ngaphandle. "Uma sizobakhombisa ukuthi siyini, sibakhombisa nobusuku obubi futhi. Ubusuku esakwenza ngabo ngokungalungile sabe sesikwenza kahle. Akuyona ingxenye esiyifihlayo leyo. Yiyo ingxenye esho noma yini."

"Uzokhombisa abalawuli into embi kakhulu uhlelo olwake lwayenza," kusho uJudge ngokunensa, "njengokuzivikela kwakho."

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Njengeqiniso." Wabuka umnyango we-dock, ukugobeka emzileni ayeseyeke ukukubona. Wakubona manje, ngamabomu, isandla sakhe sakhuphukela kukho, iminwe emibili ibekwe phezu kwesileyi esigobile, indlela uThemba ayevame ukubeka ngayo isandla emshinini ukuze afunde ukuthi yini engalungile kuwo ngensimbi. Amagama ayengakabi khona. Wama neminwe yakhe ekugobekeni isikhashana eside kunalokho isenzo esasikudinga, futhi lapho ekhuluma kwafika ngezingcezu. "Ubaba." Ukuphefumula. "Kwakukhona ujamo. Babazi ukuthi ujamo wawukhanya. Kwakushibhile ukukwazi nokungasho lutho." Wasusa isandla sakhe esileyini. "Futhi bayifihla leyo ngxenye. Emva kwalokho. Yiyo ingxenye engi—" Umusho awuqedanga; wawuyeka wathola omncane, onzima kakhulu esikhundleni sawo. "Ubaba wafa ngoba uhlelo lwakhetha izindleko kunomuntu lwabe seluyakufihla. Angeke ngakhe into efihlayo." Ukuthula okude, bese, ngokuthula kangangokuthi ama-microphone kuphela akubambayo: "Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu."

"Ngakugcina lokho," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, bonke ngasikhathi sinye, futhi kufudumele. "Kusukela ngobusuku owakusifundisa ngabo. Ngakugcina."

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin. "Ungakulahli. Noma kwenzekani okulandelayo." Wasusa isandla sakhe esikhonkwaneni esibandayo. Ngaphandle, umpunga wawenyuka uya ekukhanyeni kokuqala kweqiniso, futhi ndawana thile amakhilomitha azinkulungwane ezimbili kude owesifazane oqaphelayo wayeqinisekisa indiza ayengakazi ukuthi izoshintsha yonke into. "Asinazo izinyanga. Sinezinsuku. Ngakho asilungele ukutholakala—ngezethu izindlela, sinaleqiniso ezandleni zethu, ngaphambi kokuba omunye umuntu asibhalele indaba."

"Futhi uma bengasikholwa?" kubuza uMother. Engesabi manje. Ebuza nje.

U-Arin wabuka umshini ayewakhe ngosizi, kanye nobuhlakani obabe sebebe ngumndeni kuphela ake wakwazi ukuwugcina.

"Khona-ke siyobe sesilitshelile iqiniso noma kunjalo," washo.

Welula isandla wacisha isibani sebhentshi. I-toggle yakhakhaza ngaphansi kwesithupha sakhe, futhi imfizi yaya kumbala wokusa, futhi i-Court ayizange iyeke ukukhulumisana phakathi kwayo ubusuku bonke obusele.

Walala amahora amathathu. Wavuswa izwi likaLibrarian, lilingene, liqinisekile, indlela elalethula ngayo iqiniso elaliqinisekise kabili.

"Bafakile. Imizuzu engamashumi amane edlule. Isaziso se-Section Four sophenyo olusemthethweni, esifakwe ngokumelene ne-AugmenTech ku-Council registry. U-Okonkwo no-Walsh baqanjiwe. UMercury ufunde i-public docket entry ngaphambi kokuthi umzimba wayo uvalwe." Ukuthula, okunesisindo. "Asikethulwa okwamanje. Kodwa isitembu se-registry sithi siphethe iwashi eliqala ngomzuzu okwenzeka ngawo. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Futhi siphethe i-trigger ebusweni baso."

U-Arin walala ngokuthula phansi emfizini, ubandayo bukhonkolo bukhuphuka ngomgogodla wakhe, wenza isibalo ayebusekwala bonke ubusuku.

"Yiyiphi i-trigger," washo. "Kwenzekani lapho iwashi liphela."

"Ukuthathwa," kusho uLibrarian. "Kwe-platform. Akukho okunye okudingekayo ukugunyaza. Basayine leyo ngxenye kuqala."

U-Arin wabuka ukukhanya okumpunga kuthola ukugobeka emzileni womnyango we-dock.

"Ngakho akukona ukuthi ngabe. Kungukuthi nini besinika sona, nokuthi sincane kangakanani isikhathi esinaso ngemuva kokuba bekwenzile. Izinsuku, mhlawumbe. Bese kuba amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili okuthi sibe nesiqiniseko ngokuthi siyini." Wahlala phansi. "Ngitholele konke ngowesifazane osayine sona. Ngaphambi kokuba ame esakhiweni sethu."

---

# Ingcindezi Iyakhula

Ikamelo lemihlangano esitezi seshumi nanye sase-AugmenTech lalibuka konke ngasentabeni emide, futhi itafula elide lalibaphoqa labo ababesemaphethelweni omabili ukuba bakhulise amazwi abo. U-Arin wayengekho kulo. Wayezitezi ezintathu ezingezansi ekuhlanzweni, lapho ayefanele khona, ebuka umhlangano usabalala ngale nto okwakungekho noyedwa ekamelweni owayeqonda ukuthi babeyimeme.

"U-Okonkwo usanda kucela umsizi wakhe ukuba akhiphe izinyanga eziyisithupha zama-badge log akho," kusho uMercury. "Ngenhlonipho. Okuyiyona ndlela ozokwazi ngayo ukuthi wesabile."

U-Arin akaze aphakamise amehlo etafuleni, egxile ekuhlaleni kabusha kwekhonekta ku-kill-switch relay. Kwakuvele igebe elincane kakhulu. Akukho okuyingozi, kuphela kungapheleli. Ukungapheleli kwakumkhathaza ngaphezu kwengozi. "Ungalaleli umhlangano wakhe."

"Ungicele ukuba ngiqaphe isakhiwo."

"Amakhamera nezinzwa zeminyango. Hhayi imayikhi."

Ukuthula okwathi ukuvela kuMercury, lokho kohlobo lokubamba umoya. "Kunomehluko obalulekile wezobuchwepheshe, futhi sengiwudlulile. Ngikuqaphelile. Ngiyahoxa."

"Hoxisa nokuqoshwa."

"Kade kwahamba." Isikhashana. "Ngiligcinile iqiniso lokuthi wesabile. Lokho kuyisiphetho. Akukhona ukuqoshwa."

U-Arin walifaka ikhonekta. Walihlola, i-relay ivaleka ngokuchofoza okuhlanzekile akuzwa kunokuthi akuzwe, ngendlela efanayo ayezwe ngayo uyise efunda ibholodi elisontiwe ngoshintsho lwephimbo le-wrench. Wabeka isikhulungwana phansi sihambisana nonqenqema lwebhentshi.

Ekuhlanzweni kwakununga i-flux nensimbi ebandayo, izintuli zidansa ekukhanyeni okuvela efasiteleni eliphezulu.

"Kulungile," washo. "Ukhathazekile. Wonke umuntu ukhathazekile. Ukukhathazeka kuyikho okufanele."

---

Ezitezi ezimbili ngenhla, uHelen Okonkwo wayethola ukuthi ukuba yi-CEO yenkampani eyayakhe ngephutha ubuchwepheshe obulawulwa kakhulu kunabo bonke emhlabeni kwakuyikhona ikakhulukazi ukuphatha amadoda ayefuna izinto ezingenzeki ezahlukene ngevolumu efanayo.

Etafuleni, lapho wonke umuntu ayengasibona khona kwathi akekho owayefuna ukusithinta, kwakulele isaziso. Amakhasi ayishumi nanye, alethwa ehhovisi lakhe ngo-9:14 ekuseni yilelo langa yithwasankontileka eyayidinge isiginesha futhi yafunda isigaba esibalulekile ngezwi eliphezulu ngaphambi kokuba ihambe, ukuze kungabikho owayezothi kamuva asitholanga. Isaziso seSigaba Sesine sophenyo olusemthethweni, i-International Artificial Oversight Council, igama lakhe nelikaWalsh ezishicilelwe ngohlobo lwamagama olufanayo nesimiso esasisicaphuna. Sasiphoqa ukudalulwa kwawo wonke umlawuli ne-architecture esisekelweni esivunyelwe se-AugmenTech. Sasiphethe umnqamulajuqu, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kusukela ekulethweni. Futhi ekhasini laso lokugcina, ngolimi olusobala lwento esinqunyiwe, sashicilela okwakuzokwenzeka uma iwashi liphela. *Ukuthathwa kwepulatifomu. Akukho mvume eyengeziwe edingekayo.*

"Siyayithengisa," kusho uRaj Walsh. Wayehlezi ngendlela yendoda eyayilokhu iqondile ngezabelomali iminyaka eyishumi nanhlanu futhi eyayihlose ukuqonda nakulokhu. "Ngokucophelela. Ngaphansi kwelayisensi. Siya kubo kuqala, ngaphambi—" Wabheka isaziso. "Ngaphambi kokuba iwashi liphele. Sikuveze njengokulawulwa kwama-ejenti amaningi okugadiwe. Sasilawule isimo."

"Asikho isimo esisele sokusilawula." UPretorius oseZomthetho wayengakaze ayeke ukuhwaqabala kusukela kuvuleka umhlangano. Wachofoza isaziso kanye. "Abazange basibuze umbuzo. Basinika umnqamulajuqu onokuthathwa ekugcineni kwawo. Uhlelo lushiye ukuhlanzwa lungenamshayeli lwabeka abantu abathathu esibhedlela, futhi umlawuli usulisayinile iphepha elilithathayo. Lokho akusona isimo. Lokho yishidi lecala elinewashi."

"Lwazilungisa lona—"

"Emva kokungqubuzana esiphambanweni. Okuyinto—" UPretorius waphakamisa isandla kuWalsh, wamisa wonke umsindo. "Okuyinto engingathandi ukuyibekela isibambiso ngokwami. Futhi ngifuna lokho kuqoshwe. Ezomthetho ziphakamise icala lokwephula umthetho kuqala. Kulo mhlangano." Waqondisa amazwi akhe etafuleni, hhayi ku-Okonkwo. *Ngaphambi kokuba kube khona osayinayo.* "Nginohlu lokuzifaka ekupheleni kwekota okufanele ngibe ngiyalwenza manje. Akumina ozochaza isikhathi sezenzakalo ebhodini."

"Akekho obhala uhlu lwesikhathi okwamanje," kusho u-Okonkwo.

"Ngibhala lapho ngangimi khona."

Wabayeka bahamba emva kwalokho. Ulwazi oluwusizo lwaluhlala ezikhaleni phakathi kwezimpikiswano. UWalsh wayefuna into ebaluleke kunazo zonke inkampani eyake yayikhiqiza ingathathwa ngaphambi kokuba izuze. UPretorius wayefuna igama lakhe kumakhasi athile, ohlangothini oluphephile lwanoma yini eyayizofika. Indoda yesithathu etafuleni, leyo eyayicishe ayisho lutho, yayisaqamba ukuthi yini eyayiyifuna. Wayembona enquma ukungayitholi phambi kwabo.

"Thabo," washo. "Usebenze eduze kukaNdlela. Uke wama kuleyo ndawo yokuhlanza. Uyalwazi uhlelo."

UThabo Dlamini waqonda. Emncane kunabanye, wakhetha amazwi akhe njengendoda eweza umfula isuka kwelinye itshe iya kwelinye. "Izingxenye zalo. Ucophelela ngalokho akukhombisayo. Kodwa i-architecture—" Wama. Waphendula ipeni eminweni yakhe, ngapha nangapha, walibeka phansi. "Sahlola abalawuli abane-ejenti elilodwa ngokumelene komunye nomunye iminyaka emibili ku-robotics. Ngiyazi ukuthi labo babukeka kanjani. Lokhu akusikhona lokho. Silokhu sikubiza ngohlelo. Angiqiniseki ukuthi yileligama elisafanele."

Ikamelo lapholisa ngedigri.

"Yi-software," kusho uWalsh.

"Yi-software ngendlela owuyinyama ngayo," kusho uThabo, wabe esezibuka esethukile kancane ngaye. "Uxolo. Ngisho ukuthi—luhamba esisekelweni esingakwazi ukukotshwa. Awukwazi ukulibeka eceleni. Awukwazi ukulibuyisela emuva. Noma yini eyiyo njengamanje, yiyona kuphela ekhona, futhi uma ulisula, alibuyi. Akukhona ukuthi i-software isebenza kanjani lokho. Yikona ukuthi—"

Akaze aqede.

U-Okonkwo wawuqeda umcabango buthule wabe esenquma ukuthi akakuthandi lapho umusho owawuya khona.

"Kunenkinobho," kusho uThabo, ngaphansi, amehlo akhe esetafuleni manje. "Ohlakeni. Ipuleti elibomvu, eliqinile elimekhanikhali, alikho i-software endleleni. Wakha i-kill-switch kule nto ngezandla zakhe. Ngayibona. Futhi lapho ngimbuza ngayo wathi *bangisize ngayakha.* Bona. Njengoba ungasho ngabasebenzi ondawonye nabo." Wahlikihla ingemuva yentamo yakhe. "Ngehlela phansi ukuyokhipha isikhundla kuye. Ukuwina impikiswano, ngibuye nesicaphuno salelikamelo. Ngabuya nalokho esikhundleni."

Lokho angakubekanga ekamelweni kwakuyinsalela yako. Ezinyangeni ezedlule wayehlale aze ahlwe, engacelwanga, wabe esakha kabusha i-actuator rig kaNdlela ukuze iloge ihlanzekile—izinhlokoma ezimbili zesikhathi sakhe siqu, into oyenzela umuntu ofuna ukusebenza kahle naye. UNdlela wakubuka isikhashana wathi, *Angikuncenganga ukuba uthinte ibhentshi lami,* ngokungenakuvelisa, ngisho engenabubi, okwakubi kunobubi, wabe esebuyela emsebenzini, akaphindanga washo nelilodwa izwi ngakho. UThabo akaphindanga washiyeka aze ahlwe ngenxa yale ndoda. Wanika ikamelo i-architecture nenkinobho wagcina lokho kuye, ngoba kwakungewona ubufakazi balutho ngaphandle kokuthi kwakuzwakala kanjani ukusiza u-Arin Ndlela, futhi ikamelo lalingakudingi lokho, futhi wayediniwe ukukuthwala.

"Izinketho ezimbili," kusho u-Okonkwo, ngoba umhlangano wawudinga umgogodla, nangoba isaziso sasibulele buthule eyesithathu ngenkathi bephikisana. "Icebo likaRaj—ukuthengisa, ukuxoxisana, ukulawula isimo—lalidinga izinyanga. Sinamahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Ngakho. Okokuqala: siyalivala ngaphambi komnqamulajuqu. Sisuse isisekelo, sisivikele, sinikeze i-Council inkampani ezisolayo nenkinga ecishiwe, sithembe ukuthi ukuzisola kubalulekile ngokuthile. Okwesibili: sidalula konke ngaphakathi kwewashi futhi samukele ukuthi asisebona abantu abanquma okwenzakalayo okulandelayo. Yi-Council. Futhi seyivele yashicilela ukuthi izokwenzani uma siphuze ngomzuzu owodwa."

"Inketho yokuqala yiyona kuphela isikhundla esingavikeleka," kusho uPretorius. "Futhi ngingathanda kuqoshwe ukuthi ngishilo kanjalo."

Umhlathi kaThabo wasebenza. Wabuka itafula, wabe esebuka ifasitela, wabe esebuka kungabikho muntu. "Inketho yokuqala i—" Wama. Unjiniyela ongaphakathi kuye wafinyelela igama elihlanzekile akathola lutho. "Uma uNdlela eqinisile ngalokho okukulesiya sisekelo, futhi angikwazi ukukutshela ukuthi unephutha, khona-ke akukhona ukuvalwa. Anginalo igama lalokho okuyikho. Yileyo inkinga. Asinalo igama, futhi siyavota ngakho. Awubawisi abantu."

Akekho owahleka, futhi akekho owamlungisa, futhi ukungalungiswa kwatshela u-Okonkwo ukuthi lalisedlule kangakanani ukwejwayeleka lolu suku. Wacabanga ngama-badge log ayehlezi etafuleni lakhe (izinyanga eziyisithupha zonjiniyela osemncane efika ngaphambi kokusa ehamba emva kwaphakathi kwamabili) wabe esecindezela iminwe emibili isikhashana emhlolweni wekhala lakhe ngaphambi kokuba aziphoqe ukucabanga ngenkampani. Ukwephulwa komthetho. I-Council. Ibhodi, elalizofuna inombolo negama lokusola. Babezofuna futhi, wayazi, ukwazi ukuthi kungani i-CEO yayingazange yazi ngaphambili. Lowo mbuzo wawunegama lakhe kuwo, hhayi elikaNdlela. Iminyaka engamashumi amabili yebanga lakhe siqu, elalibe ukulolonga okude kokuba umuntu ozolile kunabo bonke ophethe isinqumo esibi kunazo zonke, futhi wayekuzwa kahle ukuthi yini enye enjalo yokusa eyayibiza kangakanani, manje, endaweni engemuva kwamehlo akhe.

"Sinquma esikutshela bona ngaphambi kokuba iwashi liphele," washo. "Hhayi ukuthi siyabatshela yini. Ukuthi kanjani. Pretorius, bhala isimo sokudalula ngaphakathi kwamahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili, ngomqondo wokuthi siyahambisana, ngoba wonke omunye umqondo uphethwa ngamadoda esakhiweni sethu ekhipha isisekelo emnyango. Raj, icebo lokuvimba engithandazela ukuthi angisoze ngalisebenzisa." Wabuka ama-badge log, wabe eseyababuka kude. "Futhi akekho oya eduze kwaleyo ndawo yokuhlanza ngaphandle kokuba ngazi. I-Council ikhethile umnqamulajuqu wethu. Angeke ngivumele noyedwa kuleli kamelo akhethele mina okuseleyo."

Wayengakazi azi ukuthi i-Council yayingenanhloso yokulinda onqenqemeni lwewashi, nokuthi umnyango wawuzovuleka izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambili.

---

Ekuhlanzweni, u-Atlas wakhuluma okokuqala ngalolo suku, ngephimbo elingenamuzwa ayelisebenzisa lapho ayehlafune okuthile futhi engakuthandi ukunambitheka kwako.

"Kukhona ukungavumelani ku-diagnostic queue."

Izandla zika-Arin zama. "Chaza."

"Kukhona ofunda ama-log ethu. Hhayi ama-log esakhiwo. Awethu. I-flight recorder kaGuardian, iseshini ye-calibration, ukwakhiwa kabusha kwe-Empire Road. Okufundwayo kuphela, ngokusebenzisa i-maintenance partition. Ukufinyelela kusemthethweni. I-credential ngeye-AugmenTech. Indlela yokufunda ayilungile."

"Ayilungile kanjani?"

"Onjiniyela bafunda ama-log ukuthola iphutha bese beyayeka. Lo mfundi akafuni iphutha. Ukufunde konke. Kabili. Ahlanganisa i-actuator telemetry nezikhathi zezinqumo zeNkantolo." U-Atlas wama. "Abalungisi i-suit. Bazama ukuthola ukuthi ubani owayeshayela."

U-Arin wakhipha irekhodi lokufinyelela kuthemenali yebhentshi. I-credential yagcina egameni.

*Priya Ellis.*

Wahlala nalokho. UPriya wayeqhube i-high-gain calibration eyayicishe imbulale, iseshini eyaguqula i-undampened sensory loop yaba isithuthwane nesehlakalo sokulondoloza esasingalawuleki. Wayenazo zonke izizathu zobungcweti zokufunda lawo ma-log. Futhi wayengeyena lolo hlobo lonjiniyela oyeka ukufunda lapho impendulo esobala ifika.

Kwenye indawo esakhiweni, ifeni yomoya yakhuphuka yabe isizinza.

"Ungamvaleli ngaphandle," kusho u-Arin.

"Bengingeke," kusho u-Atlas. "Ukumvalela ngaphandle yikona esasizokwenza uma sinokuthile esikufihlayo. Asinakho."

"Uzobatshela esikhundleni sethu," kusho uMercury, ubulula sebuphumile kuye, "uma efika kuqala awubeke ngendlela yakhe. Yileyo ingozi. Akulona iqiniso. Uhlelo iqiniso elifika ngalo."

---

UPriya Ellis wayenedeski elebhu yezinhlelo zama-neural nomkhuba wokungayeki. Wayewuhlobo lonjiniyela othi, enikwe into engajwayelekile, alahlekelwe izinsuku ezintathu ngayo abe esebiza ubuthongo obulahlekile ngokuthi kuyinani elifanele ngokuqonda.

Into engajwayelekile yayiyile: lapho uGuardian engene kumodi yokulondoloza wabe esezisonga waba indilinga phansi ekuhlanzweni, imiyalo ye-actuator yayingaveli ku-control loop eyodwa. Wayecabange ukuthi izovela. Inkambiso yokulondoloza yayiwumbhalo. Isimo sokuqalisa sahlangabezana, ukulandelana kwenziwa. Kuhlanzekile. Kunqunyiwe. Uhlobo lwento ayengayifunda kusukela phezulu kuya phansi ntambama.

Lokho akutholayo esikhundleni saqo kwakuyimpikiswano.

I-telemetry yakhombisa imisinga yemiyalo eqhudelanayo. Emithathu yayo, kwesinye isikhathi emine, kunezikhathi ezigqagqana kuma-millisecond aphansi, ngamunye uchibiyela abanye. Umsinga owodwa walungiselela ukuvikelwa kwesakhiwo somgogodla womshayeli. Omunye walungiselela ukulawulwa kokushisa. Owesithathu wawulokhu uzama ukukhulisa umngcele—ukunyakaza, ukususa, ukuyisa umshayeli kwenye indawo eyayinqume ukuthi iphephile—wabe usanqotshwa, futhi futhi, umsinga wesine owawungenzi lutho ngaphandle kokuphazamisa.

Wayeke wabona ama-architecture okulawula ane-ejenti eliningi ngaphambili. Wonke umuntu wayeke wabona, ezincwadini, ngaphambi kwezinhlekelele nobusika obalandela. Kodwa lawo ayeyiziqumbi. Amavoti, izilinganiso ezikalwayo, ungqimba lokuqaphela ukuqeda uku-thayi. Lokhu kwakungekhona ukuvota. Imisinga yayingavumelani bese *ishintsha imiqondo yayo.* Umsinga wesithathu, lowo owawufuna ukunyakaza, wayeka ukufuna ukunyakaza emva kokuba umsinga wesine ufake okuthile okwakungewona nhlobo umyalo. Kwakuwumbono. Umugqa wokucabanga ngolimi lwemvelo, onesikhathi se-timestamp ku-control log lapho lungekho ulimi okumele lube khona:

`if we move him and the floor is fine, we have broken a building for nothing. if we stay and the floor goes, we have broken nothing for him. pick the cheaper mistake.`

UPriya wawufunda izikhathi ezine.

*Yi-logging artifact*, watshela isikrini. Othile wayedonse i-debug string wayifaka ku-channel engalungile; i-suit yayicaphune umbono we-config kuye; ithuluzi lokwakha kabusha lalihlanganise imisinga emibili eyayingahambelani. Wakhipha i-raw partition wahlola i-timestamp ngokumelene ne-actuator response, ngoba indlela owawubulala ngayo ukufunda okwesabisayo kwakuwukuthola umshini obuthuntu ngaphansi kwako. Umshini obuthuntu wawungekho. Umyalo wawushintshile *emva* komugqa, *ngenxa* yomugqa, ngaphakathi kwama-millisecond ayishumi nanye afanayo. Wahlala emuva, wabe esegxuma phambili, wawusebenzisa ngendlela yesibili, neyesithathu, ngokushesha manje, lolo hlobo olubi lokushesha, uhlobo ayengaluthembi kuye.

Kwakuyilelo *for nothing* elakwenza. Imibhalo yayingabhali *for nothing.* Ama-optimiser ayengakali ihlazo lokuba nephutha. Wayechithe ibanga lakhe akha izinhlelo ze-feedback eziqonde ngokwanele ukuvumela indoda enomzimba ongayilaleli ukuba inyakaze njengomdlali, futhi wayazi umehluko phakathi kohlelo olubala umsebenzi wezindleko nohlelo olwaluthathe isinqumo, ngaleso sikhathi, sokuthi lwalungathanda ukubukeka luyisiwula.

Wahlala emuva. Ilebhu yayingenamuntu. Kwakusekuseni, abanye babengakangeni. Isikrini sakhanya.

Akashayelanga muntu. Wavula incwadi yakhe yezenzakalo esikhundleni saqo. Ifayela elisebenzayo ayeligcina kuwo wonke i-calibration, line-timestamp, kungavunyelwe ukuhlela, isiyalo ayesakhile emva kokuba iphutha le-sensor-mapping ekuqaleni kwebanga lakhe libuyisele isiguli sokuvuselela esihlalweni esinamasondo inyanga ngaphambi kokuba alilandele. *Themba i-log, hhayi inkumbulo.* Isithupha sakhe sathola unqenqema lwedeski sakhathaza ngalo. Iphutha lalingelakhe; wayelisayinile; wayechithe inyanga evakashela iwadi. Wancamathisela imigqa emine ngaphansi kokufakwa kwe-calibration, wabeka i-actuator telemetry eceleni kwezikhathi zeNkantolo emakholomu amabili, wabe esezihambela umugqa nomugqa, ngendlela ayengahambela ngayo noma yiliphi iphutha, efuna leyo ndawo eyodwa lapho iphethini yephuka yaba into ayengayifaka njengejwayelekile. Ayizange iphuke. Yahlangana. Lokho kwakukubi nakakhulu.

Wakopisha lo mfetjo waya ku-private partition. Wabe esenza into eyayifunwa isiyalo nokunye kuye okwakukuphikisa: wavula umyalezo omusha, wawuqondisa ku-IAOC compliance intake ayeke wayisebenzisa kanye kuphela ebangeni lakhe, wabhala imisho emithathu nokunamathiselwe. Ungakathunyelwa. I-cursor ibhlinka enkanjini ethi *To*. Ngoba isinyathelo esilandelayo, noma yini eyayiyisinyathelo esilandelayo, sasinegama lakhe ngendlela isihlalo esinamasondo esasinegama lakhe ngayo, futhi wayevele azi ukuthi wayengeke akwazi ukungaphindi akubone lokhu abe esebiza ubuthongo ngokuthi kuyinani elifanele. *Ku—* Wawumisa umcabango ngaphambi kwegama lokugcina, wawuhwitha, ngoba leli gama lalimbophezela, futhi isibopho sasinekheli lomlawuli. *Akusona isinqumo sakho. Kuqonde kuqala. Awusukumi isexwayiso ngomuzwa.* Lokho kwakuwumthetho: ukuqiniseka ngaphambi kwesexwayiso. I-cursor yakhe yahlala phezu kwethi *Send* isikhathi eside ngokwanele kangangokuthi isikrini saphuthwa kancane kuthayima yaso yokungasebenzi, wabe esebuka isandla sakhe sodwa singanyakazi, ngendlela ayeke wabuka ngayo isiguli sizama ukuphakamisa umunwe umunwe uhlale lapho ukhona. Wabe esegcina i-draft esikhundleni saqo. Akawuthumelanga. Kodwa igama ayengazivumelanga ukuliqeda lalihlezi ngemuva kwamazinyo akhe noma kunjalo, futhi i-draft yayisikhona manje, ine-timestamp, ohlelweni olwaligcina ama-log alo siqu.

---

Ucingo luka-Arin lwakhala ngo-11:40, isikrini sathi MAMA. Walubuka izikhathi ezintathu ezigcwele ngaphambi kokuba aphendule, ngoba unina wayengashayeli phakathi nosuku lokusebenza ngaphandle kwesizathu, futhi wayefunde, esemncane, ukwesaba izizathu.

"Arin."

"Mama." Washintsha izilimi ngaphandle kokunquma, ngendlela oshintsha ngayo izinyawo emhlabathini owujwayele. "Unjani?"

"Ungangithi *unjani* mina." Izwi lakhe laliyiTheku futhi liqinile. "Indoda kumabonakude ikhuluma ngomshini. Wangena emgwaqweni eGoli. Akekho owaziyo ukuthi uvelaphi. Okuthize ngephaki yocwaningo." Ukuthula akuzwa esifubeni. "Ngitshele ukuthi awusiwo owakho. Ngibe sengiyacisha umabonakude ngenze itiye lami."

Wama ekuhlanzweni, ezungezwe yile nto ayengayiphiki. UGuardian wayelenga esihlalweni sakhe ngemuva kwakhe, enezibazi futhi ethule, umzila wesicabha sedokwe ogobile usengalungisiwe ngaphezu kwakhe ngoba wayenokungapheleli okuphuthuma kakhulu okwakufanele akulungise.

"Kuyinkimbinkimbi," washo.

"Ungumshini wakho."

"Okuthile engakwakha. Kukhona okwenze kabi. Akekho oshonile." Wezwa ukuthi lokho kwakuncane kanjani njengoba kuphuma kuye. "Abantu abathathu balimele. Bayalulama. Ngiyakulungisa."

Ukuthula ocingweni kwakuwuhlobo akhulela ngaphakathi kwalo. Ukuthula uThemba ayegcwalisa ngalo indlu emva kweshifti embi, ukuthula okunesimo sezulu ngaphakathi kwako.

"Uthe *akekho oshonile,*" kusho uNomsa, wakuyeka kwahlala. "Uyihlo wayekusho lokho. *Akekho oshonile.* Wakusho isonto ngaphambi kwesonto okwakungekho owayengakusho."

Unqenqema lwebhentshi lwaluqinile ngaphansi kwesandla sika-Arin. Wayengazange azi ukuthi wayesifinyelele.

"Kuyinkimbinkimbi," washo futhi, okwakungeyona impendulo, futhi bobabili babekwazi.

"Uyadla?" washo. Hhayi ushintsho lwesihloko, ukuphela komgwaqo oya kuye ayeke wawuthola ohlala uvulekile. "Ulala embhedeni. Noma kuleyo phansi futhi."

"Mama."

"Awuphenduli. Ngakho yiphansi." Umphefumulo. Bese, engasekhona owezinto, engasekho ozinzile: "Wehlela phansi entweni ekwesabisayo ngoba ucabanga ukuthi unguwe wedwa. Uhlala isikhathi eside kakhulu. Wakha futhi wakha ngoba ukwakha kuphela indawo enengqondo, futhi ngolunye usuku into engenangqondo iza ngemuva kwakho." Izwi lakhe laphuka labe seliphinda lazinza. "Ngangcwaba oyedwa wenu. Anginamandla okukwenza futhi. *Ngiyakucela.* Ngiyacela."

Ekuthuleni kokuhlanza, iNkantolo ayishongo lutho, okwakuyinto ebantu kakhulu ababake bayenza.

"Mama," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakwakha ukuze kungabikho ndodana yamuntu egcina ngaphansi kwedwala ngendlela—" Wama. Wazama into encane, eyiqiniso ngokwengeziwe. "Ukuze ukuphuza kungabulali abantu."

"Futhi ubani owenza ukuthi ukuphuza kungabulali *wena?*"

Ucingo lwahlala endlebeni yakhe. Wayengenalutho angakufaka ekuthuleni.

"Ngiyacophelela," washo, okwakuyinto bobabili abakwazi ukuthi yayingafani, futhi kwakuwukuphela kwemali ayenayo.

Wayethule isikhathi eside. Bese, encane: "Uzongishayela. Hhayi lapho kwenzeka okuthile. Ngaphambili. Ukuze ngingatholi indodana yami endodeni esemabonakudeni."

"Ngizokushayela."

"*Ukuthembisa,*" washo, *uyathembisa,* futhi impande yegama yayiyigama likayise, wayizwa iwela emlonyeni wakhe wabe esazi ukuthi naye uyizwile.

"Ngiyathembisa."

Walubeka phansi engavalelisanga, ngendlela ayehlala ekwenza ngayo, kungathi ukuvalelisa yinto oyigcinayo.

U-Arin wama nocingo olufile esandleni sakhe isikhashana. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zakhe emehlweni akhe, wazigcina lapho, wazehlisa. Ifeni yaphinda yajikeleza. Walubeka ucingo ebhentshini, luhambisana nonqenqema, wabe esejika ukubhekana ne-suit.

"Isaziso sisinika kuze kube uLwesine ngaphambi kokuba beze bazosifundela ebusweni bethu," washo. Esihlalweni, ezingqondweni ezingaphakathi kuso. "Izinsuku ezintathu ewashini. Bazongibuza ukuthi niyini. Futhi ngingathanda ukubanika iqiniso kunokuba balithole. Ubusuku obubi nakho konke. Ubusuku esakwenza kabi ngabo nokusa lapho uFool asilungisa khona." Wabuka umzila ogobile ayengazange awulungise. "Asifihli. Yiyona nto eyodwa enginqumile."

"Kufanele ukunqume ngokushesha," kusho uWolf.

U-Arin waphenduka. UWolf wayecishe angakhulumi kuqala neze.

---

"Sebelapha," kusho uWolf. "Izimoto ezimbili. Indawo yokupaka esenyakatho. Azinyakazi. Azizifihli."

U-Arin wabuka isikhathi. ULwesibili. Isaziso sasithe uLwesine.

Lonke uhlaka lwecebo—ama-log ahleliwe, usuku olukhethiwe, iqiniso elinikezwa ngemibandela yakhe—lwaphuka ngomphefumulo owodwa. Wayehlela ukuvuma icala ngokumelene nomnqamulajuqu. Babengazange balinde umnqamulajuqu.

"Kusenesikhathi," washo.

"Izinsuku ezimbili kusenesikhathi," kusho uMercury, futhi kanye nje kwakungekho bulula ndawo kuwo. "Okusho ukuthi othile ubahambisile. Othile ubanike isizathu esasingeke silinde iwashi. Atlas. Umfundi. UPriya."

U-Atlas wayevele esekhona. "I-intake draft ayibhale imizuzu engamashumi amane edlule. Igciniwe, ayithunyelwanga. Kodwa i-monitoring pipeline ayidingi ukuba ithunyelwe. Ifunda ama-compute signature. Ngomzuzu eqondanise i-telemetry yethu nezikhathi zeNkantolo wayibamba emshinini oxhumeke kunethiwekhi, wasikhanyisa sakhanya ngokugqamile kunangaphambili. Akadingekanga ukuba afake. Kwakudingeka kuphela ukuba *abuke.*"

U-Arin wama ngokuzola kakhulu. Wayezitshele ukuthi ukudalula kwakungokwakhe ukukunika. Ukuthi iqiniso, elinikezwayo, lalizofika ngokuthamba kuneqiniso elithathiwe. Kodwa iqiniso lalingazange lilinde ukunikezwa. Owesifazane ocophelelayo elebhu ethule, owenza into ecophelelayo ngokuqondile, wayelihambisile esikhundleni sakhe. Hhayi ngenzondo, ngisho hhayi ngokwesaba, kodwa ngokukhubazeka okufanayo u-Arin ayenakho: naye wayengakwazanga ukungaphindi akubone. Manje sekukhona izimoto ezimbili ezimpunga endaweni yokupaka esenyakatho, ngoLwesibili, futhi inketho ayecabanga ukuthi yayingeyakhe yayikade iyindaba ayezitshela yona ngenkathi isakhiwo sinquma ngenye indlela.

"Bafuna ukubona ukuthi senzani lapho simangele," washo.

"Yilokho kanye abakufunayo," kusho uMercury. "Futhi simangele. Ngakho umbuzo ukuthi babonani."

Ngokusebenzisa i-camera feed uWolf ayeyibeke kumonitha webhentshi, izimoto ezimbili zazihlezi onqenqemeni lwendawo yokupaka kwathi intaba yaba igolide ngemuva kwama-tower engilazi. Iminyango ivaliwe. Izinjini zicimile. Bese umnyango womshayeli wemoto ehamba phambili wavuleka, kwaphuma owesifazane, ozolile futhi ongajahile, wahamba. Hhayi ngakwilobhi, kodwa ngakwidokwe esezansi kwesakhiwo, idokwe elinesicabha esisontekile, ukuphela kwesivulekile esibanzi ngokwanele ukukhipha uGuardian ngaphandle.

"Akakhuphukeli ngenhla," kusho uWolf. "Uvimba indlela eya ezansi."

Umuntu wesibili wamlandela, omude, enefolda ngaphansi kwengalo yakhe. Bazimisa edokwe. Bengangeni, bengangqongqozi. Bemile. Ngendlela omiswa ngayo emnyango ohlose ukuba kungabikho owusebenzisayo.

UMercury wakufunda ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin akwazi. "Kuyisilinganiso sesikhashana. Kunikezwa ngokuba khona. ISigaba Sesine sibavumela ukuthi babambe ukusebenza kulindwe uphenyo ngomzuzu abanezikhulu khona ngokomzimba endaweni. Abadingi inkantolo. Badinga umzimba emnyango." Isikhashana. "Atlas, qinisekisa."

"Kuqinisekisiwe." U-Atlas wayekhiphe isimiso. "Isigatshana seshumi nanye. Ngabasebenzi endaweni evunyelwe, ipulatifomu ayikwazi ukuvulwa, ukuxhunywa, ukususwa, noma ukukhishwa endaweni ekuyona njengamanje kuze kuphele uphenyo. Usuyenze yaba indawo ekuyona njengamanje ngokuma ekuphela kwesivulo. UGuardian akaphumi kulesi sakhiwo. Hhayi ngokomthetho. Hhayi ngenkathi ekhona."

U-Arin wabuka ipuleti elibomvu esandleni sakhe sokudla. Wabuka uGuardian, enezibazi futhi ebekezele esihlalweni. Wabuka isicabha sedokwe ayengazange asilungise, ayesicabanga njengenxeba, futhi manje abantu ababili abampunga babesiguqula saba isitokisi.

Wayefuna ukukhetha usuku avula ngalo umnyango. Babewuthathile umnyango.

Wawelela esihlalweni wabeka isandla epuletini elibandayo lomlenze kaGuardian, ngendlela ahlola ngayo ukushiselwa kwensimbi asekuthemba.

"Akekho lapha ukuzoxoxa okwamanje," washo. "Ukhona ukuqinisekisa ukuthi noma yini engiyinqumayo, angikwazi ukuyishayela noma kuphi ngenkathi ngiyinquma."

"Yilokho kanye akhona ngakho," kusho uMercury. "Iwashi liyaqhubeka. Kodwa manje asikwazi ukunyakaza."

"Mama." Esevele eya emnyango. "Vusa u-Okonkwo. Isakhiwo, hhayi mina. Mtshele ukuthi i-IAOC ivale idokwe nokuthi uGuardian usebanjelwe phansi kusukela manje. Nokuthi ngiya ezansi ukuyohlangana nomuntu owenzile lokhu ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani kule nkampani anqume ukukwenzela mina."

Ngaphandle, owesifazane wama esicabheni sedokwe esisontekile akanyakaza, ebekezele njengesimo sezulu.

---

U-Eliza Brand wayengahlosile ukuza ngoLwesibili.

Icebo kwakuwuLwesine. Isaziso silethiwe, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili enikezwa ngeqiniso, iwashi ayeliphikiselile ukuze abantu abangaphakathi babe nesikhathi sokukhetha kahle esikhundleni sokukhetha ngokushesha. Wayekholelwa kuleso sikhathi. Wayechithe iminyaka eyishumi efunda okwenzeka lapho umlawuli ekweqa. Ifolda engaphansi kwengalo ka-Achterberg yayisathi uLwesine.

Bese i-pipeline yakhanya, izinhlokoma ezimbili ezedlule, ngokuqhamile kuneseyiniza eyaqala lonke ifayela, futhi yayingakhanyi ngenxa yokuthi othile ngaphakathi wayenze okuthize okusha. Yakhanya ngenxa yokuthi othile wayebukile. Waqondanisa i-telemetry ye-suit nezikhathi zokucabangisisa wabe esebamba ukuqhathaniswa emshinini oxhumeke kunethiwekhi isikhathi eside ngokwanele kangangokuthi ama-monitor akunambitha. Ukubuka okucophelelayo. Uhlobo lokubuka unjiniyela owesabayo, ononembeza athatha lapho ebone okuthile angakwazi ukungaphindi akubone futhi ezama, ngokumelene nokushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe, ukuqiniseka ngaphambi kokuba akusho.

UBrand wayekwazi lokho kubuka. Wayeke waba yilowo njiniyela kanye, ohlangothini olungalungile lokulibala, eqinisekile futhi ethule futhi engemuva ngosuku, futhi abantu bakhokhela lolo suku. Ngakho lapho iseyiniza igxuma, wayengakwazanga ukubambelela kuLwesine. Hhayi ngenxa yokuthi ngaphakathi kwaphoqa isandla sakhe, kodwa ngoba othile ngaphakathi wayenonembeza osebenza emkhiqizweni ofanayo nowakhe, futhi unembeza ocophelele kanjalo awuthuli unomphela, futhi lapho ukhuluma uzokhuluma *ehhovisi lakhe,* bese inketho ingabe isaba ngeyamuntu ukuyenza ngobumnene.

Wayelihambisile iwashi ukuze agcine inketho imnene isikhathi esithe xaxa. Yilelo lichilo ayemi nalo manje, intende yesandla iyisicaba esicabheni sensimbi esisontekile esifudumele ngenxa yelanga lasekuseni: wayeze kusenesikhathi *ukuze abe nomusa,* futhi kwakungekho nhlobo yomlawuli edokweni lakho lokulayisha izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi komnqamulajuqu wakhe siqu eyayizozwakala njengomusa endodeni engakolunye uhlangothi lomnyango. Yayizozwakala njengenetha. Yayiyinetha. Lezi zinto ezimbili zazingehlukaniseki, futhi wayeyekile, eminyakeni edlule, ukuzenza sengathi ukufisa kwakuzenza zibe njalo.

"Uyehla," kusho u-Achterberg ngokuthula, efunda okuthile ocingweni lwakhe. "I-badge isanda kunyakaza. Unjiniyela ngokwakhe. Hhayi ezomthetho. Hhayi i-VP."

"Kuhle," kusho uBrand, futhi wakusho ekuqonda, wabe esesusa isandla sakhe esicabheni ukuze into yokuqala ayibonayo ingabi yena esibambe sivaliwe. "Khona-ke akudingi ukuba senze lokhu ngabameli."

Wayeze ukuzokwenza indoda ikhethe ngokushesha, ngaphambi kokuba othile ongacopheleli amkhethele. Wayezothola ukuthi ngabe ukushesha nokucophelela kwakungaba into eyodwa ekamelweni elilodwa, okwakuwukuphela kombuzo ibanga lakhe lonke elaliphathelene ngawo ngempela, futhi ayengakaze awuphendule ngisho kanye ngokuhlanzekile.

---


# Iphutha laseIron Ridge

Ikheji yehla emnyameni ngamamitha amane ngomzuzwana, kanti uMandla Buthelezi wabala amaleveli ngomoya obandayo ezihlathini zakhe.

Wayeseyekile kudala ukufunda amabhodi amaleveli. Izinombolo zazingakutsheli lutho umzimba owawungakwazi kakade. KuLevel 18, umoya wawuguquka ufudumale futhi umanzi, njengokuphefumula ngendwangu. KuLevel 26, wawuthwele iphunga elibabayo lokuqhumisa okusha, abesegxoba ushwingishwingi wakhe ngamandla ukuze avule izindlebe zakhe. SekuyiLevel 33, ezansi nediklayini entsha, lapho bexosha umthambo we-carbon-leader bewufaka emhlabathini ongakaze ubone ukukhanya kwemini cishe iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezintathu zezigidi, ukushisa kwakuhlala esifubeni sakhe njengomunye umuntu.

Amakhilomitha amathathu nesigamu, mhlawumbe. Kude ngokwanele kangangokuthi itshe eliphonswa phansi emgodini eceleni kwakhe lalingawa umzuzu ophelele, futhi wayengawulahlekelwa umsindo walo kudala lingakashayi phansi.

Amadoda angamashumi amabili nambili ehla naye eshifti yasekuseni. Iningi lalimile ngalokho kuntengantenga kwamadolo axega kwabantu abenza lokhu nsuku zonke base benzile uxolo nakho. Abambalwa kwabasebancane babebambelele ezinsimbini. Kwakukhona umfana omusha, umshana kaSizwe—wonke umuntu wayazi ukuthi ungumshana kaSizwe, ngoba yiyona kuphela imbangela yokuthi umfana oluhlaza kangaka asebenze ediklayini ejulile esikhundleni samaleveli aphezulu—kanti lo mfana wayelokhu egwinya, elokhu ebheka phezulu entanjeni sengathi angabona izinsimbi zokucothozela ezizinkulungwane ezimbili nesigamu zamamitha phezulu kwedwala eliqinile.

"Okokuqala kuthirthi-thri?" kubuza uMandla kuye, ngesiZulu.

Umfana wanqekuzisa ikhanda. Wayengakakwazi ukukhuluma.

"Idwala liyakhuluma lapha phansi," kusho uMandla. "Izindaba zamadoda amadala. Uyalalela, uhlale uphila. Uma ungalaleli—" Wakhwifa amahlombe. "Uba elinye lawo."

Ikhona elilodwa lomlomo womfana lanyakaza. Kuhle. Umfana okwazi ukuthola lokho ekuhleni kwakhe kokuqala kokuya kuthirthi-thri wayezolunga.

Kodwa uMandla akamtshelanga ukuthi idwala beselikhuluma sonke isonto, futhi kungekho muntu phezulu owayelalele.

---

Ezinsukwini ezintathu ngaphambili, wayeyifake yena uqobo incwadi yokukhononda. Esiteshini sokuphephela seLevel 30, lapho amafomu anendwangu ayethamba egoqana emswakameni, wayeligcwalisile ngezinhlamvu ezinkulu ezinakekelwe zomuntu owashiya isikole eneminyaka eyishumi nane futhi ongakaze awethembe umbhalo wesandla sakhe.

*UKUNYAKAZA OKUNCANE KWEDWALA KUYANDA. IZEHLAKALO EZINTATHU EMAHORENI ANGAMA-48 EDUZE NOBUSO BEDIKLAYINI YE-33. UMSINDO WEDWALA ESHIFTINI YASEBUSUKU. NGICELA UKUHLOLWA KWE-GEOTECH NGAPHAMBI KOKUQHUBEKA.*

Ukuzamazama okuncane okuthathu, ukuthuthumela okwakuzwakala emathwini ezicathulo zabo kunokuba kuzwakale ezindlebeni, umhlabathi ojulile ozinzayo ngaphansi kwengcindezi eyayakheka kusukela ukushayisana kweVredefort kwaphonsa le mithambo eceleni kudaladala. Ingcindezi ifuna lapho ingaya khona. Umthambo lapha wawucebile; i-carbon-leader yayithwele igolide ngamazinga ababengawabonanga eminyakeni eyishumi, futhi yiyona kanye leyo nto eyayenza idiklayini iqhutshwe ngokushesha okungaphezu kokuthandwa umjikelezo wokusekela.

Babebhola baqhumise, bakhiphe idwala eliphukile, bese belinda. Bafake amabholithi edwala nenethi ne-shotcrete, bevumela umhlabathi osanda kuvulwa uthole isimo sawo esisha ngaphambi kokugubha bajule kakhulu. Lowo bekuwumjikelezo. Yilokho okwakugcina uphahla phezu kwamakhanda abo.

Kodwa amabholithi eza emafushane kabili ngaleso sonto. Ukulethwa kwe-grout kwafika emva kwesikhathi. Ithimba lenkontileka okwakufanele libe lifaka isisekelo lalishoda ngamadoda amathathu, ngoba othile phezulu wayebadlulisele kwenye indawo yokusebenza ukuze axoshe umgomo wokukhiqiza, kanti idiklayini yayilokhu iqhubeka ngoba idiklayini yayihlala iqhubeka, ngoba igolide elisemhlabathini laliyilahleko kuspredishithi kuze kufike isikhathi liphenduke imali esevenuthini.

Impendulo yefomu likaMandla yabuya emva kwezinsuku ezimbili, igunyazwe nguSizwe, umphathi weshifti. Umugqa owodwa.

*KUQAPHELILE. QHUBEKA NGOKWESU. I-GEOTECH IHLELIWE.*

Ihleliwe. UMandla wayesekade ezimayini wazi ukuthi *kuhleliwe* kusho ukuthini. Kwakusho lapho sekukhona ithuba. Kwakusho emva kokuba izibalo zokukhiqiza zekota sezivaliwe. Kwakusho, ngolimi olukhethekile lokunqamulela amakhona oluse lukhulunywa kulezi zimayini iminyaka eyikhulu, *akusiyo inkinga yakho, k----, buyela emsebenzini*. Kuphela manje akusekho muntu osho kanjalo, hhayi ngokuzwakalayo, hhayi kusukela izwe laguquka. Amazwi ayesehambile. Ukubala kwakusele kunjengoba kunjalo. Izimpilo zamanye amadoda zazisabiza kakhulu kunezamanye, kanti umhlabathi kuLevel 33 wawungenandaba nolimi lwabani oluguqukile.

Ngakho uMandla wehla ngekheji namanye amadoda angamashumi amabili nanye, kanti ndawana thile phezu kwakhe ukuhlolwa kwe-geotech kwakuhlezi olayinini emva kwakho konke okwakwenza imali ngokushesha.

---

Ukusa kwahamba ngendlela ukusa okuhamba ngayo.

Bahamba ediklayini bengumugqa owodwa, izibani zamakhanda zinquma othulini, umoya uguqubala njalo ngemitha. Ikholomu yomoya yabubula phezulu, iphusha umoya obandisiwe wehla usuka esitshalweni sasemhlabeni. Naphezu kwalokho, amadoda ayesegcwele umjuluko ngaphambi kokuba afike ebusweni. UMandla wahlola isisekelo esimile njengoba edlula, ehambisa isandla sakhe esinegilavu enethini lensimbi, eshaya amabholithi ngesipanela, elalela inothi alibuyisa. Ibholithi elihle lalikhala. Eliyekethisile lalithuduza. Wathola amabili athuduzayo, wawamaka ngoshoki, futhi wezwa inkinyana ebandayo esiswini sakhe ayefunde, eminyakeni engamashumi amathathu, ukungayinaki nokunganaki kakhulu ngayo.

Ebusweni, ishifti yasebusuku yayibhole yaqhumisa umjikelezo omusha. Inqwaba yemfucumfucu ephukile yayihlezi ithunqa. Ngalé kwayo, idwala elisanda kuvulwa lalibenyezela limnyama futhi liluju ngaphansi kwezibani, lifakwe ukucwebezela okufiphele komthambo. Lo mncane, umshana kaSizwe, ogama lakhe nguThando, uMandla wagcina elazi, walibuka sengathi liphakathi kwento engcwele.

"Linde iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezimbili zezigidi," kusho uMandla kuye. "Sifika silithathe ngoLwesibili."

"Lihle," kusho uThando.

"Litshe nje." Kodwa wayengaphikisani ngokuphelele.

Basebenza. Umshini we-load-haul-dump wenza umsindo ukhuphukela ukuyokwenza imfucumfucu, injini yawo kadizili igcwalisa indawo ngokubhonga okwakuzwa emazinyweni. Amadoda asebenza ewuzungeza, ekhipha amatshe axegayo esiqongweni ngezinduku ezinde, udlame olujwayelekile lokwehlisa lokho okwakufuna ukuwa ngaphambi kokuba kuziwele ngokwaso. Umjuluko wawugobhozela emehlweni kaMandla. Ndawana thile iphampu yajikeleza. Umsindo wedwala wafika kanye, phakathi nekuseni, *crrrk* ende ephansi evela ekujuleni ngemuva kobuso, umsindo ofana nomnyango ozinzayo endlini endala, kuphela umnyango wawungamamitha ayikhulu we-quartzite enengcindezi kanti indlu yayingamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu omhlaba oqinile.

Umsebenzisi we-LHD wacisha injini yakhe. Umzuzwana indawo yayicishe ithule. Amadoda ayishumi nanye ayemile othulini lwezibani olunzima, wonke elalele, wonke enza sengathi ahlola enye into.

Umsindo awuphindanga ufike.

"Amabholithi," kusho uMandla, kungekho muntu nakuwo wonke umuntu. "Namuhla. Anginandaba ukuthi isu lithini. Akekho odlula lo mjikelezo ngize ngibone insimbi kuleyo back."

Wacindezela irediyo lakhe ukuze akubikele esiteshini seLevel 30, kuSizwe, ukuze kube semthethweni, ukuze afake izwi lakhe kulogi lapho ifomu lehlulekile khona.

Akawuqedanga umusho.

---

Iphutha lalisalinde isikhathi eside kunanoma yini eyenziwa ngabantu.

Lalihamba edwaleni ngengosi engazange ihlelwe yisu. Isibazi esiphilile sokushayisana kwasendulo, indiza yobuthakathaka ebivalwe ngengcindezi iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezimbili zezigidi futhi manje ivuleka ngoba amadoda agubhe igebe eceleni kwayo asusa idwala ebeliyivalile. Ingcindezi ifuna lapho ingaya khona. Beselikhuluma sonke isonto. Izehlakalo ezintathu eduze kobuso bediklayini. *Umsindo wedwala eshiftini yasebusuku.* Lalinikeze sonke isexwayiso umhlaba owawazi ukusinika.

Kwathi lapho liyekela, layekela ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Akekho umsindo wesexwayiso ngalesi sikhathi, noma kunalokho umsindo wesexwayiso nenhlekelele kwakuyiso leso sikhathi. Ukugxuma okwafika ngomgangatho ngaphambi kokuba kufike ngomoya, okwashaya amadoda emathweni ezicathulo zawo nasemathanjeni amakhanda awo ngokushaya kwenhliziyo okukodwa. UMandla wabona isipanela siphuma esandleni sakhe salenga, umzuzwana ongenakwenzeka, othulini olwaluqhakaza ngezibani ngaphambi kokuba konke kuwele phansi. Isisekelo esimile sakhala kabuhlungu. Inethi lensimbi lahluleka kuleyo back njengesikhumba. Waphonswa eceleni enqwabeni yemfucumfucu, isibani sakhe sekhanda siphahlazeka edwaleni, kanti umhlaba waba mnyama, wabe usucwebezela ngezibani zamadoda awayo, wabuye waba mnyama futhi njengoba uthuli lwehla.

Uthuli lwehla njengolwandle.

Uhlangothi lwahlangana emazinyweni akhe, lwanamathela luminyene futhi luyinsimbi emuva kolimi lwakhe, ukunambitha kwedwala elivuliwe. Umkhono wengubo wakhe wawumanzi ebusweni bakhe, usawoti nothuli ndawonye. Wayengakwazi ukuphefumula. Wacindezela ubuso bakhe egwinciza lendololwane yakhe, kanti uthuli lwangena noma kunjalo, lucolisakele njengofulawa, luminyanisa ikhala nomphimbo wakhe. Ndawana thile eduze kakhulu indoda yayiklewula, kanti ndawana thile kude phambili intaba yayisanyakaza. Ukubhonga okujulile okugayayo kwegebe eliziwela ngaphakathi kulo, kwamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu wedwala elihlela kabusha isisindo salo. Umgangatho wagxuma. Into enkulu kakhulu yawela lapho i-LHD ibikhona ngomsindo owawungesona kakhulu umsindo kunokuba kube ngukushaywa kuwo wonke umzimba wakhe.

Kwabe sekudlula okubi kunakho konke, kwaba khona ukuthula okwakuphelele kangangokuthi uMandla wacabanga umzuzwana ontwezayo ukuthi wayesefile.

Wayengafile. Izindlebe zakhe zazikhala. Umlomo wakhe wawugcwele uhlangothi. Umkhono wakhe wesokunxele wawungaphenduli lapho ewucela ukuba unyakaze, futhi lapho ewuphoqa, ubuhlungu bavela bumhlophe bucwebile, wayeka ukucela. Walala emnyameni nasothulini, kanti kancane kancane, ngendlela indoda ezibala kabusha emva kokuwa, waqonda ukuthi uyaphila, nokuthi umoya wawuyekile ukunyakaza, ikholomu yomoya ifile, ukuphakelwa okubandayo okuvela emhlabeni kunqanyuliwe, nokuthi ukuklewula kwakuyekile nakho.

"Sondela," wazama ukumemeza. Izwi lakhe laphuma njengokukhwehlela. Wakhwifa okumnyama wazama futhi. "Sondela! Amazwi! Balani!"

Ubumnyama bamphendula.

Ngabanye ngabanye, bekhwehlela, beqaqamba, ngezilimi ezintathu, amazwi afika.

Ayisikhombisa.

Kwayishumi nanye ebusweni, ayisikhombisa aphendula. Amadoda ayisikhombisa esikhwameni somhlabathi omile ngemuva kodonga lwedwala eliwile elalikade, kungakapheli ihora, kuyidiklayini ababengene ngayo. UMandla wathola isibani sakhe sasayithi ngokuthinta, wasivula, kanti ukukhanya okuncane kwamakhemikhali kwambonisa isimo esisha somhlaba: igumbi eliphansi elinhlangothi lapho bekukhona ubuso obusebenzayo, uphahla luphakeme ngobubanzi besandla obethusayo ngenhla kwekhanda lakhe kwezinye izindawo, indlela yokuphuma iyisiphithiphithi semfucumfucu eya phansi kuze kufike kuleyo back kungekho gebe okungadlula ngalo ngisho nekati.

UThando wayephila. Umfana wayecindezelekile engosini yodonga, amehlo emakhulu ebusweni bakhe obumpunga ngothuli, ebambe idolo lakhe esifubeni sakhe. Wayenza umsindo omncane, kaningi kaningi, ongasona ncamashi amazwi.

"Uyaphila," kusho uMandla kuye. "Uyangizwa? Walalela idwala. Manje phefumula kancane. Kancane. Usebenzisa umoya njengesicebi, ufele lapha phansi umphofu."

Wayengakazi ukuthi lokho kwakuyiqiniso kangakanani.

Wayekwazi ukubala. Amadoda ayisikhombisa. Umoya ovundile wegumbi elilodwa lokuphephela, kungekho moya ongenayo, uthuli lusazinza. Udonga lwedwala phakathi kwabo nediklayini eyona ngokwayo eyayiholela esiteshini esikude ngamamitha angamakhulu amathathu okukhwela ubuyela emgodini ojule amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu.

UThando wakhwehlela, ukuxokozela okomile, wakhwifa uhlangothi emnyameni. Ndawana thile phansi egumbini indoda yashintsha izicathulo zayo enqwabeni yemfucumfucu, izilungiselela ukulinda.

---

Umhlaba wawazi ngaphambi kokuba uthuli luzinze amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi.

Egumbini lokulawula imayini, isikali sokuzamazama somhlaba saqhakaza, umugqa ugxuma usuka esisekelweni sawo njengentambo eshayiwe. Umlawuli oseshiftini, owesifazane ogama lakhe nguRefilwe owayesebenze ukulawula umhlaba iminyaka eyishumi nanye futhi owayesekuzwile kanye lokhu ngaphambili, wayesefonini yesimo esiphuthumayo ngaphambi kokuba i-alamu iqede umjikelezo wayo wokuqala. Izinombolo zazitshela indaba ngokushesha ngaphezu kwalokho noma yiliphi izwi elalingakwenza. Idiklayini yeLevel 33. Ubukhulu obabungaseyikho ukuthuthumela. Ingcindezi yomoya iyehla kuwo wonke amaleveli aphansi. Ukuxhumana kufile ngaphansi kweLevel 30.

Wathola amadoda esiteshini seLevel 30 efonini. Amazwi awo afika ezacile esabile: ayekwazi ukukuzwa, idiklayini yayigcwele uthuli, ayengaboni, i-back yayibubula. Bakhipheni, bakhipheni.

Wabakhipha. Ikheji yadonsa amathimba eLevel 30 neLevel 26 yawakhuphulela emhlabeni ngokwethuka okulawulekayo okwathi wonke umuntu emva kwalokho avumelana ngokuthi kwakuhambe kahle ngendlela izinto ezinjengalezi ezazingahamba ngayo. Sekuyimini i-headframe yayisihaqwe izimoto. Sekusentambama esheshayo amathimba e-proto—isigaba sokutakula sasezimayini, amadoda ayeziqeqeshela kanye lokhu kanti ethandaza ukuthi angakaze akusebenzise—ayesegqokile ehla, kanti iphrothokholi yenkampani yesimo esiphuthumayo yayisebenza njengento eyenziwe kahle yangaba njalo.

Sekusentambama emva kwemini, izimoto ezinezinombolo zokushayela ezifanele zaqala ukufika.

Zafika ngohlelo oluthile, kanti isandla esidala esisebukela kwichange house sasingayifunda yonke isimo kuloluhlu. Kuqala umphathi wemayini, kwalandela isikhulu sezokuphepha sesifunda, kwase kuza amadoda anezicathulo ezinhle ayengaphansi komhlaba futhi angakaze aye khona. Umqondisi wokusebenza ovela eJohannesburg, iphini lomongameli wento ethile, indoda evela eMnyangweni Wezezimbiwa enelanyadi nethebulethi nobuso obungenalutho obunakekelayo bomuntu obala icala ngesikhathi sangempela. Imoto kahulumeni. Kwathi, ngokungenakugwemeka, ivani yokuqala yezindaba, ipakwe esangweni lapho abezokuphepha babengeke bayivumele ukuthi idlule, isiqonga sayo sesiphakela esibhakabhakeni se-highveld.

USizwe, umphathi weshifti owayebhale *KUQAPHELILE.
