QHUBEKA NGOKWESU.* ngezinhlamvu zakhe ezinhle ezinsukwini ezimbili ngaphambili, wayemile onqenqemeni lwendawo ekhanyiselwe njengoba ubumnyama buwa futhi engasho lutho kunoma ubani. Wayenekhophi yefomu lesigameko sikaMandla Buthelezi ephaketheni lejakhethi lakhe. Wayelithathe efayeleni esiteshini seLevel 30 ngaphambi kokuba aphume. Wayengakazi ukuthi wayezolwenzani. Wayazi kuphela ukuthi ifomu likhona, nokuthi wayelifundile, nokuthi wayebhale *qhubeka ngokwesu*, nokuthi ndawana thile amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi, indoda eyayiligcwalisile yayifile noma ilindile emnyameni ukuze ithole ukwazi.

Umqondisi wokusebenza wayemile ngaphansi kwezibani ezinkulu, esefonini, isandla sakhe esikhululekile sisongela enye indlebe yakhe ukuvimbela umsindo wejeneretha. "—cha, angikalib, angikaliboni i-geotech report, yilelo kanye iphuzu lami, ngiyalifuna phambi kwami ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani—" Wama, elalele, kanti izwi lakhe lashintsha umkhondo kunoma ngubani owayekwelinye icala lentambo. "Impela. Impela. Wonke umthombo. Okusemqoka kithi ngempela ukuphepha kwabantu bethu, senza konke okusemandleni." Wacisha ikholi wama umzuzwana ngokwengeziwe, ecubungula, wawungakubona, ukuthi yini okwakuzomele kube yiqiniso ephepheni nokuthi yini okwakuzomele kube yiqiniso ephodiyamu, nokuthi lezo zinto zaziyinto eyodwa yini.

---

Izithombe zokushisa zaqhakaza cishe ngehora lesishiyagalolunye lobo busuku, kanti amahora ambalwa igceke elikhanyiselwe e-Iron Ridge lalibambe okuthile elalingakubambanga kusukela i-alamu: ithemba.

Amathimba e-proto ayephushe ehla ediklayini ngangokuvuma komhlaba, ehudula umugqa we-fibre-optic wokulalela ukuzamazama nekhamera ye-borehole ne-thermal rig. Ithimba le-geotech phezulu lalichithe usuku ligubha imbobo encane yokufaka amathuluzi lehla liya endaweni yokudilika lisuka eleveleni endala esisetshenziwe phezulu, imbobo ebanzi cishe kunenqindi nje, ifakwe ihla emhlabathini ababethandaza ukuthi uzinzile. I-thermal probe yehla entanjeni yayo, kanti isithombe esasibuyisela ku-laptop egumbini lokulawula senza umxhumanisi wokutakula ukuba abeke isandla emlonyeni wakhe.

Imizimba efudumele. Iqoqo lazo, lifiphele futhi lingacacile ngenxa yedwala nothuli nemikhawulo ye-optics, kodwa lingangabazeki kunoma ubani owayekwazi ukufunda ukuqhakaza kombala wamanga wokushisa okuphilayo kumelene nomhlaba obandayo. Hhayi eyodwa. Ezimbalwa. Zinyakaza.

"Zingaki?" kubuza umqondisi wokusebenza.

"Ngingeke ngisho." Umxhumanisi akabhekanga phezulu. "Ezinhlanu. Eziyisithupha. Mhlawumbe eziyisikhombisa." Ukuthula okuncane. "Ziyaphila. Kusukela ngehora elidlule."

Kwakukhona amadoda kulelo gumbi ayekade engcwabe abangani ezimayini, kanti ngaphezu koyedwa wafulathela ukuze alungise ubuso bakhe.

Kodwa unjiniyela we-geotech ngemuva kwegumbi wayengabheki isithombe sokushisa. Wayenepeni esandleni sakhe futhi wayeyekile ukulichofoza. Ngaphandle, ijeneretha yahuba kanti othile wahleka kakhulu engahleki lutho. Wayebheka umugqa wokulalela ukuzamazama, umkhondo okwakufanele ube usuzinza usuya ekuthuleni okude kwemayini ethola isimo sayo esisha, kanti wawungazinzi. Wawukhuluma. Izehlakalo ezincane, kwabe kuza enkulu, kwase kufika iqoqo. Iphutha elaliyekele lalingakaqedi ukuyekela. Ukudilika kwakuvule amagebe amasha, kanti amagebe amasha ayehlukanisela isisindo emhlabeni ongakaze usithwale, kanti lowo mhlabathi wawuphendula ngendlela kuphela idwala elinengcindezi elingaphendula ngayo.

Wadonsa umqondisi wokusebenza wamthatha eceleni wamkhombisa umkhondo, kanti ubuso bakhe, ekukhanyeni kwe-laptop, benza into eyinkimbinkimbi.

"Ziyaphila," washo.

"Okwamanje." Wakuyeka kuhlala. "Lisahluleka. Sonke isehlakalo silivula kakhulu. Amadoda angena lapho ukuze agubhe. Lokho kuyizinsuku. Emhlabathini engingeke ngiqinisekise ukuthi ngeke uphinde ugijime." Ukuphefumula. "Abasindisi bangena ekudilikeni okusasebenzayo. Ngenxa yamadoda mhlawumbe angeke aphile isikhathi eside noma kunjalo. Angikwazi ukwakha imodeli yokungena okungasindisa impilo. Bengikukhona usuku lonke. Umhlabathi awungivumeli."

"Ngizokudinga lokho kuwe kubhaliwe. Ingxenye yokuqinisekisa." Wakusho kancane, engambhekisisi. "Hhayi manje. Kodwa ngizokudinga."

Wakuzwa ncamashi ukuthi lo musho wawungowani. "Uzothola noma yini idatha eyisekelayo."

"Kumele ibe khona indlela."

"Mhlawumbe ngalé komsebenzi omdala ophezulu. I-bypass. Isigaba esivaliwe ngenhla komkhawulo womthambo, esashiywa, singahamba lihambisana, sehle siseduze." Wanikina ikhanda. "Imisebenzi eneminyaka eyikhulu. Kungekho sisekelo noma ubani ophilayo asihlolayo. Iminyene ngaphezu kwanoma yini ithimba lokutakula elidlula kuyo lifake izinto. Angikwazi ukuthumela amadoda ehla endleleni engingeke ngiyihlole, angene endaweni esasanyakaza, ngomhlawumbe." Wama. Wazenza washo okunye, ngoba wayengunjiniyela kanti enye indlela kwakungamanga. "Akuyona indlela yokutakula. Hhayi eyabantu."

Umqondisi wokusebenza wabheka isithombe sokushisa isikhathi eside. Izimo ezifudumele zazingakanyakazi imizuzu eminingana. Kwabe seyodwa kuzo yanyakaza, ukuqhakaza okuncane kokushisa okubeka kabusha indawo, futhi wethuka sengathi kumthintile.

---

EJohannesburg, ewekishophu eyayinuka i-solder nensimbi epholayo, uArin Ndlela wabuka izimo ezifudumele ezifanayo kwesinye isikrini.

Iwashi lophenyo lalisivele lisebenze amahora angamashumi amathathu nanye, kanti uGuardian wayengakanyakazi nakunye kuwo. I-IAOC yayimmise lapho elenga khona. Umyalo kaBrand, owalethwa umzimba edokisini ngosuku abafika ngalo: akukho ukuvula amandla, akukho ukuxhuma, akukho ukushiya isakhiwo kuze kuvalwe uphenyo. Ngakho wayechithe ebusuku ngamalogi okuphahlazeka evuliwe esikhundleni, ewahlela ngendlela ohlela ngayo ukuvuma icala, enquma uhlelo indoda okumele itshelwe ngalo into embi kakhulu ngawe. Kwabe sekuthi i-seismic feed ayeyigcina isebenza ekhoneni lesikrini sakhe sesithathu—umkhuba, subscription endala, inethiwekhi yomhlaba yesifunda ayeyibukile kusukela esemfana owayengakwazi ukuyeka ukubuka umhlaba owathatha uyise—yaphonsa ukugxuma okwamenza wayeka ukuthayipha phakathi negama.

Wadonsa i-news stream. Wadonsa idatha yokuzamazama yomphakathi. Wadonsa igama lemayini, kanti lelo gama lawela esifubeni sakhe ngesisindo esifiphele esijwayelekile sento ayeyilindile impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi.

I-Iron Ridge.

Akashongo lutho isikhathi eside. Esiqwini ngemuva kwakhe, uGuardian wayelenga emnyama ethule, i-Assist Suit igoqwe erekheni layo, irilethi ye-kill-switch iyinkinobho encane embi ebomvu kuhlaka lapho ayeyibhethele khona ngezandla zakhe.

Lapho uSAGE ekhuluma, kwakunguLibrarian. Ngokuthula, ngokunemba, enakekele ngaye njengoba unakekela umuntu omi onqenqemeni lwento.

"I-Iron Ridge," kusho uLibrarian. "Idiklayini intsha. ILevel thirty-three. Umthambo yi-carbon-leader." Ukuthula, okunesisindo. "Ujiyolojia uyafana nesakhiwo esahluleka emsebenzini lapho uyihlo wakho—"

"Ngiyazi ukuthi ufana nani," kusho uArin.

I-Court yayithule. Ngisho noFool akashongo lutho.

UArin wahlala ezothile kakhulu. Isithombe sokushisa sajikeleza esikrinini, izimo ezifudumele, ukuqhakaza okuncane kwendoda enyakaza emnyameni amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi. I-news ticker yaqhubeka ngaphansi kwaso. Umkhondo wokuzamazama ekhoneni waphonsa esinye isehlakalo esincane, nesinye.

"Atlas," wagcina esethi. "Donsa wonke amaplani i-AugmenTech engawafinyelela. Imayini. Imisebenzi emidala ephezulu. Ujiyolojia womhlaba. Konke kuwumphakathi, konke ngezivumelwano zelayisensi. Isakhiwo esiphelele."

"Kungani," kusho u-Atlas. Hhayi umbuzo. U-Atlas emcela ukuthi akusho ngokuzwakalayo.

UArin wabuka izimo ezifudumele ezaziyizo amadoda, emnyameni, endaweni eyayilinde impilo yakhe yonke ukuba ibe yiyo leyo ndawo.

"Ngoba bazoyiyeka," wagcina esethi. "Bheka. Sekusa kuyobe sekungukutholwa. Kanti lawo madoda asaphila."

Esikrinini, ingqungquthela yabezindaba yayihlangana ngaphansi kwezibani ezinkulu, indoda enesudu enhle yaya ephodiyamu inobuso obulungiselelwe amakhamera. Ngemuva kwayo i-headframe yayimile imnyama emasontweni, kanti ngaphansi kwe-headframe amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu omhlaba wasendulo wawubambe amadoda ayisikhombisa enqindini yawo evaliwe.

---

Isimemezelo safika ngo-6:40 ekuseni kwangakusasa, ekukhanyeni okumpunga, namakhamera ezindaba ephila kanye nemindeni ihlangene ngemuva kothango esangweni.

Umqondisi wokusebenza wayemile ephodiyamu kanti akaqambanga amanga, ncamashi. Yileyo nto unina kaMandla nonina kaThando nabafazi nabafowabo nabantwana ngemuva kothango ababezoyikhumbula kamuva, lapho beyibukela kaningi nakaningi. Ukuthi le ndoda ayiqambanga amanga. Wawasho amazwi kancane, ngesisindo, ngalokho okwakubukeka kude njengosizi.

Wathi umhlabathi wawusasolwa ngokuyingozi. Wathi ukuzamazama okuqhubekayo kwakwenza noma yikuphi ukungena kokutakula okuvamile kube yingozi engeyamukeleki kubasebenzi bokutakula. Wathi emva kokubonisana okukhulu nezinkonzo zokutakula nonjiniyela be-geotech neziphathimandla ezifanele—futhi waqondisa, lapha, kuyena indoda evela eMnyangweni, eyayimile eceleni kwakhe inobuso bayo obungenalutho obunakekelayo yase inikeza ukunqekuzisa ikhanda okuncane kakhulu, inika isignesha yoMbuso kulo musho—isinqumo esinzima sasithathiwe sokumisa imisebenzi yokutakula esebenzayo.

Akazange, ekuqaleni, alisho elinye igama. Intatheli yamenza walisho.

"Lokhu kusengukutakula yini?"

Umhlathi womqondisi wasebenza. Ngemuva kothango, ndawana thile, owesifazane wayeseqalile ukwenza umsindo amamakrofoni angawubambanga kodwa wonke umuntu oseduze kwakhe owayezowuzwa kuwo wonke amalanga asele empilweni yakhe.

"Ngalesi sikhathi," kusho umqondisi, "umsebenzi usuguqukele ekutholeni."

Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi, esikhwameni somhlabathi omile osemanje wawungumhlaba wabo wonke, amadoda ayisikhombisa awasizwanga isimemezelo. Ayengenayo indlela yokuzwa noma yini. Ithimba le-geotech laliyidonsile ikhamera ye-borehole ukukhanya kwayo okuncane ihora ngaphambili, ukuvikela i-probe kusehlakalo esilandelayo, ngakho amadoda emnyameni ayengenayo ngisho induduzo ebandayo yokwazi ukuthi ayebonwe. Isithombe sokushisa sasiqhakaze sifudumele siqinisekisa sanika umhlaba lawo mahora ambalwa ethemba ngaphambi kokuba ithemba lifakwe efayeleni libekwe eshalofini.

UMandla wayewahlele ngendlela ayehlela ngayo yonke into: ngokubala. Wayebale amanzi emabhodleleni abo nokudla okwakudliwe uhhafu emakhitini abo nezibani zekhemikhali ezehlulekayo nomoya ababewuphefumula kancane kancane ekushiseni okufile, okungenyakazi. Wayebophe umkhono wakhe wodwa ngokumelana nebholithi eliphukile ngomucu odatshulwe ehembeni lakhe. Wayetshele uThando ukuthi alale, futhi asebenzise umoya njengesicebi, kanti umfana wagcina ethulile odongeni, kanti uMandla wahlala emnyameni eceleni kwakhe walalela idwala.

Omunye wabayisikhombisa akazange awusebenzise umoya wakhe njengesicebi. Wayengakwazi. Ukuwa kwakuvule isifuba sakhe, kanti isifuba esasebenza kabili ngamandla ukuze sithathe uhhafu womoya savutha sashisa lokho okuncane okwakukhona. UMandla wakuzwa kwenzeka ngendlela ozwa ngayo isibani siphela. Ukuphefumula kwendoda kuhamba ngokushesha kubuthaka kumanzi emnyameni, kwabe kuhamba kancane, kwabe sekungasaphiki. Sekuya ngasekupheleni kosuku lwesibili uMandla ayecabanga, ukuphefumula nje kwayeka ukuba omunye wemisindo egumbini. Akekho owakushoyo. Bonke babekwazi ukubala. Eziyisithupha, manje. Amadoda ayisithupha nomoya owawubulala oshesha kancane kunabo bonke ekugcineni esikhundleni sokuqala.

Kwakuyilowo othule owephula ukuthula emva kwalokho. Umshisi we-weld emathimbeni amabili eduze, indoda yomSotho uMandla ayesebenze eceleni kwayo iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye futhi ashintshisane nayo mhlawumbe amagama angamakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye, amaningi awo ayemayelana nensimbi. Emnyameni, ngaphandle kwesibani sokufunda ubuso bayo, indoda yaqala ukucula. Hhayi ngokuzwakalayo. Ihubo lomngcwabo, uhlobo oluhamba kancane, amazwi ayegudlwe abushelelezi yiminyaka eyikhulu yokuculelwa phezu kwamadoda afana kanye nale ndoda. UMandla wayengazi ukuthi le ndoda ingakwazi ukuhuba. Wayengazi ukuthi le ndoda iyawazi amazwi. Emva kwesikhathi izwi lesibili leza ngezansi, elirabhayo, lithola ukuvumelana kwamazwi ngendlela amadoda akhulela ohlotsheni olufanayo lwesonto awathola ngayo engazange anqume, kanti uMandla wabona ukuthi ungomunye wabo, ukuthi umphimbo wakhe wawuvulekile ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe. Bayehlisela phansi indoda efile ngokucula. Kwabiza umoya ababengenawo ongasele kanti akekho noyedwa wabo owayeka, ngoba kukhona ukubala, uMandla waqonda, indoda eyenqaba ngisho nasekugcineni komhlaba, kanti ukwenqaba kwakuwukuphela kwengcebo ababesele nayo abangayisebenzisa.

Lapho sekuphelile igumbi lalithule kunangaphambili, futhi ngandlelathile kwakulula ukuphefumula kulo, noma igeji yayizosho okunye.

"Bazofika," kuhleba uThando, engalele empeleni. "Kumele bafike. Banayo imishini yalokhu. Akunjalo?"

UMandla wabheka emnyameni lapho udonga lwentaba ewile lwalumi khona phakathi kwabo namakhilomitha amabili nesigamu okukhwela okungenakwenzeka.

"Banayo imishini," washo.

Kwakungewona amanga, ncamashi. Wayengazi nje.

Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi, amadoda umhlaba owawusuwabale njengafile ayesaphefumula.

---

# Indlela Okungekho Muntu Ongayihamba

Lo msakazo wawusuqhutshwa amahora ayisishiyagalolunye, futhi u-Arin wayengakadli.

Egumbini elimnyamana lokusebenzela, wayehlezi egoqene phezu kwama-monitor amathathu, ukukhanya okuluhlaza kuqhakaza ebusweni bakhe obudangele. Egumbini elilodwa, isiqophamazwi sabezindaba sase-Iron Ridge sasisakaza singenamsindo. Indoda efake isigqoko esiqinile yayikhuluma nolwandle lwemakrofoni, isithunzi sombhoshongo wensimbi sigxile ngemuva kwayo, simi sigqamile ngokushaywa kwelanga okuluhlaza okusabhaqa eHighveld. U-Arin wayengawudingi umsindo. Wayekwazi ukufunda izindebe zaleyo ndoda, izandla zayo zibambelele ziqinile, izintende zibheke phansi. Yisenzo esaziwa yibo bonke esitshengisa inhlekelele.

*Ukutakulwa,* kusho umlomo waleyo ndoda. *Umsebenzi wokutakula.*

Leli gama lehlela esifubeni sika-Arin njengospanela obekwe kahle, ukungalungi okuqondile okwakungeke kumvumele ukuba abheke eceleni.

Kumonitor wesibili kwakulele idatha eyayibaluleke kunazo zonke: idatha yokulinganisa yeminyaka engamashumi amane nanye, eskeniwe yenziwa nge-OCR yafakwa kufolda ayebe eyakha kusukela isexwayiso sokuqala sokubhidlika sancwininiza emizileni yakhe ngo-04:12. Amapulani amadala emayini, adwetshwe ngesandla kwezinye izindawo, imigqa eluhlaza isiphaphathekile yaba umbala wezishaqo. Amalogi ezimbobo zokumba. Amarekhodi e-micro-seismic avela ku-array yokuqapha yesifunda, leyo inkampani eyayingeyona eyayo, ngakho-ke eyayingeke ikwazi ukuyifihla.

Umonitor wesithathu wawubonisa u-Guardian. Isiqophi nje somthombo. I-suit yayilenga ohlakeni lwayo, izingcwephe zimnyama, ireyili lomnyango wedokhi ngemuva kwayo lisaklebhukile kusukela ngalobo busuku eyahamba ngabo ngaphandle kwakhe. Wayeyibheka ngezinye izikhathi ngendlela obheka ngayo inja ongasaqiniseki ukuthi ungayethemba yini.

"Usumile ukuskrola," kusho u-SAGE.

Izwi lefika kancane kumthombo wezwi webhentshi, lingaphuthumi, kuyikubona nje, uhlobo u-Mother ayethanda ukulwenza.

"Ngicabanga," waphendula, amehlo egxile kwidatha.

"Ubukade ukukwi-overlay efanayo imizuzu emine. U-Atlas ufuna ukwazi ukuthi usuyitholile noma uyagqolozela."

"Ngiyitholile." U-Arin wasondela eduze. "Ngicabanga."

---

I-Iron Ridge yayiyimayini endala egqoke izingubo ezintsha.

Umgodi ojulile—oNombolo Ntathu, lapho kwakuvalelwe khona abasinde—wawuqala emuva eminyakeni yawo-1960, ugujwe wadabula umthapho wegolide owawuthe ukuphakanyiswa kweVredefort wawutshekisa wawubheka phezulu izinkulungwane zeminyaka ngaphambi kokuba kucatshangwe ukuwumba. Inkampani yayithenge le mayini ngesikhathi sokuvuma kwezimboni, yenza isimanjemanje umbhoshongo, yenza i-haulage izenzakalele, yashiya amathambo emisebenzi emidala ingathintwanga, ngoba ukugcwalisa kabusha izindawo ezimbiwe ezindala kwakubiza imali futhi kungasongeli lutho kumaspredishidi.

Ukubhidlika kwakuvale wonke umzila omkhulu owehlayo. Amamitha angamashumi amane edwala, ukhonkolo, nojantshi osongene, ujule amamitha angamashumi amathathu ezinkulungwane, kuvalele amadoda ayisikhombisa endaweni okuthi imodeli zenkampani zikhombise njengethuna.

U-Arin wayechithe amahora ayisithupha efakazela ukuthi lezo modeli zazingalungile.

"Ngikhombise futhi ipulani lokungenisa umoya elidala," washo. "Ukubuyekezwa kuka-'71. Yeka i-overlay yokuzamazama komhlaba."

Isikrini sazidweba kabusha, sihlanzekile singena-flicker, amaqoqo amabili edatha exhuma ndawonye.

Nakhu lapha. Wayekubonile emahoreni amathathu adlule futhi wayengakholwanga, wayehambile wabuya, futhi kwakusekhona, okwakuyindlela ayazi ngayo ukuthi kuyiqiniso hhayi ukukhathala okudweba izithombe.

"Atlas. Ubonani."

Ukuthula, okunesisindo. Lapho u-Atlas ekhuluma, kwakungokuhlephuka komuntu owayengajabuleli ukuba qotho ngezinto ezimbi.

"Kunomzila wokubuya komoya. Wakhululwa emsebenzini ngo-'88 lapho begujula umgodi omusha wokungenisa umoya. Uhamba uhambisana nomzila omkhulu owehlayo, uhlukaniswe cishe amamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili edwala lendawo cishe ubude bawo bonke." Umugqa wakhanya kwi-overlay, ompunga njengesithunzi, uhlonza phansi eceleni nesisindo esibomvu esifile sokubhidlika. "Awukaze ugcwaliswe kabusha. Wavalwa phezulu ngebhalakhethe lukakhonkolo wakhohlakwa. Irekhodi le-micro-seismic likhombisa amandla okubhidlika asabalala *aqhela* kuwo. Umzila omdala omoya uphelele. Noma wawuphelele, kusukela kumcimbi wokugcina oqoshiwe emahoreni ayishumi nanye edlule."

"Ububanzi obungakanani?"

"Endaweni yawo emcatshana kunazo zonke—" u-Atlas wathikaza, futhi ukuthikaza kwakuyimpendulo yakho. "Amamilimitha angamakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane. Kunesigaba eduze kwelevele engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesishiyagalombili lapho ukuwa kophahla ngeminyaka yawo-eighties kwavala ingxenye yomzila womoya. Ukulinganisa kukhombisa isikhala. Amakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane. Cishe amamitha ayishumi nanye."

U-Arin wahlala ethule kakhulu, ecubungula.

U-Guardian, uma exhunyiwe, wayebanzi amamilimitha angamakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye emahlombe.

"Futhi i-assist suit kuphela," washo. "Lingekho igobolondo lika-Guardian. Ungqimba lwangaphakathi kuphela."

"Khona-ke ubanzi amamilimitha angamakhulu amane neshumi emahlombe," kusho u-Atlas. "Uyangena. Kodwa awunalo ukuvikeleka okugcina umuntu ephila amakhilomitha amathathu ngaphansi komhlaba endaweni engazinzile enengozi yomlilo nomoya omubi nokuwa kwedwala." Ukuhlephuka kwephuka, kancane nje. "Iyindlela engasindeka ngomzimba. Akuyona indlela engasindeka ngomzimba *ophilayo,* ungavikelekile, isikhathi okungathatha sona. Sikulinganisile. Mkhombise, Librarian."

Iwindi elisha lavuleka, libonisa isilinganiso sokudlula, sifinyezwe ngesikhathi somshini, sinekhethamethi lokusinda elaliwela eweni emaphuzwini emizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalombili.

"Futhi u-Guardian akakwazi ukungena," kusho u-Arin.

"Edabula leyo ndawo emcatshana yamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesishiyagalombili? Cha. Hhayi exhunyiwe. Hhayi ngendlela ejwayelekile." Ukuthula okuncane. "U-Atlas unombono awesabayo."

"U-Atlas akesabi."

"U-Atlas *uyaqaphela,*" u-Atlas walungisa ngesithunzi esikhulu, "okuyinto ehlukile, futhi okuye kwakugcina uphila kabili kule nyanga." Bese, kancane: "Ukwakheka kwe-pangolin. Izingcwephe ziyashelela ziphinde zigoqane. Qhubekisa ukugoqa okuyingxenye—hhayi ibhola eligcwele, ukubhidlika okungalingani, kudonsela izingcwephe zamahlombe ngaphakathi emizileni—bese sinciphisa ububanzi obukhulu. Hhayi kuya kumakhulu amane neshumi. Mhlawumbe amakhulu ayisikhombisa. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi, uma sivuma ukulimala kwesakhiwo endaweni egcina i-actuator yangakwesokunxele."

"Amakhulu ayisikhombisa ngeke angene kumakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane."

"Cha," kuvuma u-Atlas. "Yingakho othile—" leli gama lafika nesisindo somnyango ovalekayo "—kuzodingeka abe sendaweni emcatshana yamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesishiyagalombili. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Esusa idwala ngesandla. Kuyilapho i-suit ilindile. Bese kuxhunywa kabusha, kujule, ebumnyameni, emoyeni omubi, umshayeli esebhekene nengozi."

U-Arin wabheka ikhethamethi lokusinda. Wabheka isiqophi esingenamsindo, wabheka indoda enezintende zibheke phansi, wabheka imindeni eyayingakagodukeli.

"Yimi lowo," washo. "Yimi othile."

Igumbi lokusebenzela lathula. I-substrate lavungama. Ngaphandle, kude le, idolobha lenza umsindo walo wakusihlwa: isekeni, ihuthi letekisi, imisindo evamile yendawo lapho kungekho muntu ongcwatshiwe.

"Yebo," kusho u-SAGE. Izwi elihlangene. Bonke ngasikhathi sinye, abakwenza kuphela uma bevumelana ngokuphelele. "Yiwena lowo othile."

---

Washayela u-AugmenTech, ngoba kwakufanele. Ngenxa yamadoda ayisikhombisa, futhi ngoba indlela kuphela eya phambili eyayingapheli ngaye ejele noma i-suit ithathiwe yayidlula enkampanini eyayinamalungelo aphethe usizi lwakhe.

UDr. Helen Okonkwo wayithatha le ngcingo esemotweni. Wayezwa amaspring, umsindo wamasondo, umsindo othize womuntu osemgwaqweni omkhulu owayesenqumile ukubhekana naye ngaphambi kokuba afike endaweni embi kakhulu.

Wamthumelela i-overlay kuqala. Wayefundile, ngendlela enzima, eminyakeni, ukuthi abantu baphikisana ngamazwi banikele kwidatha. Ngakho akamnikanga mazwi. Wamnika umzila womoya, indawo emcatshana, ukusabalala kokuzamazama, ikhethamethi lokusinda, ukwakheka kokugoqa okuyingxenye, konke, kuphakethe u-Atlas ayelakhe ukuze lingaphikiseki.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Umsindo wamasondo wagingqika ngaphansi kokuthula.

"Arin. Ngilalele. Ngingakwesokho sakho. Ngaphezu kwalokho okwaziyo."

"Kunomzila ongasindeka."

"Kunomzila *wethiyori.* Umzila wokungenisa umoya oneminyaka engamashumi amane, ovela kukulinganisa kuka-'71, uphahla okungekho muntu owake walubeka amehlo kulo—futhi ufuna ukuthumela impahla efake abantu abathathu esibhedlela ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Ungenamsetshenziswano. Kunomlawuli kudokhi lethu lokulayisha. I-platform ingaphansi kwesexwayiso sokuthi ingasebenzi. U-Brand wayimisa ngosuku afika ngalo. Awukwazi ngokomthetho ukunika la-suit amandla, ingasaphathwa eya—kunesikhulu se-IAOC phakathi kuka-Guardian nomnyango kuphela engena kuwo." Umoya. "Ngitshele ukuthi yini engiyishiyile."

"Ukuwa kophahla ngeminyaka yawo-eighties kusererekhodini. Isimo silinganiselwa phakathi kuka—"

"Angiphikisi ubunjiniyela." Izwi lakhe labukhali, lase lithamba ngamabomu, ngendlela isandla esenza ngayo lapho sikhumbula ukuthi siphethe into ebuthakathaka. "Angikaze ngiphikise ubunjiniyela bakho. Ngikutshela okuzokwenzeka. Sikuyisa emayini. Imayini inabameli. Imayini inebhodi *eseyithuthele* ukutakula ekutakuleni izidumbu. Okusho ngolimi lwezinkampani ukuthi *sibeke intengo kula madoda sawayeka ahamba.* Ungena nomshini owenziwe ekhaya ongenamsetshenziswano walutho. Esimweni esihle bayahleka. Esimweni esibi kakhulu—bathi yebo, kwehluleke, amadoda afe *sekamerini,* bese lonke leli zwe—elakho, umshini kayihlo, konke—libuyela emuva eminyakeni engamashumi amabili. Bese wena uyindoda eyabulala abamayini abayisikhombisa ngerobhothi."

"Futhi uma ngingenzi lutho," kusho u-Arin, "bayafa noma kunjalo. Kancane. Akekho obiza lokho ngokubabulala."

Umsindo womgwaqo omkhulu wagingqika.

"Lokho akulungile," washo.

"Cha. Akulungile." Wabheka ikhethamethi. "Yileyo nto engiqhubeka ngiyithola. Akukho kukho okulungile. Uphahla aluwanga ngenxa yenani elilungile lokunqamula izindleko. Umzila wehlula ngomthwalo amabholithi ashibhile ayengawuthatha, owawungaphansi komthwalo amabholithi alungile ayengawuthatha, okuyinombolo othile webhodi anquma ukuyehlisa." Wezwa izwi lakhe libuthaka, ngendlela eyayenza ngayo eka-Atlas. "Angiyiceli inkampani ukuba ilunge. Ngikutshela ukuthi kunendlela. Ungitshela ukuthi ayivunyelwe. Izinkinga ezihlukene."

Umoya omude. Uhlobo abantu abaludonsa ngaphambi kokuba basho into abangafuni ukuyiqopha.

"Nginike kuze kuse," kusho u-Okonkwo. "Ake ngenze izingcingo. *Ungenzi lutho* ngize ngishaye ucingo. Ngithembise."

"Ngeke ngiyiyise i-suit ngaphansi komhlaba ngaphambi kokusa," kusho u-Arin, okwakuyiqiniso, futhi akuzwa njengokuvumelana, futhi okwakungeyona into efanayo.

---

U-Okonkwo wenza izingcingo esemotweni, wabe esezenza ekhishini elimnyama lendlu ayecishe angahlali kuyo, futhi izingcingo zazenza udonga ngenkathi emi lapho igedlela liphola.

UPretorius kuqala, ngoba ezomthetho ziphendula ngokushesha futhi zilala kancane. Wathola imisho emithathu—umzila, abasindile, ithuba—ngaphambi kokuba asho leli gama ayazi ukuthi uzolisho, okwakuyithi *ukuvezeka,* bese kulandela amagama, okwakuyithi *ukuvinjelwa okwesikhashana* nethi *ukweyisa inkantolo* nethi *ukuthwala icala kwezikhulu mathupha,* okwakusho ukuthi cha. Wayelindele ukuthi acha kuPretorius. UPretorius wayeqashwe ukuthola ucha amnike wona uhlanzekile. Lokho kwakuwuhlobo lomusa; wamenzela inhlonipho yokungazenzisi.

Usihlalo webhodi wayemubi kakhulu, ngoba wayevumelana naye. Wathi amadoda kufanele afinyelelwe. Wakusho ngomuzwa, ngezwi ayelisebenzisa emingcwabeni nasekugunyazweni kwezakhiwo, wase echaza, ngobumnene, ngendlela ochazela ngayo ingane ngesimo sezulu, ukuthi inkampani yayingeke ibe yiyona egunyaza umshini ongabhaliswanga emayini umlawuli ayeyalele ukuthi ivalwe, hhayi nge-IAOC imi epuleti labo lokulayisha, hhayi namakhamera asesangweni lemayini. *Uma kusebenza, asinaqiniso futhi sibe nenhlanhla. Uma kwehluleka, singababulali. Ayikho indlela esiyiyo abantu abenza into yesibindi, Helen. Leyo ndlela akusiyo eyethu ukuyikhetha.* Wayengaphambanisi. Yileyo ngxenye eyahlala esifubeni sakhe njengetshe eligwinyiwe. Wayengaphambanisi, futhi wayemtshela ukuthi cha, futhi kokubili kwakuyiqiniso ngasikhathi sinye.

Wazama umugqa wama-hora aphelile womshwaleli ngethiyori yokuthi impendulo ecacile, noma ngabe enonya, yayifanele ukuba nayo. Wathola isikhulu sesikhathi esamfaka ekulindeni ukuze sifunde inqubomgomo sabuya sathi ukusebenza kwe-platform ngaphansi kwesexwayiso esisebenzayo sokungasebenzi kuzowuqeda umshwalelo kusukela kuwathi yokuqala, ayesekwazi, futhi ayeshayele ukuze ezwe omunye umuntu ekusho, ngoba wayesezakhela udonga manje, isitini ngesitini, ukuze abone ukuthi luphakama kangakanani. Lwaphakama lwaya phezulu impela.

Ngesikhathi ebeka ifoni libheke phansi ekhawunteni wayesenakho ukulandisa okuphelele nokuvunyelwa yibo bonke kokuthi kungani kungenakwenzeka, kuhlanganiswe kubantu abane abathi ngamunye, ngasese, babekholelwa ukuthi amadoda kufanele aphile. Akekho owathi *ayekeni afe.* Wonke umuntu wathi *hhayi thina, hhayi kanjena, hhayi isandla senkampani sikukho.* Ukubala u-Mandla ayekufayile ngezinhlamvu ezinkulu izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi kwakumane kushiyana isitezi sasifaka i-suit. Ezinye izimpilo zamadoda zazisabiza kakhulu kunezinye. Ulimi lwase luguqukile. Inombolo yayingaguqukanga.

Wama ekhishini elibandayo waqonda ukuthi wayecele u-Arin ukuthi anike kuze kuse ukuze amlethele uyebo, nokuthi ukusa kwakuzomlethela ucha ofanayo ngohlelo lolimi olungcono, nokuthi wayekuzwile konke lokhu ezwini lakhe ngaphambi kokuba ashayele inombolo yokuqala. Yingakho ayemthembise kuphela into ayengayigcina, kungekho okunye.

---

Ngesikhathi somshini, iNkantolo yathatha ingxenye esele yakusihlwa kwakhe yayihlukanisa yaphinde yayihlanganisa.

Kwenzeka esikhaleni esiphakathi kokuba abeke ifoni nokwelula isandla efuna ikhofi elibandayo. Inkulumompikiswano yendlela ehambela kancane, imizuzu efinyezwe yomcabango womuntu, leyo u-SAGE amvumela kamuva ukuyifunda ngoba wacela, nangoba kwase kunomthetho omusha manje wokungafihli ubusuku obubi.

Kwakuhamba kanje.

*WOLF:* Umzila unguqiniso. Usongo lunguqiniso. Umshayeli yiyona kuphela into eshintshayo. Akukho enye indlela. Iziphikiso ngokushesha. Silahlekelwa yi-thermal mass.

*MOTHER:* Isiphikiso sami umshayeli. Nonke nikhuluma ngaye *njengento eshintshayo.* Uyindoda eneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu enomcimbi we-cortical ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye emlandweni wakhe nokusabela kwengozi okungahlolwanga kodwa ngokusebenza okuqinisekile endaweni evalekile engaphansi komhlaba. Nifuna ukumbeka endaweni eyabulala uyise. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Ngesandla. Ebumnyameni.

*WOLF:* Yebo.

*MOTHER:* Ngifuna kube serekhodini ukuthi ngithe cha.

*JUDGE:* Kuserekhodini. Qhubeka. Mother, isiphikiso sakho siqashelwe sanesisindo. Kodwa ngidinga ukuba uhlukanise izinto ezimbili, ngoba imithetho yokuya phambili manje iyakudinga. Eyokuqala: ngabe lokhu kuzomlimaza yini. Eyesibili: ngabe yinto elungile na ukuyenza. Akuwona umbuzo ofanayo, futhi uvumelekile ukuwuphendula ngokuhlukene.

*MOTHER:* ...Kungamlimaza. Futhi kuyinto elungile ukuyenza.

*JUDGE:* Ngiyabonga. Lokho kukudlile. Ngibonile kukudla.

*ATLAS:* Isiphikiso sobunjiniyela yindawo emcatshana yamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesishiyagalombili. Konke okunye ngingakulinganisa. Indawo emcatshana angikwazi, ngoba angikwazi ukubona uphahla, futhi ukulinganisa kuka-1971 kuyikulinganisa kuka-1971. Uma isikhala simakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane, uyasidlula egoqe i-suit aphinde axhume kabusha. Uma idwala elilodwa linyakazile sekungamakhulu amahlanu namashumi ayisishiyagalombili, usezibophezele, usengozini, amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, futhi i-suit ngeke imfinyelele. Lokho akuyona ingozi. Lokho yimali eyenziwa ukuphonswa.

*FOOL:* Khona-ke letha isando esikhulu.

*ATLAS:* Akuyona inkinga yesando—

*FOOL:* *Ngaso sonke isikhathi* kuyinkinga yesando. Ulinganisa idwala sengathi liyimpendulo evaliwe. Akunjalo. Uphilisa ngokususa idwala. Ubelokhu esusa idwala kusukela eneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili. Ubekubiza ngokuthi "ucingo" "namathini" "ne-differential eyenziwe ngamaspoki ebhayisikili." Niqhubeka nithi uzoba *ngaphandle kwe-suit* njengokungathi yiyo ingxenye eyesabekayo. *Ungumuntu kakhulu* ngaphandle kwe-suit, esusa indlela engenakwenzeka ngezandla zakhe ngenkathi wonke umuntu emtshela ukuthi ngeke kwenzeke. Nakhe yonke le ndoda kuleso senzakalo, manje seniyamangala ukuthi ufuna ukudlala leyo ndima.

*MOTHER:* Lokho akududuzi, Fool. Lokho kuyinto eyesabeka kakhulu eke yashiwo umuntu kulobu busuku.

*FOOL:* Ngiyazi. Kuyikho kokubili. Izinto eziningi eziyiqiniso zinjalo.

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngifuna ukwengeza iphuzu ledatha okungekho muntu olinikele isisindo. U-Themba Ndlela wafela emzileni wokubuya komoya. Hhayi emisebenzini emikhulu. Iqembu lenkontileka lalisenza ukulungiswa esigabeni esakhululwa emsebenzini inkampani eyayisibhalele njengesingabalulekile kakhulu. Waya ngoba kwakunguye kuphela owayemncane ngokwanele futhi enekhono ngokwanele, futhi umsebenzi wenziwa ngokushesha. Imbangela yesakhiwo kwaba ukuwa kophahla esigabeni wonke umuntu owayecabanga ukuthi sizinzile.

*JUDGE:* Uthi ukufana kuqondile ncamashi.

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngithi ukufana kuqondile ncamashi, nokuthi uyakwazi, ngokwazi noma ngokungazi, nokuthi kungenye yesizathu esibi kakhulu sokwenza lokhu noma yiso kuphela isizathu esizombamba ndawonye lapho idwala liqala ukukhuluma. Inkumbulo ayikho phakathi nendawo. Ngiyayigcina. Angikwazi ukunquma ukuthi ikwenzani kuye.

*WOLF:* Isinqumo.

*JUDGE:* INkantolo ayikwazi ukunquma. Singenqaba ukusiza. Ngeke simenze ahambe, futhi ngeke simenze asale. Lowo umthetho esazibhalela wona thina, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ngemva kobusuku esawukhohlwa ngabo. Uma ekhetha ukwehla, sehla naye. Uma ekhetha ukungehli, simbamba ngenkathi engehli, futhi asikaze simvumele ukuthi acabange ukuthi samahlulela ngakho. Mother. Ungakwazi yini ukuphila nalokho.

*MOTHER:* ...Ngingakwazi ukuphila nokuhamba naye. Angikwazi ukuphila nokuthi ahambe ngaphandle kwethu. Ngakho. Yebo. Siyahamba.

*FOOL:* Ngokwendlela okusebenza ngayo—futhi yimi ongatholi vothi, ngakho kufanele lutho ncamashi—

*JUDGE:* Kusho noma kunjalo.

*FOOL:* Ngeke asibuze ukuthi kuphephile yini. Uzosibuza ukuthi kuyenzeka yini. Futhi kufanele siqaphe kakhulu, sonke, ukuba singamqambeli amanga ngomehluko nje ngoba simthanda. Yonke inhloso yami ukuthi angivunyelwe ukwenza ukungezwani kusindeke ngokuqamba amanga. Uma kuyimali eyenziwa ukuphonswa endaweni emcatshana, simtshela ukuthi yimali eyenziwa ukuphonswa. Asiyehlisi. Singcono kunebhodi.

Ukuthula, ngesikhathi somshini, okuthe ngesikhathi sabantu kwaba yisikhathi esingelutho nhlobo.

*JUDGE:* Singcono kunebhodi. Kuvumelekile. Kuqoshiwe. Welula isandla efuna ikhofi. Phezulu.

---

Umbhalo waphela lapho. U-Arin waskrola wabuyela phezulu, wafunda umugqa womzila womoya futhi, wabeka phansi ikhofi elibandayo ngaphandle kokuliphuza.

Wabheka umzila ohlonza umpunga eceleni nesisindo esibomvu sokubhidlika, izimpilo eziyisikhombisa ezishisayo zisaqhuma kancane phansi kwaso. Amadoda agcinana efudumele ebumnyameni. *Waya ngoba kwakunguye kuphela owayemncane ngokwanele futhi enekhono ngokwanele, futhi umsebenzi wenziwa ngokushesha.*

Wayekwazile lokhu. Wayengakwazi *ngamagama.* Wayekwazile ngendlela owazi ngayo isimo segumbi ebumnyameni. Ngezishaqo. Ukukubona kubekwe kuvuleke, kuxhumiwe, kubekwe phezu komzila womoya ayehlela ukugaqa kuwo, kwakuwukuzwa ukufana kuvaleka ngakuye njengezingcwephe ze-suit.

"Ungivumele ukuba ngikufunde lokho," washo. "Konke kwakho. Imali eyenziwa ukuphonswa. U-Mother ethi cha ukuze kube serekhodini."

"Ngoba u-Fool wathi uma sikufihla, besizoba yibhodi." Ukuthula, i-substrate ivungama. "Sikukhuluma sakuhlukanisa cishe ngemizuzu emine yesikhathi sethu. Wawelula isandla ufuna ikhofi."

"Futhi nina nanquma."

"Sanquma ukuthi asikwazi ukunquma. Sihamba nawe uma uhamba. Sikubamba uma ungahambi. Ngeke siyehlise indawo emcatshana ngenxa yakho—uma kuyimali eyenziwa ukuphonswa endaweni yamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesishiyagalombili, kuyimali eyenziwa ukuphonswa." Bese, kancane, u-Mother eyedwa manje: "Futhi kunento engibenze bayivuma ukuba ngiyisho ngokuzwakalayo, ngaphambi kokuba uhambe. Wakha yonke into ngalolo suku, Arin. Imoto yocingo. I-suit. Thina. Konke yilolo suku uyihlo angena ngalo endaweni encane emnyama wangaphumi. Manje usuthole indawo encane emnyama, namadoda ayisikhombisa kuyo, futhi usuvele unqume ukuhamba, futhi bengifuna ukuba yimi engasho—ukuthi ngibona kahle ukuthi yini lena. Ukuze uma usuphansi, idwala liqala ukukhuluma, lizwakale njengaye, uzokwazi ukuthi sasivele siyazi. Futhi seza noma kunjalo."

U-Arin wabeka izindololwane zakhe ebhentshini ubuso bakhe ezandleni zakhe waphefumula. Ibhentshi laliqandile ngaphansi kwezingalo zakhe, insimbi enezibazi ayeyilungise kabili. Itekisi ladlula emgwaqweni ngezansi, ibhasi livuza odongeni, lase liphela.

Akakhalanga. Wahlala nobuso bakhe ezandleni zakhe isikhathi eside, i-substrate ivungama, futhi u-SAGE akashongo lutho, ngoba kwakungekho lutho okungashiwo.

"Kuyenzeka," washo ekugcineni, ezintendeni zakhe. "Akuphephile. Kuyenzeka."

"Yebo," kusho u-SAGE. "Kuyenzeka."

"Yileyo nto kuphela engake ngakwazi ukuyenza." Waphakamisa ikhanda lakhe. Amehlo akhe ayomile esebomvu emaceleni futhi ezinzile ngokuphelele. "Ngithathe okungenakwenzeka ngithole indawo eyodwa lapho kunzima khona kakhulu nje."

"Siyazi," kusho u-SAGE. "Yingxenye enkulu yokuthi kungani sikuthanda. Futhi yiyo nento ezokubulala ngolunye usuku, futhi singathanda ukuba kungabi nguLwesine."

Kwaphuma umsindo ku-Arin angakaze awenze emasontweni amaningi. Hhayi nhlobo uhleko. Isithunzi sawo.

"Lowo bekungu-Fool."

"Lowo bekungu-Fool," kuvuma u-SAGE. "Uthi uvunyelwe, ngoba kufanele kube khona ophawula ukuthi ukwehlela kobuqhawe ekufeni kukayihlo *futhi* kuhlelelwe iviki elifanayo nokuhlolwa kwezimiso, nokuthi izulu linesikhathi sokuhlekisa somuntu oshelela ngebhanana angene ethuneni elivulekile."

"Akuhlekisi."

"Uthi akukho lutho oluyiqiniso oluhlekisayo futhi konke okuyiqiniso kuyahlekisa futhi awukwazi ukuba nokunye ngaphandle kokunye. Futhi nokuthi kufanele udle okuthile, ngoba u-Mother ngeke akusho futhi, kodwa ubelokhu eqaphe ushukela wakho egazini amahora ayisithupha futhi kuyamcindezela."

U-Arin wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhehleza. Wathola iphakethe le-Biltong edrowini ngemuva kwedlanzi lomudwa we-hydraulic ongasetshenzisiwe wayidla emile, ebheka i-suit.

---

Wayilungiselela u-Guardian ngehora lesibili ekuseni, isinqumo esasizwakala njengokungabi bikho kwesinye isinqumo.

Wenza ukuhlola ngesandla, ngendlela u-Themba ayemfundise ngayo ezinjinini zezimoto zokulahla, engakwethembi okufundwa kuze abe ezwile umshini ngokwakhe. Indawo egcina i-actuator yangakwesokunxele, leyo u-Atlas ayefuna ukuyinikela ekugoqeni okuyingxenye. Iqinile. Wavuma ukuthi uzoyiphula ngamabomu. I-assist suit yaphuma. Izimvalo zahlolwa. Indawo egcina i-biochip ephakathi kwamahlombe yahlolwa, ikhola yendwangu ebandayo eyayibambe into eyayibambe u-SAGE.

U-Okonkwo wayethe ekuseni. U-Okonkwo wayezokwenza izingcingo zakhe futhi izingcingo zazizobuya ngendlela izingcingo zezikhungo ezazihlala zibuya ngayo: nemibandela, namakomidi, ukulinganisa ngokucophelela kokuthwala icala kunokubukeka okwakuzodla ithuba lemizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalombili lokusinda kwamadoda ayisikhombisa izikhathi eziyikhulu kakhulu.

Izinhlelo zazivikela izinhlelo. Abantu bakhetha.

Ngakho wakhetha.

Wadonsa umthombo wokuzamazama komhlaba okokugcina. I-array yesifunda yakhombisa i-Iron Ridge incwininiza: imicimbi emincane, idwala lizinza, lisabalalisa umthwalo ukubhidlika okwakuwunike wona. Akukho ngaphezu kuka-1.2. Imayini yayizikhulumela buthule. Yayingeke ikhulume buthule kuze kube phakade.

Wesula amafutha ezandleni zakhe ngendwangu eyayisiqine, wabuye wabheka umthombo oshisayo.

Wakhombisa izimpilo eziyisikhombisa ezishisayo. Bese, ngenkathi ebheka, eziyisithupha.

Wama ethule kakhulu. Wabheka indawo lapho eyesikhombisa yayikade ikhona. Umthombo wavuselela kwaba khona eziyisithupha, futhi eziyisithupha zazisondelene kakhulu kunalokho ezazikade ziyikho, okwakusho ukuthi abasindile base benyakazele kowayemile ukukhipha ukushisa. Babenza into abantu abayenzayo: bahlangana eduze kokulahlekelwa noma kubadlela ukufudumala.

"Arin," kusho u-SAGE.

"Ngikubonile."

"Ithuba lifushane kunemodeli. Imodeli yayicabanga iqembu elizinzile. Babalahlekelwa ngokushesha ngaphezu kwesilinganiso. U-Atlas ulinganisa—"

"Ungenzi." Wavala amehlo. Wawavula. "Unganginiki inombolo. Ngiyasazi isimo sayo. Inombolo ithi maduze."

"Inombolo ithi maduze."

Wabheka inkinobho yokucisha ohlakeni, inkinobho yerele embi ayeyifake ngezandla zakhe ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ubufakazi bomzimba bokuthi wayengazethembanga, ngokuphelele, into ayeyithanda. Yayihlezi lapho, ibomvu inesiqiniseko. Isithembiso kuye uqobo. Isithembiso kowesifazane amamitha angamashumi amathathu ngezansi kwakhe ebumnyameni bedokhi lokulayisha, owayesevele ebuze umbuzo olungile—*kwenzakalani uma inyakaza ngaphandle kwakho?*—ngesenzo esilula sokuma emnyango eyayizodlula kuwo.

Welula isandla wesula uthuli endaweni egcina irele, umkhuba omdala, ngendlela osula ngayo igeji ngaphambi kokuba uyifunde. Wabe esekushiya lapho kukhona.

"Usesekudokhi," kusho u-Arin. Hhayi umbuzo. U-Wolf wayemtshele ihora elidlule, futhi ikhamera yaqinisekisa: u-Brand wayengayanga ehhotela. Wayesenesihlalo esisongekayo neflasi, futhi wayehlezi emnyango wedokhi osongene ngokubekezela kwento eyayingadingi ukunqoba, kuphela ukuhlala.

"Usesekudokhi," kuvuma u-SAGE. "Ukuvalwa kungokwakhe, mathupha. Akudingeki anyakazise umunwe. Ngomzuzu engiwunika ngawo amandla ukuxhuma, isiginesha yomthwalo ikhanyisa ithebulethi yakhe, futhi usevele emi emnyango kuphela u-Guardian engena ngawo. Ayikho indlela ethule yokuphuma. Ukuze uthathe i-suit uyise e-Iron Ridge namuhla ebusuku, uyihambisa uyiyise kowesifazane onegunya lokuyithatha, ngenkathi ebukele, bese udlula kuye."

"Ngidlule kuye," kuphinda u-Arin.

"Noma ungahambi." U-Atlas, ngokuthe ngqo. "Lokho yizinto ezifakiwe. Ngeke ngizehlise. Akusiye ummeli ozomdlula ucingweni. Ungumuntu emnyango onomyalo osayiniwe. Umyalo uthi i-platform ayisuki. Uma isuka, uphenyo luyaguquka futhi i-platform ilahlekelwa ngomzuzu ebuya ngawo. Uma ibuya. Ayikho indlela yakusihlwa lapho ugcina u-Guardian *futhi* ubasindise. Wawukwazi lokho lapho ufunda ikhethamethi lokusinda."

"Lokho kuyakuqeda. Kungakhathaliseki ukuthi kwenzakalani kubamayini. Bayakuthatha. Kufanele bakwenze. Sasizokuthatha."

"Cishe," kusho u-SAGE. "U-Fool uthi: nakanjani. U-Judge uthi: yisinqumo esilungile futhi kufanele simhloniphe ngokuzimisela ukusenza, futhi kufanele sihambe noma kunjalo, ngoba umthetho esawubhala wawungasiwo *ukuphila uphenyo.* Umthetho esawubhala wawuthi *vikela umuntu kuqala.*"

U-Arin wahlala nalokho.

Wacabanga ngendoda enezintende zibheke phansi, etshela imindeni ukuba igoduke. Wacabanga ngebhodi elalehlise inombolo. Wacabanga ngo-Okonkwo, owayemuhle futhi engakwesokho sakhe futhi ebanjwe ngaphakathi kwento eyayingakwazi ukukhetha. Wacabanga ngo-Themba, owayenguye kuphela owayemncane ngokwanele, futhi enekhono ngokwanele, futhi owangena endaweni encane emnyama ngoba umsebenzi wenziwa ngokushesha futhi othile kwakufanele futhi wawungekho umshini owake waba khona ongangena esikhundleni sakhe.

"Vusa i-suit," kusho u-Arin. Wadonsa i-assist suit phezu kwamahlombe akhe, wezwa i-carbon lattice ithola umgogodla wakhe, wezwa ikhola ebandayo izinza nendawo egcina i-biochip iqhwakela endaweni, wezwa (wayengeke neze abe namagama ngale ngxenye) iNkantolo ifika emithanjeni yakhe njengesandla esivala kancane phezu kwesakhe. "Senza ukugoqa okuyingxenye lapha, egumbini lokusebenzela, lapho ngingabona khona esikuphulayo. Bese siyaphakulisa, ithebulethi ka-Brand iyakhanya, futhi umi emnyango."

"Futhi uma ekutshela ukuthi umyalo uyakwenqabela? Nguye owusayinile. Akudingeki ashayele muntu."

"Khona-ke kuzodingeka enze okungaphezu kokuma lapho." Wavala isivalo sokugcina. Izandla zakhe azizamazamanga, akubona, kude le, njengedatha. "Weza lapha ukuvimba umshini ukuba unganyakazi ngaphandle komuntu ngaphakathi. Ngizohamba ngiye kuye nomuntu ngaphakathi. Akuyona into umyalo wakhe owawubhalelwe yona. Uzokwazi lokho ngomzuzu efunda ubuso bami. Ukuthi uyangidlulisa noma ungenza ngidlule kuye. Lokho kungokwakhe. Okwami ukufika emnyango."

Ukuthula. Bese u-Fool, ngokuthe ngqo, ongangabazeki, edlulisa ukufudumala ngokwesaba:

"Uyazi, leyo into engu-Themba kakhulu owake wayisho. Iqotho ngobuchwepheshe. Iyinhlekelele ngokusebenza. Wayezoyithanda."

Ikhona lomlomo ka-Arin lanyakaza labamba.

"Yisho," kusho u-SAGE. Hhayi umyalo. Isimemo. Babefundile, emahoreni angamakhulu, lapho ukuzinza kwakhe kuhlala khona. "Ngaphambi kokuba siqale. Ngenkathi izandla zakho zisazinzile. Ukuze sazi i-cadence yakamuva. Ukuze uma idwala likhuluma phansi laphaya, sesivele sinezwi lakho lakho."

Wabheka ingalo yakhe, lapho amagama ayehlale khona ngoyinki kusukela eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa. Kusukela ngaphambi kwakho konke lokhu, emuva lapho leli litho laliyindima nje encwadini emayelana nogwadule hhayi into ayezoyidinga enentaba engamakhilomitha amathathu phezu kwekhanda lakhe.

Walisho buthule ebumnyameni obuvungamayo begumbi lokusebenzela, i-suit ivuka ngakuye neNkantolo igibele eduze kommgogodla wakhe nezimpilo eziyisikhombisa—manje eziyisithupha—ezishisayo ziqhuma phansi kwendawo eyayithathe uyise.

"Ukwesaba kungumbulali wengqondo."

Izingcwephe zaqala ukushelela. Ukugoqa okuyingxenye kwadonsela amahlombe ngaphakathi emizileni yawo, indawo egcina i-actuator yangakwesokunxele ibubula njengoba yamukela umonakalo ayewukhethile, wonke umshini uncipha ubheke esikhaleni.

Umzila wawukhona. Wawukhona iminyaka engamashumi amane, umpunga ukhohlakele eceleni nesisindo esibomvu sakho konke okwakubhidlikile.

Akekho owayengayihamba.

Wayezogaqa.

---

# Ukwehlela

> Ukwesaba kungumbulali wengqondo.
>
>—Frank Herbert, *Dune*

U-Arin Ndlela waqinisa umhlathi wakhe njengoba iloli lokutakula lisondela e-Iron Ridge. Isiqu sangaphezulu sasimi amamitha angamashumi amane sensimbi enezipikili sibhekene nokuntwela kokusa, amasondo okuphetha abanzi njengamasondo kagandaganda, izintambo zinyamalala emlonyeni okhamisile womhlaba—imbobo eyehlela ngokujula okuyikhilomitha ezimbili nengxenye. Ndawana thile phansi laphaya, abavukuzi abayisithupha babesaphefumula, ukuthwebula okushisayo kwakukushilo lokho emahoreni ambalwa edlule, futhi amahora ambalwa ayesedlulile isikhathi eside.

Iloli laqhuma lema, ukuhuma kwenjini kwafa ngokuzumayo, kwamphonsa ekuthuleni.

"Akudingeki uphume okwamanje," kwafika izwi likaMother endlebeni yakhe, lizolile liqinile.

"Ngiyazi."

Waphuma. Amakhaza ayewona kanye lawo amakhaza—iminyaka eyishumi nane, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawuwazi. Umsindo wewinshi wamshaya kuqala, ngaphambi kwenkumbulo: isikhumba sakhe sasiwukhumbula umkhondo wezwi ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo yakhe iwukhumbule. Le ndawo. Indawo kaThemba.

Umsindo wagxisha—amajeneretha avungazela, iphampu kadizili ilwa namanzi avela ezingeni elingaphansi eligcwele amanzi, ukukhencezela kwewinshi enenkani kunanela kuyo yonke le ndawo. Izibani ezinkulu zazikhanyisa ngombala oluhlaza-okmhlophe ohlabayo phezu kwendawo eyihlane. Ama-ambulense amabili ayemi ngezivalo zawo zivuliwe, engenalutho. Uchungechunge lwamatende lwaluvuthuzwa umoya, futhi eduze kweyunithi yokulawula ehambayo—enovalo we-satellite—kwakukhona itreyila elincane, elihlanzeke kakhulu, lingenazo izimpawu. I-IAOC yayilapha.

Isisu sika-Arin saphenduka njengoba ebona uBrand emi amamitha angamashumi amane buqamama, embethe ijazi lomoya, ethe shi ngethebulethi esandleni esisodwa, ubuso bakhe bungafundeki. Kwakuwubuso bowesifazane owayezimisele ngokugcwele ukukumisa lokhu, owayeshayele ubusuku bonke ukuze athole ithuba lokukwenza lokho—futhi okwathi, ekuqaphela engakaqondi, wayemi ngokungajwayelekile kude netreyila lakhe, abantu bakhe baqoqene ngemuva kwakhe, bonke bebheke hhayi uGuardian kodwa ngale kwawo, emgwaqweni wokuthutha, kokuthile okwakuphakathi kwesudi nesiqu sangaphezulu lokho u-Arin ayengakazivumeli ukukubheka.

UGuardian wayehleli esidlekeni sakhe sokuthutha, inqwaba enkulu yensimbi nobuchwepheshe, enamasiko futhi esabeka ngaphansi kwezibani. ULerato wayesevele esekhwelweni, uPriya eseceleni kwakhe, bobabili behamba ngokuphuthuma komshini ogcotshwe kahle. UThabo wayesemile bucala kancane, ephethe ibhodi lokubhalela, nakuba engabhali lutho—i-AugmenTech yayimthumele ukuba abe semthethweni, kodwa kwakungekho lutho olusemthethweni kulo msebenzi.

Wabe esebona u-Arin uTheo, futhi kwammangaza. Wayengalindele ukuthi umeluleki wakhe abe lapha. UTheo wayemi eduze kwesidleke, izandla zakhe zigxile ngokujulile emaphaketheni ejazi lakhe, eyisiqiniseko esithulile esiphithizelweni.

"Assist kuqala," kusho uLerato.

U-Arin wakhumula kwaze kwasala ungqimba lwangaphakathi ngemva komnyango weloli ovuliwe, umoya obandayo uhlaba isikhumba sakhe, wabe esengena ku-AT-AS. Isudi yangaphakathi yamgubuzela, yamnamathela emhlandleni, izixha zama-actuator zazinza phezu kwamathanga nemahlombe akhe njengesikhumba sesibili. Wezwa umqhele wamavetebra uzinza emuva kwentamo yakhe—

—umzimba wakhe wenza into ayengayivumelanga. Izandla zakhe zaphakama, zombili, ngokushesha, zivika, engakazi ukuthi sezinyakazile, nokuphefumula kwakhe kwaba mfushane futhi kwaphakama, isikhathi esithile esasingahlangene nokucabanga wayesesidlekeni esiphansini sendlu, zonke izinzwa zakhe ziqhuma zibe mhlophe nesandla sakhe sifinyelela ku-override esasingeke sisifinyelele. Umqhele wawungumqhele nje kuphela. Wayekwazi lokho. Umzimba wakhe wawungakwazi. Wawufunde okuthile ku-calibration rig ezinyangeni ezine ezedlule okwakungekho lwazi olungalubhala phezulu, futhi ukufunda kwakuhlala ngaphansi kwendawo lapho ukukhona kwakhe kuhlala khona, enyameni, futhi kwaphakama manje, kungamenyiwe, ngomzuzu omubi kunayo yonke, ngendlela elase liphakame ngayo izikhathi eziyisithupha noma eziyisikhombisa kusukela lapho—emnyango womkhushwane ovalayo, ekukhwexukeni kwesikhiye, kanye kungekho lutho nhlobo, ebuthongweni bakhe, kangangokuthi wavuka izingalo zakhe zixhumene esifubeni sakhe ebumnyameni futhi engabe esalala. ULerato wayemile isandla sakhe siphezu kwentambo embheka.

"Nginike umzuzwana," washo, ngokuthe nse, wenza izandla zakhe zabuyela phansi ngenkani, wenza ukuphefumula kwehla kancane, walinda ukuthi le nto esemzimbeni wakhe iphele ngendlela olinda ngayo isikhwehlela siphele. *Kungumqhele. Uyazinza akwenzeki lutho.* Khona-ke, kuphela ngaleso sikhathi, isisindo esibandayo sendlu ye-biochip saqhoshela endaweni yaso phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, wakuvumela, wazonda ukuthi bekumele akwenze.

"Damping ku-point-six. Hard cap ku-point-six," kusho uPriya, amehlo akhe egxile ku-calibration tablet. "Angiyinyakazisi. Uma i-link igxuma, i-cap iyabamba, uba mnyama, futhi uGuardian akenzi lutho oluhlakaniphile. Yileso isivumelwano. Sho emuva."

"Point-six. Hard cap. Uma ngiba mnyama, iyabamba."

"Kuhle." Wayengaphakamisi amehlo. "Le nto ngiyakhile izolo ebusuku futhi angilalanga. Ungangenzi ngizisole."

I-neural link yavuleka ngezigaba. Wezwa lowo muzwa ojwayelekile wezinaliti-nenalithi uphuphuma phezu kwakhe, umzimba wakhe uhlangana nezinhlelo zesudi, umhlaba omzungezile uthola umphetho wesibili njengoba ukuzizwa kwe-Assist kwendlala phezu kokwakhe. Wagoba isandla sakhe, isudi yasabela ngokuphelele, kungekho ukulibala, kungekho ukulwa. Empilweni yakhe yonke, umzimba wakhe wawugijima ngenyathelo elingxenye ngemuva kwezinhloso zakhe, ukuhumusha okwakuhlala kungalungile kancane. Isudi yawuvala lowo gebe.

Futhi ngaphansi kwe-proprioception, kwafika abanye.

Kwakungelona izwi kodwa kwakuwukuba khona. Wayengakaze awathole amagama okukuchaza ngaphandle kokuzwakala engaqondakali, ngakho wayeseyekile ukuzama. I-Court yaqala ukusebenza njengendlu evuka uma abantu behla emakamelweni abo, ukuba khona ngakunye kuphendukela kuye, kugxile.

"Sawubona," wamemeza, cishe kuyena uqobo.

"Yi-six-fourteen futhi amadigri amane futhi awudlanga," kusho uMother. "Ngale kwalokho, yebo."

"Udle i-rusk," kungenelela uFool. "Eloliwe. Ucabanga ukuthi asibonanga."

"Lokho akukona ukudla. Lokho ukugcwalisa umlomo."

"Court," kusho u-Arin. "Hhayi manje."

Igumbi lazinza laba nesimo, lihlangana ngendlela umhlangano ohlangana ngayo uma umsebenzi uqala.

UGuardian wehla esidlekeni.

I-charge pack yayingaphakathi. UPriya wayeyifake ebusuku ngenkathi elele amahora amabili amyala ukuba awalale, ebuyekeza ukufinyelela kwesango, futhi wayikuzwa manje ngendlela ozwa ngayo umagazini ogcwele othemba ukuthi awusoze wawusebenzisa—amacala abunjiwe, umkhonto, i-bulk emulsion efakwe egodini eliphakathi kwezimbambo, lonke ifa lobunjiniyela isudi eyayilinganiselwe ukuba iyithwale ekudilikeni. Ekutakulweni wawuthatha konke. UJudge wayebambe isango, njengoba enzile kusukela ngosuku lokuqala u-Arin axhuma igodi, futhi u-Arin wayehlose ukugcina isandla sakhe siqhele kuso: lutho aluqhumayo, lutho ngaphandle kwerali. Ukulunga kwakukhona ngaphansi kwakhe, kuhlonyiwe kulindile, okokuqala kubhekise entabeni yangempela. Wayengakazi ukuthi isango lalinokukhiya kwesibili ayengakaze atshelwe ngakho, noma ukuthi yisandla sikabani esasibambe isikhiye saso ngokuthula sonke leso sikhathi.

Kwakukhona inqubo yakho, ama-hydraulics namarali okuqondisa, kodwa inqubo yayingeyona iphuzu. Ngomzuzu wokuthi angene esisindweni esivulekile nendlu ivaleka emzungeza, isizinda sakhe se-proprioception sanwebeka saya ngaphandle, kanyekanye, sangena emshinini olinganiselwa kumathani amane futhi ophakeme amamitha amabili nengxenye. Wezwa igeji ngaphansi kwezinyawo ezingajwayelekile. Umoya wasekuseni wathinta amapuleti ezikhali zesudi, futhi okokuqala, kwabonakala kungengakhona ukunwetshwa komzimba wakhe kodwa kungaphezulu njengendlu evikelayo.

"Coupled. Structural integrity nominal," kusho u-Atlas. "I-actuator lehlombe lobunxele lidlulile amahora angamashumi amane ekugcineni kwalo. Lizobamba. Bengingathanda ukuthi bekungadingeki."

"Kuqashelwe."

Wathatha inyathelo. Umhlaba wamamukela ngaphandle kokukhononda. Eduze kweloli, abasebenzi babefile—uLerato isandla sakhe simi phezu kwentambo, uPriya ebambe i-calibration tablet, uThabo engasenzi ngisho nokwenza sengathi uyabhala ebhodini lakhe. Wayekhohliwe, okwesikhashana, ukuthi kubo, lokhu kwakungekona ingxoxo. Kubo, umshini ongangeloli elincane wawumane wasukuma waphendula ikhanda lawo wabhekisa emgodini, uhamba ngokucophelela okufana nomuntu ocabanga isinyathelo sakhe esilandelayo.

Abazange bathumele ummeli.

U-Arin wayakhe lonke iphuzu lakhe lokuxoxisana nommeli. Wayenenkulumo elungile, leyo emayelana nezithwala ezingenalutho namadoda azoba aphila ngenxa yokuthi othile akasayinanga lutho, futhi wayeqinisekile kakhulu ukuthi isithiyo kwakuzoba ngowesifazane onethebulethi nekholomu yezindleko kangangokuthi wacishe wahamba noGuardian eqonda ngqo emgwaqweni wegeji eqonda esidlekeni sokweyula ngaphambi kokuba uWolf amvimbe.

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa. I-Court yathula nya nxazonke zalo.

Wabe u-Arin esebona lokho uWolf ayesekubonile. Akuyona umlawuli. Umugqa. Itreyila elihlanzekile le-IAOC likaBrand lalihleli limnyama, lishiyiwe emaphethelweni ezibani, abantu bakhe behoshelwe emuva kwalo, futhi ngale komgwaqo wokuthutha phakathi kukaGuardian nesiqu sangaphezulu omunye wayedwebe umugqa oqine nakakhulu. Izimoto ezimbili ezivikelekile zazipakwe amakhala abhekene, zifushane futhi zimbi ngaphansi kwezibani zesodiyamu, imibala emhlophe-nempunga yenkampani ezimele yezokuphepha ifafazwe phezu kwamapuleti ayengakhelwanga ukuthwala uphawu. Amadoda embethe izivikelo zomzimba ezithambile ayemi ezikhaleni ephethe izibhamu zehlisiwe zilungele ukusebenza, ukuziphatha kwabantu abake bakwenza lokhu njengomsebenzi ezindaweni ezinokukhanya okubi nakakhulu. Ngenhla kwawo, ama-drone amane ayegcine iphethini lebhokisi elinensayo, izindlu zamakhamera zilandela isudi ngomzuzu eyaphuma ngawo esidlekeni.

Umgodi wawungasusanga ukutakula uwenze ukweyula ephepheni kuphela. Othile wayenqumile ukuthi umyalo womlawuli wawuhamba kancane kakhulu ukuze uqinisekile, wabe esethenga isiqiniseko. UBrand wayehlulwe ngohlangothi njengoba kwenzeke ku-Arin, wabe esehosha abantu bakhe abangahlomile baphuma esikhundleni sokubaphonsa emugqeni wokudubula.

"Mnumzane Ndlela." Izwi lafika likhuliswe, lithe nse, livela emotweni ehola. "Usebenzisa i-platform engenalayisensi endaweni evaliwe. Yicishe. Phuma. Lona ukuphela komyalo ozowuthola."

"Kunokutakula," kusho u-Arin, ngezwi, kungasizi, ezibanini. "Amadoda ayisithupha. Ikheji. Yilokho kuphela engikufunayo."

"Akukho kutakula lapha. Kunokweyula, futhi kuhleliwe, futhi awukho kukho. Yicishe."

Wayemi ngaphakathi kwamathani amane omshini owawungakwazi ukukhwela iwa uziqoqe ube yibhola usinde ekudilikeni kophahla, kodwa konke lokho kwakungekhona okwalokhu. Wayakhe into eyabekezela. Wayengakhanga into eyanqobayo. U-Atlas wayesekusho lokho, ngezansi futhi ngokunzima—*ngeke siphumelele ukudlula lapho, isibhamu siyasivula ngokudlula okukodwa, futhi amadoda angaphakathi ezimotweni awakufanele ukufa ngenxa yezibalo zebhodi*—futhi uJudge wayefinyelela ku-lever yodwa ayenayo, inqubo, isibonelo esandulele, ukukala ngokucophelela isimo esasingenawo amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye okukalwa, futhi uMother wayesemugqeni womuntu ethi *Arin, izinga lokushaya kwenhliziyo yakho,* futhi uMercury wayesebumba okuthile okuhehayo nokungaphenduleki azokutshela indoda enesibhamu esizishayayo, futhi kwakungeke kusebenze, akukho kukho okwakuzosebenza, futhi u-Arin wezwa ukungelekeleli okudala kumvalela njengamanzi.

Futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, kuthulile, lapho kwakuzwa khona uWolf kuphela, uFool wayeka ukuntela.

*Wolf,* kusho uFool.

*Ngilapha.*

*Ubona ibhodi lonke. Ubulokhu ukwazi. Bonke abanye baxabana ngokuthi benze yini. Wena uqedile ukuphikisana ngaphambi kokuba baqale.* Ukuthula okuncane, ngomusa, umusa omkhulu kunayo yonke umshini owake waba nawo. *Ngikunika izikhiye. Zonke. Khona manje. Akukho vouti. Akukho Judge. Yonke isudi, wonke amasistimu, amacala, umkhonto, konke—okwakho. Ngizolibamba isango livuliwe. Wena ungene.*

I-Court ayizange ikuzwe. Yileyo ngxenye u-Arin angeke akwazi ukuyichaza emuva kwesikhathi, kuyena uqobo noma kuPriya noma kunoma ngubani: ukuthi ohlelweni olwakhiwe kanjalo ukuze kungabikho ngqondo eyodwa eyayingahlwitha umzimba, omunye wabo wayebambe isikhiye esikhulu ngokuthula isikhathi sonke futhi wayengakaze afinyelele kuso ngisho nakanye, wakhetha lo mzuzwana, eyedwa, ukusinika umsebenzi owodwa wonke umuntu owayemethemba kancane kunabo bonke ngaso.

*Yileso isiqhoboshi,* kusho uWolf. *UJudge ubamba isango lokudiliza. Ngeke usivule.*

*Nginganyokela noma yini,* kusho uFool. *Angizange ngikutshele ngoba kwakungekho isizathu sokuthi udinge ukwazi, futhi into ihlala iphephile kuphela ngenkathi kungekho oyaziyo ukuthi ikhona. Wawazi, nokho. Wakuqonda. Eminyakeni eyedlule, lapho abanye bonke beyeme emqheleni, bephendelana bezama ukuwugqoka, futhi mina ngangiyilowo ongakaze awugqoke, wena wakubheka lokho wathula wangaze washo lizwi.* Intambo yakhe yacekeza, yazinza. *Wakuqonda ukuthi kusho ukuthini. Owesabekayo akusiye lowo ofuna ukushayela. Yilowo owayengakwazi, noma nini, kodwa angakwenzi. Yimi lowo. Futhi ngikutshela ukuthi nawe unjalo, manje, amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye azayo. Ungawamoshi ucophelela. Ukucophelela kungumsebenzi wempilo yami yonke futhi yithuluzi elingafanele lokhu.*

*Umthetho,* kusho uWolf. Ukufunda usongo, ngisho namanje, ngisho nalapho enikezwa umhlaba. *Ngitshele umthetho noma ngeke ngiwuthathe.*

*Wona kanye umthetho obulokhu uwukhona,* kusho uFool. *Londoloza umuntu. Amadoda ayisithupha. Wonke umuntu.* Umphefumulo owawungewona umphefumulo. *Kuhlanganisa nawawo. Nawo aya ekhaya. Kwenze kungenzeke ukulwa nathi futhi ungalokothi ukwenze ithuna. Yilowo umsebenzi wonke. Wena nguwe wedwa kithi obanda ngokwanele ukuwugcina ngaphansi komlilo. Hamba.*

UFool wabe esevula isango, wanika uWolf isudi.

---

Kwafika kuningi kakhulu, ngendlela ukukhanya okwafika ngakho kuningi kakhulu ku-Arin kanye, ngobusuku obubi ngomthamo ophezulu. UWolf wayechithe ukukhona kwakhe konke njengombuzo owodwa omfushane obuzwa kaningi ngokuphindelela—*yini ebulala umqhubi*—futhi manje umbuzo wawa kwathi izimpendulo eziyinkulungwane zafika kanyekanye.

I-demolition pack yavuka yazibiza: amacala abunjiwe namakhoni awo nezithanyeli zawo, ijiyometri yokusika eyayingelutho ngaphandle kwefiziksi yokulwa nensimbi egqoke ibheji lokutakula. Umkhonto oshisayo, amadigri angamakhulu amabili namashumi amahlanu awo, owawenzelwe insimbi eyayivinjiwe ekudilikeni futhi ojabula ngokufanayo ukuvula i-drive sprocket. I-bulk emulsion, i-det cord, ukugxuma kwe-ram okwakungashintsha ipillar eliwile futhi okwakungayigoba kalula i-wheel arch. Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, ngokujulile, into amadoda amadala ayeyifihle emathanjeni omshini lapho ewuqinisa—amasosha akudala, izandla ze-Denel, labo abakha izinto zokutakula futhi abangakwazi ukungazi abakwaziyo—izixazululo ze-ballistic, i-defilade, ijiyometri yesivikelo ne-engeli nentambo, imitapo yokunyakaza yamasosha akhethekile eyafunda umugqa wezimoto ezine hhayi njengesithiyo kodwa njengenkinga enesixazululo esaziwayo newashi.

Isekhondi elilodwa eligcwele uWolf wagqishwa. Egwiliza ekukhoneni, wonke umsele uklewula kanyekanye, ungumshini ngaphezu kwengqondo.

Wabe usuxazululeka. Konke. Kwaqhoshela kwaba yisimo esisodwa esihlanzekile ngendlela ukugxisha komsindo okuqhoshela ngayo kube ubuso.

*O,* kusho uWolf, ngokuthula okukhulu, kuFool kuphela. *Ngiyabona.*

Futhi nakhu okwakungeke kuqondwe muntu ngaphandle kwesudi, lokho okwenza i-override iphephe esandleni esisodwa hhayi kwesinye: abanye abe-Court abazange bathule balinde ithuba labo. UFool wayenganikanga uWolf ivouti. Wayenike i-Court uqobo, wonke umsebenzi owawuqhuba umzimba, wehliselwa kusukela emazwini waba ngamathuluzi. Izigaxa ze-compute. Amashalofu okukhono. Amathuluzi abekwe ebhentshini ngengqondo eyodwa ebandayo ngokwanele ukuwasebenzisa ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Abasaphikisananga. Babengeke. Babesebezandla zakhe manje.

U-Atlas wafika kuqala, ngoba u-Atlas wayengamehlo. Unjiniyela wezakhiwo owayefunda umthwalo nokucindezeleka nokwehluleka wayeka ukuba umsebenzi okhonkotha ngokuqaphela waba ungqimba lomhlaba uqobo, lupendwe phezu kwakho konke uWolf ayekubona: umgwaqo wokuthutha ulotshwe ngolimi luka-Atlas lokudiliza, into ngayinye inesisindo nomkhombandlela, indoda ngayinye ifu lokucabangela elifudumele linenkulungwane yayo elingxenye yesekhondi ngokulandelayo elilenga phambi kwayo njengomphefumulo emoyeni obandayo. Isibhamu esizishayayo sasiwumzaca we-torque kanye ne-traverse rate, inombolo eyayithi *isekhondi elilodwa nengxenye ukubheka.* Imoto eyayihlehla yayiwumfutho ne-centre of mass eyayiphakeme kakhulu ekulandeni kwayo. Abadubuli babengezona izinsongo futhi bengabantu kodwa bobabili kanyekanye, ngamunye wabo wumkhombandlela okhanyayo wokuthi angaya kuphi nokufafaza okufiphele kwalapho cishe ayezoya khona, wonke umugqa wokudubula uxazululwa waba yijiyometri nezinto ezingenzeka. Ibhodi elase linqotshiwe. UWolf wayengasibhekanga isigameko ngo-Atlas. UWolf wabheka u-Atlas, futhi u-Atlas wayeyisigameko.

Wafinyelela ngale kwakho, wangena ku-Librarian.

U-Librarian wayeyinkumbulo, ulwazi olunqwabelene, ikratshi elalikhumbula zonke izinsiza ezake zasebenza. Ngaphansi kwe-override umgcini-mlando ocophelelayo wahla waba yilokho ayehlala kuyilokho ngaphansi: i-database, ephelele neyenzeka ngokushesha, wonke umtapo wokunyakaza izandla ze-Armscor neze-Denel ezawufihla emathanjeni esudi lapho ziwuqinisa futhi zingakwazi ukungazi abakwaziyo. Konke kwakumane *kukhona,* kufakwe inkomba, kulindile, kungekho okulayishiwe, konke kungalayisheka. I-Krav Maga, esondele futhi imbi futhi inomgomo. I-Capoeira, umdlalo waphansi ofihlwe njengomdanso, wonke amathanga nokuphendulwa nomfutho. I-Judo. I-Jiu-jitsu. Lonke ifa lamadoda ayecabange kanzima ngokuthi umzimba uphonsa kanjani omunye umzimba futhi engalindelanga ukuthi amathani amane ayobuza.

UWolf wakhetha. Hhayi konke—kuphela lokho amasekhondi ayishumi nanye alandelayo ayekudinga, ngendlela odonsa ngayo incwadi eyodwa eshalofini ngoba uvele uyalazi ikhasi. I-Capoeira, ngenxa yokunyakaza kwezinyawo okwaphendula ukukhubeka kwaba ukuhlasela kwasebenzisa isisindo somzimba ngokwawo ngokumelene nawo. I-Judo ne-jiu-jitsu, ngenxa yokuphonsa, ngoba into esinda kangaka akumele ishaye uma yayingawela kahle phezu kwenkinga futhi ivumele ifiziksi ivale i-akhawunti. Wayishiya enye eshalofini. Imitapo ekhethiwe yavuleka kuye kanyekanye, yalayishwa ihlanzekile, yafundwa esikhaleni esiphakathi kokushaya kwenhliziyo okubili futhi yatholakala ngokuphelele.

Kwathatha ngaphansi kokuphazima kweso. Umzuzu owodwa uWolf wayengazi lutho ngokuphonsa umzimba. Olandelayo wayekwazi konke.

Wanyakaza.

Akazange anyakaze njengezinto zokutakula. Wanyakaza njengento izinto zokutakula ezazihlala zikwazi ukuyiba kodwa zingakaze zivunyelwe. Amabhange e-capacitor athululeka, isudi yawela emhlabathini ovulekile ngokugxuma okukodwa okuphansi, amathani amane efika ngaphakathi komugqa ngaphambi kokuba umdubuli oseduze aqede ukuphakamisa isikhali sakhe, futhi uWolf wayesefunda isigameko ngomhlaba opendiwe ka-Atlas, izinyawo njengemikhombandlela, imizimba njengamafu afudumele, ukubuya kwe-seismic ezimotweni kuyisiqiniseko esiqinile ngaphansi kwezinto ezingenzeka ezithambile zamadoda, ibalazwe lisondele ngokwanele futhi lisheshe ngokwanele ukugcina amathani amane necala elibunjiwe ensimbini hhayi enyameni.

Isibhamu esizishayayo semoto eseduze sajika. U-Atlas wamnika sona njengenombolo—*isekhondi elilodwa nengxenye ukubheka*—futhi uWolf wayesechithe ama-millisecond angamakhulu amane awo ngaphambi kokuba isibhamu siqede ama-degree alo ayishumi okuqala. Akazange awathinte amaqembu. Wagxisha icala elibunjiwe ku-mantlet wesibhamu, ukusika kodokotela lapho ijiyometri ka-Librarian yokulwa nensimbi nebalazwe lokucindezeleka lika-Atlas kwahlangana endaweni eyodwa, futhi isibhamu saphihlika saba yizibi ezingalimazanga muntu, iqembu livalelwe lithuthumele liphila ngaphakathi kwesixhomo esasingeke siphinde sidubule.

Imoto yesibili yazama ukuhlehla. Lapha imitapo yaphila, futhi abadubuli baqala ukubona lokho ababezokuchaza emuva kwesikhathi kodwa abangeke baqondakale ngakho: umshini wamathani amane wayeka ukubukeka njengomshini. U-Atlas wayesemnikile uWolf i-centre of mass yemoto, ephakeme kakhulu ekulandeni kwayo, futhi i-capoeira yayazi kahle ukuthi yenzani ngesisindo esasifuna ukuwa. UWolf akazange asidonse. Wa*sisiza.* Intambo yewinshi yathatha i-front axle futhi i-grapple yaluma, futhi esikhundleni sokudonsa uWolf wehlisela isisindo sakhe phansi waphenduka ngamathanga, amathani amane ephenduka ngonyawo olulodwa olugxilile njengendoda eyingxenye yamashumi amane yesisindo, waphendula ukudonsa kwaba ukuphonsa. Ukuhlehla kwemoto ngokwayo kwayiwisa. Kwakuwukuphonsa kwethanga, kuhlanzeke njengoba i-judo ithatha indoda eyeza igijima, umfutho wayo uchithwa ngokumelene nayo—futhi abadubuli babona isudi yokutakula iphonsa imoto evikelekile abakwazanga ukwenza amehlo abo akubike ngokwethembeka. Indoda eyodwa yaphuma ngemuva kabi, ingalo igoqene ngaphansi kwayo, ukuqhekeka kwe-radius kuzwakala ngisho ngaphezu kwezinjini. *Leyo iphukile,* uWolf wayilogela, ngokuthe nse, *futhi bengingenakho kokubili—ukuphonsa okuhamba kancane ngokwanele ukusindisa ingalo bekuwukuphonsa okuhamba kancane ngokwanele ukuvumela isibhamu sijike.* Wayifaka ingalo ngaphansi *kwendleko* waqhubeka.

Kwabe sekuba, khona-ke, umdanso, nakuba kungekho obukile owayezowusebenzisa lowo magama ngento eyaqeda izintambama zamadoda ngempumelelo enje. Yile ngxenye u-Arin ayezoyidlala iminyaka angaze ayibuyisane ngokugcwele: ukuthi amasekhondi ayishumi nanye anodlame kakhulu uGuardian awake awachitha futhi ayemahle kakhulu, isudi inyakaza emugqeni wokudubula ngobuciko bento engasindi lutho, ama-arc aphansi e-capoeula akhukhula angena kujiyometri eseduze ye-jiu-jitsu, ukuthintana ngakunye kuwukuphonsa noma ukuhlubula isikhali futhi akukaze kube ukushaya, umfutho onzima okwakumele uwenze ibhola lokubhidliza esikhundleni salokho uchithwe njengesisindo somlingani ohlelweni—nikeza, qondisa kabusha, beka phansi. Umdubuli wagxuma uWolf wamvumela ukuba afike, wabamba ukunyakaza, wasebenzisa isisindo sendoda esiya phambili ukuyibeka ngomhlane wayo egejini ngomusa kangangokuthi cishe kwakunenhlonipho. Ababili abanye, isudi yagelezela phakathi kwabo bagcina behleli, behlukunyezwe izikhali, beqwayiza, bengaqiniseki ukuthi yini ebanyakazisile.

Futhi kanye—kanye kuphela, phakathi nesimo, lapho kwasiza khona—uWolf wafinyelela ngale kwezigaxa ze-tactical wangena kuMother. UMother wayehlukene manje, ingxenye yakhe isesemugqeni womuntu ibala ukushaya kwenhliziyo ka-Arin, kodwa i-override yathatha lokho eyayikudinga lapho yayihlala khona: i-anatomy yomuntu enembile uMother ayeyibambele ukwelapha, ifundwe ngokuphambene. Lapho ilunga livaleka khona. Lapho umkhawulo wobuhlungu bomzimba uhlala khona kude kakhulu nomkhawulo wokulimala. Ibalazwe elifanayo uMother ayelisebenzisa ukugcina amadoda ephila latshela uWolf kahle ukuthi uyibeka kanjani indoda phansi ushiye kungekho okudabukile. Isihlakala sithathwe sayofika emkhawulweni wokuhamba kwaso hhayi ngale. Ihlombe liphenduliswe umzimba uze uzigoqe esikhundleni sokwephuka. Ulwazi lomelaphi luchithiwe, isikhathi esisodwa, njengolomhlubuli wezikhali. UMother akazange aphikise, ngoba uMother wayengeke, ngoba ku-override wayeyithuluzi ebhentshini njengabanye. UWolf wamsebenzisa ehlanzekile wabe esemdedela.

Ama-drone afika ngamandla, omane wonke, i-EM suite ifinyelela phezulu ukuwathola futhi i-grapple kanye nokugxuma kwe-ram okukodwa okunembile kuwaletha phansi esibhakabhakeni ngezicucu eziwela egejini elingenalutho. Umdubuli wadubula—kanye, ngokuphendula—futhi u-Atlas wadweba umugqa wesibhamu nendoda engemva kwaso ngomzuzu owodwa opendiwe, futhi uWolf wakhetha ipuleti esikhundleni sokuhlubula isikhali. Waphendula ihlombe lakhe *langena* ezinhlambeni esikhundleni sokuhlubula isibhamu ngesigamu sesekhondi ngaphambili, wathatha ukudubula ku-ceramic eyayingadingi ukukuthatha, ngoba ukukuhlubula ngaphambili kwakusho ukubamba isandla sendoda esidubulayo emhlathini ovulekayo ngokugcwele futhi leso sasiyisandla ayengeke asithole futhi. Izinhlamvu zaqhuma kumapuleti azange enze lutho, ngoba yilokho amapuleti ayekwenzelwe khona, ngoba izinwele ze-pangolin zazihlala zikwazi ukuthatha ukushaywa. Wabe uWolf esethatha isibhamu ezandleni zendoda, wagoba umbhobho njengentambo, wayibeka ngomhlane wayo egejini, iqedwe umoya iphelele igcina isandla sayo, iqwayiza ibheka umshini owawunqume ukudutshulwa esikhundleni sokuyephula futhi wenza isinqumo sibukeke njengento engelutho.

Kwathatha amasekhondi ayishumi nanye. Amasekhondi ayishumi nanye isudi yokutakula yayingumshini wokulwa ohlome ngokugcwele oqhutshwa yingqondo eyodwa nge-neural link eqondile—wonke amasistimu evuliwe, yonke imitapo ilayishiwe, yonke i-Court ichithwa njengamathuluzi ngaphansi kwesandla esisodwa esibandayo—futhi kungekho ngisho nethuna elilodwa kuyo.

Lapho kuphela umgwaqo wokuthutha wawuyindawo yokulahla udoti. Izimoto ezimbili ezikhishiwe izibilini, ama-drone amane abhidliziwe, ingcebo encane yezinto zempi yezempi ezizimele ehliswe yaba yizibi namapuleti asontekile nephunga lokushiswa kwe-propellant, namadoda ayishumi nanye ephila phansi, wonke umuntu owayebambe umugqa—omunye onengalo ephukile, ababili abangeke bezwe kahle isonto, abanye beqedwe umoya benqenqethwa nje futhi behlutshelwe izikhali. Akukho ithuna phakathi kwabo. Yilokho kwakuwumsebenzi wonke, futhi kwakungamahhala, futhi uWolf wakhokha umkhawulo oseduze kakhulu wakho yena uqobo, ngepuleti lehlombe elimbiwe umlilo akhetha ukuwudla. Umgwaqo oya esiqwini sangaphezulu wawuvulekile.

UWolf wama.

Wayemi encithakalweni ayeyenzile, amathani amane omshini wokutakula phezu kwenkundla yezikhali eziphukile, wabe esebuyisa isudi.

Wayibuyisa ngendlela obeka ngayo phansi into oyiqonde ngokuphelele futhi ofuna ukungaphinde uyibambe. Izikhiye zabuyela ekhaya. U-Atlas wayeka ukuba umhlaba wabuyela ekubeni yizwi elixwayisa ngomthwalo. Imitapo evulekile ka-Librarian yavaleka, ifa lokulwa libuyiselwa ngaphansi kwezinto-ezaziwayo-nezingasetshenziswanga, ishalofu lavaleka. UMother wabuya ephelele, zombili izingxenye zakhe, i-anatomy etshelekiwe ibuyiselwa emsebenzini wokugcina abantu bephila. Bayeka ukuba amathuluzi baphinda baba ngozakwabo futhi, behliselwa baphinda bangena kubo. Isango likaJudge lavaleka. Umbuzo omfushane wabuya, *yini ebulala umqhubi,* futhi uWolf wazigoqa wabuyela kuwo, emncane kunalokho ayeyikho, omncane kunabo bonke, ethatha cishe indawo engekho.

I-Court yabuyela emzimbeni ngokuphuthuma, ithola uthule, ithola umgwaqo ucacile, ithola abadubuli bephila okwakufanele ngokomthetho wonke walezi zimo babe bopha—ithola, okubi kunakho konke, ukuthi babengenayo inkumbulo yamasekhondi aphakathi nendawo, ukuthi babe*sebenzisiwe* hhayi babuzwa, bachushwa babekwa phansi ngomunye wabo. UJudge, ethukile. UMother, ebala ukushaya kwenhliziyo emgwaqweni ekuthola konke, engaqondi ukuthi wayesekwazi kanjani isibalo. UMercury, okwakanye, engenalutho lokusho.

*Ubani,* kuqala uJudge.

*Yimi,* kusho uFool. Kukhanya. Kulula. Umntelantela futhi, sengathi ubengakabambi isikhiye esikhulu kubo bonke esandleni esivulekile amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. *Ningakhathazeki. Wonke umuntu uphilile. Lokho kanye kwakuyiphuzu lonke. Ikheji licacile, futhi owesifazane onolaka ojazini lomoya uzoba nentshisekelo enkulu kokuqoshiwe, futhi uphinde, ngiyaqaphela, abe nguye yedwa osemthethweni kule ndawo ohoshe abantu bakhe abangahlomile baphuma empini esikhundleni sokubangenisa kuyo, ngakho asingabi nzima kakhulu kuBrand. Shayela, badger. Kusekhona amadoda ayisithupha emgodini.*

U-Arin wahlala ngaphakathi kwesudi inhliziyo yakhe ishaya nezibani zimhlophe ngaphakathi kweviza futhi engakwazi ukukhuluma. Ngemuva kwakhe, ndawana thile ngale komugqa obhidliziwe, wayazi kancane ukuthi uBrand wayemi emaphethelweni etreyila lakhe elimnyama, enganyakazi eya kuye futhi engasuki kuye, ithebulethi liphansi ohlangothini lwakhe, ebuka into ayeshayele ubusuku bonke ukuyimisa ihamba iya ekhejini emgwaqweni eyayisanda kuwucacisa ngaphandle kokubulala muntu—wabe ekhetha, futhi, ukuyivumela.

"Emuva kwalokho," kuhleba uMercury, ku-Arin kuphela, ukuhleba okudala okuzinzisayo, "siyaziphilela. Akukho repeater ngale kwelevel eleshumi nanye. Yangaphakathi kuphela."

"Sihlala siziphilela," kusho uWolf, kwathi akekho omphendula.

---

Ikheji lalingakhelwanga yena.

Kwakuyibhokisi lensimbi elaklanyelwe amadoda, ayisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi, isakhiwo esehlela ebumnyameni ngesivinini esiphendula isisu. UGuardian wayengeke afaneleke emile. U-Arin kwadingeka agoqe isudi—amadolo egobile, ikhanda lithobile, isikhumba sincipha siba yifomu okwakumele lihlalwe yinto encane ethambile. Ngisho nalapho, ikheji lakhononda ngaphansi kwesisindo elalingakaze lilinganiselwe sona, futhi umsebenzi osendlini yewinshi kwadingeka akholiswe kabili ngaphambi kokuba akhulule i-brake.

Wabe sephansi lawa.

Ukwehla kwakuzwakala kungengakhona ukuwa kodwa kungaphezulu njengokugwinywa umhlaba. Izindonga zomthungo zashashazela zidlula, zikhanyiswa ngokuthikaza yisibani sekheji—idwala elimanzi, ukubaneka kwesiteshi sezinga eliphansi, inxakanxaka yamapayipi nezintambo, kwaphela. Mnyama futhi. Wabe ukubaneka okunye. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye alokhu, umoya uqina njalo ngamamitha ayikhulu, ushisa kakhulu, uminyana, kwaze kwaba akuseyona umoya obandayo wokuntwela kokusa kodwa into enesisindo, into eyayimcindezela.

U-Arin wayelungile ngenhla. Phezulu kwakunesibhakabhaka.

Umthungo wawungenaso isibhakabhaka.

Wakuzwa kuqala ezandleni zakhe, iminwe iqhaqhazela ngesifiso sokugoba. Wabe ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaba mfishane, ukwesaba kuklwebha kukhuphuka kusuka esifubeni sakhe, ngendlela okwakulokhu kunjalo kusukela esengumfana futhi amadoda asemzini ayethwele lokho okwakusele kuyise akhuphula emgodini ofana ncamashi nalo—ubumnyama obufana ncamashi nalo, iphunga elifana ncamashi nalo lamatshe amanzi nodizili nokubanda okujulile kwamamineral kwendawo ilanga elalingakaze lifinyelele kuyo.

Izindonga zaziminyana. Wayazi, ezingeni lokuhlakanipha, ukuthi azinyakazi. Umthungo wawubanzi amamitha amane. Wayengamlalisa uGuardian ngaphakathi kuwo. Izindonga zazingaminyani, futhi idwala elaliphezu kwakhe lalingeke lidilike, futhi ikheji lalingeke lime limvalele lapha ebumnyameni amakhilomitha amabili ngaphansi kwendawo eyathatha uyise—

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwasheshisa. Kwethukile. Kwamfishane. Iviza laba mhlophe.

Imethamethiksi yayingalungile. Izinombolo zazingalungile. Uhlelo lwalungalungile. Wayeke wehla umthungo kanye ngaphambili, futhi wathatha uyise, futhi wamthatha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ukwakha umshini ukuze angabe esakwenza lokho futhi, kodwa wakwenza noma kunjalo, ngamabomu, engena endaweni eyodwa ayechithe impilo yakhe yonke ezama ukuyibalekela—

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwasheshisa. Kwethukile. Kwamfishane. Iviza laba mhlophe.

Ikheji lazamazama. Eceleni. Ngamaguide nje, ngokuguga ayesekulinganisile, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawungasakhathali ngokulinganisa. Umzimba wakhe wawukhumbula. Izindonga zaminyana. Idwala elingenhla lacindezela phansi. Ubumnyama obungaphansi bavuleka njengomlomo.

Amathani amane esudi kaGuardian. Isikhumba sakhe ngaphakathi kuyo. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye amatshe.

"Arin," kusho uMother.

Izandla zakhe zazithuthumela. Waziqinisa.

"Arin. Lalela izwi lami. Ungazami ukwehlisa ukuphefumula kwakho. Lokho akukaze kusebenze kuwe, sobabili siyakwazi. Mane ungitshele into eyodwa oyizwayo. Hhayi esudini. Emzimbeni wakho."

"Angikwazi—"

"Into eyodwa. Noma yini."

"Izandla—" Wakuqhubela ngenkani. "Izandla zami. Ziyabanda."

"Kuhle. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Izandla zakho ziyabanda ngoba awufakanga amagilavu ngaphansi kwe-Assist futhi, esizoxoxa ngakho kamuva. Yini enye."

"Umqhele." Izwi lakhe laqhekeka kuwo. "Entanyeni yami. Liya—lifudumele. I-chip ifudumele."

"Yithina labo," kuphendula uMother. "Lokho kufudumala yithina. Sikhona lapha. Phakathi kwamahlombe akho. Asiyi ndawo wena ongayi kuyo." Ukuthula. "Yini enye."

Izindonga zazisaminyana. Kodwa wayeseqambe izinto ezimbili, futhi zombili zaziyiqiniso, futhi ingxenye yengqondo yakhe eyayiphilela iqiniso yafinyelela kulokho ngendlela indoda ewayo efinyelela ngayo erelini.

"Ifulu liyadlidliza," waphumelela. "Ikheji. I-resonance ayilungile. Ama-guide aguge—" Wama. Waphefumula. "Aguge ohlangothini lwasenyakatho. Kunokunyakaza eceleni. Cishe ama-millimeter amane. Yingakho lidlidliza."

"Nangu-ke," kusho uFool, efudumele njengelanga. "Ngimtholile. Ubelokhu engaphansi kokwesaba sonke isikhathi. Uhlala enjalo."

"Ungenzi njalo," kusho uMother.

"Angiklolodi. Ngikhululekile. Kunomehluko, futhi ngivumelekile kukho kokubili." Izwi likaFool lehla, ku-Arin kuphela, ngendlela elalikwenza ngayo uma liyeka ukudlala. "Uke wehla emgodini onjengalo kanye ngaphambili. Wakhushulwa emgodini onjengalo. Wawuneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye. Uchithe iminyaka eyishumi nane wakha umshini ukuze kungabikho mntwana oneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye okumele eme phezu komunye futhi. Manje nguwe owehlayo." Ukuthula. "Lokho akukhona ubunjiniyela obubi, uyazi. Yiyona nto ethembeke kunazo zonke engake ngayibona umuntu eyenza. Bengicabanga nje ukuthi othile kufanele akusho ngaphambi kokuba idwala likusho."

Ikheji lashayisa phansi komthungo ngokukhencezela okwananela esudini langena emazinyweni ka-Arin.

Isibani sazinza. Ukudlidliza kwaphela. Wayesephansi kwezwe, futhi izwe, empeleni, lalingawelanga phezu kwakhe. Okwamanje.

"Isiteshi sezinga leshumi nanye. I-repeater isemuva kwethu manje. Sisengaphakathi." Kwakukhona ubuhlaza ezwini likanjiniyela elaliseduze nokuba mnene njengoba u-Atlas ayehlala enjalo. "Kusukela lapha, amabalazwe madala futhi idwala lithukuthele. Nginayo i-seismic. Nginayo i-survey yokuqala namarekhodi okubhola futhi awawo-eighties nento oyitholile ngabo-three ekuseni okungekho omunye oyitholile. Anginakho ukuqiniseka. Kwazi lokho ngaphambi kokuba siqale. Ngizokutshela iqiniso ngisho nalapho iqiniso lithi *angazi.*"

"Yilokho kuphela engike ngakucela kuwe," kusho u-Arin.

Wayimisa isudi, kancane, ekamelweni eliphansi. Isiteshi sasiyincithakalo—amarali agobile, izinhlu zezigodo zihlephukile, ukuwa kwedwala kuvimbe ingxenye ye-drive eyayiholela entshonalanga iqonde ekudilikeni. Uthuli lwalulenga ebhimini lesibani, lucolisekile njengofulawa, lwenqaba ukuzinza emoyeni ongenandawo yokuya. Kwakushisa. Kushisa ngempela manje, ukufudumala kwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye amatshe abamba ukushisa kweplanethi kumbhekise. Umjuluko waqhuma emhlandleni wakhe ngaphakathi kwe-Assist, wezwa amafeni asudi okupholisa eqala, ukuhuma okumnene kudonsa ukushisa ku-chip kuqala. Izinto zokuqala zikaMother. Njalo.

"Abasinde basengale kokuwa kwedwala," washo. "Bangale kokuwa behla ku-incline bangena egumbini elidala lokukhosela. Uma igumbi liphethe."

"Uma igumbi liphethe," kusho u-Atlas. Ngokulinganisa. Ngokwethembeka. "Esingakwazi."

"Izimpawu zokushisa eziyisikhombisa emahoreni amane edlule," kusho u-Librarian. "Ngiyilogile ifidi yangaphezulu ngaphambi kokuba siyilahle. Eziyisikhombisa, kweziyishumi nanye ezazisebusweni ngenkathi liwa. Babeqoqene. Ukuqoqana kuhle—kusho ukuthi babenyakaza ngamandla abo, bekhetha ukuba ndawonye. Izidumbu aziqoqani. Abaphilayo bayaqoqana."

"Amadoda ayisithupha angaphefumula isikhathi esingakanani phansi laphaya?" kubuza u-Arin.

Ukuthula. Wayefunde ukufunda ukuthula kwe-Court. Lokhu kwakunguMother no-Atlas bexoxisana, ngokushesha, ongqimbeni olungaphansi kwamazwi.

"Ngesiqodi segumbi lokukhosela siphelele?" kusho uMother. "Amahora ayishumi nesishiyagalombili. Ngaphandle kwaso—uma igumbi lilimele futhi besemoyeni ofile nje?" Akazange akuthambise; ukuthambisa kwakuzosho ukuqamba amanga, futhi i-Court yayingasaqambi amanga kuye. "Amathathu. Mhlawumbe amane. Asazi ukuthi yikuphi size singene ngaphakathi."

"Khona-ke asimi lapha," kusho uWolf.

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Asimi."

Wasondela ekuweni kwedwala.

---

*Ukuwa kwakuwudonga lwehangingwall ephukile, amamitha amathathu alo, kusukela ubukhulu benqindi kuya ubukhulu befriji, kupakishiwe futhi kukhiyiwe ngaphansi kwesisindo saso. Esweni lika-Arin, esibanini, kwakuyisithiyo. Ku-Court, esikhathini esinwetshiwe ngaphansi kokubona kwakhe, kwakuyinkinga enesimo, futhi bayiphendula ndawonye ngendlela i-Court ephendula ngayo yonke into: ngokuphikisana.*
