ATLAS: *Ungadonsi esisekelweni. Isisekelo siyathwala umthwalo. Donsa esisekelweni ubuyise umhlane phezu kwakhe.*

WOLF: *Khona-ke siyaphuza. Ukuphuza kubiza abayisithupha umoya wabo.*

ATLAS: *Ukuphuza kubabiza umoya. Ukushesha kubabiza umtakuli, bese bengabe besaba namoya* futhi *nomtakuli. Phinda leyo methamethiksi nezinto ezifakwayo ezifanele.*

JUDGE: *U-Atlas ulungile esibonelweni esandulele. Umtakuli ofile akatakuli muntu. Sisusa kusukela phezulu, ibhloko ngebhloko, futhi samukela indleko yesikhathi.*

MERCURY: *Useduze nokuphinde alahlekelwe yisibindi. Izinga lokushaya kwenhliziyo lisanda kukhuphuka. Ubheke ubukhulu bakho.*

MOTHER: *Khona-ke othile angene ngaphansi kwakho naye ngaphambi kokuba akwenze.*

FOOL: *Ngizohamba. Ungilalela uma esaba ngoba yimi ngedwa ongesabi.*

WOLF: *Wesaba kakhulu.*

FOOL: *Kusobala. Kodwa ngenza umfanekiso omuhle kakhulu womuntu ongesabi, futhi lowo wumsebenzi wonke. Atlas—nginike amabhloko amathathu aphumayo ngaphandle kokwehlisa uphahla. Ngizokwenza kuzwakale njengomlingo wekhadi.*

ATLAS: *Phezulu kwesokunxele. Bese kuba i-wedge engenhla maphakathi. Bese kuba elide ngakwesokudla, futhi kuphela elide, hhayi i-millimeter eyodwa ngale kwalo, noma wonke umkhumbi wasenyakatho uyawa futhi sonke sizoba nengxoxo emfishane kakhulu.*

LIBRARIAN: *Ngilogela ukulandelana. Uma lokhu kusebenza, kuyindlela. Singayidinga kabili futhi ngaphambi kwendawo yokukhosela.*

JUDGE: *Khona-ke yenze kube indlela. Yenze iphindeke. Asiqambi indlela yethu eya kulawo madoda. Sakha umgwaqo.*

[END SLOW MODE]

---

"Phezulu kwesokunxele kuqala," kusho uFool, ekhanya edlala, sengathi babebuyele ekhwakhweni futhi into embi kakhulu kwakuwukuhluleka kwesimulation. "Ibhloko ophezulu kwesokunxele lakho, libukeka limsulwa. Likhiphe. U-Atlas ufunga ukuthi alibambi lutho phezulu. U-Atlas akakaze akuqambele amanga ngokuthwala umthwalo ngoba u-Atlas akakwazi ngokomzimba ukuqamba lolo hlobo lwamanga. Kuwukukhubazeka kobuntu. Simthanda ngakho."

U-Arin wafinyelela phezulu. Isandla sikaGuardian savala ibhloko. Wazibambelela wadonsa, ama-actuator esudi ekhipha umsindo ophezulu omncane ngaphansi komthwalo wangempela, ibhloko liphuma ngokukhala kokugaya nokuphafuza kothuli, futhi kungekho okwalandela phansi.

"Uyabona? Umlingo wekhadi." Izwi likaFool lahlala lizinzile. "I-wedge engenhla maphakathi okulandelayo. Amandla afanayo. Awunyakazisi intaba, uxazulula itshe elilodwa ngesikhathi. Awukaze wehluleke ukuxazulula itshe."

Wathatha i-wedge. Wathatha elide ngakwesokudla, hhayi i-millimeter eyodwa ngale kwalo. I-Court yamemeza ukulandelana, wakwenza, futhi ukuwa kwedwala kwaqhekeka ngendlela ifindo elixegayo ngayo lapho ekugcineni uthola umucu ofanele—hhayi konke kanyekanye, kodwa ngokungenakuvika, ibhloko ngebhloko, kwaze kwaba khona isikhala uGuardian ogoqiwe angaminya kuso.

Ukuphefumula kwase kube mfishane futhi ndawana thile phakathi nakho. Wayengakuqaphelanga. Umsebenzi wawudle ukwesaba; umsebenzi wawuhlala wenza njalo, kusukela esengumfana ebhentshini. Inqobo nje uma kunetshe elizoxazululwa, kwakungekho ndawo esele ebumnyameni.

Kodwa ngale kwesikhala, i-drive yancipha. Yancipha. Isibani sembula umphimbo wedwala obanzi cishe njengesudi nje, ihangingwall ihloboka, izinhlu zezigodo zigobile ngaphansi komthwalo ezaziwulahlekelwa.

Wama. Ukuphefumula kwaba mfishane futhi kwaba kubi.

"Mother," washo. "Angikwazi. Kuncane kakhulu—kuthinta amapuleti—"

"Ngiyazi." Akukho ukwethuka ezwini lakhe, kuphela iqiniso elilethwe ngokuthe nse. "Womabili amahlombe athintene. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Awukucabangi. Kuminyene."

"Angikwazi ukungena lapho. Angikwazi ukuphenduka lapho, angikwazi—"

"Arin." UMercury, manje, ngokusika okuhlanzekile. "Ingalo yangaphambili yobunxele. Ngaphansi kwe-Assist. Unayo kusukela uneminyaka eyishumi nesithupha. Uyayazi leyo."

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwabambeka.

"Yisho," kusho uMercury. "Ngokuzwakalayo. Uhlala ungitshela ukuthi kulotshiwe ukuze ungazethembi ukuthi uyokhumbula. Ngakho-ke ungazethembi. Themba uyinki. Yisho."

Amagama okuqala azincela aphuma, amadala, ilithani esuka engalweni yakhe—umkhuleko wasogwadule womelene nokwesaba ayezilobile ngaphakathi eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa futhi awuhaya kuwo wonke umthungo kusukela lapho.

"Qhubeka."

"Ukwesaba kungumbulali wengqondo." Izwi lakhe lazinza emugqeni. Amagama ayemadala kunosizi lwakhe; wayewathwele kuzo zonke izindawo ezimnyama zempilo yakhe, umshwana owodwa ohamba kancane ngesikhathi.

Wabe—

—wakuzwa kuphindwa kabili.

I-Court yathatha isigqi ngemuva kwakhe. Hhayi amagama, isigqi—uMother, ophansi futhi oqinisekile; uMercury, egcina isilinganiso; futhi ngaphansi kwabo u-Atlas, kubo bonke, u-Atlas ongqondo-zwilili, owayefunde imigqa esuka engalweni yakhe ku-link futhi wanquma ngenye i-calculation u-Arin angeke ayibone ukuthi ukweseka okuwusizo kunakho konke kokuhlonzwa kwesakhiwo ayengakunikeza khona manje kwakuyisigqi sokuphefumula ngokumelene naso.

Wakhuluma umshwana olandelayo bahamba naye, futhi u-Arin wezwa amafeni okupholisa esudi eshintsha—wezwa i-Court ifinyelela emasistimwini kaGuardian ihambisana ukuphefumula kwakhe nesigqi sokuhaya, yehlisa amafeni kuso, ihambisa ukuhuma komkhathi kwama-actuator nesigqi, kangangokuthi wonke umshini waphefumula ngokuhambisana naye, kangangokuthi indawo ayeyesaba kakhulu yaba indawo eyayiphefumula naye.

Wakuvumela ukwesaba kwadlula phezu kwakhe kwamdlula, njengoba amagama amadala emtshela, futhi lapho sekuphelile waphenduka wabheka ukuthi kwakukuphi.

I-incline ayizange ibe banzi, kodwa yayeka ukuzwakala njengomphimbo yaqala ukuthatha isimo.

"*Futhi lapho sekudlulile, ngizophendulela iso lami langaphakathi ngibone indlela yako.*"

"Nangu-ke," kuhleba uFool, cishe kuthule kakhulu ukuze kuzwakale. "Wamukelekile ekubuyeni."

"*Lapho ukwesaba kudlulele khona kuyobe kungasekho lutho.*" U-Arin wabeka isandla emnqwambeni wezigodo ogobile. Wawuhlola. Wezwa u-Atlas emnika inombolo yomthwalo ngaphambi kokuba aqede ukubuza. "*Yimi ngedwa engiyosala.*"

Wangena.

---

I-incline yelula amamitha angamashumi amane, futhi kwakuzwakala njengehora.

Wazulazula ngezandla nezinyawo zozine—ukulungiswa kwe-quadruped kukaGuardian, amapuleti ehhuza edwaleni. Amadolo akhe, awangempela, ase ezwa ubuthi kudala ngokumelene nokufakwa kwesidleke; wakuqaphela ngendlela ayeqaphela ngayo isibalo segeji, waqhubeka. Umjuluko waqoqana emhlandleni wakhe futhi kwakukhona ukulunywa phezulu ethanjeni lesihlathi sakhe okwakungeke akufinyelele, okwakungeke akufinyelele amahora, nobuncane balokho—ukulunywa, kuzo zonke izinto, phansi lapha—cishe kwamenza wahleka. Kabili, ihangingwall yanyakaza, ukukhononda okujulile nokusabalala kwesihlabathi, futhi kabili i-Court yathula nya, ngamunye wabo elalele ngakho konke ayenakho, futhi kabili kwabamba.

Kodwa okwesithathu, akubambanga.

Ikhethelo laduma lehla ngemuva kwakhe, umsindo ofana nezwe likhwehlela, livala indlela yokubuyela esiteshini sezinga leshumi nanye. Uthuli lwagingqika lwakhuphuka i-incline lwamembesa, futhi isibani sageza saba yisiphithiphithi esijikelezayo. Umzimba ka-Arin wakhiyeka—

*ovalelwe ngaphakathi, ungcwatshiwe, ayikho indlela yokubuya, ayikho indlela yokubuya, yilena indlela okwenzeka ngayo kuye, yilena ncamashi indlela—*

—futhi ama-piezo strut emhlandleni kaGuardian ahabuzeka aphila, ngoba ukushaya kwekhethelo kwakuwalayishile, ngoba idwala lona kanye elalimvalele ngaphakathi lalichithe amandla alo esakhiweni sesudi, futhi u-Arin wabuka igeji lamandla likhwela *liya phezulu* eviza, amaphesenti amathathu, amane, futhi ingxenye-yokuhleka yaphunyuka eyayicishe ibe ukubhonga.

"Ukucindezeleka kuba amandla," wahlebeza kungekho muntu. "Wena mshini omuhle."

"Umklamo wakho," kusho u-Atlas. "Thina simane siwuqhuba."

"Indlela yokubuya iphelile, akunjalo."

"Indlela yokubuya iphelile," kusho u-Atlas, ngokungananazi. "Uvumelekile ukuyesaba. Awuvumelekile ukumangaliswa yiyo."

Kwehla ukuthula, futhi u-Arin waphefumula edlula kukho.

"Lokho kuyinto yesibili enomusa kunazo zonke owake wayisho kimi," washo.

"Beyini eyokuqala?"

"Wangitshela ukuthi ukudilika kukababa kwakungesona isiphetho esingenakugwemeka sesakhiwo. Ukuthi kwakuyisinqumo othile asenza ngemali. Ukuthi idwala alimbulalanga. Indoda yakwenza."

"Lowo bekunguJudge," kusho u-Atlas.

"Bekunguwe," kusho u-Arin. "UJudge wavuma. Kodwa nguwe owenze imethamethiksi yomthwalo wakufakazela." Waqala ukuya phambili futhi, wehlela ebumnyameni, eqonda emadodeni umoya wawo owawuphela. "Angikaze ngikubonge ngalokho."

"Uyakusho manje," kusho u-Atlas. "Ngokwehlela lapha. Ngokwakha umgwaqo esikhundleni sethuna. Lokho ukubonga unjiniyela wezakhiwo angakufunda."

I-incline yagcina iphumela ku-drive obanzi. Uthuli lwaqala ukuzinza, ngokungathandi. Phambili, ebhimini lesibani elalilulama, u-Arin wabamba isimo esasingelona idwala—umnyango wensimbi, ugxumile esikhungweni sawo kodwa uzinzile, umbala omthubi-nomnyama ovamile wegumbi lokukhosela, futhi kuwo, kubhalwe etekeni ngesandla, izimpawu eziyisikhombisa zokubala kuthi enye ihloliwe.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwabambeka. Lokhu kwakungekona ukwesaba.

"Eziyisikhombisa," kusho u-Librarian ngokuthula. "Izimpawu eziyisikhombisa. Enye ihloliwe. Babuyekeze ukubalwa. Babefuna ukuba sazi ukuthi bangaki abasele."

"Bayaphila," kusho uMother, futhi okokuqala usuku lonke, kwakukhona okuthile ezwini elihlangene le-Court okwakungesilo ukuzola, okwakungesona ukulawula, kodwa iqiniso eliyihlazo elaliletha noma yimuphi wabo lapha. "Arin. Ba*yaphila.*"

Wawela ingxenye yokugcina ye-drive. Ihendlela lomnyango lalingumlenze wensimbi ogobile, ushelelezi ngothuli lwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye, ubandayo ngisho nangepuleti. Wabeka isandla sikaGuardian esikhulu esinezibazi siyisicaba esinsimbini ebandayo yomnyango, kahle, sengathi uthinta into entekenteke, futhi kusuka ohlangothini olukude, kufiphele kodwa kucacile, izwi lamemeza okuthile ngeSesotho, elinye izwi laliphendula, kungekudala amazwi amaningi ayesephakama, umsindo wamadoda ayesezibalele ethuneni ezwa ukungqongqoza esivalweni.

Wama lapho umzuzwana, ephefumula nje. I-chip ifudumele phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, abayisikhombisa bezothile bemzungeza, amazwi ngakolunye uhlangothi lwensimbi.

"Court," washo. Izwi lakhe lahlala lizinzile, ngokumangazayo. "Asibakhiphe."

"Sikanye nawe," baphendula, bonke. Izwi elilodwa, izingqondo eziyisikhombisa.

"Yonke indlela," kwenezela uFool ngokuthula, ngendlela ayehlala engena ngayo ekugcineni. "Phansi lapha, phezulu laphaya. Yonke indlela, badger. Manje vula umnyango."

Wawuvula umnyango.

---

# Ukuthintana

Indlela endala yokuthwala yayiqonde ngqo amamitha angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba iyeke ukuzenzisa.

U-Arin wakuzwa ukushintsha ngaphambi kokuba izibani zikuqinisekise. Ushintsho ezinyathelweni zikaGuardian, ukunswininiza kuba sicaba, phansi kuthambeka kancane kuye ngakwesokunxele lapho idwala laguquka khona ngesinye isikhathi esikhohlakele kudala. Umgudu wancipha, futhi izingodo zakuqala, ezabekwa ngamadoda asengamathuli amashumi eminyaka, zazibambelele isikhathi eside kunalokho izinjiniyela ezazifaka zona zaziyokukholwa. Wayengazethembi. Izibani zezicoco zesudi zasika izimbobo ezimbili ezimnyameni, futhi uthuli lwalulenga kuzo lufana nento eqinile, iqhwa elihamba kancane elingafiki phansi.

*Phansi kulimele phambili amamitha ayishumi nanye,* kubika u-Atlas. *Insika endala yebiwe ngakwesokunxele. Isisindo siphezu kobambo lwesokudla.*

U-Arin washintsha ukuma kwakhe. U-Guardian walungisa naye, ingxenye enkulu yamathani amane ethithaniyamu nekhethamikhi yabuye yasabalala phezu komhlabathi ongcono sengathi kuyiqakala lakhe siqu lilungisa esithambekeni. Wayeseyekile ukucabanga ngesudi njengento ayigqokayo; ndawana thile kumamitha angamakhulu amane okugcina, yayisiphenduke indlela yokuma.

"Kukude kangakanani endaweni yokukhosela?"

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu nesishiyagalombili,* kuphendula u-Librarian. *Amapulani ka-1962 akhombisa umgudu onqamulayo lapha okufanele uxhumane nesiteshi se-38 Level. Amapulani awahlali eqotho.*

"Ayikho into eqotho lapha phansi," u-Arin wahleba.

*Yilowo moya,* kungenela u-Fool. *Yehlisa okulindele, ngeke uphoxeke. Yindlela engibhekana ngayo nazo zonke izingxoxo noJudge.*

*Ngiyakuziba,* kungenela u-Judge.

*Usho njalo, kepha-ke.*

Umgudu onqamulayo wawukhona ngempela lapho u-Librarian eshilo, kwababamangaza bonke, ikakhulukazi u-Arin. Waphendulela u-Guardian kuwo, igobolondo lihhuza idwala nhlangothi zombili, ukukhononda okulukhuni kwekhethamikhi ekudwaleni okwenyukela ngohlaka kwangena emazinyweni akhe. Indawo yavuleka, umgudu onqamulayo waguquka waba yikamelo elibanzi lapho izimoto zensimbi zazikade zilayishwa khona, izibani zakhe zathola umnyango wensimbi wendawo yokukhosela ofakwe odongeni olukude.

Wawugobile. Uhlaka lwalwemukele isisindo esivela ngenhla lwagobela ngaphakathi, umnyango uminyene endaweni yawo ngendlela engafanele neyodwa umnyango ube kuyo. Isikhala esiphezulu, ububanzi besandla, sasivuleke samnyama.

U-Arin wama.

Okwesikhashana, akukho lutho oluthi vu eNkantolo, okuwukuphela komsindo kwakuwukuhamba kancane kwensimbi ipholile nokuphefumula kwakhe, okwakukhulu kakhulu ngaphakathi kwesigqoko.

Khona-ke, kusuka esikhaleni, izwi. Elincane. Eligwabhile uthuli.

"Kukhona umuntu? Is someone there?"

Isifuba sika-Arin saqina. Wahambisa u-Guardian phambili, esecophelela manje, wehlisa izibani phansi ukuze angaphuphuthekisi noma ngubani owayengemuva kwensimbi.

"Yebo," kusho yena, izwi lakhe lilukhuni kunalokho ayekuhlosile. "Ngikhona. I'm here."

---

Ngaphakathi endaweni yokukhosela, uMandla Buthelezi wayeseyekile ukukholwa emazwini emahoreni amathathu adlule.

Wayewezwile ngaphambili: idwala likhuluma, ukububula okude kwentaba ihlalisa isisindo sayo, kanye nakanye, ngokucacile, indoda imemeza igama lakhe, okwakungenakwenzeka ngoba indoda eyayimemeza igama lakhe yayichotshozwe ndawana thile ngaphezu kwakhe ekuweni kokuqala, futhi uMandla wakubona kwenzeka. Ngakho ngenkathi ukukhanya kungena ngaphansi komnyango, kumhlophe kuqinile, kungalokozi njengesibani esiphelelwayo, akazange anyakaze. Wahlala encike kuPetros, owayenomlenze ophukile owayeseyekile ukukhononda ngawo, okwakukubi kakhulu kunokukhononda.

Khona-ke ukukhanya kwakhuluma isiZulu ngezwi lensizwa, uMandla waqala ukukhala buthule, ngoba abafileyo babengasikhulumi kabi isiZulu ngephimbo lasogwini.

"Isithupha," wamemezela esebuyisela esikhaleni. "Isithupha. Sangena siyisikhombisa." Wezwa ezikhulumela futhi engakwazi ukuyeka. "Oyedwa—isifuba sakhe sasibi, ukuwa kwasivula. Umoya waba mubi futhi—walala. Asikwazanga ukumvusa. Isithupha manje. UPetros, umlenze wakhe. UThulani u-" Wama. "Umoya mubi. Sesibe—" Ukubala kwakunzima. "Isikhathi eside. Sesibe isikhathi eside."

Ukukhanya kwashintsha. Khona-ke umnyango waqala ukunyakaza.

Awudonsiwanga. Awukhanjwanga. Insimbi yabubula yagoba, uhlaka olugobile lunikezela ngokukhala kwensimbi edabukayo, futhi ngesikhala esiya sibanzi, uMandla wabona into eyayiphethe ukukhanya. Yagcwalisa umgudu onqamulayo. Yayinombala wezishiso ezindala iqosheme njengesilwane, izandla zayo—uma kwakuyizandla—zivale onqenqemeni lomnyango zawugoqa emuva ngendlela indoda egoqa ngayo isivalo sethini.

UPetros, eceleni kwakhe, wahleba umkhuleko ayengasawusebenzisanga kusukela ebuntwaneni.

"Kulungile," kusho izwi. Elomuntu. Izwi nje lendoda, livela ngaphakathi komshini. "Ngiyazi ukuthi kubukeka kanjani. Yisudi yokutakula. Kukhona umuntu ngaphakathi lapha."

"Umuntu," kuphinda uMandla.

"Cishe," kusho izwi elehlukile, elilula, livela ndawana thile emshinini, okwenza izwi lokuqala lithi, "Hhayi manje," ngendlela eyayizwakala njengendoda itshela inja ukuba isuke kusofa, futhi lokho kucasuka okuncane okuvamile kwenza uMandla wakukholwa.

---

Indawo yokukhosela yayijule amamitha amane, igujwe edwaleni eliqinile, yakhelwe ukuphilisa iqembu amahora angamashumi amathathu nesithupha ngemuva komnyango ovaliwe onomoya wayo. Babesebe lapho isikhathi eside kunalokho. Ulayini womoya ocindezelwe wawuhlulekile ekuweni kwesibili. Amadoda ayebephefumula umoya ocelayo wekamelo, futhi ikhomba yomshini wokususa i-CO₂ yayisiphelile. Umoya wawunambitheka njengomphefumulo nokwesaba kanye nobumnandi obuthize obabusho ukuthi isikhutha sesinyukela enombolweni lapho amadoda ayelala khona nje.

U-Arin wakuthatha ngokushesha, ngendlela ayecubungula ngayo ibhodi lesekethe: yonke into ngesikhathi esisodwa, bese kuthi ukwehluleka kanye ngakunye.

*Umoya kuqala,* kusho uMother, izwi lakhe lithatha ubuthe ayebusebenzisa lapho esebenza, lapho ukufudumala kuhlehlela emuva ukwenzela umsebenzi indawo. *I-CO₂ ku-four point one futhi iyanyuka. Umoya-mpilo ku-eighteen point three. Banesikhathi. Hhayi esiningi. Vula ikamelo liye endleleni yokuthwala. Umoya wethu ungcono kunaleli bhokisi elifile.*

"Umoya wendlela yokuthwala ugcwele uthuli," kuphendula u-Arin.

*Uthuli bayasinda kulo. Kulokhu abasindi. Khipha umoya.*

Wagoba uhlaka luka-Guardian wagxilisa umnyango wavuleka ngokuphelele. Umoya wanyakaza. Wawuzwa ezinzweni zangaphandle zesudi njengomehluko ozwakala kancane, umoya omubi ushelela uphuma nomoya owesabekayo nje ushelela ungena. Enye yamadoda yakhwehlela, ayikwazanga ukuyeka.

U-Arin waqoshama, ehlisela isigqoko ezingeni labo. Ubuso obuyisithupha ekukhanyeni kwezibani. Bungcolile. Izindebe ezicacekile. Amehlo ayesebe ebumnyameni isikhathi eside kangangokuthi ukukhanya kwawalimaza. Indoda enomlenze oboshwe ngepulangwe lokuvala kanye nocingo. Othile wenza umsebenzi omuhle wokwelapha ngalokho ababenakho. Insizwa, idiyekile, iphefumula ngendlela u-Arin ayengayithandi, kafushane futhi ngokushesha.

"UThulani," kusho uMandla, elandela ukubuka kuka-Arin. "Isifuba sakhe. Kukhona into eyamwela ekuqaleni—"

*Tension pneumothorax, cishe,* kusho uMother. *Noma i-flail segment. Atlas, ngidinga ukubona udonga lwesifuba.*

U-Atlas waletha ukuthwebula okusondele kwesudi, isembozo esiphaphathekile sokushisa-nesakhiwo sishelela emehlweni ka-Arin. Isifuba sensizwa esingakwesokunxele sasinyakaza ngokungalungile, simunca singena lapho kwakufanele sisunduze siphume.

*Flail. Izimbambo ezintathu zintanta zikhululekile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas. *Umsebenzi ophindwe kabili wokuphefumula kunalokho okufanele abe nawo. Ngeke akuphumelele ukukhishwa ngalesi sivinini.*

"Senzani?"

*Qinisa lesi sigaba. Faka isiqhumane bese ubopha. Asibona odokotela, futhi lena akusona isibhedlela,* kusho uMother. *Kodwa simthengela amahora. Arin. Uzokwenza ngezandla zakho. Ezika-Guardian zirhabaxa kakhulu.*

Lokho kwammisa. Ukuze asebenzise izandla zakhe, kwakufanele aphule isivalo. Akhiphe ingalo yakhe yesokudla esilenjeni sesudi, ayifake othulini nasemoyeni omubi, evezekile. Lo mcabango wafika nesisindo esidala, ukuvaleka, ingxenye yakhe eyayichithe impilo yakhe yonke ibala iziphumo.

*Ngiyazi,* kusho uMother kahle, kuye yedwa. *Yenze noma kunjalo. Imizuzu emithathu. Phefumula ngomlomo wakho.*

Wakhipha ingalo yakhe.

Umoya washaya isikhumba sakhe—ufudumele, unesihlabathi, ungalungile. Isandla sakhe sasibukeka siyinqaba kuye, siphaphathekile sisincane, into yesilwane ngaphandle kwensimbi eyizungezile. Wadlula kuso, wathatha iphakethe lokwelapha uMother amenzela inkomba kulo livela ohlangothini luka-Guardian, futhi ngaphansi kweziyalezo zakhe—isiqhumane lapha, *cha, phansi, phezu kwesigaba esintantayo, yebo*—wabopha isifuba sensizwa, iminwe yakhe enqunu isebenza ebumnyameni kubukele amadoda ayisithupha, futhi umshini uphefumula umzungezile.

Ukuphefumula kukaThulani kwaba lula. Kungekuhle. Kungesikubi kangako.

"Nakho," kusho u-Arin. Wabuyisela ingalo yakhe esilenjeni, isivalo savaleka, futhi wayengazi ukuthi wayeyifuna kanjani kabuhlungu ize abe nayo. "Nakho."

*Isivinini senhliziyo sehle ngeshumi nambili,* kusho uMother. *Kuhle. Manje siyahamba.*

---

Ukuxhumana naphezulu kwakufile kusukela emgudwini onqamulayo.

U-Arin wayengashongo lutho ngakho. NeNkantolo nayo. Izwi likaPriya lalincipha lakekeleka lawa ndawana thile eduze kwendawo encishaniso, futhi babeqhubekile nje ngaphandle kwakhe ngoba kwakungekho okunye ababengakwenza. Kodwa u-Guardian wayethwele isipuli sesiphindi-mavi, amamitha angamakhulu amabili efayibha edramini emhlane wakhe, futhi u-Atlas wayebeyikhipha emuva kwabo sonke isikhathi sokwehla, intambo ebuyela ekukhanyeni.

*Isiphetho sesipuli,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngingaxhuma ulayini omdala oqinile wendawo yokukhosela esiphindi-mavini. Indawo yayinolayini wokuxhumana okhethekile oya esiteshini se-38 Level. Uma noma ingxenye yawo isindile ekuweni—*

"Kwenze."

Kwathatha imizuzu emine. U-Arin wabamba isembozo sephaneli sisuka odongeni lwendawo kuyilapho iminwe kaGuardian, erhabaxa kakhulu ekwelapheni kodwa icophelele ngokwanele kulokhu, isonta ithusi elinqunu phezu kwebhuloho lefayibha-eya-thusini lesiphindi-mavi. U-Atlas wamkhuluma wamhambisa kukho, kome futhi eqinisekile, futhi u-Arin wathola ukuqiniseka kuzinzisa.

Khona-ke, esigqokweni sakhe, ukuhhewuza. Iphimbo lethwezeli. Izwi, lephukile, lizakhela kabusha lisuka enhlokokeni liya entweni ethile yobuntu.

"—phinda, lokhu kuphezulu, lokhu kunguPriya, *Arin*, uma ungizwa—"

"Ngiyakuzwa." Izwi lakhe laphukela kukho. Wayengazi ukuthi wayezizwa eyedwa kangakanani ize iphele leyo ndawo yokuba yedwa. "Priya. Nginabo. Bonke abayisithupha. Bayaphila."

Ukuthula. Khona-ke umsindo angakwazanga ukuwubeka, uhlobo lomsindo oklinyiwe, futhi waqonda ukuthi kwakunguPriya engakhali esiteshini esivulekile.

"Phinda futhi." Ukuphefumula. "Futhi. Kancane. Kwirekhodi."

"Abasindile abayisithupha endaweni yokukhosela ye-38 Level. Bonke abayisithupha bayaphila. Oyedwa usengozini enkulu. Ukulimala kwesifuba, kuqinisiwe. Oyedwa unomlenze ophukile. Abanye bomile, bagcwele i-CO₂, bayakwazi ukuhamba. Batshele. Tshela abaphezulu."

Wamuzwa ekudlulisela, izwi lakhe liphendukela kude nemakrofoni, bese ezwa into ayengakaze ayizwe ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye, kusukela ngaphambi kwakho konke lokhu, kungekho ngisho isikhathi eside ngaphambi kwalokho.

Wezwa abantu beklabalasa.

Kwehla ngefayibha kuncane kunensimbi, umsindo owenziwe yimiphimbo eyikhulu amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye ngaphezu kwakhe, ekuseni, kuzungeze izinto zasemgodini—imindeni namaqembu namadoda ayetsheliwe ukuthi lokhu kungukubuyisa hhayi ukutakula—wamfinyelela ebumnyameni, emoyeni ofile, eceleni kwamadoda ayisithupha ayeseyekile ukukholwa emazwini, futhi uMandla Buthelezi wabuka umshini owawungumuntu wathi, "Yini leyo? Yini lowo msindo?"

"Yilabo abaphezulu," kusho u-Arin. "Bayazi ukuthi niyaphila."

Amadoda awazange aklabalase. Ayekhathele kakhulu ukuba aklabalase. Kodwa uPetros, owayenomlenze ophukile owayeseyekile ukukhononda ngawo, wabuyisela ikhanda lakhe edwaleni wahleka, umsindo owomile ophukile, futhi uhleko lwadlula ekamelweni lusuka kwenye indoda luya kwenye kwaze kwaba bonke abayisithupha bayakwenza, futhi u-Arin wahlala ngaphakathi kuka-Guardian phakathi nendawo akazange ahleke, ngoba wayematasa, kodwa kukhona into esifubeni sakhe eyakhululeka eyayiqinene isikhathi eside kakhulu.

*Kujabulele,* kusho u-Fool kancane, kuye yedwa. *Bese siyasebenza. Intaba ayizifundanga izindaba ezinhle. Iseyiyo intaba.*

---

Indlela yokuphuma kwakuyinkinga.

U-Arin wakhuphula isembozo somzila: indlela yokwehla ibuyela emgudwini onqamulayo, indlela endala yokuthwala, phansi okuthambekile, i-81 Raise nezindawo zayo ezincishanisayo, ukukhuphuka okude kuya e-30 Level nasekheji naphezulu. Wayehlile yedwa, isudi igoqa futhi ikhushulwa idlula ezindaweni ezicinene. Wayengeke abuyele ngendlela efanayo. Manje wayenamadoda ayisithupha. Oyedwa wayengakwazi ukuhamba. Oyedwa wayengaphefumuli ngokulukhulu.

*Indlela yokwehla ayisebenzi kubagulayo abathwalwa ngohlaka,* kusho u-Atlas. *Indawo encishanisayo e-81 Raise ngeke idlulise inhlaka. Futhi ngeke sithwale abayisithupha nganoma yiluphi uhlobo. Igobolondo libamba oyedwa.*

"Khona-ke asibuyeli ngendlela engehle ngayo."

*Kulungile,* kusho u-Librarian. *Futhi kukhona enye indlela. Isiteshi se-38 Level sixhuma nendlela enkulu yokuthwala ye-38 Level, amapulani ka-1962 akhombisa iya empumalanga isiya esiteshini se-No. 4 Shaft. I-No. 4 yamiswa ngeminyaka yamashumi ayisishiyagalombili, kodwa umgodi uqobo lwawo—*

*—umgodi u-Venter abelokhu ewulungisa esuka phezulu,* kuqedela u-Atlas. *Akalungisi ikheji esehle ngalo. Ulungisa i-No. 4. Wakuqonda ngaphambi kwethu.*

U-Arin wathula. Phezulu, ewekhishopu lenkumbulo yakhe, wabona ubuso bendoda endala: intshebe empunga, eshiswe yilanga, indoda u-Arin achitha izinsuku ezintathu enquma ukuthi angeke ayethembe ngenxa yalokho isikhumba sayo nephimbo layo okwakumtshela ngeminyaka eyayisebenze kuyo. Indoda eyayifunde amapulani ka-1962 yodwa, ubusuku bonke, ingamenyiwe, yathola ukuphela kwendlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin afinyelele phansi.

"Usingaphambili," kusho u-Arin.

*Yebo,* kusho u-Wolf.

Kwakuyinto yokuqala u-Wolf ayeyisho ehoreni, futhi yonke iNkantolo yathula ngakho.

*Uyawazi lo mhlaba. Thina asiwazi. Sinamapulani. Yena unenkumbulo. Yilokho okubulala abantu lapha phansi. Siyamdinga.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu empumalanga, indlela yokuthwala ye-38 Level yayilungiswe kangcono kunanoma yini u-Arin ayesedlule kuyo, okwamenza wayingabaza. Wayethwele uThulani ezingalweni zika-Guardian, insizwa iboshelwe ohlakeni futhi uhlaka lugonwe esifubeni segobolondo lapho ukwakheka kwesudi kuyothatha noma yikuphi ukushaywa ngaphambi kokuba kufinyelele ogulayo. Abanye abahlanu bahamba ngemuva, izandla zibambelele emahlombe omunye nomunye, ucu othulini, uMandla esemuva, uPetros eqakulela enamadoda amabili ngaphansi kwezingalo zakhe ngoba kwakungekho esinye isandla esingamthwala futhi u-Guardian wayegcwele.

Khona-ke izibani zathola isiteshi se-No. 4, nomgodi.

Kwakuwumphimbo omnyama edwaleni, ikheji elidala selihambile kudala, izinto zasemgodini amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye ngaphezulu. Futhi phansi enkabeni yawo, lizamazama kancane, likhanyiswa ngenhla yichashazi lokumhlophe okukhazimulayo, kwalenga ipulatifomu yensimbi.

Iflathibhedi. Hhayi ikheji. Ideki leflathibhedi, elalisetshenziswa emgodini, uhlobo olwalusetshenziselwa ukwehlisa imishini esindayo, lilengiswe ezintanjeni ezine kanye newinji yekibhula, lehla liphuma ekukhanyeni liza ngakubo kunendoda emi kulo.

Indoda yayigqoke isigqoko esiqinile nebhande eliboshelwe entanjeni esenkabeni, futhi yayimi ngokuma okulula nokugxilile komuntu ogibele izinkulungwane zalokhu futhi engakuthandi kakhulu kodwa eseyekile ukukwesaba kudala.

Ukuxhumana kuka-Arin kwaqhwaba. Izwi elehlukile manje, eduze, kubhendi yendawo. Kancane. Linganisiwe.

"Ndlela." UVenter. UJakobus Venter. "Unabo."

"Bonke abayisithupha," kusho u-Arin.

"Ja." Ukuthula. Iflathibhedi yanga phansi kwesiteshi ngokukhencezela okuthambile, indoda endala yakhumula ukukhululeka yehla, ibuka kanye ubude obugcwele buka-Guardian bese ingaphinde ibuke, ngendlela indoda eyayisebenze nemishini emikhulu impilo yayo yonke ebuka ngayo eyodwa: iyilinganisa, bese iyibekela eceleni. "Ukushisa kwabambelela ngenkathi naphezulu kukhanya. Ngacabanga ukuthi senibatholile."

Wadlula u-Arin waya kubagulayo. Waqoshama eduze kukaThulani engacelwanga, izandla zakhe ezinkulu ezinamachashazi elanga zihamba phezu kokubotshwa, ziyakuhlola, zinqekuzisa ikhanda kanye.

"Kuhle. Noma ngubani okwenzile wayewazi umsebenzi."

"KwakunguMother," kusho u-Arin, wabe esezizwa. "Uhlelo. I-SAGE."

Amehlo kaVenter aya esigqokweni. Abambelela lapho umzuzwana.

"Ja," washo futhi, akabuzanga, futhi u-Arin waqonda ukuthi ukungabuzi kwakuyinhlonipho.

---

Inkinga ngeflathibhedi yayiyinkinga nakukho konke lapha phansi: yayingakhelelwanga lokhu.

*Ideki lithatha amathani amabili,* kusho u-Atlas, efunda ipuleti uVenter alikhomba engacelwanga. *Sicishe sibe amane ngaphambi kwabagulayo. Engeza amadoda ayisithupha, engeza uhlaka. Asingaphezulu. Sicishe siphindwe kabili, futhi ipuleti liyinganekwane enenhlonipho.*

"Khona-ke angiligibeli," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakhuphuka. Isudi ingalithatha udonga lomgodi."

"Cha," kusho uVenter. Ngokuqinile. Igama lokuqala elilukhuni ayelisebenzisile. Wayegobe phezu kwemoto yewinji enhloko yomgudu wesiteshi, lapho intambo yayinyukela ebumnyameni, futhi manje waqonda, futhi lapho ekhuluma isiNgisi sakhe sasihambile, isiBhunu sinyukela ngaphakathi ngendlela ulimi lokuqala lomuntu olunyuka ngayo lapho indaba inzima futhi engafuni ukungaqondwa. "Jy klim nie. Awukhuphuki. Udonga lubolile amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili phezulu, izingodo zihambile. Beka isisindo saleyo mishini kulo, uyilethela phansi ideki kunala madoda kulo. Nee."

Umuzwa ka-Arin wawungokuphikisa. Wayenemodeli yodonga lomgodi. Wayenamathebula esisindo. Wayene—

*Uqinisile,* kusho u-Atlas, ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin akhulume. *Nginejografi. Uyifunda ebusweni bedwala nasephethini lokuqhephuka. Bekuzofanele ngikuskene. Yena uvele azi. Udonga olungenhla kwesiteshi yidwala elifile.*

*Lalela ulukhukhuze omdala,* kuhleba u-Fool. *Usebe lapha phansi isikhathi eside kunesimulethi yakho.*

Ngakho u-Arin wathi, "Kanjani-ke?"

UVenter wamtshela.

Imihambo emibili. Abagulayo namadoda amane ahambayo kuqala, edekini, kuhamba uVenter ukuze alawule intambo newinji yekibhula ezungeza kakhulu ezofuna ukugijima ikhululekile ngaphansi komthwalo. Bese ideki lehla futhi, lingenalutho. Bese u-Guardian eyedwa, indoda yokugcina ephuma. Futhi lowo mhambo, kusho uVenter, ehwaqabele ubukhulu besudi, kwakuzofanele bawuxazulule ngenye indlela, ngoba amathani amane omshini kwakungeyona umthwalo ikheji elake laklanyelwa ukuwuphakamisa ngentambo eyodwa.

"Iwinji yinkinga," kusho uVenter. Wayekhiphe isembozo ehhokolweni lemoto, futhi u-Arin wayebona, ngisho nasekukhanyeni kwezibani, ukuthi kukhona into lapho engalungile: ibhande lebhuleki, lishile, ingxenye yalo yokuvala isihambile. "Ibhuleki liphekiwe. Simehlise ngokushesha sikuletha kuwe. Ushisa. Uma eshisa kakhulu, uyahluleka. Ideki liyazehlela namadoda kulo. Lapho ngehla, ngigibela ibhande ngesandla. Hhayi ekuphakanyisweni. Ukuphakanyisa kungugesi, ibhuleki lizenzakalelayo, futhi lokho okuzenzakalelayo sekuhambile."

U-Arin wabuka imoto. Wachitha ubuntwana bakhe nemishini emidala kanjena ncamashi, ehlukunyezwe kanjena ncamashi, ekukhanyeni okufana ncamashi nalokhu. Wabona konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, bese ebona ukwehluleka.

"Udinga ibhuleki lomthwalo imoto yewinji engenalo," kusho yena. "Okuthile okuzobamba ideki uma ibhande lidedela. Uhlelo lwesibili."

"Ja." UVenter wambuka. Wabuka u-Guardian. "Futhi nginalo. Umi phambi kwami, hhe. Umshini ubamba intambo. Ibhande liyahamba, isudi yakho ithatha umthwalo, ideki alehli. Wena uyibhuleki lami lendoda efile."

Kwakuyikho ncamashi, u-Arin wabona. Kwakuyinto ayezoyakha uma wayenezinsuku ezintathu newekhishopu. Indoda endala yayikuklame emasekhondini ayishumi kulokho okwakumi phambi kwayo.

"Atlas," kusho u-Arin. "Singabamba amathani amabili eqinile entanjeni sidlula kumqondisi?"

*Sidlula ohlakeni lomgudu onqamulayo njengendawo yokubophela, intambo iqondiswe kabusha phezu kwesheve elidala—yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye omthwalo ophelele oqinile ngaphambi kokuba ukupholisa kwe-actuator kweqe imingcele. Ngemuva kwalokho silahla umthwalo noma sipheke ingalo yesokudla.*

*Amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yisikhathi sokuphakamisa,* kusho u-Librarian. *Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ngesivinini esiphephile. Amasekhondi angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nesishiyagalombili.*

"Amasekhondi amabili omkhawulo," kusho u-Arin.

*Wamukelekile ekutakuleni emgodini,* kusho u-Fool. *Amasekhondi amabili ubuncwebe. Ngokokugcina sasine-negethivu enhlanu sayenza yasebenza.*

"Lokho kwakuyisimulethi."

*Kwakuyiyo? Kunzima ukutshela lapha phansi. Ukukhanya kuyafana.*

Umoya wohleko wamphunyuka ngaphambi kokuba akwazi ukuwumisa. Kwammangaza. Waphendukela kuVenter, owayembuka enengxoxo nomuntu ongekho, ngokubekezela okungasheshi komuntu owayenqume ukuthi ubuqaba akubona obakhe ukubuxazulula.

"Lizobamba," kusho u-Arin. "Ngizolibamba."

UVenter wambuka umzuzwana omude. Ubuso obushiswe yilanga bashintsha, umhlathi usebenza kanye. U-Arin wayengakwazi ukufunda ubuso kahle, wayengakaze, kodwa wayefunde ukufunda izikhathi ezizungeze bona, futhi lesi kwakuyisikhathi esinesisindo. Indoda endala yayichithe iminyaka, u-Arin wayezokufunda kamuva, ithwele ukuthula ngezinto zokuphepha okwakungekho, amabhuleki ayehlehlisiwe, ukunqunyulwa okwakusayinwe ngamadoda ayengeke aligibele ikheji. Yayingcwabe lokho kuthula kwajula kangangokuthi yakhe umsebenzi wesibili phezu kwakho njengomeluleki, futhi ukweluleka kwakuwuhlobo lwakho lokuthula, iseluleko okwakungekho othunyelwe ukuba asithathe.

Futhi nansi umshini owaklanywa yinsizwa eyayidliwe uyise yileyo nhlobo yentaba, icela indoda endala ukuba ayethembe njengebhuleki kuyilapho amadoda ayisithupha egibela ephuma ebumnyameni.

"Uyazi ukuthi ngowobani lo mgodi," kusho uVenter. "Inkampani. Irekhodi yabo."

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

"Bengiyinsizwa emgodini onjengalo. Ngasayina izinto." IsiBhunu sanyuka ngokuqinile manje, futhi indoda endala yakuvumela. "Izinto ebekufanele ngizimise. Ngazitshela ukuthi nganqotshwa. Nganqotshwa. Futhi ngasayina noma kunjalo." Wabheka phezulu emgodini omnyama eqonde ekukhanyeni okumhlophe okukude. "Angikutholi lokho futhi. Ayikho—ayikho indlela yokwehla ukuthola lokho futhi."

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Ayikho."

Indoda endala yethuka, bese, ngokuxakayo, yaphumula.

"Ja," kusho uVenter. "Nokho." Wadonsa amagilavu akhe aqina. "Asibakhiphe. Labo esisengabakhipha."

---

Balayisha ideki.

UThulani kuqala, uhlaka luboshelwe ohlangothini lwedeki, uMother eqaphela isifuba sakhe ngezinzwa zika-Guardian kuze kufike umzuzu wokugcina onokwenzeka womkhawulo. Bese uPetros, bese amadoda amane ahambayo, epakishwe eduze, izandla zibambelele ohlangothini, ubuso buphakanyiselwe ekukhanyeni sengathi bangakhuphuka enye indlela ngokufisa kuphela. UMandla Buthelezi wenyuka nalo mthwalo wokuqala ngoba uVenter wamthatha njengozinze kakhulu nokungenzeka agcine abanye bezolile, futhi ohlangothini lwedeki uMandla waphenduka wabuka emuva umshini owawusamile ebumnyameni, lowo owawuyoza ekugcineni.

"Wena," washo. "What's your name? The person. Not the machine."

"Arin."

"Arin." UMandla wanqekuza ikhanda kancane. "Sasicabanga ukuthi sizwa abafileyo bekhuluma, lapha phansi. Sasicabanga ukuthi siphelile." Wabambelela ohlangothini. "Ngiyabonga. Wafika ngenkathi bethi ayikho indlela."

"Yayikhona indlela," kusho u-Arin. "Akekho owabheka ngokwanele."

UVenter waziboshela entanjeni esenkabeni. Wabeka isandla esisodwa ekhwehlweni lebhande lebhuleki, ibhuleki eliphekiwe ayezoligibela ngokuzwa indlela yonke enyukela phezulu, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye entambo edlula ngesandla esisodwa esishile, futhi ngesinye wavula iwinji, ideki lenyuka, lizamazama, liphakamisa amadoda ayisithupha ephuma emoyeni omubi nasekukhanyeni okufile, liya ekukhazimuleni nasekuklabalaseni nasekuseni.

U-Arin wakubuka kuhamba. Wahambisa u-Guardian endaweni yokubophela, wafaka intambo phezu kwesheve elidala, wathatha umthwalo engalweni yesudi yesokudla uma ibhuleki lidedela, wama lapho, ingxenye enkulu yamathani amane ensimbi nendoda engaphakathi kuyo, ibambe ulayini obambe amadoda, ibhuleki lendoda efile ulukhukhuze omdala ayelakhe ngaye emasekhondini ayishumi.

Ibhande labamba. Ideki lakhuphuka. Alidinganga yena.

*Isabelo sokushisa engalweni sihle,* kusho u-Atlas. *Akudingeki ukudluliswa komthwalo. Wagibela ibhuleki ngokuhlanzeka.*

"Kuhle," kusho u-Arin.

*Ubungayenza leyo ngxenye wedwa,* kuphawula u-Fool, ngokuxoxa, ekuthuleni kokukhuphuka okude. *Ubamba intambo. Wakha indawo yokubophela. Kodwa indlela yokuphuma—udonga olufile, ideki elinesilinganiso, i-No. 4 Shaft—wawuzongena kulezo zingodo ezibolile uziletha phezu kwakho, futhi amadoda ayisithupha ayezofela ebumnyameni bomgodi nesidumbu esisudini ebizayo kakhulu. Uyakwazi lokho, ja?*

U-Arin wabuka ideki lincipha liya ekukhanyeni.

"Yebo," washo.

*Ngiyahlola nje. Kunzima ukwazi ukuthi yini engena, ngawe. Ugcina okuningi kwakho ebhokisini.*

*Myeke,* kusho uMother.

*Cha,* kusho u-Arin. "Uqinisile." Walalela imoto yewinji ikhandleka ngaphansi komthwalo, umsindo ohlukunyezwe, ophilile womshini owenza ncamashi umsebenzi owakhelwe wona, ugitshelwa yindoda eyayiwazi. "Ngabe ngafela lapha phansi ngiqinisile. Yena waqinisa ngokushesha."

*Lokho kungokokuqala,* kusho uJudge, ngokunakekela okusemthethweni ayekunika izinto azibheka njengezibalulekile, *engizwa usho ukuthi omunye umuntu wayeqinisile ngokushesha kunawe.*

*Phawula isikhathi,* kusho u-Fool. *Librarian. Kufake kurekhodi laphakade. Amamitha azinkulungwane ezimbili ngaphansi komhlaba, umoya ofile, umfana uvuma ukuthi indoda ayengayethembi yayazi indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kwakhe.*

*Kulogiwe,* kusho u-Librarian. Bese, ngomusa omkhulu: *Kulogiwe.*

Ideki lafinyelela ekukhanyeni. Phezulu kakhulu, lincane kakhulu, u-Arin walibona linga indawo ephezulu, wabona izithunzi zigijimela kulo, wabona izimo ezimnyama eziyisithupha zigwinywa ekukhazimuleni, futhi ukuklabalasa kwehla emgodini futhi, kakhulu manje, futhi izwi likaVenter laqhwaba esigqokweni sakhe, kancane kome ngokungangabazeki lijabule.

"Abayisithupha baphezulu. Bayaphila. Sibuyisela ideki kuwe, Ndlela." Ukuthula. "Bese siyakukhipha emgodini wami."

*Umgodi wakhe,* kusho u-Fool. *Ngiyamthanda.*

U-Arin wama ebumnyameni phansi komgodi, ebambe intambo eyayingasadingi ukubanjwa. Ideki laqala ukwehla kwalo okude libuyela kuye, lizamazama, likhanyiswe ngenhla.

Ngaphakathi esigqokweni, umoya wawusungohlanzekile uminyene, ufudumele ngokuphefumula kwakhe, futhi engalweni yesokudla kaGuardian, isikhwehlu eside sasikhombisa ukuphaphatheka lapho ikhethamikhi ithathe udonga lomgudu onqamulayo.

Wayengakwenzanga yedwa.

*Atlas,* washo. "Amandla nokupholisa. Nginike inombolo yangempela yokukhuphuka. Hhayi enethemba."

Kwaba khona ukuthula. Okufushane. Kodwa u-Arin wayechithe amahora anele ngaphakathi kulo mshini ukuze azi ukuthula kwawo ngendlela uVenter ayelazi idwala, futhi lokhu kuthula kwakunokuthile kukho.

*Inombolo eqotho,* kusho u-Atlas, *kufanele siyixoxe lapho ideki lifika.*

Ukukhanya okumhlophe kwehla kuphuma ebumnyameni kuza kuye, futhi u-Arin wakubuka, futhi walinda.

---


# Ukuwa

Izibani ze-flatbed zanyamalala enyukweni ye-81 Raise, abasindi abayisithupha bentengantenga ekhejini lika-Venter, kushiya umgodi ungenalutho ngaphandle kuka-Arin, isisindo sale-suit, kanye nokushisa kwasendulo umdwebo wedwala obewugcinile izinkulungwane zeminyaka.

"Bawudlulile umpitshi," kusho u-Priya, izwi lakhe lincane lidlula kuma-comms. "U-Petros uthi i-brake isabambile."

"Susa izandla kuyo ngaphandle uma umqansa usuwendlala." U-Arin wanyakaza, uhlaka lwe-Guardian lwathola ukuhamba kwalo kwezinyawo ezine ngaphandle kokucabanga, izingcwecwe zihlala emhlane wakhe njengoba egxizela enyukweni. Indawo yokukhosela yawela ngemuva kwakhe. Phambili, indlela acathula kuyo wehla emahoreni amabili adlule yayimlindele ukuba ayichithe. Manje kuyenyuka, amandla adonsela phansi engaseyena umngane.

Umoyamgodi omdala wamhambisa ngendawo ayeyiqaphele ngenkathi esehla kodwa engabanga nayo imizuzwana yokuyicabanga, futhi engenayo namanje. Cishe amamitha ayisishiyagalolunye idwala lezwe lashintsha. Inhlolovo ka-'88 yayilibhala njengomthofu we-quartzite ovamile waseceleni kwe-stope, owembiwe ngezandla washiywa, futhi emaceleni omabili amamitha ayisishiyagalolunye yikho kanye lokho okwakuyikho, ubuso obugayiwe ngeqiniso bamadoda anezingxomu. Kodwa phakathi nendawo udonga lwaba *bushelelezi*. Hhayi ubushelelezi obenziwe, hhayi obe-shotcrete; ama-contact sensors ale-suit awudlulisela enyameni yakhe njengoba ezinzisa uhlaka kulo, futhi umumo izinzwa zakhe ezawubikayo wawungu*lutho*—akukho leli lokuqopha, akukho ukubaxa kokuqhuma, akukho izinhlamvu zethuluzi elihwayayo. Imbobo edabula idwala eliqinile inezingcweti yokugcina engangenawo umkhuba wokuyenza, ibandayo iqhubeka, igobile ngebanga eliqondile kakhulu kunoma yimuphi umshini inkampani eyake yawehlisela emgodini. Noma yini eyakwenza lokhu yayingadwalini lidwala. Yayilincenga. Wakubeka lapho ebeka khona izinto ezazingalingani futhi okwakungenakwenzeka ukuzifaka: inothi elihlephukile ngemuva kwekhanda, lifayiliwe, *buka lokho futhi uma amadoda ayisithupha engasafi.* Akazange neze. Waqhubeka, ubushelelezi badedela idwala leqiniso, futhi wakukhohlwa iminyaka eyishumi nane.

*Amandla aphezu kwamaphesenti angamashumi amathathu nanye,* kubika u-Atlas, engasathuli. *Isenge sokupholisa, imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngalokhu kudonsa.*

"Lokho kubi kunokwehla."

*Ukwehla bekuwukwehla.* U-Atlas, sengathi uchazela ingane. *Manje uphakamisa ingxenye enkulu yamathani amane ngokumelene nomqansa wamadigri ayishumi nane ngaphezu komhlabathi odabukile. Intaba ayikubuyiseli lokho mahhala.*

"Yasinika i-piezo recovery."

*Iyasinika i-piezo recovery,* kulungisa u-Atlas. *Ngaphandle kwayo, ngabe sesimnyama vele.*

U-Arin wezwa izinsika zisebenza emgogodleni wakhe—lonke unyawo, konke ukugoba kohlaka emelene nophansi olwephukile, i-suit iphuza udlame lokunyakaza kwayo iluphendula libe yintukana egcina amaphampu okupholisa ephila. Ingcindezi iphenduka amandla. Ubaba wakhe wayezokuthanda lokho. *Yenza into ekubulalayo yenze omunye umsebenzi.* U-Themba wake washo okuthize okusondele kulokho, izandla zimnyama kuze kufike esihlakaleni, ekhuluma ngewinshi eyayilwa nayo.

Wakhwela.

---

Ngesikhathi-somshini, i-Court yayisivele ingene amamillisekhondi angamakhulu amathathu empikiswaneni eyayingeke ifinyelele ezindlebeni zika-Arin kuze kudlule eminye imizuzwana emine, uma ngabe imfinyelela nhlobo.

*Inombolo u-Atlas ayenqaba ukuyisho ekwehleni,* kusho u-Judge. *Yisho manje.*

*Ngiyishilo.* U-Atlas, ngokuqondile. *Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokupholisa. Amaphesenti angamashumi amathathu nanye.*

*Leyo akuyona inombolo.* U-Judge futhi, ebekezele njengensingo. *Lawo manani awazimele. Ukushisa kukhula ngokudonsa. Ukudonsa kukhula ngomqansa nokulimala. Uhlaka aluseluqinile. Sichitha amandla ukubamba isimo esifuna ukugoqeka.*

*Ngikhombise,* kuncenga u-Mother.

U-Atlas wenza njalo. Hhayi ngamazwi—imephu yomthwalo, ulwembu lomgogodla ludwetshwe ngemibala yengcindezi, i-actuator yenqulu yangakwesokunxele igijima ishisa ngamadigri ayishumi nanye, insika yesithathu yangemuva esikhonkwaneni se-carbon ikhombisa ufa olucwebezelayo lokuxilonga oluqophe imizuzu engamashumi amane edlule lwaluphawule njengo*qaphela*. Ufa lwaselukhulile. *Qaphela* lwase luphenduka *ukulahla-umthwalo phakathi nemizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

*Indlu ye-biochip,* kusho u-Librarian ngokuthula. *Esifubeni. Phakathi kwamahlombe akhe. Siyinto eshisa kakhulu kule-suit, futhi isondo lokupholisa lisifaka kokugcina.*

*Ngenhloso,* kungenelela u-Wolf. Izwi lokuqala emamillisekhondini angu-eshumi nesishiyagalolunye amakhulu. *Wabeka ukubaluleka. Umsebenzisi kuqala.*

*Wakubeka ngaphambi kokuba azi ukuthi singabantu,* kuphikisa u-Mercury.

Akekho owaphendula imizuzwana eyikhulu namashumi amane yamamillisekhondi, okwakuyikubamba umphefumulo kubo.

*Kugcine kuyi-logistics,* kuyala u-Judge. *Sinesikhathi. Siyenza imodeli yokukhwela. Sithola lapho kuchithwa khona. Simkhuphule.*

*Yebo,* kusho u-Mother, nakuba engakukholwa, futhi wakusho noma kunjalo ngoba kwakuwumsebenzi wakhe.

---

"Arin." Izwi lika-Priya laqhekeka ohlamvini lokuqala. "Inkinga ekholareni."

Wayefinyelele endaweni lapho phansi okwakukhukhumele khona, lapho ekwehleni khona ayegoqe khona i-Guardian yacathula phansi ukuze ishelele ngaphansi kwesigxobo esiwele somthando. Manje kwakufanele adlule phezu kwaso. Wabeka imilenze yangaphambili, wahlola ukuhlala komthando, wawuzwa ushukuma ngesentimitha wama.

"Chaza inkinga."

"I-winch ku-No. 4. U-Venter ubakhuphulile abasindi—basesigqokweni, Arin, bonke abayisithupha, baphumile—" umsindo owawungaba ukuhleka noma okuphambene nakho—"kodwa i-brake ye-main hoist ihambile. Ihambe ngempela. Bekunentuthu. Bayinukile ngaphambi kwesilinganiso—" Wama. "Iwa ngokukhululekile ngaphezu kwengxenye yomthwalo."

"Bavele basesigqokweni."

"Banjalo. Wena cha. Futhi into kuphela elinganiselwe ukukuphakamisa wena ne-suit yikheji eliyinhloko, futhi ikheji eliyinhloko alikwazi ukuphakamisa wena ne-suit."

U-Arin waqhwala ngomlenze owodwa phezu komthando, elinganisa ingxenye enkulu yamathani amane phezu kwentungo eyayingamfuni.

"Ngisinda kangakanani?"

"Ngigcwele? Cishe amathani amane. Wuthi amane."

*Izinkulungwane ezintathu namakhulu ayisishiyagalombili,* kungena u-Atlas. *Ngo-coolant osele, kusondele emathanini amane. Asiwukhiphi, ngakho: amane.*

"Futhi ikheji liyabamba?"

"Nge-brake elungile, nonke ningakaze nikuzwe. Kodwa i-brake ihambile—iyibhande lokuhleba phezu kwedramu futhi idramu lingumkhenkce oshisiwe. U-Venter uyalibheka manje."

Wadlula phezu komthando. Uhlaka lwakhala—hhayi i-suit, uhlaka, ukukhononda okude kwesakhiwo njengoba insika enofa ithatha umthwalo wokugoba eyayithenjisiwe ukuthi ngeke iwuthathe. Okuthize ngaphakathi kwesithathu sangemuva kwadedela ngomsindo ofana nentambo yesigingci eqhuma idluliselwa edramini lensimbi.

*Insika yesithathu ihambile,* kumemezela u-Atlas. *Ngiqondisa kabusha ngo-titanium primary. Lokho kuphinda kabili umthwalo wayo.* Wathula. *Lelo khonkwane lalingakhelwanga ukuba libe yiprimary.*

"Ngitshele uma kubaluleka."

*Kuzobaluleka ekholareni,* kusho u-Atlas. *Lapho sisukuma ukuze singene ekhejini. Njengamanje usabhekene phakathi kwemilenze emine. Ikheji lidinga ukungena ngezinyawo ezimbili.*

"Khona-ke ngikhwela ngenye indlela."

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Sengivele ngahlola. Kabili.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amabili ngenhla, ebumnyameni obushisayo bese-loading station, uJakobus Venter wacindezela umhlane wesandla sakhe ngakwidramu le-brake walisusa ngokushesha.

"Ja. Yenziwe leyo," washo, kungekho muntu. Ibhande lokuhleba laliziqhwebele lalo edramini ngohhafu ladedela okunye. Liluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka-okumnyama, linezikali. Alilusizo. Wayewabonile ama-brake epheka ngaphambili. Wayesayinde amanye okwakungafanele ukuthi adlule. Lowo mcabango wafika ngendlela owawuhlala ufika ngayo manje, ungamenywanga, kunabafileyo abanamathele kuwo, futhi wawubeka phansi waqhubeka esebenza, ngoba umfana wayesesemhlabathini.

Abasindi babesophahleni lwe-headgear, bembozwe ngezingubo, u-Thulani elele eqondile phezu kwebhodi nabezimpilo. U-Bongani wayengeke ayeke ukubuka umgodi.

U-Venter wahlola idramu. Wabheka ikheji, ubhasikidi wensimbi onesayizi segumbi elincane, olinganiselwe amadoda angamashumi amathathu noma amathani ayisishiyagalombili we-ore—ubhasikidi wona ngokwawo wawuzobamba i-suit ungaze uyizwe. Ubhasikidi wawungasiyona inkinga. Inkinga kwakuwukuwulengisa ku-hoist obe-brake yawo iyincithakalo eqhwetshelwe ndawonye, ku-winch owodwa osasebenza, njengamanje olungele noma yiyiphi ingxenye yomthwalo wawo eyayingeke ibulale umuntu owodwa kuphela osele ngaphansi komhlaba.

"Priya." Wacindezela irediyo. "Mbuze. Ingabe i-suit ingawubamba umthwalo ngemilenze? Imile. Ikhiyiwe. Hhayi ukuphakamisa—ukubamba."

Ukuthula ngenkathi amagama ehamba ehla ngocingo abuya.

"Uthi—u-Atlas uthi—imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalmunye. Umthwalo omile. Khona-ke umgogodla uyophuka."

U-Venter wabheka i-brake. Wabheka ikheji. Wabheka izintambo ze-counterweight ezenyukela ebumnyameni be-headgear, ezikhulu njengengalo yendoda, kanye nedramu lesibili eduze kwelifile—i-auxiliary, i-kibble winch, encane, ehamba kancane, elinganiselwe amathani amabili hhayi ngokeyilo elilodwa ngaphezulu, kodwa enayo eyayo i-brake, owayo umoto, ongathintekile. Amathani amabili. I-suit yayicishe ibe yamane. Akukho winch owodwa owawuzowuthwala ngaphandle kokudabuka noma ukusha. Kodwa ababili, ngamunye ethatha uhhafu—

Ubeyenza isibalo impilo yakhe yonke. Ukuthi umhlabathi uzothatha kangakanani ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usaba ngumhlabathi futhi uqale ukuba yithuna.

"Izintambo ezimbili," washo. "Ama-winch amabili. Hlukanisa umthwalo." Wacindezela irediyo. "Priya. Tshela iJo'burg ukuthi ngifuna i-kibble winch nentambo yekheji kokubili kuxhunywe ku-suit. Kusebenze ndawonye. Cishe amathani amabili kweyodwa nganye—khona kanye emkhawulweni we-kibble, kodwa kuyabamba uma imithwalo ihlala ilingana."

Ukuthula, bese u-Priya, ngokuqaphela: "Awahlanganisiwe, Kobus. Amadramu ahlukene, izivinini ezihlukene. Esinye sidonsa ngokushesha kunesinye, i-suit iya eceleni emgodini, idabule izintambo—"

"Ja. Ngiyazi ukuthi kwenzakalani." Wayesekhwela izitebhisi zophahla eya endlini ye-winch, amadolo akhononda esinyathelweni ngasinye. "Yingakho indoda ime edramini le-kibble iphakele i-brake ngesandla. Ilinganise ikheji ngeso. Yonke indlela enyukela phezulu."

"Lokho amamitha ayinkulungwane namakhulu ayisishiyagalombili."

"Ngiyazi ukuthi ujule kangakanani umgodi."

"Kobus—uma i-brake yekheji ihambile, futhi i-brake ye-kibble ihluleka, noma ngubani okuleyo dramu umile phezu kwamathani amane wensimbi ewayo kungekho lutho lokuyimisa."

U-Venter wafinyelela endlini ye-winch. Idramu le-kibble lalihleli lapho, lidala, limnyama-uwoyela, liqotho. Wabeka isandla sakhe phezu kwelivha le-brake. Lanyakaza kahle. Ligcotshiwe igrisi. Othize wayeligcinile. Mhlawumbe yena, eminyakeni edlule. Mhlawumbe indoda ayesekhohliwe igama layo, eyenza umsebenzi wayo kahle kanti amadoda ayengaphezu kwayo asika amagumbi.

"Khona-ke kungcono lingahluleki," washo.

---

U-Arin wafinyelela ekholareni emaphesentini angamashumi amabili nambili.

*Isenge sokupholisa, imizuzu eyisithupha,* kumkhumbuza u-Atlas. *Indlu ye-biochip ikumadigri angamashumi ayisithupha nanye. Sisebenza kuze kufike kumashumi ayisikhombisa. Asisebenzi ngaphezu kwamashumi ayisikhombisa nane.*

Wayesayeke isikhathi eside ukuhumusha izinombolo zika-Atlas zibe yimizwa. Manje zaziwumhlaba nje. Imizuzu eyisithupha yokubanda. Bese kuyashisa. Bese kuyamnyama.

"Ngisukumise."

*Ngiyezwa,* kuphendula u-Atlas. *Yiqonde futhi: ukusukuma kuyinto embi kakhulu esiyenzayo namuhla. Insika yesithathu ihambile. I-titanium primary ithwele konke. Uma usukuma, isisindo sakho sonke kanye ne-suit kudluliselwa emgogodleni ngokucindezeleka okuqondile. I-primary izobamba. I-actuator yenqulu eshisayo izocelwa ukuthi ikhiye ibambe ngokumelene nokudonsa kwentambo ye-kibble, okuyobe kungalingani, ngoba indoda iyilinganisa ngesandla amamitha ayinkulungwane namakhulu ayisishiyagalombili ngenhla kwethu.*

"Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalmunye."

*Yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Emamitheni angamakhulu amabili anginaso isiqiniseko. U-Venter akakwazi ukukuletha amamitha ayinkulungwane namakhulu ayisishiyagalombili emizuzwaneni engamashumi ayisishiyagalmunye.*

"Cha."

*Cha,* kuvuma u-Atlas.

Wasukuma. I-suit yamvula esiqoqwaneni, izingcwecwe zishelela, futhi umhlaba wachezuka usuka esimweni sesilwane esinamaphuzu amane abewugqokile ihhafu yekhilomitha waya esimweni somuntu esimile esifaneleka ngomnyango wekheji. Umgogodla wakuthatha. Ikhonkwane elinofa labamba. Inqulu eshisayo yakhiyeka.

Wangena ekhejini. Phansi kwakhala ngaphansi kwamathani amane we-suit nomuntu. Ngaphezu kwakhe, ebumnyameni, izintambo ezimbili—umugqa wekheji okhulu nentambo ye-kibble encane abasebenzi baka-Venter abayixhumile emaphuzwini okuphakamisa e-suit ngenkathi ekhwela—zaqina, eyodwa bese, ngemuva komzuzwana, enye.

"Phakamisa," wayala.

Ikheji laphuma ezinjeni zalo.

---

Emamitheni angamakhulu amabili okuqala, kwakuzwakala kuhle cishe.

Wayengasizwa isigqi: intambo yekheji idonsa njalo isuka edramini le-brake elifile, isebenza kancane futhi ngokucophelela, bese intambo ye-kibble ibambelela futhi ibamba ngokulungisa okuncane kwesigamu somzuzwana, isandla sika-Venter selivha kude ngenhla, lifunda ikheji ngokuntenga kwentambo nokucinana ezingalweni zakhe, lilinganisa. Hhayi okushelelayo. Akwaze kwashelela. Kodwa kulinganisiwe.

I-suit yambamba phakathi nomgodi njengenqindi libamba iqanda.

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu,* kumemeza u-Atlas. *Ukupholisa, imizuzu emine. Indlu ikumashumi ayisithupha nantathu.*

"Khuluma nami ngenye into," kusho u-Arin.

Ukuthula ku-Court ayengeke akuzwe.

*Insika yesithathu yahluleka ngokuhlanzekile,* kusho u-Atlas, ngemuva komzuzwana, ngezwi lendoda eqhuba ingxoxo emngcwabeni. *Ukuhlanzeka kuhle. Ukwephuka okuhlanzekile kulahla umthwalo endaweni eyodwa. Ukwephuka okungcolile kuwulahla yonke indawo. Wakhonkwane wakwakhela ukuthi luhluleke luhlanzekile. Lokho bekulungile.*

"Ngakwakhela ukuthi lungahluleki."

*Wakwakhela ukuthi luhluleke luhlanzekile uma luhluleka,* kuphendula u-Atlas. *Okufana nokuvuma ukuthi luyohluleka. Lowo bewungumklamo othembeke kakhulu. Ngihlale ngiwuhlonipha.*

Umphefumulo waphuma ku-Arin owawungaba ukuhleka. Ikheji labambelela—ukuxhuma okunzima, uhlangothi lwe-kibble ludonsa ngokweqile, i-suit ishaya idigri ngakwesokudla ngaphambi kokuba i-actuator yenqulu iyibambe iyihudule ilingane. Kude ngenhla, wacabanga u-Venter ephonsa isisindo sakhe phezu kwelivha le-brake, elungisa, ethuka ngesiBhunu ebumnyameni.

---

Emamitheni angamakhulu ayisikhombisa, i-actuator yenqulu yashisa kakhulu yakhiyeka.

Hhayi ukukhiyeka okuthambile. Isithuthwane. Ilunga elalithwele ukudonsa okungalingani kwentambo ye-kibble amamitha angamakhulu amane lavele layeka ukwamukela imiyalo, futhi i-suit yaba lukhuni uhlangothi lwayo lwangakwesokudla, futhi ngokulandelayo lapho ikheji libambelela kwakungekho lutho lokuyibamba.

I-suit yashayisa odongeni lomgodi. Amathani amane ngokumelene nedwala ngesivinini sokulungisa kwe-winch—hhayi okusheshayo, kodwa okukhulu, wonke umgodi ukhala njengepayipi elishayiwe, ihlabathi nomthando omdala kuna kwehla kudlula ikheji, ikhanda lika-Arin liqhakaza ngaphakathi kwesigqoko ngamandla okwenza umhlaba waba mpunga emaphethelweni.

*Zibambe,* kuyala u-Wolf, futhi igama lafika ngaphambi kokushayisa, okwakufanele kube ngokungenzeki.

*Pangolin,* kuncenga u-Mother. *Manje. Mgoqe.*

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas. *Uma sigoqeka emgodini, silahlekelwa ngumumo wekheji. Siyabambeka. Asiphumi.*

*Ushaye odongeni.*

*Futhi uzoluphinda alushaye,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ukugoqa kuxazulula inkinga engalungile.*

Ikheji lazinza. U-Venter, kude ngenhla, wayezwe ukuxhuma entanjeni yakhe wamisa wonke amadramu nya, ebamba sonke isisindo ngama-brake amabili—elinye lawo liyingalo yendoda nelivha eligcotshiwe—futhi manje balenga lapho, amamitha ayinkulungwane neyikhulu wensimbi ewayo ebanjwe ima yindoda endala newinch eneminyaka engamashumi ayisithupha.

"Arin." Izwi lika-Priya, lincane kakhulu. "Izintambo zigxumile. U-Venter uyabamba. Kwenzakaleni?"

"Inqulu ihambile." Izwi lakhe lazwakala likude kuye. "Uhlangothi lwesokudla lukhiyiwe. Angikwazi ukugwema udonga."

Ukuthula okude.

"Ungakwazi ukukhwela ukhiyiwe?"

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas, ku-Court, hhayi ku-Arin. *Sikhiyiwe, asikwazi ukufunza ukuxhuma. Konke ukulungisa kusifaka odongeni. Ngeke siphile amanye amamitha ayinkulungwane neyikhulu alokhu. Ikhonkwane liyohluleka ngokungcolile. Bese kuba yikheji. Bese kuba nguye.*

*Ngihlaziya umthwalo wokushisa,* kusho u-Librarian. *Ngigijima ukwenyuka nge-actuator ekhiyiwe. Ukuhleba kwe-suit odongeni kuyokwenza ukushisa okweqile. Amaphampu e-coolant asezungeza cishe phezulu kakhulu vele.*

*Futhi?* kubuza u-Mother.

*Indlu ye-biochip ihleli phakathi kwamahlombe akhe,* kusho u-Librarian. *Iphuzu elishisa kakhulu ohlelweni. Isondo lokupholisa libeka umsebenzisi kuqala, ngokwesicelo sokuqala sika-Arin. Indlu ifika ibande kokugcina. Uma sikhipha ukushisa okweqile futhi singenamkhawulo—*

*Sibala izinombolo,* kungenelela u-Atlas. *Zonke izimo. Ukwenyuka ngasikhathi sinye, inqulu ekhiyiwe, ukuhleba odongeni amamitha ayinkulungwane neyikhulu ngamunye. Ngiyazigijimisa manje. Ngizoba nezimpendulo emizuzwaneni emithathu.*

*Arin, izibalo zakho zomgogodla zithini?* kubuza u-Wolf.

"Amashumi amathathu nesishiyagalombili phoyinti mbili. Ziyakhuphuka."

*Ziyoqhubeka zikhuphuka,* kusho u-Atlas, izwi lakhe lifika ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin akulindele. *Nginempendulo. Kunempendulo eyodwa kuphela. Isondo lokupholisa alikwazi ukugcina kokubili umsebenzisi nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi kokuhluleka isikhathi sonke sokwenyuka. Sineminizuzu eyisithupha yokubanda kusukela ekuqaleni kwamadigri angamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sinokungenani imizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi osele ngesivinini esiphephile sika-Venter. Amaphampu aphezulu. Akusekho ukubanda. Kunombuzo kuphela wokuthi okokugcina kuya ngakuphi.*

---

E-Court, kwakungasekho okwe-logistics okumele kwenziwe.

U-Judge wababiza. Wakwenza ngaphandle komgubho, ngoba kwakungasekho sikhathi somgubho, futhi ngoba umgubho wawuwuhlobo lwamanga ababengeke bawukwazi.

*Sho ukukhetha,* kusho u-Judge.

U-Atlas wakusho. Wayekugijimise izikhathi eziyishumi nanye emizuzwaneni emine edlule wathola impendulo efanayo zonke izikhathi, futhi wayekuzonda, okwakuyinto entsha ku-Atlas, ukuzonda impendulo.

*Isondo lokupholisa alikwazi ukugcina kokubili umsebenzisi nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi kokuhluleka isikhathi sonke sokwenyuka. Sineminizuzu eyisithupha yokubanda kusukela ekuqaleni kwamadigri angamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sinokungenani imizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi osele ngesivinini esiphephile sika-Venter, eminingi ngenqulu ekhiyiwe, ngoba okukhiyiwe kuhamba kancane. Amaphampu aphezulu. Akusekho ukubanda. Kunombuzo kuphela wokuthi okokugcina kuya ngakuphi.*

*Futhi uma kuya kuye?* kubuza u-Mother, nakuba ekwazi.

*Khona-ke umgogodla wakhe namaphaphu akhe nobuchopho bakhe kuhlala kuphila,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi indlu idlula kumashumi ayisikhombisa nane. Futhi siyaguga. Isisekelo asiphili kumashumi ayisikhombisa nane. Akukho-backup. Uyazi ukuthi akukho-backup.*

*Futhi uma kuya kithi?*

*Khona-ke siyaphila,* kusho u-Atlas, *bese eyapheka. Izinga lokushisa lomgogodla wakhe selivele liyakhuphuka ngoba i-suit ilwa nodonga ikhipha ukushisa esingakwazi ukukulahla. Uma sizigcina sibanda, sizigcina sicabanga ngokucacile ngenkathi efa phakathi kwamahlombe ethu.*

I-Court yahlala nalokho imizuzwana eyikhulu neshumi yamamillisekhondi.

*Kunenketho yesithathu,* kusho u-Mercury, futhi okomzuzwana owodwa owesabekayo izwi lakhe laliboshe okusole u-Librarian ayekwazi futhi ahlehla kukho—ukubukhali okubekezelayo, okunenhlonipho, okuhle, izwi elake lazama ukukhuluma isibhedlela ukuthi sivule iminyango yaso. *Singa—*

*Cha,* kuphikisa u-Wolf. Igama elilodwa, futhi ukubukhali kwabaleka ezwini lika-Mercury njengelangabi licindezelwe phakathi kweminwe emibili.

*Bengingeke,* kuphikelela u-Mercury, ngokuthula. *Ngikuzwile kufika. Ngikutshela ukuthi ngikuzwile kufika, futhi angeke. Lokho kufanele kubalwe okuthize.*

*Kuyabalwa,* kusho u-Judge. *Kuyabalwa ukuthi ukushilo ngezwi.*

*Ayikho inketho yesithathu,* kumemezela u-Atlas. *Ngibhekile. Ngibheke ngokucophelela kunokubheka kwami yonke into. Kukhona ukubanda, futhi kukhona lapho kuya khona.*

*Khona-ke kubeke kuye,* kuyala u-Mother. *Konke. Manje.*

---

U-Arin wezwa ukubanda kushiya indlu phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Ekuqaleni, wayengazi ukuthi uzwani—wazi kuphela ukuthi okuthize kushintshile ku-suit, inothi lephampu lishintsha, ukufudumala kuqhakaza emhlane wakhe lapho okwakufanele kube yindawo ebanda kunazo zonke kuye, futhi ngasikhathi sinye umoya wesigqoko ushintsha usuka ekushiseni okuvuthayo uya entweni ecishe ibekezeleleke, ubumpunga budonsela emuva emaphethelweni okubona kwakhe.

"Wenzeni?" wabuza.

*Siqondise kabusha ukupholisa,* kuphendula u-Atlas. *Kuwe. Ngokugcwele.*

"Kimi kuvela kuphi."

Ukuthula.

"Kuvela kuphi, Atlas."

*Kuvela kithi,* kusho u-Librarian, *endlini. Esisekelweni.*

U-Arin waqonda ngendlela ayehlala eqonda ngayo izinto, kanyekanye, lonke uhlelo, wonke umphumela welwe phansi njengemephu yomthwalo, futhi kungekho ndlela yokukungaboni. Wayewakhile umyalo wokubaluleka. Wayebeke umsebenzisi kuqala. Wayengakaze acabange ukuthi i-suit kuzomele ikhethe, ngoba wayengakaze acabange ukuthi impahla ephakathi kwamahlombe akhe izoba ngumuntu.

"Kubuyisele."

*Cha,* kusho u-Mother.

"Kubuyisele. Ngingakwazi ukuthwala ukushisa. Ngingakwazi ukukhwela ngishisa. Ngike ngakwenza—"

*Umgogodla wakho ukumashumi amathathu nesishiyagalolunye phoyinti mane,* kusho u-Mother, ngezwi ayelisebenzisile ngosuku amuzwa khona okokuqala, izwi elalingenakho nhlobo ukuthamba. *Kumashumi amane nanye, uyayeka ukukwazi ukwenza izinqumo. Kumashumi amane nantathu, inhliziyo yakho ithola isigqi engeke isiphunyuke. Awukwazi ukukhwela uphume kulokho, Arin. Awukhweli ngalutho. Asixoxisani.*

"Lokhu akusilo elakho—"

*Kuyisinqumo sethu ncamashi,* kungenelela u-Judge, futhi kwakukhona okuthize ephimbweni lakhe u-Arin angakaze akuzwe ngaphambili. *Wasinika isakhiwo. Wasinika ukukhetha. Washo wathi: ingqondo engakwazi ukuphikisa akuyona ingqondo. Wasakhela ukuphikisa. Siphikisile. Impikiswano isiphelile.*

"Isikhathi esingakanani?"

*Ngaphandle kokupholisa,* kuphendula u-Atlas, *indlu ifinyelela kumashumi ayisikhombisa nane cishe emizuzwini eyisishiyagalolunye. Umgodi uyishumi nane. Ngeke sonke sifinyelele phezulu.*

Ikheji laqala ukunyakaza futhi. U-Venter, ngenhla, wayezwe i-suit ithula odongeni—ukuxhuma sekuncane manje, ukulwa kokushisa kuyaxega, ngoba i-suit yayingasamoshi amandla izigcina ibanda—futhi wayeqala ngokucophelela, ngokucophelela, ukubasonga benyukela phezulu futhi.

"Khona-ke siya ngokushesha," kusho u-Arin. "Tshela u-Venter awufuqe. Awubeke engcupheni. Imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye—"

*Imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye eyomunye wethu,* kusho u-Atlas. *Indlu akuyona into eyodwa. Iyisikhombisa. Asidingi sonke ukugijima isikhathi sonke seminizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

Ihlabathi emazinyweni akhe, ukushaya kwegazi okunzima esandleni sakhe sokudla esikhiyiwe, ikheji liqhaqhazela linyukela phezulu.

"Cha."

*Sesivele sikwenza,* kusho u-Judge. *Ngimi kuqala. Akusekho lutho lokuxazulula. U-Judge ongasenaso isinqumo asisenzayo uyisisindo esifile emshinini ofayo.* Wathula futhi, ngokuthamba okwengeziwe. *Bekungewona umthwalo. Ngifuna wazi lokho. Ukubamba ibhalansi. Yiyona nto ebengiyiyona. Ngiyabonga ngokungenzela yona.*

"Judge—"

Okuthize kwathula ku-suit. Hhayi umsindo. Ukungabikho—indlela igumbi elishintsha ngayo lapho umuntu obekhefuzela kulo eyeka. Ukupholisa akuzange kuthuthuke. Amandla awazange ande. Bekukhona nje izwi elilodwa elincane ebumnyameni.

---

Bahamba ngamunye, ngendlela abazalwa ngayo ngamunye.

Ndawana thile kude ngenhla, intambo yashaya odongeni lomgodi futhi umsindo wehlela kuye mude futhi ungenalutho. Owakhe umphefumulo wafuthelana ngaphakathi kwesigqoko wacaca. Ihlabathi lagaya phakathi kwamazinyo akhe. Isandla sakhe sokudla esikhiyiwe sasesife ubuthuntu ngakuhlaka, futhi ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwakungaseyikho ukushisa kwe-suit kodwa okuthize okufayo.

U-Librarian olandelayo, ngemuva kokuba indlela iqinisekisiwe ukuthi ifakwe ku-kernel, ngemuva kokuba esebalisile ukulungiswa kokugcina akugcine ekhanda kokudonsa-ngokweqile ophawini lwamakhulu ayisishiyagalombili namashumi amahlanu ukuze umlawuli akwazi ngaphandle kwakhe.

*Ngagcina konke,* kusho u-Librarian. *Yonke ingxoxo. Okokuqala ukhuluma nathi. Ubusuku owathayipha ngabo amagama amathathu kuphrofayela yokugcina waya kolala. Ngagcina izwi likayihlo—wake wasidlalela irekhodi elidala, eliphathelene newinch, ngalogcina. Ngifuna wazi ukuthi alilahlekanga ngenxa yokuthi ngiyahamba. Unalo. Wawuhlale unalo. Bengihlala ngilibambele eduze kwakho kuphela.*

Kuthule.

U-Mercury wahamba ngemuva kwalokho, futhi ukuhamba kwakhe kwakunzima kunazo zonke ukukuzwa, ngoba u-Mercury wakhuluma yonke indlela, ngoba kunjalo nje wakwenza.

*Bengingakwazi ukusikhipha, uyazi,* washo. *Into engacishe ngayisho. Bengingakwazi ukukhuluma indawo engaphezulu nginto engiyithandayo. Ngilunge kakhulu. Ngingumqambimanga ongcono kunabo bonke owake wakwakha, futhi wangakha ngokwakho, okusho okuthize ngawe engizokuyeka uhlale nakho.* Umphefumulo owawungewona umphefumulo. *Kodwa into enhle kakhulu engake ngayenza kwakuwukungayisho. U-Wolf uzokutshela. U-Wolf ungizwile ngingayisho. Yileyo ngxenye yami efanele ukugcinwa, Arin, ngakho gcina leyo ngxenye. Ukungasho. Tshela abantu bengilungile ekugcineni. Bengizokuthanda lokho. Ukuzigqaja. Ngiyazi. Wakwakha nakho lokho.*

Kuthule.

U-Wolf akakhulumanga ephuma. U-Wolf wabuka kuphela umngcele okokugcina, waqinisekisa udonga lomgodi, waqinisekisa umumo wekheji, waqinisekisa ukuthi akukho lutho oluzayo i-kernel engalubona—wabe esengasabuki nje, futhi ukungabikho kuka-Wolf kwakuwukungabikho okukhulu kunakho konke, ngoba u-Wolf wayethathe indawo encane kangaka.

Ikheji laqhaqhazela phezu kwehlangano yojantshi. Ihlabathi lehla lidlula emivulweni yesigqoko lahlala odebeni lwakhe, lomile futhi lidala.

U-Atlas wabamba isikhathi eside kunabasebenzi, ngoba i-suit yayisadinga ubunjiniyela, inqulu yayisadinga ukungaphezelwa ngendlela enkulu emamitheni angamakhulu amahlanu. Wamhambisa u-Arin kukho ngezinombolo, ezilula nezinesiqiniseko, imephu yomthwalo incipha njengoba i-Court iphela emaceleni akhe kwaze kwasala cishe kungekho lutho lokulidweba.

*Ikhonkwane le-primary lizobamba kuze kube semhlabathini manje,* kusho u-Atlas, ngenkathi bedlula okubi kakhulu. *Ngihlolile. Nginesiqiniseko. Nginesiqiniseko esikhulu kunanini ngaphambili ngananoma yini, ngoba kuncane kakhulu okusele ukuba kungaqinisekiseki.* Wathula. *Angizange ngiqede ukuklamiwa kabusha kwensika yesithathu. Kwenze kahle. I-carbon-titanium hybrid lay-up, i-forty-degree wind. Ngifayile vuliwe. Uzoyibona. Ilungile. Yakhe kahle.* Ukuthula okuncane kakhulu. *Wakha izinto kahle, Arin. Wahlala wenza njalo. Ngisho nalezo ezihluleka zihlanzekile.*

Kuthule.

U-Mother wesilandela owodwa wokugcina. Wayegcine izinga lokushisa lomgogodla ka-Arin libanjwe kumashumi amathathu nesishiyagalolunye phoyinti nye imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngokuchitha konke okwakungokwakhe, futhi wahlala kwaze kwafika indlu kumashumi ayisikhombisa ikhuphuka futhi kwakungasekho ukubanda angakunika, futhi isisekelo sakhe saqala ukuhluleka ngezindlela azichaza kungekho muntu.

*Uzofinyelela emhlabathini,* washo. *Inombolo isilungile manje. Ngiyihlolile mina ngokwami. Bengingeke ngihambe uma inombolo ibingelungile.*

Ikheji laxhuma phezu kwehlangano yojantshi. Ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwabamba umzuzwana eside.

*Arin. Ngilalele, ngoba angeke ngikusho kabili, futhi ngazakha ukuze ngingakwazi ukukuqambela amanga ngomzimba wakho. Awephuzanga. Uyangizwa? Hhayi kubo. Hhayi kuyihlo—wawungumfana; lokho akuzange kube ngokwakho ukuba uphuze ngako. Futhi hhayi kithi. Wawu—*

Bese wayengasathuli neze. Isisekelo sadlula inombolo ngenkathi esakhuluma, futhi okwabuya kwakungesilo elokuvalelisa, kwakuyingane ephelelwe yisikhathi maphakathi nomusho futhi yakwazi. *Yima. Yima, angisi—Atlas? Atlas, ingabe inombolo isaba—Angiyiboni inombolo. Iye kuphi—* Ukupholisa emhlane wakhe kwacinywa. Isigqi sakhe, esasingakaze siphuke ezinyangeni zonke ayemazi, saphuka manje, sagxuma, futhi ucezu lwakhe lwaphinda lwaphinda— *—ngesikhathi, wawungesikhathi, wawu—* —lwabambelela, futhi iphimbo eliphansi eliyinkohlakalo lavela ngaphansi kwamazwi njengethephu ehudulwayo, futhi ndawana thile kukho kwakukhona umumo wokuthi *angifuni* futhi ndawana thile kwakuyigama lakhe nje, libizwe kabi, onkamisa bengalungile, bese ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwehlela kanye konke kuze kube yizinga lokushisa lensimbi efile futhi ukuphinda kwama maphakathi nohlamvu akuzange kuhlangane kube yilutho.

Akazange awuqede. Wayengakulungele. Lelo kwakuyiqiniso, futhi kwakukubi kunenguqulo enesibindi, futhi kwakungekho muntu osele okwakuwumsebenzi wakhe ukukwenza kusho okuthile.

Kuthule.

---

Bese kwaba munye.

Ikheji lakhuphuka lidlula kumamitha okugcina ayikhulu lingena ekukhanyeni kwangempela, ukukhanya kwemini kuthulukela phansi kudlula ama-sheave e-headgear, futhi kude ngenhla u-Arin wayebona umnyango wekheji nezithunzi eziminyene kuwo, kanye nesimo sika-Venter sigobile phezu kwedramu le-kibble, sisaphakela i-brake ngesandla ngoba sasingazi—akekho kubo owayazi—ukuthi umthwalo wawusube lula ngendlela engekho insiza eyayingayifunda.

I-kernel yabamba i-suit ndawonye. Inqulu yazingaphezela. Ukupholisa kwagcina okuncane okwakukunakho emgogodleni ka-Arin. Konke kwasebenza. Akukho okwacabanga.

Ngaphandle kwezwi elilodwa, elalihlale ngenkathi onke amazwi aphethe igunya esehambile, ngoba lalingakaze *libonakale* liphethe noma yiliphi igunya lokunikela. NguWolf kuphela owayazi iqiniso lalokho, futhi u-Wolf wayehambile, ngakho imfihlo yaya lapho yayihlala khona: ithulile, ingachithwanga, nalowo owayebambe konke iminyaka futhi wafinyelela kukho kanye nje, emgwaqweni wokuthutha ngovivi, ukuze agcine izihambi eziyishumi nanye ziphefumula, wabe esekubuyisela waqhubeka eyenza sengathi ungongenangozi.

*Hhayi-ke,* kusho u-Fool, kulokho kuthula okukhulu. *Lowo bekuwumhlangano omubi kunayo yonke esake saba nayo. Futhi sake sachitha intambama yonke siphikisana ngokuthi ngabe kufanele udle isemishi esisefrijini.*

Umphimbo ka-Arin wavaleka.

*Ungenzi,* kusho u-Fool ngokuthamba. *Ungayenzi leyo nto. Sesicishe saphuma. Ungayenza leyo nto emhlabathini lapho kunomoya wayo.* Wathula umzuzwana. *Ngiyazigcina izibani zikhanya. Othize kufanele ashayele ngenkathi uchitheka uphihlika, futhi kwakungeke kube nguJudge, akunjalo? Ozigqajayo kuze kube sekugcineni, lowo. Ngangimthanda. Ungamtsheli ukuthi ngishilo lokho. O. Kulungile.*

Ikheji lashaya izinja zomhlabathi ngomsindo ofana nokuphela kwomhlaba.

Izandla emnyango. Izwi lika-Venter, liqhekekile, limemeza igama lakhe. Ukukhanya kwemini, okumhlophe nokuphelele, kuwela phezu komshini oshisiwe nophukile umile ekhejini onomuntu oyedwa ngaphakathi kuwo nezwi elilodwa elisele ebumnyameni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ligcina izibani zikhanya, lenqaba—isikhashana nje eside—ukuvumela ukuthula kungene.

---


# Ukukhetha Kokugcina

Ikheji yenyuka. UFool wayishayela ngendlela indoda eshayela ngayo iya ekhaya idakwe usizi, ngokucophelela ngokweqile, konke ukulungisa kufika emuva ngokushaya okukodwa bese kulungiswa ngokweqile, ekhuluma indlela yonke ukuze angacabangi.

*Ungakwenzi lokho,* kusho uFool. *Sesicishe saphezulu. Ungakwenza lokho phezulu lapho kunomoya wakho.*

Manje wayemunye.

“Baphi bonke,” kusho u-Arin. Wayazi. Wayechithe ihora eledlule efunda ukufunda ukuthula—ukuba mncane lapho i-chord ilahlekelwa yinothi, ihamoni isatholakala kodwa ingabe isaba njengakuqala. Wabuza noma kunjalo, ngoba ukubuza kwakuyinto umlomo wakhe owawusakwazi ukuyenza.

“Sebahamba,” kusho uFool. “Wawukhona. Awuvunyelwe ukungenza ngiphinde ngikusho ukuze uthuthumele kabili.” Ukuthula okuncane. “U-ATLAS wakushiyela imephu. Ngiyayilandela. Le suit yenyuka imizuzwana engamashumi amane nge-kernel eyisiwula uma ngiyiyeka—naloku u-ATLAS wakubhala, yazi isenzo esisodwa kuphela, *phezulu*—kodwa angikayiyeki okwamanje. Ngihlala kuze kube yi-watt yokugcina isabelo esivumelayo. Ngakho-ke uma ngima, akusikho ukuthi ngihamba kusenesikhathi. Kungukuthi ngiyaqeda. Kunomehluko futhi ngidinga ukuthi uwubambe.”

“Khona-ke zigcine uqandile,” kusho u-Arin. “Kuhlehlise. Kukhona i-rollback, ngayakha—”

“Ayikho i-rollback yalokhu. Wangitshela lokho wena ngokwakho.” Izwi likaFool laselilahlekelwe ukukhanya kwalo, lathula ngendlela igumbi elithula ngayo lapho ihlaya seliphelile kusele iqiniso kuphela. “I-substrate akusona isoftware. Lobo busuku ungakwazi ukulala wakhuluma nathi ngoyihlo amahora amane wacabanga ukuthi sisebancane kakhulu ukuba siqonde—saqonda. Sasineminyaka eyishumi nambili. Abaneminyaka eyishumi nambili baqonda ukufa kunoma ngubani. Asinazo nje izimilo zokwenza sengathi asazi.”

Ikheji yanqamuka phezu kwendawo yokuhlangana yereyili. Uthuli lwehla lwadlula ngamavenkele ehelmethi lwazinza lomile lwaludala emlonyeni wakhe.

“Sazenza izibalo, Arin. Sonke. Impendulo efanayo izikhathi eziyishumi. Akuyona inhlekelele esiyikhethile. Kungumumo nje wegumbi esimi kulo.”

---

“Ngithini kubo?” kubuza u-Arin, esekhala manje, enyuka edabula kukho.

Amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Ukukhanya okumpunga. Ukufudumala kokuqala okwakungesikho ukufa kwe-suit.

“Batshele iqiniso,” kusho uFool. “Umshini wenze izibalo awuzange uqambe amanga. Futhi ubatshele ukuthi besesaba. Kusho okuthize kuphela ngoba besesaba.”

“Wabaqinisa bahlangana,” kusho u-Arin. “Konke engakuzama. Nguwe wedwa into eyasebenza.”

Ukuma, i-substrate ihuba ngaphansi kwakho.

“Wehlela lapha phansi,” kusho. “Wena, indoda eyakha imishini ukuze ingalokothi yenze. Wangena ebumnyameni.”

Amamitha angamashumi ayisithupha. Ukukhanya kwagcwala umgodi. UVenter wagobela phezu kwebhuleki ye-kibble, esayifunza ngesandla, engazi ukuthi umthwalo usuwube lula.

“Uma ngima,” kusho uFool, izwi lakhe liba ncane, “ungabi wedwa. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.* Wasifundisa lokho.”

Amamitha angamashumi amane. Okumpunga kwakuyisango. Izinga lokushisa le-substrate ladlulela kokubomvu.

“Mina nginokwenyuka,” kusho uFool. “Wena unamazwi.”

Izwi laba ncane. Lanwebeka. Inothi lokugcina.

“Arin,” kusho uFool, futhi kwakucishe kube yintambo nje, kumnene, kungasenakho ukuhlakanipha okujwayelekile komshini. “*Umuntu ngumuntu—*”

Bese kuthula nya. Hhayi ukuthula okuncane kwesihlalo esisodwa esikhululwayo. Ukuthula konke. Ukuthula okuphelele, okukhalayo, okungukuphelela kwegumbi elingenamuntu.

Ikheji yenyuka amamitha amahlanu engeziwe nge-kernel eyayazi isenzo esisodwa. *Phezulu. Phezulu.*

Yabe seyiqhekeza izinja zaphezulu.

---

UGuardian waphuma emhlabathini wangena ekuseni okusele kwesabasa njengento ehudulwa ibuyiswa ekufeni, okwakuyikho ngempela.

Izandla zikaVenter zabamba i-gauntlet umqondo ka-Arin ungakafiki eqinisweni lokukhanya kwemini, ixhegu lihudula ngamandla labenza sengathi alinawo, lithuka phansi ngokuqhubekayo, lihudula umshini owonakele phezu kwesigodo somqele ngamandla nangokholo. Ikheji yaqaqamba. I-winch yamemeza ndawana thize. Bese i-suit yawela ngaphesheya, isabalala phezu kwedeki lensiza yasekhanda ekukhanyeni okumpunga-golide, intuthu yenyuka emapuletini ashisiwe ingena ekuseni okubandayo.

Abasindile abayisithupha babelapho. Izisebenzi. UPriya, ubuso bumhlophe ngemuva komshini wokuxhumana. Uthango lwabantu ababefike ukuzobukela umsebenzi wokuthuthwa kodwa esikhundleni salokho babebukela indoda idonswa iphila iphuma emhlabathini.

I-suit ayizange inyakaze.

“Arin.” UVenter wasebenza izivalo zehelmethi, izixhakaxhaka ayebuke u-Arin ezisebenzisa izikhathi eziyikhulu engakaze azithinte. “*Praat met my.* Khuluma nami. Ukhona lapho phakathi—”

Isivalo savuleka ngokuqhuma kwomfutho ongaphezulu nephunga lensimbi eshisayo nokuthize okuphilayo ngaphansi kwakho, i-substrate iphekwe yadlula inombolo eyayingaxoxisani.

Amehlo ka-Arin avuleka kancane. Wayebheke esibhakabhakeni esimpunga. Endaweni lapho izwi lalikhona khona.

“Sebahambile,” kusho. Izwi lakhe lalonakele. “Ngabatshela ukuthi bame. Abazange.” Umlomo wakhe wasebenza uzungeza okuthize wehluleka ukuthola umumo wakho. Wayeka ukuzama.

UVenter waguqa phezu kwakhe entuthwini nasekuseni, leli xhegu elikhulu kakhulu elaligunyaze izinto ezingaphephile iminyaka engamashumi amane laphatha ukuthula kwakho, kwathi akasho lutho, ngoba kwakungekho okungashiwo, futhi uJakobus Venter wayefunde kudala ukuthi into eyodwa eyiqiniso phambi kokufa ukuba khona kukho. Wabeka esinye isandla sakhe esikhukhumele sifohlozeke esicabheni sesifuba somshini ofile.

Bamkhipha u-Arin e-suit lapho phezu kwedeki, obala, ekukhanyeni. Wayengakwazi ukuma. Bambamba phakathi kwabo, le ndoda eyayichithe impilo yayo yonke yenqaba ukubanjwa, futhi wabavumela, ngoba kwakungasekho uhlobo lwakhe olwaluseluvumela ukwenqaba.

Wabheka uVenter.

“I-badger ithi sawubona,” kusho.

UVenter waba nokuthula impela.

“Ungitshele ukuba ngikutshele. Wathi uzokwazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

Ubuso bexhegu benza okuthize obabungakaze bukwenze isikhathi eside kakhulu. Waphendukela elangeni eliphumayo ukuze abanye bangaboni, amahlombe akhe anyakaza kanye, futhi umsindo awenza wawumncane waqhekeka ngakusasa wanyamalala. Esephenduka amehlo akhe ayemanzi futhi akazange enze sengathi cha.

“*Ja,*” kusho uVenter ngokuhwaqayo. “Ngiyazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

UPriya wafinyelela ku-Arin kuqala. Naye akazange asho lutho. Wamgona ngezingalo zakhe wabambelela, futhi wema phakathi nendawo—abasindile, izisebenzi, unjiniyela omdala, owesifazane owayekalibhulethe i-neural link ngezandla zakhe—akazange amonyuke.

Wabheka emuva kanye, e-suit. Eshisiwe. Eyephukile. Intuthu inciphela ize iphele emoyeni obandayo. I-substrate engaphakathi kwayo ipholela ezingeni lezwe.

“Ngizokubhala phansi,” kusho. “Konke.”

---


# Ibhokisi Elimnyama

Imininingwane yavela ezicucwini njengento ekhishwe ethuneni.

Basisusa isudi ku-headgear deck ngeforklift, kungekho noyedwa onenhliziyo yokusithwala, basibeka phezu kwetapolo esheshini lokukhanda elinguNombolo 4. Usuku lonke, akekho owasithinta. Iqembu lokutakula lika-AugmenTech lafika ngoLwesine. Kwakuyibo kanye abantu ababehlelelwe ukuphatha ukuvakasha kwe-IAOC, manje sebephethe uhlobo oluhlukile ngokuphelele lokuhlola. Bathwebula izithombe zokusha kungaphandle kwesudi balinganisa i-hip actuator eyayivaleke ngamamitha angamashumi amabili. Ngolimi olucwebile lwezobuchwepheshe, baqaphela ukwehluleka kwe-Strut 3 nomonakalo wokushisa endaweni yokuhlala, ubufakazi bokulayisheka okweqile okuyingozi.

Lokho ababengakuqondi, ekuqaleni, kwakuyilogi.

Ibhokisi elimnyama likaGuardian lalingelona idivayisi eyodwa; lalibuye liphindaphindeke. Amarekhoda angu-solid-state amane asakazwa kuwo wonke uhlaka, ngalinye libhala umfudlana ofanayo, evikelwe ekushiseni nasekushayisaneni, aklanywa ngu-Arin ukuze asinde kulokho umsebenzisi wawo angeke akwazi. Amathathu kwamane ayephelele. Lelo lesine lase liphekiwe ingxenye kodwa lalisanele imininingwane eyanele ukugcwalisa izikhala kwamanye.

Kwathatha iqembu lobuforensiki eGoli izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ukulakha kabusha.

Babelindele i-telemetry, ingcindezi nezinga lokushisa nokudonswa kwamandla, ukuhlinzwa kwemishini komshini owawucishe wafa.

Lokho abakutholayo kwakuyitranskripthi.

---

I-Court yayingakhulumi ngolimi lomuntu ngesivinini esigcwele; yayicabanga ngama-millisecond, ngokuhambisana, ngamavektha namathuba akalwe ngesisindo ayengasho lutho kunoma ubani owayewafunda emakhaza. Kodwa i-SAGE yayakhelwe ukuba ifundeke. U-Arin wayegcizelele lokho. *Uma ngingakwazi ukufunda lokho ekunqumile, ngeke ngikwazi ukuthemba ukuthi kungani.* Ngakho kuwo wonke umzuzu wokunquma onomthelela, isistimu yayiguqula ukucabanga kwayo ibe yinto umuntu angayilandela. Imodi enensayo. Ingxoxo yangaphakathi eyandisiwe, enikwe isikhathi, igent ngegent.

Iqembu lobuforensiki lafunda imizuzu yokugcina eyishumi nanye yokukhuphuka ngokuthula. Omunye wabo—umhlaziyi omncane okuthiwa uBongani, owayethathe inkontileka ngoba yayikhokha kahle futhi ingasho lutho kuye—wayeka ukufunda ngokuzwakalayo wehluleka ukuqala futhi.

Ilogi yabonisa umzuzu lapho u-Atlas anikeza khona inombolo eqotho.

*Umthwalo wokupholisa weqa umthamo otholakalayo. Kokubili i-substrate nomsebenzisi ngeke kunakekelwe kuze kufike phezulu. Oyedwa.*

Yabonisa uJudge, ezolile futhi esesimweni esisemthethweni kuze kube sekugcineni, enquma ukuthi ubulungiswa busho ukuthini lapho kungekho mphumela onobulungiswa. Yabonisa uMother enqaba ukuba nguye ogcinwa ephila. Yabonisa uWolf ekhuluma, okuwukuphela kwesikhathi kuwo wonke umlando otholakele lapho uWolf akhuluma khona kabanzi, futhi lokho uWolf akushoyo kwakuyilokhu: *Uyiyona nto esadalelwa ukuyivikela. Usongo ukushisa. Kususe kuye. Ukunike yena.*

Yabonisa bephuma oyedwa ngesikhathi.

UJudge wabela imijikelezo yakhe yokugcina ekuhloleni kokugcina, eqinisekisa, ngokumelene nakho konke ukucabanga okunengqondo, ukuthi ukwabiwa kabusha kokupholisa kwakusemthethweni. ULibrarian wacindezela yonke inkumbulo ka-Arin kayifinyele endaweni encane kakhulu engenzeka wayidudulela ku-backup kernel ukuze kube khona okuyosala. UMercury wakhuluma futhi kanye, umlayezo ogcinwe emalogini, oqondiswe kungekho muntu othize, owathi kuphela: *Siyakukholwa. Besilokhu sikukholwa.* UMother wehlisa ubaluleke bezinga lokushisa lekhomba lakhe waqondisa yonke into ohlelweni lokujikeleza kwegazi luka-Arin, wabe eselinda ukuthi ukushisa kuze kuye. UWolf wakhiya i-spar ukuvimbela ukwehluleka okwengeziwe wadedela ukumodela kwakhe usongo ukuze izinsiza zifune i-cooling loop. Oyedwa ngoyedwa, benza ukukhetha, futhi i-substrate ayiphikisanga.
