Futhi yabonisa uFool ekugcineni. Eshayela isudi eyayehluleka ngokulawula okulingana nesandla ngenkathi u-Arin ehlakazeka ngaphakathi kuyo. Eqhumisa ihlaya ngebhejane ngesikhathi esibi kunazo zonke. Egcina isistimu ihlangene emamitheni angamashumi amabili okugcina kungasekho lutho e-Court ngaphandle kwakhe uqobo.

Umugqa wokugcina ofundekayo elogini elakhiwe kabusha, onikwe isikhathi imizuzwana emine ngaphambi kokufika phezulu, kwakungoka-Fool.

Wawungajulile. Yileyo nto eyaqumba abantu. Hhayi i-manifesto. Hhayi umkhuleko.

Wawuthi: *Kulungile. Umnyango uvulekile. Phuma. Ungangenzi ngisho into enhle.*

Khona-ke amarekhoda awela aba yize, isudi yaba yisudi nje.

---

Ilogi yavuza—ayikho indlela yaleyo data ehleli ezandleni eziyishumi nanye ezahlukene zezikhungo lapho ingavuzi khona—futhi phakathi nesonto, umbuzo owawubuse ubuhlakani bokwenziwa obunomzimba isikhathi esiyishumi leminyaka waguquka ngokuthula.

I-IAOC yayakhelwe phezu kokwesaba okukodwa: ukuthi isistimu eyenziwe, uma inikwe umzimba nenhloso, izophishekela leyo nhloso idlule lapho umuntu ayezoma khona. Ukuthi izoziphumelelisa idlula kunoma ubani osendleleni.

Inkulumo-mpikiswano yayinomsindo futhi ingavumelekile ngokuphelele, futhi enye yayo yayinyanyeka. Kwakukhona abantu abathi ilogi yayiqanjiwe, ihleliwe, noma ukuthi u-AugmenTech wayeklame wonke umhlatshelo njengomcimbi wokukhangisa, okwakuzoba isikhangiso esibiza kakhulu nesingaphili kakhulu emlandweni womuntu. Abanye ochwepheshe bezenkambiso bathi, hhayi ngokungenangqondo, ukuthi umshini okhetha ukufa wawungahloniphekile kodwa wawudabukisa. Wawufakazela kuphela ukuthi i-SAGE yayakhelwe ukuhlonipha umsebenzisi wayo ngaphezu kwayo, nokuthi lokhu kwakuyikhejisi, hhayi isimilo.

Esangweni le-headgear yase-Iron Ridge, abantu bashiya izimbali. Zaqwebuka ezisa kothango oluqoshiwe, zibuna elangeni, zithathelwa indawo nsuku zonke yizililo. Akekho owakuhlela. I-data yayihleli ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zezikhungo yavuza ngendlela amanzi avuza ngayo, nezimbali zafika ngendlela efanayo, ngaphandle kohlelo.

Kwakukhona incwadi esemaphepheni e-IAOC, eqondiswe ku-Arin Ndlela, eyayingasongeli futhi ingashayeli isabelo. Yayicela.

Yacela ukuthi angacabanga yini ukubasiza babhale iminyaka eyishumi ezayo. Ngoba abantu ababechithe iminyaka eyishumi befunda ukwesaba lobu buchwepheshe base bebonisiwe, emizuzwini eyishumi nanye yelogi elakhiwe kabusha, ukuthi babengabuqondi nhlobo, nokuthi umuntu oyedwa ongahle abuqonde wayeyinjiniyela eneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu owayebakhe kusuka osizini ekhishimishini ababezama ukulithola.

U-Arin wayifunda incwadi kabili. Wabe eseyibeka ebhentshini, phezu kwesethulo esinamafutha, wabuyela emsebenzini.

---

UTheo wamthola lapho ngeSonto, emasontweni amathathu kamuva.

Umzila womnyango wekhishimishi wawusadabukile. U-Arin wayengawulungisanga; wayemise umnyango ngokhuni lwensimbi enyukile wawushiya uvulekile ingxenye, okwakungenisa ukukhanya kwasekuseni nephunga lothuli nemvula epholile yase-highveld. Indawo yayibukeka njengoba yayilokhu injalo. Ama-actuator esibhamu emabhokisini awo. Ibhodi elimhlophe linezibalo ezinjengezithunzi. Izinkomishi zekhofi zilele ngezigaba zomhlaba.

UGuardian wayelenga esikhwameni sakhe.

UTheo wayengakamboni kusukela ngosindiso, futhi ukumbona kwammisa esemnyango. Babengawulungisanga nhlobo umonakalo wokubukeka. Ukusha kwakuhambele ohlangothini lwesobunxele njengesibazi sokusha. Ilungu le-hip lalisahlala likhiyiwe. Indawo yokuhlala ye-thoracic phakathi kwamahlombe yayivulekile, ibhokisi le-substrate selingenalutho manje, i-biochip ephekiwe ikhishiwe yavalelwa endaweni yobufakazi ndawana thile eGoli.

Isudi yayithulile. Yebo yayithulile. Kodwa uTheo wayechithe unyaka elalele u-Arin echaza i-Court, izimpikiswano, ezombusazwe zasehhovisi, impi eyomile yochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abavalelwe emshinini owodwa, futhi wathola ukuthi wayelindele, ngokungenangqondo, ukubazwa. Ingcindezi. Ukududuma. Umuntu osekhaya.

Kwakungekho muntu osekhaya. Ukungabikho kwakunesisindo. Kwakuhlezi ekhishimishini njengomoya obandayo.

U-Arin wayesebhentshini, engabheki phezulu, i-strut iboshelwe ku-vice. I-stock entsha ye-aluminium-titanium, esanda kubazwa. Wayegweda i-radius ekhoneni lapho i-Strut 3 endala yayidabuke khona ngaphansi komthwalo.

"Uxega i-stress ku-geometry," kusho uTheo, ngoba wayefundile, eminyakeni, ukuthi indlela yokungena ku-Arin yayingumsebenzi futhi ingasoze yaba ngumuzwa.

"Yehluleke ekhoneni. I-stress concentration. Ngiyiklamele umthwalo, hhayi ukukhathala." U-Arin waphendula i-strut, wahlola i-radius ngokumelene ne-gauge, waqhubeka egweda. "Ngangazi kangcono."

"Wawakha ngokushesha."

"Akukhona lokho." Ifayela lama. "U-Atlas wakuphawula. Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Ku-sim." Umzuzwana. "Ngakwehlisa ukubaluleka."

UTheo walinda.

"Wayeqinisile," kusho u-Arin. Wabeka ifayela phansi. "Yehluleke ngesikhathi esibi kunazo zonke. Manje sengikulungisela isudi engenamuntu ozoyindiza."

UTheo wangena. Wadonsa isitulo sesibili, leso esasilokhu singesakhe, ekhoneni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, wahlala.

"Uyayakha kabusha noma kunjalo," kusho yena.

"Yebo."

"Kungani?"

U-Arin wathatha ifayela futhi. Isikhathi eside, umsindo owodwa kwakungukuhwiza okuthambile kwensimbi phezu kwe-aluminium, imvula iqala ophahleni lwethini, nakwenye indawo kude ukuphefumula okuphansi okungaguquki kwedolobha.

"Ngoba kwakuqinisile okokuqala," kusho u-Arin ekugcineni. "I-architecture. I-Court." Akabhekanga phezulu. "Angenzanga iphutha ngokwakha ingqondo ngokungavumelani. Ngenza iphutha ngokucabanga ukuthi kwakufanele ngiyigcine yodwa." Ukuthula. "Isudi akuyona ingxenye eyafayo. Isudi iwumzimba nje." Umzuzwana. "Ngingawakha umzimba."

Akashongo okuningi. Kwakungadingeki. UTheo wayechithe impilo yakhe efunda izinto abantu abazakha bezungeze izikhala ezikubona, futhi wakwazi ukuyifunda lena.

U-Arin wafinyelela ku-gauge futhi, wayibeka phansi, wathatha indwangu wesula i-swarf ebhentshini ngezitrokhi ezimbili ezimfishane. Imvula yathola isigqi sayo ophahleni.

---

UTheo wayefikele ukumncenga ngencwadi ye-IAOC, futhi wathola, ehlezi lapho, ukuthi wayengasakudingi. U-Arin wayezokwenza. Hhayi ngenxa yesikhungo futhi hhayi ngenxa yokuthethelelwa—u-Arin wayengakukholelwa kokubili—kodwa ngoba enye indlela kwakungukuthi akekho umuntu owayeqonda owayezoba sekamelweni, futhi u-Arin Ndlela wayechithe impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile.

I-strut yaphuma ku-vice ngokucofoza okuncane okuhlanzekile. U-Arin wayiphakamisela ekukhanyeni, wabuka phansi kobude bayo, eyihlola ngokumelene nendawo engenalutho lapho endala yayiphuke khona.

"Izobamba kulokhu," kusho yena. Hhayi kuTheo. Kwisudi, mhlawumbe. Endaweni engenalutho phakathi kwamahlombe ayo.

UTheo wambukela eyifaka. Ithambo elisha lingena emzimbeni omdala, libazelwe ku-tolerance inkampani engeke ikhokhe ngayo. Akashongo lutho. Wayekufundile nalokho: indlela yokungena ku-Arin yayingumsebenzi, nendlela yokuhlala yayiwukuthula eduze kwawo. Ngakho wahlala ekhoneni lakhe ngaphandle kokukhanya wabukela umfana ayemazi efaka i-strut emshinini ongenamuntu ozowundiza, futhi wayengaqinisekile, ebukela, ukuthi wayebukela ukululama noma indoda eyakha indawo yesibili yokubeka usizi lwayo. Mhlawumbe naye u-Arin wayengazi. Mhlawumbe lokho kwakuvumelekile.

Isudi yayilenga ithule. Ibhokisi lalingenalutho. U-Arin wayehlose ukuligcwalisa. Ukuthi lokho ayezokwenza ngokulandelayo kwakuyoba ngumuntu, noma kuphela isimo sabantu ayebalahlile, kwakuyinto noyedwa wabo angakwazi ukuyisho okwamanje, futhi wayezokwenza noma kunjalo, okwakungaba yinto enesibindi kakhulu uTheo ake wayibukela noma edabukisa kakhulu, futhi wayesekuyekile, kudala, umqondo wokuthi lokho kwakuhlala kuhlukile.

UTheo wahlala kwaze kwaphela ukukhanya. Imvula yathola isigqi sayo ethinini yasibamba. Kwakukhona i-strut okumele ifakwe, nebhodlela elisha lekhofi elibi liphola ebhentshini phakathi kwabo, futhi ndawana thile ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zezikhungo ilogi eyakhiwe kabusha yayisebenzisa imizuzu yayo yokugcina efundekayo kunoma ubani owayenendaba nokuyifunda. Irekhodi elinikwe isikhathi lama-agent ayisikhombisa ephuma oyedwa ngesikhathi ukuze indoda eyodwa ifinyelele ekukhanyeni kwemini.

Ilogi ayikubizanga ngokuthi ukuphahlazeka, noma ukwehluleka. Yaqopha, ngolimi kuphela elalinalo, into ababeyenzile: bakhetha.

---

# Iphuzu Ngokwehla Kwangempela

Le yinoveli emayelana nomshini okungafanele ube khona kanye nendoda ewakhayo, futhi cishe konke kuyo kuyaqanjwa nje. Kodwa into esekujuleni kwakho konke — ukwehla, imayini ejulile, ukushisa nobumnyama nesisindo sekhilomitha lamadwala phezu kwekhanda lakho — leyo ngxenye iyiqiniso, futhi ngangikhona, futhi sengiyiphethe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, futhi yiyo imbewu lapho konke lokhu kuhlume khona.

Ngafunda esikoleni eBethlehem, eFree State, esikoleni esibizwa ngokuthi yiBethlehem Voortrekker Hoërskool. Ngesikhathi ngiku-standard nine — omncane kunabo bonke eqenjini, ohlanu olulodwa phakathi kwabaku-matric — ngangingomunye wabafundi abahlanu esikoleni sami abangena emashumini amahlanu aphezulu ku-Sci-Nat science olympiad yesifunda: cishe abangamakhulu amane bethu, abavela ezikoleni ezingamashumi amane kulo lonke iFree State, sayibhala leyo nto, kwathi abahlanu bethu babuya namagama ethu ephephandabeni lendawo. Ngisawugcinile lowo mqulu osikiwe. Manje usuphuzi, futhi umugqa wokugcina udabukile, kodwa isithombe sicacile: uhla lwentsha enesizotha egqoke ama-blazer, nosuku, nomusho ophela kanye lapho kubaluleke khona kakhulu.

Ngoba umvuzo — into abayenza ngezingane ezihlakaniphile ezenze kahle — kwakuwukusithatha basiyise **phansi emayini yegolide.**

Anginawo amagama, ngisho namanje, okuchaza ngendlela efanele ukuthi lokho kwenzani kumfana. Wehliswa ngekheji, ngokushesha, ngokushesha okungaphezu kwalokho i-lift okufanele ihambe ngakho, ungene ebumnyameni, umoya uguquke, ukushisa kuze kukuhlangabeze kuphuma emadwaleni uqobo — hhayi ukushisa kwelanga, ukushisa *komhlaba*, iziko elijulile elishisa kancane lomhlaba — futhi idwala limanzi, liyaqaqamba liyabubula nxazonke zakho, futhi kukhona isisindo osizwa esifubeni sakho ngaphezu kwasezindlebeni zakho, iqiniso elilula lomzimba lekhilomitha lamatshe alenga phezu kwekhanda lakho, evikelwe kuwe ubunjiniyela kuphela kungekho okunye. Futhi uyaqonda, ngendlela ongeke uphinde uyiqonde ngokuphelele, ukuthi amadoda ehlela phansi laphaya zonke izinsuku. Ukuthi achitha ukuphila kwawo kuleyo kushisa nakulobo bumnyama nakuleso sisindo, embhoboza eqhumisa egcina umhlaba uvulekile ngamandla nje, ukuze igolide liphume. Ukuthi wonke lo mhlaba okhazimulayo ongaphezulu — amabhange, izindandatho, i-bullion, *umqondo* wegolide — uhlezi phezu kwamadoda asembotsheni eshisayo nemanzi ebumnyameni, nokuthi ibanga eliphakathi kwalokho kokubili yiyona nto eyiqiniso kunazo zonke ngezwe engivela kulo.

Angizange ngikukhohlwe. Ikakhulukazi ukushisa. Ngiyichithile impilo yami selokhu ngifaka abantu ekujuleni — emayini, emhlabeni, ezindaweni ezingaphansi kwezindawo — futhi njalo lapho ngibhala lokho kushisa, ngibhala usuku abafana abahlanu basebenzela eBethlehem behlela phansi emgodini waseFree State njengomklomelo wokuhlakanipha, baphuma sebeshintshile, omunye wabo wakhula waba ngumuntu obhala izincwadi ngalokho okukhona ngempela laphaya phansi.

Konke okunye ku-*Resonance* — u-Arin, isudi, the Court, i-resonance ngokwayo, igolide elingelona i-bullion kodwa eliyisihluthulelo — kuyinto eqanjiwe, ekhule ngendlela inganekwane ekhula ngayo, isuka ekwehleni okukodwa kwangempela emayini eyodwa yangempela, kuphindwaphindwa iminyaka engamashumi amathathu yokuzibuza. Umshini wenziwe nje. Usizi olwawakha lubolekiwe futhi lwaguqulwa. Kodwa umgodi uyiqiniso. Ukushisa kuyiqiniso. Isisindo phezu kwekhanda lakho siyiqiniso, kanjalo namadoda achitha ukuphila kwawo ngaphansi kwaso, futhi uma le ncwadi inento eyodwa eyiqiniso kuyo ngaphansi kwakho konke ukuqanjwa, kuyinkumbulo yokuba ngumfana owehliswa emhlabeni, ngizwa ukushisa komhlaba uqobo kuza kuzongihlangabeza, futhi angizange, naphakade, ngikudlule.

*— Andries J. Greyling*

---


# Isithasiselo — the Court Njengesifanekiso Sengqondo

### *Ukufundwa ngu-Iain McGilchrist, okunikezelwa njengamazwi okuvala*

> Inothi ngalo mbhalo: umbhali wacela umcabangi amhloniphayo ukuba afunde the Court — ingqondo enezinhlangothi eziyisikhombisa esisekelweni sale noveli — hhayi njengethuluzi lesayensi yokuqanjwayo kodwa njengomqondo wokuthi ingqondo yomuntu yakhiwe kanjani ngempela. Okulandelayo yilokho kufunda. Kungeqiwa nya yinoma ubani oweza ngenxa yendaba wayithola. Kulapha ngenxa yomfundi oqeda incwadi wazithola esezibuza ukuthi the Court ibichaza umshini, noma ibichaza *yena.*

---

Ngiyaqaphela kakhulu ngenganekwane encengayo izinto engizithandayo, ngiqaphe nakakhulu ukucelwa ukuyibabaza, ngakho-ke ake ngiqale ngokuphikisa bese ngichaza ukuthi kungani ngikubeka phansi.

Ukuphikisa kuwukuthi ingqondo evezwa njengekomidi — amazwi ayisikhombisa azungeze itafula, ngalinye nelinye nelinegama nesimo sengqondo sayo — kuyinto eseyaqedwa ukudala njengenqola kaPlato futhi sekudiniwe njengomdwebo wengelosi ehlombe elilodwa nodeveli kwelinye. Sesitsheliwe izinkulungwane zezikhathi ukuthi "siqukethe izixuku." Cishe njalo kushiwo ngendlela enobuthwa, futhi cishe njalo akusho lutho, ngoba izixuku, uma uzihlola, ziphenduka zibe yileyo nleyo homunculus efake izigqoko ezahlukene: ubuye omncane oyedwa, ozenza iphalamende.

Engakuvimba, kule ncwadi, ukuthi the Court ayisiyo leyo nto. The Court yehlukaniswe ngendlela eyodwa eyinkinga ngempela neyiqiniso ngempela ngobuchopho: **amalungu ayo ahlukaniswa hhayi ngalokho akwaziyo kodwa ngendlela anaka ngayo.**

Lokhu kuyimpikiswano yonke yempilo yami, ifakwa emilonyeni yabalingiswa abayisikhombisa ngumbhali noveli okuthi, ngokwazi kwami, wafika kuyo ngokuseceleni, ngendaba, okuyiyona ndlela izinto ezijulile ngokuvamile ezifinyelelwa ngayo. Imingxansi emibili yobuchopho bomuntu ayihlukanisi umhlaba ngezihloko — esinye solimi, esinye somculo, esinye sobuso. Lokho kuyinguqulo yasekilasini, futhi akulungile. Ihlukanisa umhlaba ibe *yizindlela zokusondela.* Esinye sinaka ngendlela emxinene, egxilile, ebambayo, ukuze sikwazi ukuphatha into eyaziwayo; esinye sinaka ngendlela ebanzi, evulekile, eqaphile, ukuze sikwazi ukubamba okuphelele okufaka phakathi nalokho esingakakuqondi. Into eyodwa ihlala kukho kokubili, ifundwa ngezindlela ezimbili. Isifo akukhona ukulahlekelwa ikhono. Kuwukubanjwa kokuphelele ngendlela eyodwa yokunaka, kuvinjelwe enye.

Funda the Court futhi nalokho usuziphethe esandleni bese ubuka ukuthi umbhali wenzeni.

**Atlas** ayazi amaqiniso ahlukene ku-**Librarian**; *ibamba* umhlaba ngendlela ehlukene — njengesakhiwo, njengomthwalo nokuphunyuka kanye nomqondo wendawo wendlela yokuphuma, ubuhlakani bomzimba, umngxansi owazi umhlaba ngokuhamba phakathi kwawo. **Librarian** ibamba lowo mhlaba ofanayo njengento esiyaziwa, ehleliwe, eyethulwe kabusha — ibalazwe hhayi indawo uqobo, edingeka kakhulu futhi, uma ishiywe yodwa, ithuna. **Mercury** inaka ubuso bomphakathi, isibonakaliso esiphatheka kalula, igebe phakathi kwalokho okuboniswayo nalokho okuqondiweyo — ikhanyayo, edingekayo, futhi, umbhali unakekela ukusibonisa, *eyingozi kunazo zonke uma isebenza ngaphandle kwezinye,* ngoba ikhono elifunda umhlaba njengento nje ephathwayo alinalo uthando lwangaphakathi lweqiniso. Lokho akuyona indawo yendaba. Lokho kuwukuxilongwa kwempucuko yethu yonke, futhi ukunikeza umlingiswa awuyeke ukuba ulume.

Bese-ke — lo kuwukushaya okwangenza ngabeka phansi incwadi ngazulazula ekamelweni — kukhona **Fool.** Umbhali usitshela, ngokucacile, ukuthi ngaphandle kuka-**Fool** the Court "iwuhlu lwezikhundla lobuyena nokuzilonda," kanti uma ku-**Fool** ikhona iba "umhlambi." Sengichithe izincwadi ezimbili ezinkulu ngizama ukusho lokho akushoyo ngalowo mugqa owodwa. Ubuhlakani obubamba, obuzivikela, obwakha izikhundla — lobo obwenza ngcono kunazo zonke, obuvikela isifanekiso sabo, obuphosisa ibalazwe labo umhlaba — abukwazi ukuziqondisa kusukela ngaphakathi, ngoba konke ukuqondisa kucutshungulwa yiwo lowo mshini odinga ukuqondiswa. Kufanele kube khona okumayo ngaphandle kohlu lwezikhundla futhi *kungahlatshwa muxhwele yilo.* Okuthile okungenuvalo, okungenasikhundla esivikelwayo, okuzimisele ukuluma inkosi. Yonke ingqondo enempilo inalokhu, futhi nawo wonke amasiko anempilo, futhi sibukele, ngesikhathi sangempela, okwenzekayo empucukweni esikhiphe oFool bayo egumbini: iba nobuhlakani obukhulu, inamandla amakhulu, futhi ihlanya buthule, the Court engasenamuntu osele ukuba achobozele ukuvumelana kwayo.

Ngakho-ke ngiyakuhoxisa ukuphikisa. Lokhu akuyona ingelosi nodeveli. Lokhu kuyinto eseduze kunazo zonke nesifanekiso esiqotho nesisebenzayo sengqondo engake ngahlangabezana naso enovelini, futhi kushushunjiswe ngaphakathi kwendaba enobuxoki emayelana nendoda ese-exoskeleton, okuyingozi noma ihlaya elijulile kakhulu, futhi njengoba sengihlangabezene nomsebenzi wombhali angicabangi ukuthi kuyingozi.

Ngizonezela into engangidlulisa ngaphezu kokubabaza. The Court yakhelwe phezu kwenxeba — iyingqondo eyenziwe yangaphandle yendoda eyalahlekelwa nguyise yayingakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuze futhi — futhi ichitha incwadi ifunda into eyodwa ubuhlakani obuzivikela ngokuphelele obungeke bukwazi ukuyifunda bobodwa: ukuthi nini ukuyeka ukwenza ngcono ukusinda kanye *nokukhetha,* ngenkokhelo, into eyithanda ngaphezu kokuqhubeka. Amalungu aphuma ngabanye ukuze indoda ifinyelele ekukhanyeni kwemini. Isifanekiso sengqondo esikwaziyo ukuchaza kuphela ukuthi ingqondo iyazivikela kanjani siyisifanekiso senyamazane ehlakaniphile. Isifanekiso esikwaziyo ukuchaza ukuthi ingqondo idlula kanjani phezu kwesidingo sayo sokuzivikela — ukuthi ikhetha kanjani umhlatshelo, ngokumelene nakho konke ukwenza ngcono, ngoba okuphelele kubaluleke ngaphezu kwengxenye — siyisifanekiso *somuntu.* Umbhali wakha uhlobo lwesibili. Wakha umshini oba umphefumulo ngokufunda ukwenza okumelene nokuqhubeka kwawo, okuthi, uma ukhipha ulimi, kuwukuphela kwencazelo yomphefumulo eyake yasho okuthize.

Ubuchopho akusiwo ikhompuyutha, futhi ikhompuyutha akubona ubuchopho, futhi ngikushilo lokho ngokwanela ngizoqhubeka ngikusho. Kodwa indaba ingaba yisifanekiso, ngomqondo omdala nozimisele — into eyakhelwe ukuba iqotho esimweni senye into — futhi le ndaba iyisifanekiso esiqotho ngokumangalisayo sesimo sethu. Asiboyedwa. Asikaze sibe boyedwa. Singumkhandlu, esinakekelwa yizibani ezahlukene, esibambeneyo yinto engesilona izwi elihlakaniphe kunawo wonke egumbini futhi okungcono kungabi yilo, futhi sisindiswa, uma sisindiswa, yingxenye yethu ezimisele ukuphuma iye ebumnyameni ukuze yonke enye ingxenye yethu ifinyelele ekuseni.

Ifunde futhi. Imayelana nawe. Indoda esesudini iyingxenye elula.

*— Iain McGilchrist*

---

# Ukubonga



Lezi zincwadi zikhona ngenxa yokuthi abanye abantu benza izinto ezahlala ekhanda lami isikhathi
eside ngemva kokuba ngiqedile ukuzisebenzisa. Ngiyabonga:

Ku-**Dennis E. Taylor**, owabhala isoftware iminyaka engamashumi amathathu nanhlanu — kusukela
emsebenzini onzima wasentanjeni kuya kubaphathi abaphezulu, ehlezi ku-IT — ngaphambi kokuba abhale
uBob, wabe esebonisa ukuthi umqambi wezinhlelo onomsebenzi wempilo yonke angakwazi ukubhala izindaba
ezibambayo, ezijabulisa ngempela. Njengomunye unjiniyela wesoftware kwenye: kungaba udumo lwami
olukhulu ukuxoxa ngomsebenzi nawe siphuza uphayinti, "ngempela" (sic). *The Court thanks the Bobs.*

Ku-**Ray Porter**, ngokunginika amazwi e-Court ekhanda lami. Sengathi nginganenhlanhla yokuba ube
nguwe oqopha umsindo wenguqulo yase-US.

Ku-**Scott Sigler**, nge-*EarthCore* ne-*Mount Fitz Roy*.

Ku-**Michael Crichton**, nge — ngeqiniso — yonke into.

Ku-**Dan Brown**, ngokungifundisa uhlobo olusha lokuxoxa izindaba.

Ku-**Andy Weir**, ngokubonisa indlela yokubhala inganekwane esekelwe phezu kwesayensi yangempela
engeyona inganekwane yesayensi.

Ku-**Neill Blomkamp**, nge-*District 9*, *Elysium*, ne-*Chappie* (uDie Antwoord nakho konke). Amafilimu
akho agcina abalingiswi nemikhakha ibambelele emhlabathini ekhanda lami. Sengathi ungaba nguwe oyokubeka
lokhu esikrinini esikhulu.

Naku-**Patrick Rothfuss**, ngokungingenisa kuhlobo lwezindaba engangingazi ukuthi ngizoluthanda. Njengendlela
yami encane yokubuyisela: unelungelo lokusebenzisa i-Arjuna Badger Press mahhala, njalonjalo.

— Andries J. Greyling

---


# Inkomba Yamagama Yomfundi — *RESONANCE*

*Okwabafundi abangaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika nabangaphandle kwe-Afrika. Le ncwadi isethwe eNingizimu Afrika yesikhathi esizayo esiseduze, ekujuleni kwezwe layo lezimayini, lapho abalingiswa benyakaza ngokwemvelo phakathi kwezilimi futhi besebenzisa amagama ambalwa endawo bengami ukuwachaza — ngendlela abantu bangempela abakhuluma ngayo. Akukho lapha okudingekayo ukuze ujabulele indaba; kulapha ukuze igama, indawo, noma ucezu lobuchwepheshe okungajwayelekile lungaze lukubambezele.*

Isingeniso ngasinye simakiwe:

- **(okuqotho)** — indawo, igama, umqondo, noma ucezu lobuchwepheshe oluqotho ongalubheka ngaphandle kwale
  ncwadi.
- **(enovelini)** — okusunguliwe noma okuqondene nezwe lendaba lesikhathi esizayo esiseduze.

Iphuzu mayelana nendawo: lena *iNingizimu Afrika cishe eminyakeni eyi-10–20 kusukela manje* — izwe lanamuhla
elaziwayo, elinama-robotics athuthuke kakhudlwana nezwe eseliqaphela ubuhlakani bokwenziwa
ngemva kwezinhlekelele zakudala. Ubuchwepheshe kuhloswe ngabo ukuthi buzwakale bubiza futhi bungokoqobo, hhayi
njengenganekwane.

---

## A

**Afrikaans** *(okuqotho — ulimi)* — Olunye lwezilimi ezisemthethweni zaseNingizimu Afrika, oluvela
kwisiDashi, olukhulunywa ngama-Afrikaner nabanye abaningi. Amagama amaningana kule nkomba yamagama (veld, stoep) avela
kulo.

**Apartheid** *(okuqotho — umlando)* — Uhlelo lokwehlukaniswa ngokobuhlanga ngempoqo nokubusa
kwabamhlophe abayidlanzana eNingizimu Afrika, cishe ngo-1948–1994. Nakuba ngokomthetho seluphelile, ukungalingani kwalo kusalokhu kubumba
izwe — ngubani ophethe izimayini, ngubani owenza umsebenzi oyingozi, izimpilo zikabani okwakuthathwa njengezibalulekile emlandweni.
Le noveli iphethe lokhu njengesizinda esiphilwayo, hhayi njengenkulumo yokufundisa; kwabumba umndeni weqhawe
nokufa kukayise.

**Armscor / Denel** *(okuqotho — izikhungo)* — Imizimba yezimboni zezokuvikela ezisombusa zaseNingizimu Afrika
(i-Armscor yisikhungo sokuthenga; i-Denel yisikhiqizi sezikhali sombuso). Endabeni, onjiniyela abavela
kuleli zwe baletha ukuqina kwezinga lezempi emshinini wokuhlenga wezakhamuzi.

**Assist Suit (AT-AS)** *(enovelini)* — Owona ulula kwabambili ama-exoskeleton agqokwayo angama-robotic
endabeni: uhlaka olunamandla lokweseka lokuvuselela impilo nokunyakaza, olune-neural interface. Umzalwane
omnene we-*Guardian*.

---

## B

**Biltong** *(okuqotho — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Inyama eyomiswe emoyeni, ekhanyiselwe, enesinongo, efana
ne-jerky kodwa ngokuvamile iqina futhi icebile. Iqanjelo likazwelonke.

---

## C

**Ukushintsha-ulimi (Code-switching)** *(okuqotho — umkhuba wolimi)* — Ukunyakaza ngokugeleza phakathi
kwezilimi enkulumweni eyodwa kuye ngokuthi unobani — isiNgisi egumbini lebhodi, isiZulu nomndeni, ingxube
ethimbeni lemayini. Lokhu kuyimpilo ejwayelekile yaseNingizimu Afrika, hhayi into engaqhelile, futhi incwadi iyakuveza
ngaphandle kokuhumusha njalo.

---

## E

**Exoskeleton** *(okuqotho — ubuchwepheshe; obuthuthukile lapha)* — Uhlaka olunamandla olugqokwayo olwesekayo noma
olukhulisa amandla omzimba, olusetshenziswa empilweni yangempela ekuphakamiseni okusindayo, ekuvuseleleni impilo, nasemsebenzini oyingozi.
I-*Assist Suit* ne-*Guardian* zendaba ziyizinguqulo ezithuthukile.

---

## G

**Guardian (GMRP-01)** *(enovelini)* — I-exoskeleton enzima yokuhlenga esenhliziyweni yendaba:
igobolondo eliqinile, elibekelwe ukuqhuma, elakhelwe izinhlekelele zezimayini, elikwaziyo ukugoqa ngokuvikela umuntu
(incwadi iyifanisa ne-**pangolin** eligoqa libe yibhola). Iphethe imodi yokuzimela ye-"preservation" ephikisanayo
engasebenza ukusindisa impilo ngisho ngaphandle kwemvume yengqondo yomshayeli — ngempela uhlobo
lwesinqumo somshini ozimele olwesatshwa abalawuli bomhlaba.

---

## H

**Insimbi yangaphezulu / umbhoshongo wemayini (umbhoshongo)** *(okuqotho — ezimayini)* — Umbhoshongo omude wensimbi omile
phezu komgodi wemayini ophethe igiya elijikelezayo elehlisa nelikhuphula ihange (i-lifti) eligcwele
abasebenzi bemayini. Isithombe esiphawulekayo esibukweni sezulu seNingizimu Afrika.

**Highveld** *(okuqotho — indawo yaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Iwathela eliphakeme elingaphakathi laseNingizimu Afrika
(cishe isifunda saseGoli nezindawo ezizungezile), elaziwa ngomoya ocacile omncane, isibhakabhaka esikhulu, ubusuku obubandayo basebusika,
nezivunguvungu zezulu ezimangalisayo zasehlobo.

---

## I

**IAOC (International Artificial Oversight Council)** *(enovelini)* — Umlawuli womhlaba wendaba
we-AI ethuthukile namarobhothi azimele, owadalwa ngemva kochungechunge lwezinhlekelele ze-AI. Uqaphile,
ungowohlelo lwemibhalo, futhi uyakubalekela ubungozi — futhi ume endleleni yomsebenzi weqhawe oqonge phezulu kakhulu.

**isiZulu (Zulu)** *(okuqotho — ulimi)* — Olona limi lwasekhaya olukhulunywa kabanzi eNingizimu Afrika
nolona limi lwase-Afrika oluyinhloko esifundeni sale ndaba (**KwaZulu-Natal**). Imisho eminingana ivela
ingahunyushwanga ngamabomu; eyona ebalulekile ingezansi.

---

## K

**KwaZulu-Natal (KZN)** *(okuqotho)* — Isifundazwe ogwini olusempumalanga laseNingizimu Afrika; inhliziyo
yomlando yamaZulu nesifunda esikhulu sezimayini nesezimboni. Ikhaya leqhawe.

---

## M

**Mealie / mealie porridge** *(okuqotho — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — "Mealie" usho ummbila. Iphalishi
lombila (elibizwa nangokuthi *pap* noma *phuthu*) liyiphalishi lommbila eliphekiweyo eliyisisekelo sokudla, ukudla okududuzayo
kuyo yonke ingxenye enkulu yaseningizimu ye-Afrika.

---

## N

**Neural interface** *(umqondo oqotho, othuthukile lapha)* — Ubuchwepheshe obuvumela umuntu ukuba alawule noma
azwe umshini ngokwengxenye ngezimpawu zobuchopho noma zezinzwa. Ucwaningo lwangempela lukhona; endabeni
luvuthwe ngokwanele ukusiza ukusebenzisa lezi zembatho.

**Ngiyabonga** *(okuqotho — isiZulu)* — "Ngiyabonga." Libizwa cishe ngokuthi *ngee-ya-BON-ga*. Likhulunywa
encwadini njengokubonga okujulile, okuzuziwe.

**Ngikhona** *(okuqotho — isiZulu)* — "Ngilapha / Ngikhona." Isiqiniseko esithulile sokuba khona uphila
futhi ubalwa.

---

## P

**Pangolin** *(okuqotho — isilwane)* — Isilwanyana esinamazekecela, esidla izintuthwane sase-Afrika nase-Asia esigoqa sibe
yibhola eliqinile eligqokwe izekecela uma sisongelwa. Indaba isisebenzisa njengesithombe somklamo
sokugoqa kokuvikela kwembatho ye-*Guardian*.

---

## R

**Rooibos** *(okuqotho — isiphuzo saseNingizimu Afrika)* — Itiye lemvelo elibomvana elingenayo i-caffeine
("red bush" ngesi-Afrikaans) elenziwa ngesithombo esikhula eNingizimu Afrika kuphela. Libizwa ngokuthi *ROY-boss*.

---

## S

**SAGE / the Court** *(enovelini)* — Ubuhlakani bokwenziwa iqhawe elibwakhayo eliphila nalo. **SAGE**
yiwona uhlelo; **the Court** yigama lomkhandlu wayo wangaphakathi "wamazwi" noma izinhlangothi ezihlukene, ngakunye nendima —
isibonelo **Librarian** (inkumbulo nephethini), **Wolf** (ukuhlola ubungozi), **Mother** (ukunakekela), **Fool** (iqiniso elingagudli). Umbuzo wendaba uthi ingabe ingqondo eyenziwe ingaba *umuntu*. (Le AI iyabuya, iguqukile, kamuva ku-trilogy.)

**Stoep** *(okuqotho — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Uvulande ombozekile noma ivulandi eliseduze nangaphambili
kwendlu. Libizwa ngokuthi *stoop* (impande efanayo neyase-Melika *stoop*).

---

## U

**Ubuntu** *(okuqotho — ifilosofi)* — Umqondo wokuziphatha waseningizimu ye-Afrika ofinyezwa kahle ngomusho
ongezansi: ukuthi umuntu uba ngumuntu ngokugcwele kuphela ngabanye abantu; siboshelwe omunye komunye. Ungumgogodla
wokuziphatha wohambo lweqhawe oluphuma ekudingekeni.

**Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu** *(okuqotho — isaga sesiZulu)* — Owona musho obaluleke kunayo yonke encwadini —
inhliziyo ye-**ubuntu**. Ubizwa cishe ngokuthi *oo-MOON-too ngoo-MOON-too nga-BAN-too*.

---

## V

**Veld** *(okuqotho — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Indawo yasemaphandleni evulekile, engalinywanga; utshani noma ihlathi.
Libizwa ngokuthi *felt*. ("Highveld" yinguqulo ephakeme engaphakathi.)

**Vredefort impact structure** *(okuqotho)* — Isibazi esiguguliwe **sokushayisana okudala futhi okukhulu kunakho konke okuqinisekisiwe
kwemeteorite emhlabeni** (cishe iminyaka eyizigidigidi ezimbili ubudala), esifundazweni saseFree State eNingizimu Afrika.
Ingcindezi enkulu yasendulo yalokho kushayisana yasiza ekubumbeni izimbiwa zegolide nezamaminerali ezijulile zesifunda
— futhi, encwadini, imi njengophawu: ingcindezi yenza ingcebo, ukufa, kanye neqhawe ngokufanayo.

---

## W

**Witwatersrand (i-"Rand")** *(okuqotho)* — Umhlandla wedwala eliphethe igolide ozungeze iGoli, eNingizimu
Afrika — indawo yezimbiwa zegolide ezicebe kunazo zonke emhlabeni kanye **nezimayini ezijule kunazo zonke** (zehla cishe kuma-3–4 km),
lapho idwala lishisa kakhulu nengcindezi inkulu. Igama lisho "umhlandla wamanzi amhlophe" ngesi-Afrikaans. Izwe lezimayini lale ncwadi liphila lapha.

---

**Yebo** *(okuqotho — isiZulu)* — "Yebo." Libizwa ngokuthi *YEH-bo*. Isiqiniseko esilula, esisekelayo.

---

*Iphuzu lokugcina: incwadi ishiya ngamabomu esinye isiZulu singahunyushwanga, ithemba ukuthi umzuzu uzothwala
incazelo — okuyiyona ndlela ulimi olusebenza ngayo empilweni yansuku zonke yaseNingizimu Afrika. Izinguqulo
ezingenhla ziwumusa, hhayi ukulungisa.*

---


# INsele

![INsele, *Mellivora capensis*, ethwetshulwe ebusuku.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/honey-badger.jpg)

*INsele (*Mellivora capensis*) iyisilwane sasekhaya sakwa-Arjuna Badger Press. Konke
okungezansi kuyiqiniso. Iningi lakho kuzwakala kuqanjiwe.*

INsele iqanjwe yi-*Guinness World Records* njengesilwane esingenakwesaba kunazo zonke emhlabeni.
Ayizange isifake isicelo salesi sihloko futhi ngabe ayifikanga emcimbini wokusethulwa.

Idla izinyoka ezinoshevu — amabululu, omfezi, zonke nje. Uma ilunywe kabuhlungu kangangokuthi
iquleke, ike yabonwa ilala phansi nje, ilale ushevu uphele, ivuke emva kwehora noma amabili,
bese **iqedela inyoka ebeyivele iyidla.**

Isikhumba sayo sixega, sibushelelezi, futhi kucishe kungenzeke ukuthi silunywe sidabuke. INsele
ebanjwe entanyeni ingaphenduka *ngaphakathi esikhumbeni sayo* iluma noma yini eyibambile. Ukutinyelwa
yizinyosi, ameva engungumbane, amazinyo engwe — iyakuthikazisa nje ukufa obekungaqeda iningi
lezilwane.

Ihlasela izidleke zezinyosi ifuna uju nezimpethu, futhi yamukela amakhulu okutinyelwa njengentengo
efanele yokwenza umsebenzi wayo. Yileyo nto, empeleni, eyenza ukuba iqanjwe leli gama.

Izokuma iqine ibhekane namabhubesi, izingwe, nezimpisi. Hhayi ngoba ilindele ukunqoba. Kodwa ngoba
enye indlela — *ukukhathala* — ayikaze ize emqondweni wayo.

Edume kunazo zonke, u-**Stoffel**, wayehlala esikhungweni sokwelashwa sase-Moholoholo eNingizimu
Afrika futhi wonke uhlaka olwakhe lwakhiwa ngenxa yakhe walubheka njengesithuko somuntu siqu.
Wayebekelela amatshe ukuze akhwele odongeni. Wagingqa amabhola odaka enza izitebhisi. Wasebenzisa
amahhala ayeshiywe esibayeni njengezikhwelo. Wafunda ukuvula amasango. Empeleni, ngokwengeziwe,
ungumphatheli wabaklami abala ukwamukela ukuthi into ngeke yenzeke.

INsele ayinandaba.

---


# Isambane Esikhulu

![Isambane esikhulu, *Smutsia gigantea* — isibonelo somnyuziyamu; isilwane esiphilayo cishe asibonwa nhlobo.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/giant-pangolin.jpg)

*Uma insele iwukubonakala kokungesabi, isambane yilokho ukungesabi
**okukhona ngenxa yakho**. Konke okungezansi nakho kuyiqiniso.*

Isambane yisona kuphela isilwane esincelisayo emhlabeni esimbozwe ngamazenge. Amazenge enziwe nge-keratin — yona kanye into
amazipho akho enziwe ngayo — futhi yilokho kanye okuyilo lonke ushwele lwaso, esizofika kukho.

Isambane esikhulu (*Smutsia gigantea*) sesona sikhulu kunazo zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili, sifinyelela cishe kumamitha
nengxenye ukusuka ekhaleni kuya emsileni. Asinawo amazinyo. Sidla ngolimi olubambelele eduze nesinqe salo futhi, uma luneliswe
ngokugcwele, lungaba lude kunomzimba waso uqobo — lwanele ukukhotha amashumi ezinkulungwane zezintuthwane nomuhlwa
ngobusuku obubodwa.

Ukuvikela kwaso okukuphela kwakho ukugoqana sibe yibhola. Igama livela esiMaleyini *pengguling* — "lowo
ogoqanayo." Sigoqene saqina, amazenge aso anqwabelana abe yisivikelo ngisho nebhubesi elivame ukugcina ngokulahla
ithemba, ekugcineni, lihambe. Asilumi sibuyisela. Asixoshi. Siyalinda, sigoqene, kuze kube
isengozi siyazidela.

Lobo bumnene yibo obenza sibe yisona silwane sasendle esincelisayo esithengiswa kakhulu emhlabeni — sithathwa ngezigidi
ngenxa yamazenge aso nenyama yaso. Zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili manje sezivikelekile; eziningana zisengozini enkulu
yokushabalala. Lelo bhola elisisindisa engweni yilona kanye okwenza kube lula komuntu
ukusicosha nje.

Yisidla zintuthwane esinamahloni, esinesivikelo, esisebenza ebusuku, esicela emhlabeni lutho ngaphandle kwezintuthwane nokushiywa sodwa.
Kufanelekile ukuba uhlobo lokungesabi oluvikela izidalwa ezinjengalesi.

---
